    B.cyl("bibir_dudukan", (0, 0, h - 0.045), STOOL_R - 0.005, 0.03, B.mat("plastik_dingklik_gelap", (0.04, 0.04, 0.05), 0.4), verts=28, bevel=0, **P)
    for k in range(4):
        a = k * math.pi / 2 + math.pi / 4
        o = B.box("kaki_dingklik", (math.cos(a) * 0.095, math.sin(a) * 0.095, (h - 0.04) / 2), (0.035, 0.035, h - 0.04), m, **P)
        o.rotation_euler = (-math.sin(a) * 0.12, math.cos(a) * 0.12, a)            # kaki sedikit melebar ke bawah
    B.cyl("cincin", (0, 0, 0.05), STOOL_R - 0.01, 0.015, m, verts=20, **P)
    import kit
    kit.ring("tepi_dudukan", (0, 0, h - 0.005), STOOL_R - 0.006, 0.008, m, root, seg=32, tseg=6)      # bibir dudukan membulat
    B.box("lubang_angkat", (0, 0, h + 0.0005), (0.09, 0.022, 0.002), B.mat("plastik_dingklik_gelap", (0.04, 0.04, 0.05), 0.4), bevel=0, **P)
    return root


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ rig
class Rig:
    """Rig tokoh 2D v2.
    akar (posisi) -> hadap (LOCKED_TRACK ke kamera = cara sprite ditampilkan) -> cermin (scale.x +-1: tampak 3/4 kiri/kanan)
      -> kaki/badan, pivot_kepala -> kepala/wajah/mata/alis/mulut, bahu -> lengan_atas -> siku -> lengan_bawah -> tangan -> benda.
    Arah BADAN (dunia) terpisah: c['facing'] = [[frame, [x, y] | None]]; dari sudut badan vs kamera dipilih tampak
    depan / 3/4 / belakang. Benda pegangan & dingklik memakai orientasi dunia (tidak ikut berputar bersama kamera)."""

    def __init__(self, c, rig_dir, cam, emission, look=None):
        self.c, self.look = c, look
        self.meta = json.loads((Path(rig_dir) / "rig.json").read_text())
        if not self.meta.get("arm2"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"rig.json tokoh '{c['name']}' versi lama; jalankan langkah 'tokoh' lagi")
        self.u = c["height"] / self.meta["canvas"][1]
        self.cam, self.emission, self.dir = cam, emission, Path(rig_dir)
        self.vis = {}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- bangun
    def build(self):
        c, u, (W, H) = self.c, self.u, self.meta["canvas"]
        self.W, self.H = W, H
        self.root = B.empty(c["name"], c["loc"][0][1:])
        for f, x, y, z in c["loc"]:
            self.root.location = (x, y, z); self.root.keyframe_insert("location", frame=f)
        B.interp(self.root, "LINEAR")
        contact_shadow(f"bayangan_{c['name']}", self.root, c["height"] * W / H * 0.75)
        self.base_z = -(H - self.meta["foot_y"]) * u
        # Phase 4B-B5: presentasi (skala seragam thd kamera; layar identik) di antara akar dan 'hadap'.
        # Dipakai saat duduk agar bidang sprite berada di depan dudukan DUNIA (dudukan tidak lagi ikut kamera).
        self.pres = _empty(f"presentasi_{c['name']}", self.root)
        self.pres_k = {}
        self.face = _empty(f"hadap_{c['name']}", self.pres, (0, 0, self.base_z))
        con = self.face.constraints.new("LOCKED_TRACK")
        con.target, con.lock_axis, con.track_axis = self.cam, "LOCK_Z", "TRACK_NEGATIVE_Y"
        self.mirror = _empty(f"cermin_{c['name']}", self.face)
        to_local = lambda px, py: ((px - W / 2) * u, (H - py) * u)
        self.to_local = to_local
        nx, nz = to_local(*self.meta["neck"])
        self.head = _empty(f"kepala_{c['name']}", self.mirror, (nx, DEPTH["kepala"], nz))
        # Phase 4C: 'fitur' = wajah/mata/alis/mulut (bukan tengkorak); digeser turun saat menunduk (pitch kepala 2D)
        self.fitur = _empty(f"fitur_{c['name']}", self.head)
        self.eyes = _empty(f"mata_{c['name']}", self.fitur, (0, DEPTH["mata"] - DEPTH["kepala"], 0))
        E, Hd = self.meta["elbow"], self.meta["hand"]
        Wr = self.meta.get("wrist", Hd)
        self.arms, self.elbows, self.hands, self.wrists, self.overlay = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
        for side in (-1, 1):
            sx, sz = to_local(*self.meta["shoulder"][str(side)])
            nm = "lengan_kiri" if side < 0 else "lengan_kanan"
            self.arms[side] = _empty(f"bahu_{nm}_{c['name']}", self.mirror, (sx, DEPTH[nm], sz))
            self.elbows[side] = _empty(f"siku_{nm}_{c['name']}", self.arms[side], (side * E[0] * u, -0.0006, -E[1] * u))
            self.hands[side] = _empty(f"tangan_{nm}_{c['name']}", self.elbows[side],
                                      (side * (Hd[0] - E[0]) * u, -0.01, -(Hd[1] - E[1]) * u))
            # pergelangan (Phase 4B): tangan genggam berlapis berputar di sini; sedikit DI BELAKANG lengan bawah
            # (manset menutup sambungan saat tangan ditekuk)
            self.wrists[side] = _empty(f"pergelangan_{nm}_{c['name']}", self.elbows[side],
                                       (side * (Wr[0] - E[0]) * u, 0.0003, -(Wr[1] - E[1]) * u))
        self.L = {}
        crops = self.meta.get("crops", {})
        for name in self.meta["layers"]:
            grp = name.split("_")[0]
            overlay = None
            if name.startswith("tangan_"):                              # tangan genggam berlapis
                _, style, nm_s, layer = name.split("_")
                side = -1 if nm_s == "kiri" else 1
                S = self.meta["shoulder"][str(side)]
                origin = (S[0] + side * Wr[0], S[1] + Wr[1])
                if layer == "b":
                    parent, dy = self.wrists[side], 0
                else:
                    parent, dy, overlay = self.mirror, 0, (style, side)
            elif name.startswith("lengan_atas_") or name.startswith("lengan_bawah_"):
                side = -1 if "_kiri" in name else 1
                S = self.meta["shoulder"][str(side)]
                if name.startswith("lengan_atas_"):
                    parent, origin = self.arms[side], S
                else:
                    parent, origin = self.elbows[side], (S[0] + side * E[0], S[1] + E[1])
                dy = 0
            elif grp in ("kepala", "wajah", "alis", "mulut", "fx"):
                parent = self.head if grp == "kepala" else self.fitur
                origin, dy = self.meta["neck"], DEPTH.get(grp, -0.01) - DEPTH["kepala"]
            elif grp == "mata":
                parent, origin, dy = self.eyes, self.meta["neck"], 0
            elif grp == "lengan":                                       # lapisan lengan lama (tidak dipakai)
                continue
            else:
                parent, origin, dy = self.mirror, (W / 2, H), DEPTH.get(grp, 0)
            img = bpy.data.images.load(str(self.dir / f"{name}.png"))
            img.colorspace_settings.name = "sRGB"
            rim_crop = None if grp in ("wajah", "mata", "alis", "mulut", "fx") else crops.get(name)   # fitur wajah: bukan siluet
            mat = B.char_mat(f"M_{c['name']}_{name}", img, self.look, rim_crop) if self.look else B.image_mat(f"M_{c['name']}_{name}", img, self.emission)
            o = _plane(f"{c['name']}_{name}", mat, u, origin, (W, H), crops.get(name))
            o.parent = parent; o.location.y = dy
            if grp in ("wajah", "mata", "alis", "mulut", "fx", "tangan"):
                o.visible_shadow = False
            if overlay:                                                    # jari depan: diatur per frame oleh Director
                o.location = (0, 0, 0)
                o.hide_render = o.hide_viewport = True
                o.keyframe_insert("hide_render", frame=1); o.keyframe_insert("hide_viewport", frame=1)
                self.overlay[overlay] = o
                continue
            self.L[name] = o
        return self

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- arah badan -> tampak per frame
    def _xy(self, f):
        return (_eval(self.root, "location", 0, f, self.root.location.x), _eval(self.root, "location", 1, f, self.root.location.y))

    def _cam_xy(self, f):
        return (_eval(self.cam, "location", 0, f, self.cam.location.x), _eval(self.cam, "location", 1, f, self.cam.location.y))

    def views(self, end):
        """-> {frame: (tampak, cermin, lirik)} hanya pada frame perubahan"""
        fac = sorted(self.c.get("facing") or [], key=lambda k: k[0])
        raw = []
        for f in range(1, end + 1):
            tgt = None
            for kf, t in fac:
                if kf <= f:
                    tgt = t
            if tgt is None:
                raw.append(("front", 1, 0, 0)); continue
            px, py = self._xy(f); cx, cy = self._cam_xy(f)
            dx, dy = tgt[0] - px, tgt[1] - py
            vx, vy = cx - px, cy - py
            if math.hypot(dx, dy) < 1e-3:
                raw.append(("front", 1, 0, 0)); continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(vx * dy - vy * dx, vx * dx + vy * dy))
            right = dx * (-vy) + dy * vx                       # >0: badan menghadap ke kanan layar
            m = 1 if right >= 0 else -1
            a = abs(ang)
            if a < VIEW_FRONT:
                raw.append(("front", 1, m if a > 12 else 0, 0))
            elif a < VIEW_BACK:
                prof = 1 if (self.has_profile and PROFILE_BAND[0] <= a <= PROFILE_BAND[1]) else 0
                raw.append(("q", m, 0, prof))
            else:
                raw.append(("back", 1, 0, 0))
        # buang segmen pendek (hindari kedip tampak)
        segs = []
        for f, v in enumerate(raw, start=1):
            if segs and segs[-1][1] == v:
                continue
            segs.append([f, v])
        clean = []
        for i, (f, v) in enumerate(segs):
            nxt = segs[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(segs) else end + 1
            if clean and nxt - f < MIN_SEG and v[0] != clean[-1][1][0]:
                continue
            if clean and clean[-1][1] == v:
                continue
            clean.append([f, v])
        return {f: v for f, v in clean}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- keadaan per frame
    def states(self, end):
        import bl_interact as BI
        V = self.meta["variants"]
        ev = self.c["events"]
        acts = self.c.get("rig_acts", [])
        self.view_keys = self.views(end)
        import schedule as SCH
        eat_cuts = set()
        for a in acts:
            if a["type"] == "eat":
                cyc = a.get("siklus") or [[c0, BI.EAT_CYCLE] for c0 in range(a["f0"], min(a.get("f1", end + 1), end + 1), BI.EAT_CYCLE)]
                for c0, L in cyc:
                    q = lambda k: c0 + int(round(k * L / BI.EAT_CYCLE))
                    eat_cuts |= {q(BI.MOUTH_OPEN[0]), q(BI.MOUTH_OPEN[1]), q(BI.CHEW[1])}
                    eat_cuts |= {q(k) for k in range(BI.MOUTH_OPEN[1], BI.CHEW[1], 4)}   # fase kunyah bergantian
        extra = self.director.change_frames(self) if getattr(self, "director", None) else set()
        import performance as PF
        perf = self.c.get("perf") or {}
        for key in ("mata", "alis"):
            extra |= {f for f, _ in perf.get(key, [])}
        for g0, g1, _, _ in perf.get("gestur", []):
            extra |= {g0, g1}
        cuts = sorted({f for f, _ in ev} | {a["f0"] for a in acts} | {a["f1"] for a in acts if a.get("f1")} | set(self.view_keys)
                      | {f for f in eat_cuts | extra if 1 <= f <= end})
        out, cur, view = [], "none", ("front", 1, 0, 0)
        evi = dict(ev)
        for f in cuts:
            cur = evi.get(f, cur)
            view = self.view_keys.get(f, view)
            if cur == "none":
                st = {"hidden": True}
            else:
                st = dict(V[cur]); st["hidden"] = False
            if not st["hidden"] and st.get("view") == "front":
                st["view"], st["mirror"], st["autolook"], st["profil"] = view
            if not st["hidden"] and st.get("view") != "back":             # kanal performa (Phase 4C)
                ov = PF.override_at(perf.get("mata", []), f)
                if ov == "closed" and st.get("eyes") in ("open", "wide", "happy"):
                    st["eyes"] = "closed"
                elif ov == "open" and st.get("eyes") == "happy":
                    st["eyes"] = "open"
                ob = PF.override_at(perf.get("alis", []), f)
                if ob and st.get("brows") in ("calm", "soft"):
                    st["brows"] = ob
                for g0, g1, hs, g in perf.get("gestur", []):
                    if g0 <= f < g1:
                        st["gestur_" + str(hs)] = g
            sitting = any(a["type"] == "sit" and a["f0"] <= f < a.get("f1", 10 ** 9) for a in acts)
            if not st["hidden"] and st.get("view") == "back":
                st["mirror"] = 1
                legs = st.get("legs", "") if st.get("legs", "").startswith("walk") else ""
                st["sprite"] = "belakang_sit" if sitting else "belakang" + ("_" + legs if legs else "")
                st["sit"] = sitting
            for a in acts:
                if a["f0"] <= f < a.get("f1", 10 ** 9) and not st.get("hidden"):
                    t = a["type"]
                    if t == "sit":
                        st["sit"] = True
                        if st.get("view") != "back":
                            st["legs"] = "sit"
                    if st.get("view") == "back":
                        continue
                    m = st.get("mirror", 1)                      # sisi badan (dunia) -> lengan sprite
                    if t in ("hold", "eat", "cook"):
                        st["arm_" + str(a["side"] * m)] = t
                        if t == "eat" and a.get("benda") in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok"):
                            st["arm_" + str(-a["side"] * m)] = "hold"          # tangan lain memegang piring
                            ph = SCH.cycle_at(f, a.get("siklus") or [[a["f0"], BI.EAT_CYCLE * 1000]])
                            k = ph[0] if ph else None
                            if k is None:
                                pass
                            elif BI.MOUTH_OPEN[0] <= k < BI.MOUTH_OPEN[1]:
                                st["mouth"] = "open"
                            elif BI.MOUTH_OPEN[1] <= k < BI.CHEW[1]:
                                st["mouth"] = "chew" if int((k - BI.MOUTH_OPEN[1]) // 4) % 2 == 0 else "chew2"
                    elif t == "look":
                        st["look"] = a.get("dir", 1)
            out.append((f, st))
        self.state_list = out
        return out

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- kueri per frame (dipakai bl_interact)
    def _state_at(self, f):
        cur = None
        for ff, st in self.state_list:
            if ff <= f:
                cur = st
            else:
                break
        return cur or {"hidden": True}

    def view_at(self, f):
        st = self._state_at(f)
        return st.get("view", "front"), st.get("mirror", 1)

    def profile_at(self, f):
        """Phase 4.1-B: True bila tampak profil (lapisan _s) aktif pada frame f"""
        st = self._state_at(f)
        return bool(st.get("view") == "q" and st.get("profil"))

    @property
    def has_profile(self):
        return "badan_s" in getattr(self, "L", {}) and not self.c.get("tanpa_profil")

    def hidden_at(self, f):
        return bool(self._state_at(f).get("hidden"))

    def look_act_at(self, f):
        for a in self.c.get("rig_acts", []):
            if a["type"] == "look" and a["f0"] <= f < a.get("f1", 10 ** 9):
                return a.get("dir", 1)
        return None

    def facing_at(self, f):
        tgt = None
        for kf, t in sorted(self.c.get("facing") or [], key=lambda k: k[0]):
            if kf <= f:
                tgt = t
        return tgt

    def key_vis(self, o, hide, f):
        if self.vis.get(o.name) == hide:
            return
        o.hide_render = o.hide_viewport = hide
        o.keyframe_insert("hide_render", frame=f); o.keyframe_insert("hide_viewport", frame=f)
        self.vis[o.name] = hide

    @staticmethod
    def _key_const(o, path, idx, f, val):
        getattr(o, path)[idx] = val
        o.keyframe_insert(path, index=idx, frame=f)
        fc = B.fc(o, path, idx)
        for kp in fc.keyframe_points:
            kp.interpolation = "CONSTANT"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- animasi
    def _normalize_acts(self):
        """'pegang' tepat setelah 'makan' dengan benda sama -> tetap di tangan yang memegang piring"""
        acts = self.c.get("rig_acts", [])
        for a in acts:
            if a["type"] == "hold" and a.get("benda"):
                eat = next((e for e in acts if e["type"] == "eat" and e.get("benda") == a["benda"]
                            and abs(e.get("f1", 0) - a["f0"]) <= 2), None)
                if eat and a["benda"] in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok"):
                    a["side"] = -eat["side"]

    def animate(self, end):
        P = self.meta["poses"]
        self._normalize_acts()
        states = self.states(end)
        u = self.u
        arm_keys = {-1: [], 1: []}                           # (frame, [bahu, siku])
        look_keys, sit_keys = [], []
        D = getattr(self, "director", None)
        for i, (f, st) in enumerate(states):
            f_next = states[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(states) else end + 1
            show = set()
            if not st["hidden"]:
                if st["view"] == "back":
                    show.add(st["sprite"])
                    self._key_const(self.mirror, "scale", 0, f, 1)
                else:
                    sfx = "_q" if st["view"] == "q" else ""
                    fs = "_s" if (sfx and st.get("profil")) else sfx      # lapisan kepala/wajah/badan profil (lengan tetap 3/4)
                    if st["legs"] == "sit":
                        show |= {"lutut_sit_q"} if sfx else {"kaki_sit", "lutut_sit"}
                    else:
                        show.add(f"kaki_{st['legs']}")
                    show |= {"badan" + fs, "kepala" + fs, "wajah" + fs, f"mata_{st['eyes']}{fs}",
                             f"alis_{st['brows']}{fs}", f"mulut_{st['mouth']}{fs}"}
                    if st.get("fx") is not None and not sfx:
                        show.add(f"fx_peluit_{st['fx']}")
                    self._key_const(self.mirror, "scale", 0, f, st.get("mirror", 1))
                    for side, nm in ((-1, "kiri"), (1, "kanan")):
                        pose = st["left" if side < 0 else "right"]
                        act = st.get("arm_" + str(side))
                        gest = st.get("gestur_" + str(side * st.get("mirror", 1)))
                        if gest and not act and not (D and side * st.get("mirror", 1) in D.grip_hs(self, f)):
                            pose = gest                                  # gestur dialog pada tangan bebas
                        styles = D.grip_styles(self, f) if D else {}
                        sty = styles.get(side * st.get("mirror", 1))
                        if sty and f"tangan_{sty}_{nm}_b" in self.L:          # tangan genggam berlapis (Phase 4B)
                            show |= {f"lengan_atas_{nm}", f"lengan_bawah_{nm}_lepas", f"tangan_{sty}_{nm}_b"}
                        else:
                            hand = "_tunjuk" if pose == "point" and not act else ("_genggam" if sty else "")
                            show |= {f"lengan_atas_{nm}", f"lengan_bawah_{nm}{hand}"}
                        arm_keys[side] += self.arm_motion(side, act or pose, f, f_next, st, P)
                        # bahu: posisi depan vs 3/4; lengan sisi jauh di belakang badan
                        S = self.meta[("shoulder_s" if (fs == "_s" and "shoulder_s" in self.meta) else "shoulder_q") if sfx else "shoulder"][str(side)]
                        sx, sz = self.to_local(*S)
                        far = sfx and side == 1
                        for idx, val in ((0, sx), (1, FAR_ARM if far else DEPTH[f"lengan_{nm}"])):
                            self._key_const(self.arms[side], "location", idx, f, val)
                    look_keys.append((f, st.get("look", 0) or st.get("autolook", 0)))
                if not st["hidden"]:
                    sit_keys.append((f, 1 if st.get("sit") else 0))
            for name, o in self.L.items():
                self.key_vis(o, name not in show, f)
        # lengan FK: transisi halus antar pose
        for side, keys in arm_keys.items():
            prev = None
            for f, (a1, a2) in keys:
                if prev is not None and f - TRANS > prev[0]:
                    self._key_arm(side, f, prev[1]); f = f + TRANS             # tahan pose lama, lalu transisi
                self._key_arm(side, max(1, f), (a1, a2))
                prev = (f, (a1, a2))
            B.interp(self.arms[side], "SINE", "EASE_IN_OUT"); B.interp(self.elbows[side], "SINE", "EASE_IN_OUT")
        lp = self.meta["look_px"] * u
        for f, lk in look_keys:
            self.eyes.location.x = lk * lp; self.eyes.keyframe_insert("location", index=0, frame=f)
        self._seat_prepare()
        drop = self.seat_drop
        last = None
        for f, s in sit_keys:
            if s != last:
                if last is not None:
                    self.face.location.z = self.base_z - drop * last; self.face.keyframe_insert("location", index=2, frame=max(1, f - 1))
                self.face.location.z = self.base_z - drop * s; self.face.keyframe_insert("location", index=2, frame=f)
                last = s
        self.face.location.z = self.base_z
        self._seat(end)
        tilt = (self.c.get("perf") or {}).get("kepala_miring", [])
        last = 0.0
        for f, deg in tilt:                                           # miring kepala: transisi 8 frame
            if 1 <= f <= end:
                self.head.rotation_euler = (0, math.radians(last), 0); self.head.keyframe_insert("rotation_euler", index=1, frame=max(1, f))
                self.head.rotation_euler = (0, math.radians(deg), 0); self.head.keyframe_insert("rotation_euler", index=1, frame=min(end, f + 8))
                last = deg
        if tilt:
            for fc_ in B.fcurves(self.head):
                if fc_.data_path == "rotation_euler":
                    for kp in fc_.keyframe_points:
                        kp.interpolation = "SINE"; kp.easing = "EASE_IN_OUT"
        self.head.rotation_euler = (0, 0, 0)
        B.noise(self.head, "rotation_euler", 1, 0.035, random.uniform(45, 70))
        B.noise(self.face, "scale", 2, 0.012, 28)
        for a, b in self.c["shakes"]:
            B.noise(self.face, "location", 0, 0.03, 1.5, a, b)
        for a, b in self.c["walking"]:
            B.noise(self.face, "location", 2, 0.025, 3, a, b)
        for f in self.c["pops"]:
            for ff, s in ((f - 1, 1.0), (f + 2, 1.04), (f + 7, 1.0)):
                self.face.scale = (s, s, s); self.face.keyframe_insert("scale", frame=max(1, ff))
        self.face.scale = (1, 1, 1)
        return self

    def _key_arm(self, side, f, ang):
        self.arms[side].rotation_euler = (0, math.radians(ang[0]), 0)
        self.arms[side].keyframe_insert("rotation_euler", index=1, frame=f)
        self.elbows[side].rotation_euler = (0, math.radians(ang[1]), 0)
        self.elbows[side].keyframe_insert("rotation_euler", index=1, frame=f)

    def arm_motion(self, side, pose, f0, f1, st, P):
        T = P[str(side)]
        if pose == "wave":
            base = T["wave"]; ks = []
            for k, f in enumerate(range(f0, max(f0 + 1, f1), 7)):
                ks.append((f, [base[0] + (10 if k % 2 else -10) * -side, base[1] + (12 if k % 2 else -12) * -side]))
            return ks
        if pose == "eat":                                      # suap: dari piring ke mulut, lalu kembali
            ks = []
            for f in range(f0, max(f0 + 1, f1), 48):
                ks += [(f, T["pegang"]), (f + 16, T["mulut"]), (f + 26, T["mulut"]), (f + 40, T["pegang"])]
            return [(f, a) for f, a in ks if f < f1] or [(f0, T["pegang"])]
        if pose == "cook":
            return [(f0, T["masak"])]                          # diganti IK dunia di _cook_ik
        if pose == "hold":
            return [(f0, T["pegang"])]
        legs = st.get("legs", "stand")
        if legs.startswith("walk") and pose == "down":
            swing = {"walkA": 12, "walkB": 0, "walkC": -12, "walkD": 0}[legs] * side
            return [(f0, [T["down"][0] + swing, -swing * 0.5])]
        return [(f0, T.get(pose, T["down"]))]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- tempat duduk DUNIA (Phase 4B-B5)
    def _sit_acts(self):
        return [a for a in self.c.get("rig_acts", []) if a["type"] == "sit"]

    def _seat_prepare(self):
        """tinggi duduk dari dudukan: jangkar dunia ('kursi: <properti>.duduk') atau dingklik bawaan setinggi garis
        pantat sprite. Badan turun sejauh yang diperlukan agar garis pantat tepat di permukaan dudukan."""
        m, u = self.meta, self.u
        self.seat_drop = m["sit_drop"] * u
        self.seat_scale_legs = 1.0
        for a in self._sit_acts():
            jk = a.get("kursi_jangkar")
            if not jk:
                continue
            ground = self.c["loc"][0][3]
            h = jk["pos"][2] - ground
            self.seat_drop = (m["foot_y"] - m["seat_y"]) * u - h
            knee = 1060
            sit_floor = m["foot_y"] - m["sit_drop"]
            self.seat_scale_legs = max(0.5, min(2.2, ((m["foot_y"] - knee) * u - self.seat_drop) / ((sit_floor - knee) * u)))

    def _seat(self, end):
        """Dudukan TETAP di dunia (tidak ikut kamera). Tokoh menyesuaikan diri:
          - tinggi badan dari dudukan (_seat_prepare), betis diregang/dipendekkan bila dudukan bukan dingklik bawaan
          - tampak 3/4: sprite digeser ke arah hadap agar pinggul tepat di atas pusat dudukan
          - presentasi: bidang sprite disajikan di depan dudukan (skala seragam thd kamera -> layar identik),
            menggantikan cara Phase 3 yang MEMINDAHKAN dingklik di belakang bidang sprite tiap ganti kamera."""
        u = self.u
        for a in self._sit_acts():
            if a.get("kursi", "dingklik") == "tidak":
                continue
            f0, f1 = a["f0"], a.get("f1", end + 1)
            jk = a.get("kursi_jangkar")
            if jk:
                seat_xy, half = Vector(jk["pos"][:2]), float(jk.get("lebar", STOOL_R))
                self.stool_obj, self.stool_h = None, jk["pos"][2]
            else:
                seat_h = (self.meta["foot_y"] - self.meta["sit_drop"] - self.meta["seat_y"]) * u
                x, y, z = self.c["loc"][0][1:4]
                st = stool(f"dingklik_{self.c['name']}", None, seat_h, a.get("warna_kursi"))
                st.location = (x, y, z)                                  # dunia; TANPA constraint ke kamera
                seat_xy, half = Vector((x, y)), STOOL_R
                self.stool_obj, self.stool_h = st, seat_h
                for ch in [st] + list(st.children):
                    for ff, hide in ((1, f0 > 1), (f0, False), (min(f1, end + 1), True)):
                        ch.hide_render = ch.hide_viewport = hide
                        ch.keyframe_insert("hide_render", frame=max(1, ff)); ch.keyframe_insert("hide_viewport", frame=max(1, ff))
            # pinggul di atas dudukan pada tampak 3/4 (lutut_sit_q: pinggul ~36 px di belakang garis tengah)
            keys = sorted(set(self.view_keys) | {f for f in (f0, f1) if 1 <= f <= end})
            for f in keys:
                view, m = (self.view_keys[max(k for k in self.view_keys if k <= f)] if any(k <= f for k in self.view_keys)
                           else ("front", 1, 0, 0))[:2]
                self._key_const(self.mirror, "location", 0, f, 36 * u * m if (view == "q" and f0 <= f < f1) else 0.0)
            if abs(self.seat_scale_legs - 1) > 1e-3:                    # betis menyesuaikan tinggi dudukan
                # Phase 4.2-D: pivot regangan di garis LUTUT (kanvas y ~1050), bukan titik asal lapisan (dasar kanvas):
                # sebelumnya skala 2,2 (bangku 0,475 m) mengangkat kaki ke z 0,64-1,17 m -> tersembunyi di belakang badan
                Hc = self.meta["canvas"][1]
                z_knee = (Hc - 1050) * u
                for nm in ("kaki_sit", "lutut_sit_q"):
                    o = self.L.get(nm)
                    if o is not None:
                        self._key_const(o, "scale", 2, f0, self.seat_scale_legs)
                        self._key_const(o, "location", 2, f0, z_knee * (1 - self.seat_scale_legs))
            gap = half + STOOL_GAP
            self.seat_gap = gap
            for f in range(max(1, f0 - 1), min(f1, end + 1) + 1):
                sitting = f0 <= f < f1
                cx = _eval(self.cam, "location", 0, f, self.cam.location.x)
                cy = _eval(self.cam, "location", 1, f, self.cam.location.y)
                cz = _eval(self.cam, "location", 2, f, self.cam.location.z)
                px, py = self._xy(f)
                pz = _eval(self.root, "location", 2, f, self.root.location.z)
                dh = math.hypot(cx - seat_xy.x, cy - seat_xy.y)
                gap = half + STOOL_GAP
                box = jk.get("setengah") if jk else None
                if box and dh > 1e-3:
                    # Phase 4.2-D: dudukan memanjang (bangku): bidang sprite di depan SELURUH jejak dudukan sepanjang
                    # arah pandang (fungsi tumpu kotak), bukan jari-jari -> kaki tidak tertutup badan bangku saat serong
                    ra = math.radians(jk.get("arah", 0.0))
                    vx, vy = (cx - seat_xy.x) / dh, (cy - seat_xy.y) / dh
                    lx, ly = vx * math.cos(ra) + vy * math.sin(ra), -vx * math.sin(ra) + vy * math.cos(ra)
                    gap = abs(lx) * box[0] + abs(ly) * box[1] + STOOL_GAP
                self.seat_gap = max(self.seat_gap, gap) if sitting else self.seat_gap
                k = max(0.5, 1 - gap / dh) if (sitting and dh > gap * 1.5) else 1.0
                self.pres_k[f] = k
                self.pres.location = ((cx - px) * (1 - k), (cy - py) * (1 - k), (cz - pz) * (1 - k))
                self.pres.scale = (k, k, k)
                self.pres.keyframe_insert("location", frame=f); self.pres.keyframe_insert("scale", frame=f)
            B.interp(self.pres, "LINEAR")

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/bl_rim.py
# sha256: a4dde023b5019f1483fb54ce0671cce54323799ecb0a4ebe9205e54ee8364600
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.1-C1: rim 2D tokoh -- arah & kekuatan per frame (Python, dapat diperiksa) -> properti root tokoh
'rim<i>_u', 'rim<i>_v', 'rim<i>_k' (dikunci per frame) -> driver di node 'rim<i>_*' setiap material sprite.

  L        = arah ke lampu (titik: posisi lampu - titik dada tokoh; arah: vektor tetap matahari/bulan)
  belakang = clamp(-dot(L, arah_ke_kamera) + 0.25, 0, 1)   -> lampu di depan tokoh = key, bukan rim (0)
  jatuh    = min(1, kuat / jarak^2)  (titik) | 1 (arah)
  k        = belakang x jatuh x gain
  (u, v)   = arah L di bidang sprite (sumbu lokal x/z objek 'cermin', sudah termasuk cermin), dinormalkan
Tanpa nama tokoh/cerita. Laporan ringkas dikembalikan untuk interaksi.json."""
import math

import bpy
from mathutils import Vector


def bake(rigs, rims, cam, end, step=1):
    if not rims:
        return {}
    sc = bpy.context.scene
    keys = {r.c["name"]: [] for r in rigs}
    for f in range(1, end + 1, step):
        sc.frame_set(f)
        C = cam.matrix_world.translation
        for r in rigs:
            mir = r.mirror
            Mw = mir.matrix_world
            P = Mw.translation + Vector((0, 0, 0.9 * r.c["height"] * 0.75))
            to_cam = (C - P).normalized()
            inv = Mw.to_3x3().inverted_safe()
            row = []
            for rim in rims[:2]:
                if rim["jenis"] == "titik":
                    Lv = Vector(rim["pos"]) - P
                    d2 = max(0.25, Lv.length_squared)
                    fall = min(1.0, rim["kuat"] / d2)
                else:
                    Lv = Vector(rim["arah"]); fall = 1.0
                L = Lv.normalized()
                back = max(0.0, min(1.0, -L.dot(to_cam) + 0.25))
                lo = inv @ L
                n2 = math.hypot(lo.x, lo.z)
                u, v = (lo.x / n2, lo.z / n2) if n2 > 1e-4 else (0.0, 0.0)
                row.append((u, v, back * fall * rim.get("gain", 1.0)))
            keys[r.c["name"]].append((f, row))
    rep = {}
    for r in rigs:
        root = r.root
        for f, row in keys[r.c["name"]]:
            for i, (u, v, k) in enumerate(row):
                for nm, val in (("u", u), ("v", v), ("k", k)):
                    root[f"rim{i}_{nm}"] = val
                    root.keyframe_insert(f'["rim{i}_{nm}"]', frame=f)
        # driver di setiap material sprite tokoh
        for o in bpy.data.objects:
            if not o.name.startswith(r.c["name"] + "_") or o.type != "MESH" or not o.active_material:
                continue
            nt = o.active_material.node_tree
            for n in nt.nodes:
                if n.type == "VALUE" and n.label.startswith("rim"):
                    fc = n.outputs[0].driver_add("default_value")
                    dr = fc.driver; dr.type = "AVERAGE"
                    var = dr.variables.new(); var.type = "SINGLE_PROP"
                    var.targets[0].id_type = "OBJECT"; var.targets[0].id = root
                    var.targets[0].data_path = f'["{n.label}"]'
        ks = [row[i][2] for _, row in keys[r.c["name"]] for i in range(len(row))]
        rep[r.c["name"]] = {"k_maks": round(max(ks, default=0), 3), "k_rata2": round(sum(ks) / max(1, len(ks)), 3),
                            "frame_rim_aktif": sum(1 for _, row in keys[r.c["name"]] if any(x[2] > 0.05 for x in row))}
    return rep

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/bl_utils.py
# sha256: 59d13a5049ac4a898654b40a636c59b2a2bd1ab496315fec3c27c0f5976ccd3a

"""Helper Blender (dipakai di dalam Blender saja): material, bentuk dasar, lampu, animasi noise, billboard."""
import math
import random

import bmesh
import bpy


def scene():
    return bpy.context.scene


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ animasi
def fcurves(idblock):
    ad = idblock.animation_data
    if not ad or not ad.action:
        return []
    act = ad.action
    if hasattr(act, "layers") and act.layers:           # Blender 4.4+ (layered action)
        return [f for layer in act.layers for strip in layer.strips for cb in strip.channelbags for f in cb.fcurves]
    return list(act.fcurves)


def fc(idblock, path, index=0):
    for f in fcurves(idblock):
        if f.data_path == path and f.array_index == index:
            return f


def noise(idblock, path, index, strength, scale, start=None, end=None):
    """Getaran acak (F-curve noise modifier) pada satu properti."""
    if fc(idblock, path, index) is None:
        try:
            idblock.keyframe_insert(path, index=index, frame=1)
        except TypeError:
            idblock.keyframe_insert(path, frame=1)
    m = fc(idblock, path, index).modifiers.new("NOISE")
    m.strength, m.scale, m.phase = strength, scale, random.uniform(0, 500)
    if start is not None:
        m.use_restricted_range = True
        m.frame_start, m.frame_end = start, end
        m.blend_in = m.blend_out = 3
    return m


def interp(idblock, mode="LINEAR", easing=None, cycles=False):
    for f in fcurves(idblock):
        for k in f.keyframe_points:
            k.interpolation = mode
            if easing:
                k.easing = easing
        if cycles:
            f.modifiers.new("CYCLES")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ material
_mat_cache = {}


def mat(name, color, rough=0.6, emit=None, strength=0.0, metal=0.0):
    key = (name, tuple(color), rough, tuple(emit) if emit else None, strength, metal)
    if key in _mat_cache:
        return _mat_cache[key]
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    b = m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
    b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1)
    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rough
    b.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metal
    if emit:
        b.inputs["Emission Color"].default_value = (*emit, 1)
        b.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value = strength
    _mat_cache[key] = m
    return m


def aus(m, variasi=0.08, kotor=0.25, gores=0.0, celah=0.35, rembes=0.0, warna_gores=None, skala=1.0, serat=0.0):
    """Phase 4.1-C2: keausan & variasi material prosedural (PBR tetap Principled; dipakai ulang untuk semua hero asset).
      variasi : variasi nilai warna (noise lembut, koordinat objek)       kotor : kotoran menebal ke BAWAH objek
      gores   : goresan memanjang yang membuka 'warna_gores' (cat terkelupas / logam)
      celah   : kotoran di celah/sambungan (node Ambient Occlusion -- didukung Eevee)
      rembes  : noda air mengalir turun (dinding/fasad)
    Idempoten (material yang sudah diberi aus tidak diubah lagi)."""
    if m is None or not m.use_nodes or m.get("aus"):
        return m
    nt = m.node_tree; N, L = nt.nodes, nt.links
    b = N.get("Principled BSDF")
    if b is None or b.inputs["Base Color"].is_linked:
        return m
    base = tuple(b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
    rough = b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value
    tc = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")

    def noise(scale, detail=4.0, stretch=None):
        n = N.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise"); n.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale * skala; n.inputs["Detail"].default_value = detail
        if stretch:
            mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = stretch
            L.new(tc.outputs["Object"], mp.inputs["Vector"]); L.new(mp.outputs[0], n.inputs["Vector"])
        else:
            L.new(tc.outputs["Object"], n.inputs["Vector"])
        return n.outputs["Fac"]

    def mathn(op, a, b_=None, clamp=False):
        n = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); n.operation = op; n.use_clamp = clamp
        for i, v in enumerate((a, b_)):
            if v is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                n.inputs[i].default_value = v
            else:
                L.new(v, n.inputs[i])
        return n.outputs[0]

    def mixc(fac, c1, c2, blend="MIX"):
        n = N.new("ShaderNodeMix"); n.data_type = "RGBA"; n.blend_type = blend
        for sock, v in ((0, fac),):
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                n.inputs[0].default_value = v
            else:
                L.new(v, n.inputs[0])
        for idx, c in ((6, c1), (7, c2)):
            if isinstance(c, tuple):
                n.inputs[idx].default_value = c if len(c) == 4 else (*c, 1)
            else:
                L.new(c, n.inputs[idx])
        return n.outputs[2]
    # variasi nilai: base x (1 +- variasi)
    v = mathn("ADD", mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("SUBTRACT", noise(3.0), 0.5), 2 * variasi), 1.0)
    col = mixc(1.0, base, (1, 1, 1, 1), "MULTIPLY")
    cv = N.new("ShaderNodeCombineXYZ")
    for i in range(3):
        L.new(v, cv.inputs[i])
    col = mixc(1.0, col, cv.outputs[0], "MULTIPLY")
    if serat > 0:                                                   # serat kayu: pita memanjang (arah z & x objek)
        for st in ((0.06, 0.06, 1.0), (1.0, 0.06, 0.06)):
            w = N.new("ShaderNodeTexWave"); w.wave_type = "BANDS"; w.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 9.0 * skala
            w.inputs["Distortion"].default_value = 6.0; w.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3.0
            w.bands_direction = "X" if st[0] < 1 else "Z"
            mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = st
            L.new(tc.outputs["Object"], mp.inputs["Vector"]); L.new(mp.outputs[0], w.inputs["Vector"])
            f = mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("POWER", w.outputs["Fac"], 3.0), serat * 0.5)
            col = mixc(f, col, tuple(c * 0.55 for c in base[:3]) + (1,))
    dark = tuple(c * 0.45 for c in base[:3]) + (1,)
    dirt = (0.09, 0.075, 0.06, 1)
    if kotor > 0:                                                   # kotoran menebal ke bawah (z lokal objek)
        sep = N.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ"); L.new(tc.outputs["Generated"], sep.inputs[0])
        low = mathn("SUBTRACT", 1.0, mathn("MULTIPLY", sep.outputs[2], 2.2), clamp=True)
        f = mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("MULTIPLY", low, mathn("ADD", noise(9.0, 6.0), 0.2)), kotor, clamp=True)
        col = mixc(f, col, dirt)
    if celah > 0:                                                   # celah/sambungan
        ao = N.new("ShaderNodeAmbientOcclusion"); ao.inputs["Distance"].default_value = 0.06; ao.samples = 8
        f = mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("SUBTRACT", 1.0, ao.outputs["AO"]), celah * 1.6, clamp=True)
        col = mixc(f, col, dark)
    if rembes > 0:                                                  # noda air mengalir turun
        f = mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("GREATER_THAN", noise(4.0, 2.0, (6.0, 6.0, 0.25)), 0.58), rembes)
        col = mixc(f, col, tuple(c * 0.6 for c in base[:3]) + (1,))
    rgh = mathn("ADD", rough, mathn("MULTIPLY", mathn("SUBTRACT", noise(7.0), 0.5), 0.25), clamp=True)
    if gores > 0:                                                   # goresan memanjang
        sc_ = mathn("GREATER_THAN", noise(14.0, 8.0, (1.0, 1.0, 9.0)), 1.0 - 0.12 * gores)
        col = mixc(sc_, col, warna_gores or (0.55, 0.55, 0.56, 1))
        rgh = mathn("SUBTRACT", rgh, mathn("MULTIPLY", sc_, 0.2), clamp=True)
    L.new(col, b.inputs["Base Color"])
    L.new(rgh, b.inputs["Roughness"])
    m["aus"] = 1
    return m


def glow(name, color, strength):
    return mat(name, color, 0.4, color, strength)


def srgb(c):
    """warna 0-255 (sRGB) -> linear 0-1 untuk Blender"""
    def ch(v):
        v = v / 255
        return v / 12.92 if v <= 0.04045 else ((v + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
    return tuple(ch(v) for v in c[:3])


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ bentuk
def _finish(o, name, material, parent):
    o.name = name
    if material:
        o.data.materials.append(material)
    if parent:
        o.parent = parent
    return o


def _obj(name, bm, loc, rot, smooth=False):
    """buat objek langsung dari bmesh (tanpa bpy.ops: tetap cepat walau adegan berisi ribuan objek)"""
    me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
    bm.to_mesh(me); bm.free()
    if smooth:
        me.polygons.foreach_set("use_smooth", [True] * len(me.polygons))
    o = bpy.data.objects.new(name, me)
    o.location = loc; o.rotation_euler = rot
    scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    return o


BEVEL = {"on": True}                                          # matikan untuk debug cepat


def box(name, loc, size, material, rot=(0, 0, 0), parent=None, bevel=None):
    """kotak dengan tepi membulat tipis (bevel) supaya tepi menangkap cahaya seperti benda nyata.
    bevel=None -> otomatis sesuai ukuran; 0 -> tajam."""
    bm = bmesh.new()
    bmesh.ops.create_cube(bm, size=1.0)
    for v in bm.verts:
        v.co.x *= size[0]; v.co.y *= size[1]; v.co.z *= size[2]
    smallest, largest = min(size), max(size)
    if bevel is None:
        bevel = 0.0 if (largest > 12 or smallest < 0.008) else min(0.02, smallest * 0.18)
    if bevel > 0 and BEVEL["on"]:
        bmesh.ops.bevel(bm, geom=list(bm.edges) + list(bm.verts), offset=bevel, segments=2, affect="EDGES",
                        profile=0.5, clamp_overlap=True)
    o = _finish(_obj(name, bm, loc, rot, smooth=bevel > 0), name, material, parent)
    if bevel > 0:                                                    # sudut halus, sisi datar tetap tajam
        try:
            o.data.shade_smooth_by_angle(angle=math.radians(35))   # Blender 4.1+
        except AttributeError:
            pass
    return o


def cyl(name, loc, r, depth, material, rot=(0, 0, 0), verts=12, parent=None, bevel=None):
    bm = bmesh.new()
    bmesh.ops.create_cone(bm, cap_ends=True, cap_tris=False, segments=verts, radius1=r, radius2=r, depth=depth)
    if bevel is None:
        bevel = 0.0 if (r > 3 or depth > 12 or min(r, depth) < 0.01) else min(0.015, min(r, depth) * 0.15)
    rim = [e for e in bm.edges if abs(e.verts[0].co.z) > depth / 2 - 1e-4 and abs(e.verts[1].co.z) > depth / 2 - 1e-4
           and abs(e.verts[0].co.z - e.verts[1].co.z) < 1e-5]
    if bevel > 0 and BEVEL["on"] and rim:
        bmesh.ops.bevel(bm, geom=rim, offset=bevel, segments=2, affect="EDGES", profile=0.5, clamp_overlap=True)
    o = _finish(_obj(name, bm, loc, rot, smooth=True), name, material, parent)
    try:
        o.data.shade_smooth_by_angle(angle=math.radians(40))
    except AttributeError:
        pass
    return o


def ball(name, loc, r, material, sub=1, parent=None):
    bm = bmesh.new()
    bmesh.ops.create_icosphere(bm, subdivisions=sub, radius=r)
    return _finish(_obj(name, bm, loc, (0, 0, 0), smooth=True), name, material, parent)


def empty(name, loc, rot_z=0.0):
    o = bpy.data.objects.new(name, None)
    o.location = loc; o.rotation_euler = (0, 0, rot_z)
    scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    return o


def light(kind, loc, energy, color, size=0.2, rot=(0, 0, 0), spot=None, parent=None):
    ld = bpy.data.lights.new(f"L_{kind}", kind)
    ld.energy, ld.color = energy, color
    if kind in ("POINT", "SPOT"):
        ld.shadow_soft_size = size
    if spot:
        ld.spot_size, ld.spot_blend = spot
    o = bpy.data.objects.new(ld.name, ld); o.location = loc; o.rotation_euler = rot
    scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    if parent:
        o.parent = parent
    return o


def text(body, loc, size, material, face_x):
    """teks berdiri: face_x=+1 menghadap +X, -1 menghadap -X"""
    bpy.ops.object.text_add(location=loc, rotation=(math.radians(90), 0, math.radians(90 * face_x)))
    t = bpy.context.object
    t.data.body, t.data.size, t.data.extrude = body, size, 0.015
    t.data.align_x, t.data.align_y = "CENTER", "CENTER"
    t.data.materials.append(material)
    return t


def wire(points, r, material):
    cu = bpy.data.curves.new("kabel", "CURVE"); cu.dimensions = "3D"; cu.bevel_depth = r
    sp = cu.splines.new("POLY"); sp.points.add(len(points) - 1)
    for p, v in zip(sp.points, points):
        p.co = (*v, 1)
    o = bpy.data.objects.new("kabel", cu); scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    cu.materials.append(material)
    return o


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ billboard tokoh 2D
def image_mat(name, image, emission=0.4, fade=False):
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    m.surface_render_method = "DITHERED"
    nt = m.node_tree; b = nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
    tex = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexImage")
    tex.image = image; image.alpha_mode = "STRAIGHT"
    nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], b.inputs["Base Color"])
    nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], b.inputs["Emission Color"])
    if fade:                                                   # transparansi diatur lewat warna objek (alpha)
        oi = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeObjectInfo")
        mul = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath"); mul.operation = "MULTIPLY"
        nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Alpha"], mul.inputs[0]); nt.links.new(oi.outputs["Alpha"], mul.inputs[1])
        nt.links.new(mul.outputs[0], b.inputs["Alpha"])
    else:
        nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Alpha"], b.inputs["Alpha"])
    b.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value = emission
    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 1.0
    b.inputs["Specular IOR Level"].default_value = 0.0
    return m


ALPHA_POLICY = "auto"          # auto | dithered | clip   (plan/latar 'alpha_tokoh' dapat menimpa; lihat blender_build)
SOFT_MAX = 0.35                # porsi piksel semi-transparan maksimum agar lapisan dianggap bertepi keras


def soft_fraction(image):
    """porsi piksel semi-transparan (0.08 < a < 0.92) di antara piksel yang tidak kosong"""
    import numpy as np
    n = image.size[0] * image.size[1]
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    buf = np.empty(n * 4, dtype=np.float32)
    image.pixels.foreach_get(buf)
    a = buf[3::4]
    vis = a > 0.08
    return float(np.count_nonzero(vis & (a < 0.92)) / max(1, np.count_nonzero(vis)))


def alpha_policy(image):
    """Uji A/B Phase 4D: DITHERED membuat piksel semi-transparan berbintik (perona pipi, garis tepi); BLENDED tidak menulis
    depth -> DOF mengaburkan tokoh (gagal); CLIP menghapus bintik tetapi membuat lapisan lembut keras. Maka per tekstur:
    lapisan bertepi keras -> clip, lapisan lembut (perona, bayangan) -> dithered."""
    if ALPHA_POLICY in ("dithered", "clip"):
        return ALPHA_POLICY
    return "clip" if soft_fraction(image) < SOFT_MAX else "dithered"


def char_mat(name, image, look, crop=None):
    """Shader khusus tokoh 2D (terpisah dari sprite efek/bayangan).
    warna = gambar x clamp(ambien + gain x cahaya_datang, 0, batas)
    - cahaya_datang dibaca dari Diffuse BSDF -> Shader to RGB (ikut warna lampu & bayangan, jadi tetap menyatu)
    - 'ambien' = lantai terang bertint (tokoh di sisi gelap tetap terbaca)
    - 'batas'  = plafon (wajah dekat bohlam tidak terbakar / menguning)
    - keluaran Emission murni: garis tinta & highlight mata tidak terkena spekular / AgX shoulder berlebihan."""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    m.surface_render_method = "DITHERED"
    nt = m.node_tree; N, Ln = nt.nodes, nt.links
    for n in list(N):
        if n.type != "OUTPUT_MATERIAL":
            N.remove(n)
    out = next(n for n in N if n.type == "OUTPUT_MATERIAL")
    tex = N.new("ShaderNodeTexImage"); tex.image = image; image.alpha_mode = "STRAIGHT"
    tex.interpolation = "Cubic"
    dif = N.new("ShaderNodeBsdfDiffuse"); dif.inputs["Color"].default_value = (1, 1, 1, 1)
    s2r = N.new("ShaderNodeShaderToRGB"); Ln.new(dif.outputs[0], s2r.inputs[0])
    def math(op, a, b=None):
        n = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); n.operation = op
        for i, v in enumerate((a, b)):
            if v is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                n.inputs[i].default_value = v
            else:
                Ln.new(v, n.inputs[i])
        return n.outputs[0]

    def vmath(op, a, b):
        n = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); n.operation = op
        for i, v in enumerate((a, b)):
            if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)):
                n.inputs[i].default_value = v
            else:
                Ln.new(v, n.inputs[i])
        return n.outputs[0]

    bw = N.new("ShaderNodeRGBToBW"); Ln.new(s2r.outputs["Color"], bw.inputs[0])
    lum = bw.outputs[0]
    # respons lembut: k = 1 - exp(-gain * L)  (0 di gelap, mendekati 1 di dekat lampu, tanpa terbakar)
    k = math("SUBTRACT", 1.0, math("EXPONENT", math("MULTIPLY", lum, -look.get("gain", 2.0))))
    # warna lampu dinormalkan lalu dipakai sebagian (tint_lampu)
    hue = vmath("DIVIDE", s2r.outputs["Color"], math("ADD", lum, 1e-4))
    hue = vmath("MINIMUM", hue, (1.6, 1.6, 1.6))
    t = look.get("tint_lampu", 0.5)
    mixh = N.new("ShaderNodeMix"); mixh.data_type = "VECTOR"; mixh.inputs[0].default_value = t
    mixh.inputs[4].default_value = (1, 1, 1); Ln.new(hue, mixh.inputs[5])
    amb = look.get("ambien", (0.5, 0.52, 0.6))
    top = look.get("batas", 1.0)
    span = vmath("SUBTRACT", (top, top, top), amb)               # ruang dari ambien ke batas
    lit = vmath("MULTIPLY", span, mixh.outputs[1])
    sc = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); sc.operation = "SCALE"; Ln.new(lit, sc.inputs[0]); Ln.new(k, sc.inputs["Scale"])
    shade = vmath("ADD", sc.outputs[0], amb)
    cap = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); cap.operation = "MINIMUM"; cap.inputs[1].default_value = (top * 1.1,) * 3
    Ln.new(shade, cap.inputs[0])
    mul = N.new("ShaderNodeMix"); mul.data_type = "RGBA"; mul.blend_type = "MULTIPLY"; mul.inputs[0].default_value = 1.0
    Ln.new(tex.outputs["Color"], mul.inputs[6]); Ln.new(cap.outputs[0], mul.inputs[7])
    col_out = mul.outputs[2]
    rims = look.get("rim") or []
    if rims and crop:
        col_out = _rim_nodes(N, Ln, math, vmath, tex, image, col_out, rims, crop, look)
    em = N.new("ShaderNodeEmission"); em.inputs["Strength"].default_value = look.get("kuat", 1.0)
    Ln.new(col_out, em.inputs["Color"])
    tr = N.new("ShaderNodeBsdfTransparent")
    mix = N.new("ShaderNodeMixShader")
    alpha = tex.outputs["Alpha"]
    if alpha_policy(image) == "clip":                         # Phase 4D-D1: lapisan bertepi keras -> alpha ambang (tanpa bintik dither)
        alpha = math("GREATER_THAN", alpha, 0.5)
        m["alpha"] = "clip"
    else:
        m["alpha"] = "dithered"
    Ln.new(alpha, mix.inputs[0]); Ln.new(tr.outputs[0], mix.inputs[1]); Ln.new(em.outputs[0], mix.inputs[2])
    Ln.new(mix.outputs[0], out.inputs["Surface"])
    return m


def _rim_nodes(N, Ln, math, vmath, tex, image, base, rims, crop, look):
    """Phase 4.1-C1: cahaya pinggir (rim) 2D pada tokoh dari LAMPU NYATA adegan.
    Arah (u, v di ruang sprite, sudah termasuk cermin) & kekuatan per frame dihitung di Python (bl_rim.bake) dan
    dibaca lewat driver dari properti root tokoh 'rim<i>_u/_v/_k'. Pita rim = piksel ISI (bukan tinta) yang
    tetangganya sejauh 'rim_px' ke arah lampu transparan; warna = warna lampu."""
    cw, ch = max(1.0, crop[2] - crop[0]), max(1.0, crop[3] - crop[1])
    px = float(look.get("rim_px", 44))                                  # > tebal tinta+siluet (~20 px) -> pita di isi
    uvn = N.new("ShaderNodeUVMap")
    acc = base
    bw = N.new("ShaderNodeRGBToBW"); Ln.new(tex.outputs["Color"], bw.inputs[0])
    notink = N.new("ShaderNodeMapRange"); notink.interpolation_type = "SMOOTHSTEP"
    Ln.new(bw.outputs[0], notink.inputs["Value"]); notink.inputs["From Min"].default_value = 0.10; notink.inputs["From Max"].default_value = 0.22
    for i, r in enumerate(rims[:2]):
        vals = {}
        for key in ("u", "v", "k"):
            vn = N.new("ShaderNodeValue"); vn.label = f"rim{i}_{key}"; vn.outputs[0].default_value = 0.0
            vals[key] = vn.outputs[0]
        du = math("MULTIPLY", vals["u"], px / cw)
        dv = math("MULTIPLY", vals["v"], px / ch)
        off = N.new("ShaderNodeCombineXYZ"); Ln.new(du, off.inputs[0]); Ln.new(dv, off.inputs[1])
        uv2 = vmath("ADD", uvn.outputs["UV"], off.outputs[0])
        t2 = N.new("ShaderNodeTexImage"); t2.image = image; t2.interpolation = "Linear"; t2.extension = "CLIP"
        Ln.new(uv2, t2.inputs["Vector"])
        band = math("MULTIPLY", math("MULTIPLY", tex.outputs["Alpha"], math("SUBTRACT", 1.0, t2.outputs["Alpha"])), notink.outputs[0])
        k = math("MULTIPLY", band, vals["k"])
        sc = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); sc.operation = "SCALE"; sc.inputs[0].default_value = tuple(r["warna"])
        Ln.new(k, sc.inputs["Scale"])
        acc = vmath("ADD", acc, sc.outputs[0])
    return acc


def vmath_dot(N, Ln, a, b):
    n = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); n.operation = "DOT_PRODUCT"
    Ln.new(a, n.inputs[0]); Ln.new(b, n.inputs[1])
    return n.outputs["Value"]


def billboard(name, material, height, aspect, cam, parent=None):
    w = height * aspect
    me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
    me.from_pydata([(-w / 2, 0, 0), (w / 2, 0, 0), (w / 2, 0, height), (-w / 2, 0, height)], [], [(0, 1, 2, 3)])
    uv = me.uv_layers.new()
    for i, co in enumerate([(0, 0), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1)]):
        uv.data[i].uv = co
    me.materials.append(material)
    o = bpy.data.objects.new(name, me); scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    o.parent = parent
    c = o.constraints.new("LOCKED_TRACK")                     # selalu menghadap kamera
    c.target, c.lock_axis, c.track_axis = cam, "LOCK_Z", "TRACK_NEGATIVE_Y"
    return o

"""Dijalankan DI DALAM Blender:  blender -b -P engine/blender_build.py -- <plan.json> <scene.blend>
Membangun latar, tokoh billboard, efek, dan kamera dari plan.json."""
import json
import math
import random
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import bpy
import numpy as np
from mathutils import Vector

ENGINE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ENGINE))
sys.path.insert(0, str(ENGINE / "sets"))
sys.path.insert(1, str(ENGINE))
import bl_utils as B  # noqa: E402

argv = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:]
plan = json.loads(Path(argv[0]).read_text())
blend_out = argv[1]
BUILD_ID = argv[2] if len(argv) > 2 else "tanpa_id"
random.seed(plan.get("seed", 7))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ scene & render
bpy.ops.wm.read_factory_settings(use_empty=True)
sc = bpy.context.scene
sc["build_id"] = BUILD_ID                                       # Phase 4A-A2: provenance di dalam .blend
END = plan["end"]
sc.render.fps = plan["fps"]
sc.frame_start, sc.frame_end = 1, END
sc.render.resolution_x, sc.render.resolution_y = 1080, 1920
for eng in ("BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT", "BLENDER_EEVEE"):
    try:
        sc.render.engine = eng; break
    except TypeError:
        pass
ee = sc.eevee
ee.taa_render_samples = 32
for attr, val in (("use_raytracing", True), ("use_shadows", True), ("volumetric_tile_size", "8"),
                  ("volumetric_samples", 32), ("volumetric_end", 70.0), ("use_volumetric_shadows", False)):
    if hasattr(ee, attr):
        setattr(ee, attr, val)
sc.render.filter_size = 0.9                                     # lebih tajam (default 1.5)
# Phase 4.2: pantulan dari light probe, BUKAN pelacakan ruang-layar. Pelacakan ruang-layar (SCREEN) tidak stabil antar-
# frame di GPU Colab: permukaan licin (meja aluminium gerobak, kaca jendela) "menyala"/berganti isi per frame -> kedip
# FAIL di QA video (benchmark f478, uji3 shot 2), padahal di GPU lokal hanya tampak berbintik. PROBE deterministik.
if hasattr(ee, "ray_tracing_method"):
    ee.ray_tracing_method = "PROBE"
rto = getattr(ee, "ray_tracing_options", None)
if rto is not None:
    for attr, val in (("resolution_scale", "1"), ("use_denoise", True)):
        try:
            setattr(rto, attr, val)
        except (TypeError, AttributeError):
            pass
for attr, val in (("shadow_resolution_scale", 1.0), ("use_fast_gi", True), ("fast_gi_distance", 2.0)):
    if hasattr(ee, attr):
        try:
            setattr(ee, attr, val)
        except TypeError:
            pass
sc.view_settings.view_transform = "AgX"
for look in ("AgX - Punchy", "AgX - Medium High Contrast"):
    try:
        sc.view_settings.look = look; break
    except TypeError:
        pass

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ latar: atmosfer (waktu & cuaca) + lokasi + properti
import atmosfer  # noqa: E402
import kit  # noqa: E402
import locations  # noqa: E402

latar = plan["latar"]
B.ALPHA_POLICY = str(latar.get("alpha_tokoh", "auto")).lower()                 # Phase 4D-D1: auto | dithered | clip
loc_name = locations.location_name(latar)
loc_name = "jalan_kota" if loc_name == "jalan_malam" else loc_name
meta = locations.get(loc_name)
set_mod = __import__(loc_name)
env = atmosfer.build(latar, interior=meta["dalam"])
M = kit.Mats(env)
for wm in M.win_on:                                           # terang jendela ruko/rumah (latar.terang_jendela)
    bsdf = wm.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
    if bsdf:
        bsdf.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value *= float(latar.get("terang_jendela", 1.0))
avoid = [tuple(c["loc"][0][1:3]) for c in plan["characters"]] + [tuple(p["posisi"][:2]) for p in latar.get("properti", [])]
info = set_mod.build(latar, env, M, avoid)
for p in latar.get("properti", []):
    kit.build_prop(dict(p), M, env, info, meta["lantai"])
AUS_REPORT = kit.wear_pass(latar)                               # Phase 4.1-C2: variasi & aus material (sebelum tokoh)
print("AUS", AUS_REPORT.get("jumlah"))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ kamera
cam_d = bpy.data.cameras.new("Cam"); cam = bpy.data.objects.new("Cam", cam_d)
sc.collection.objects.link(cam); sc.camera = cam
cam_d.dof.use_dof = True
cam_d.dof.aperture_blades = 0                                   # bokeh bundar (bukan segi enam)
cam_d.dof.aperture_ratio = 1.0
cam_d.clip_end = 400
if env["malam"] and float(latar.get("cahaya_isi", 1.0)) > 0:   # fill lembut dari arah kamera: angkat bayangan latar depan
    fill = B.light("AREA", (0, 0.35, 0.2), 70 * float(latar.get("cahaya_isi", 1.0)), (0.78, 0.84, 1.0), parent=cam)
    fill.data.size = 2.5; fill.data.use_shadow = False        # tanpa bayangan: tidak menambah siluet ganda
    fill.data.diffuse_factor, fill.data.specular_factor = 1.0, 0.15   # tidak memantul putih di aspal basah
import bl_camsolve  # noqa: E402

if plan.get("solver_kamera", True):                             # geser kamera minimal agar wajah subjek tidak terhalang
    cam_report, _ = bl_camsolve.solve(plan, cam, sc)
    comp_report = bl_camsolve.frame_shots(plan, cam, sc) if plan.get("komposisi_kamera", True) else []   # Phase 4.2-C
    Path(blend_out).with_name("kamera.json").write_text(json.dumps({"build_id": BUILD_ID, "shots": cam_report,
                                                                    "komposisi": comp_report}, indent=1))
    for r in cam_report:
        if r["pilih"]["yaw_derajat"] or r["pilih"]["naik_m"] or r["pilih"]["geser_m"]:
            print("KAMERA shot", r["shot"], r["kamera"], r["pilih"], r["awal"], "->", r["akhir"])
prev = None
for f, pos, tgt, lens, fstop in plan["camera"]:
    p, t = Vector(pos), Vector(tgt)
    cam.location = p
    q = (t - p).to_track_quat("-Z", "Y")
    eul = q.to_euler("XYZ", prev) if prev else q.to_euler()
    prev = eul
    cam.rotation_euler = eul
    cam_d.lens, cam_d.dof.focus_distance, cam_d.dof.aperture_fstop = lens, (t - p).length, fstop
    cam.keyframe_insert("location", frame=f); cam.keyframe_insert("rotation_euler", frame=f)
    for path in ("lens", "dof.focus_distance", "dof.aperture_fstop"):
        cam_d.keyframe_insert(path, frame=f)
for f, lens in plan["zoom"]:                                   # zoom "punch" cepat di awal shot
    cam_d.lens = lens; cam_d.keyframe_insert("lens", frame=f)
for o in (cam, cam_d):
    B.interp(o, "SINE", "EASE_IN_OUT")
B.noise(cam, "rotation_euler", 0, 0.008, 45); B.noise(cam, "rotation_euler", 2, 0.008, 50)   # kamera genggam
for a, b in plan["cam_shakes"]:
    B.noise(cam, "rotation_euler", 0, 0.02, 2.5, a, b)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tokoh
chars_dir = Path(plan["chars_dir"])
char_pos = []
import bl_rig  # noqa: E402

import bl_interact  # noqa: E402

# Phase 4.1-C1: sumber rim tokoh = lampu NYATA di adegan (practical terkuat di sekitar posisi tokoh) + matahari/bulan.
# Dipilih otomatis (tanpa nama tokoh/cerita); dimatikan dengan latar.rim_tokoh: false, kekuatan latar.rim_kuat.
if env.get("tokoh_look") is not None and latar.get("rim_tokoh", True):
    import math as _m
    from mathutils import Vector as _V
    pos = [_V(tuple(c["loc"][0][1:])) + _V((0, 0, 1.0)) for c in plan["characters"] if c.get("rig")]
    cand = []
    for o in bpy.data.objects:
        if o.type != "LIGHT" or o.data.type not in ("POINT", "SPOT", "AREA") or o.data.energy <= 0:
            continue
        lp = o.matrix_world.translation
        if not pos:
            break
        e = max(o.data.energy / (4 * _m.pi * max(0.25, (lp - p).length_squared)) for p in pos)
        cand.append((e, o, lp.copy()))
    cand.sort(key=lambda t: -t[0])
    gain = float(latar.get("rim_kuat", 1.0))
    rims = []
    for e, o, lp in cand[:2]:
        if e < 0.05:
            continue
        c_ = tuple(o.data.color)
        rims.append({"jenis": "titik", "pos": tuple(lp), "warna": c_, "kuat": o.data.energy / (4 * _m.pi) * 0.6,
                     "gain": 1.3 * gain, "nama": o.name})
    sun = next((o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type == "LIGHT" and o.data.type == "SUN"), None)
    if sun is not None and not meta["dalam"]:                    # interior: matahari terhalang tembok (shader tak tahu oklusi)
        d = _V((0, 0, -1)); d.rotate(sun.rotation_euler)
        k = min(1.0, sun.data.energy / 2.0)
        rims = rims[:1] + [{"jenis": "arah", "arah": tuple(-d), "warna": tuple(sun.data.color), "gain": 0.9 * k * gain,
                            "nama": sun.name}]                     # maks 2 sumber: practical terkuat + matahari/bulan
    env["tokoh_look"]["rim"] = rims
    print("RIM_TOKOH", [(r["nama"], round(r["gain"], 2)) for r in rims])

rigs = []
for c in plan["characters"]:
    if c.get("rig"):
        rigs.append(bl_rig.Rig(c, chars_dir / c["name"], cam, env["emisi_tokoh"], env.get("tokoh_look")).build())
        char_pos.append(tuple(c["loc"][0][1:]))
        continue
    root = B.empty(c["name"], c["loc"][0][1:])
    for f, x, y, z in c["loc"]:
        root.location = (x, y, z); root.keyframe_insert("location", frame=f)
    B.interp(root, "LINEAR")
    char_pos.append(tuple(c["loc"][0][1:]))
    parts = {}
    for v in c["variants"]:
        img = bpy.data.images.load(str(chars_dir / f"{c['name']}_{v}.png"))
        parts[v] = B.billboard(f"{c['name']}_{v}", B.image_mat(f"M_{c['name']}_{v}", img, env["emisi_tokoh"]), c["height"], 1000 / 1400, cam, root)
    for name, o in parts.items():
        for f, v in c["events"]:
            o.hide_render = o.hide_viewport = (v != name)
            o.keyframe_insert("hide_render", frame=f); o.keyframe_insert("hide_viewport", frame=f)
    for f in c["pops"]:                                        # 'pop' kecil saat ganti ekspresi
        for ff, s in ((f - 1, 1.0), (f + 2, 1.07), (f + 7, 1.0)):
            root.scale = (s, 1, s); root.keyframe_insert("scale", frame=max(1, ff))
    root.scale = (1, 1, 1)
    if not c["cat"]:
        B.noise(root, "scale", 2, 0.02, 25)                    # napas
    for a, b in c["shakes"]:
        B.noise(root, "location", 0, 0.06, 1.5, a, b)          # gemetar
    for a, b in c["walking"]:
        B.noise(root, "location", 2, 0.03, 3, a, b)            # memantul saat melangkah

if info["steam"]:
    n = 128                                                        # uap berserat: noise fraktal x jatuhan radial
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    base = np.zeros((n, n))

    def _up(g):                                                    # interpolasi bilinear (halus, tidak kotak-kotak)
        xs = np.linspace(0, g.shape[0] - 1, n)
        rows = np.array([np.interp(xs, np.arange(g.shape[1]), r) for r in g])
        return np.array([np.interp(xs, np.arange(g.shape[0]), c) for c in rows.T]).T
    for octv, amp in ((4, 1.0), (7, 0.55), (12, 0.2)):
        base += amp * _up(rng.random((octv, octv)))
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:n, 0:n]
    rad = np.clip(1 - np.sqrt(((xx - 63.5) / 64) ** 2 + ((yy - 63.5) / 58) ** 2), 0, 1) ** 1.6
    a = np.clip((base / 1.75 - 0.25) * 1.6, 0, 1) * rad
    puff = bpy.data.images.new("uap", n, n, alpha=True)
    puff.pixels = np.dstack([np.ones_like(a)] * 3 + [a]).ravel().tolist(); puff.pack()
    steam_m = B.image_mat("M_uap", puff, emission=0.12, fade=True)
    steam_m.surface_render_method = "BLENDED"                     # uap: tembus pandang halus, bukan dither berbintik
    sb = steam_m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
    for l in list(steam_m.node_tree.links):                       # warna putih bersih (bukan tekstur) -> tidak cokelat keruh
        if l.to_socket in (sb.inputs["Base Color"], sb.inputs["Emission Color"]):
            steam_m.node_tree.links.remove(l)

                o.location = Vector(g) + Vector((0.02 * math.sin(i), 0, z)); o.scale = (s, s, s); o.color = (1, 1, 1, al)
                for p in ("location", "scale", "color"):
                    o.keyframe_insert(p, frame=f)
