==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/captions.py
# sha256: 043f659dbd8a5476a00108dd8705fe38c134e341e63da52a2c34f09c8d3efc81
==============================================================================================================
"""Subtitle gaya Shorts (1-3 kata, kata aktif berwarna) -> build/captions.ass"""


def ts(t):
    h, r = divmod(max(0, t), 3600); m, s = divmod(r, 60)
    return f"{int(h)}:{int(m):02d}:{s:05.2f}"


def ass_color(hexstr):
    h = hexstr.lstrip("#")
    return f"&H00{h[4:6]}{h[2:4]}{h[0:2]}&".upper()


def run(story, log=print):
    cfg = story.data.get("subtitle", {})
    path = story.path("captions.ass")
    if cfg is False or cfg.get("aktif", True) is False:
        path.write_text("")
        log("  subtitle: nonaktif"); return
    size = int(cfg.get("ukuran", 116))                           # 110-125: terbaca di layar ponsel
    outline = int(cfg.get("garis", 8))                           # tebal garis tepi 7-9
    margin = int(cfg.get("posisi", 640))                         # jarak dari bawah layar (px, dari 1920)
    max_words = int(cfg.get("maks_kata", 3))
    hl = ass_color(cfg.get("warna_sorot", "#FFD400"))
    font = cfg.get("font", "Poppins")

    words = []
    for line in story.timeline["lines"]:
        tokens = line["text"].split()
        use_tokens = len(tokens) == len(line["words"])         # pakai teks asli (dengan tanda baca) bila cocok
        for i, w in enumerate(line["words"]):
            words.append({**w, "text": tokens[i] if use_tokens else w["w"]})
    chunks, cur = [], []
    for w in words:
        cur.append(w)
        if len(cur) >= max_words or len(" ".join(x["text"] for x in cur)) >= 16 or w["text"][-1] in ",.?!\"":
            chunks.append(cur); cur = []
    if cur:
        chunks.append(cur)

    lines = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks):
        end = c[-1]["t1"] + 0.25
        if i + 1 < len(chunks):
            nxt = chunks[i + 1][0]["t0"]
            end = nxt if nxt - c[-1]["t1"] < 0.6 else end
        texts = [x["text"] for x in c]
        texts[-1] = texts[-1].rstrip(",")
        for j, w in enumerate(c):
            t1 = c[j + 1]["t0"] if j + 1 < len(c) else end
            body = " ".join("{\\c" + hl + "}" + t + "{\\c&HFFFFFF&}" if k == j else t for k, t in enumerate(texts))
            pop = "{\\fscx80\\fscy80\\t(0,90,\\fscx104\\fscy104)\\t(90,150,\\fscx100\\fscy100)}" if j == 0 else ""
            lines.append(f"Dialogue: 0,{ts(w['t0'])},{ts(t1)},Cap,,0,0,0,,{pop}{body}")
    head = f"""[Script Info]
ScriptType: v4.00+
PlayResX: 1080
PlayResY: 1920
WrapStyle: 0

[V4+ Styles]
Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding
Style: Cap,{font},{size},&H00FFFFFF,&H00FFFFFF,&H00101010,&H90000000,-1,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,{outline},4,2,70,70,{margin},1

[Events]
Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text
"""
    path.write_text(head + "\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    log(f"  subtitle: {len(chunks)} potongan")


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/chars.py
# sha256: 65d2a0b4cb249be89e29693930be6e8345b6de7e30080633205d38a1b00235af
==============================================================================================================
"""Generator tokoh 2D kartun (PNG transparan) dari spesifikasi di story.yaml.

Contoh spesifikasi tokoh manusia (semua kunci opsional):
    kulit: sawo                 # terang | langsat | sawo | gelap | #rrggbb
    rambut: [cepak, abu]        # cepak | poni | panjang | sanggul | botak  (+ warna)
    hijab: tosca                # warna hijab (menggantikan rambut)
    topi: [topi, biru]          # topi | peci | helm | caping | kupluk  (+ warna)
    baju: putih                 # warna baju / lengan
    celana: biru_tua
    rompi: oranye               # rompi parkir (strip reflektif otomatis)
    jaket: hijau                # jaket (mis. ojol), menutupi baju & lengan
    celemek: coklat
    dress: tosca                # gaun/seragam terusan (menggantikan baju+celana)
    sepatu: hitam
    handuk: hijau               # handuk di bahu
    tas: coklat                 # tas selempang
    kumis: true
    kacamata: true
    tangan: pinggang            # pose tangan kanan saat diam: turun | pinggang
    alat: peluit                # menambah varian 'whistle'

Setiap tokoh otomatis punya banyak varian (ekspresi, pose, jalan, tampak belakang).
"""
import json

import math
from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw, ImageFilter
import numpy as np

from .common import digest
from .palette import to_rgb

W, H, S = 1000, 1400, 3
INK = (38, 30, 32, 255)
OL = 14
HX, HY, HR = 500, 430, 290
VERSION = 20  # v20 (Phase 4.2-B): blush dipotong mask kulit per tampak; v19: topi & rambut panjang profil; v18: ekspresi ngantuk-bicara; v17: bahu profil; v16 (Phase 4.1): mata 'bawah' fokus (terbuka) vs 'ngantuk'/'sedih', alis 'fokus', tampak profil (_s); v15: garis tepi jari depan lebih tipis; v14 (Phase 4C): mata 'bawah'; v13 (Phase 4B): lengan bawah tanpa tangan + tangan genggam berlapis. naikkan bila gaya gambar diubah, supaya PNG dibuat ulang

LIP = (120, 40, 45, 255)


def rgba(v, default=None):
    c = to_rgb(v, default)
    return None if c is None else c + (255,)


def darker(c, k=0.75):
    return tuple(int(x * k) for x in c[:3]) + (255,)


def style_color(v, default_style, default_color):
    """'cepak' | ['cepak', 'abu'] | {gaya: cepak, warna: abu} -> (gaya, rgba)"""
    if v is None:
        return default_style, rgba(default_color)
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return v, rgba(default_color)
    if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
        return v[0], rgba(v[1] if len(v) > 1 else default_color)
    return v.get("gaya", default_style), rgba(v.get("warna", default_color))


def P(*v):
    return [int(x * S) for x in v]


class Canvas:
    def __init__(self):
        self.im = Image.new("RGBA", (W * S, H * S), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        self.d = ImageDraw.Draw(self.im)

    def ellipse(self, cx, cy, rx, ry, fill, outline=True, ow=OL):
        self.d.ellipse(P(cx - rx, cy - ry, cx + rx, cy + ry), fill=fill,
                       outline=INK if outline else None, width=int(ow * S) if outline else 0)

    def rrect(self, x0, y0, x1, y1, r, fill, outline=True, ow=OL):
        self.d.rounded_rectangle(P(x0, y0, x1, y1), radius=r * S, fill=fill,
                                 outline=INK if outline else None, width=int(ow * S) if outline else 0)

    def poly(self, pts, fill, outline=True):
        pts = [(x * S, y * S) for x, y in pts]
        self.d.polygon(pts, fill=fill)
        if outline:
            self.d.line(pts + [pts[0]], fill=INK, width=OL * S, joint="curve")

    def limb(self, pts, w, fill, outline=True, ow=OL):
        for col, ww, on in ((INK, w + 2 * ow, outline), (fill, w, True)):
            if not on:
                continue
            self.d.line([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in pts], fill=col, width=int(ww * S), joint="curve")
            for x, y in (pts[0], pts[-1]):
                self.d.ellipse(P(x - ww / 2, y - ww / 2, x + ww / 2, y + ww / 2), fill=col)

    def line(self, pts, w, col=INK):
        self.d.line([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in pts], fill=col, width=int(w * S), joint="curve")
        for x, y in (pts[0], pts[-1]):
            self.d.ellipse(P(x - w / 2, y - w / 2, x + w / 2, y + w / 2), fill=col)

    def arc(self, cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, w, col=INK):
        self.d.arc(P(cx - rx, cy - ry, cx + rx, cy + ry), a0, a1, fill=col, width=int(w * S))

    def clipped(self, draw_fn, mask_fn):
        """gambar sesuatu, tapi hanya di dalam bentuk mask (mis. rambut di dalam kepala)"""
        layer = Image.new("RGBA", self.im.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
        draw_fn(ImageDraw.Draw(layer))
        mask = Image.new("L", self.im.size, 0)
        mask_fn(ImageDraw.Draw(mask))
        a = Image.composite(layer, Image.new("RGBA", self.im.size), mask)
        self.im.alpha_composite(a)
        self.d = ImageDraw.Draw(self.im)

    def shade(self, mask=None, mask_fn=None, dx=60, dy=40, k=0.74, blur=14, light=None):
        """bayangan sabit lembut di sisi kanan-bawah bentuk (cahaya dari kiri-atas).
        mask: gambar L (mis. alpha seluruh badan) atau mask_fn yang menggambar bentuknya.
        light: (k_terang) -> sorotan tipis di sisi kiri-atas."""
        if mask is None:
            mask = Image.new("L", self.im.size, 0); mask_fn(ImageDraw.Draw(mask))
        shifted = ImageChops.offset(mask, -dx * S, -dy * S)
        cres = ImageChops.subtract(mask, shifted).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(blur * S))
        cres = ImageChops.multiply(cres, mask)
        a = np.asarray(self.im).astype(np.float32)
        m = np.asarray(cres).astype(np.float32)[..., None] / 255.0
        a[..., :3] *= 1 - (1 - k) * m
        if light:
            hi = ImageChops.subtract(mask, ImageChops.offset(mask, dx * S // 2, dy * S // 2)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(blur * S))
            h = np.asarray(ImageChops.multiply(hi, mask)).astype(np.float32)[..., None] / 255.0
            a[..., :3] += (255 - a[..., :3]) * (light * h)
        self.im = Image.fromarray(np.clip(a, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), "RGBA")
        self.d = ImageDraw.Draw(self.im)

    def save(self, path):
        self.im.resize((W, H), Image.LANCZOS).save(path)


def taper(c, pts, w0, w1, col=INK):
    """goresan meruncing (alis, kerutan, kumis): lebar w0 di awal -> w1 di akhir"""
    import math
    n = len(pts)
    left, right = [], []
    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(pts):
        ax, ay = pts[max(0, i - 1)]; bx, by = pts[min(n - 1, i + 1)]
        dx, dy = bx - ax, by - ay; L = math.hypot(dx, dy) or 1
        nx, ny = -dy / L, dx / L
        w = (w0 + (w1 - w0) * i / max(1, n - 1)) / 2
        left.append(((x + nx * w) * S, (y + ny * w) * S)); right.append(((x - nx * w) * S, (y - ny * w) * S))
    c.d.polygon(left + right[::-1], fill=col)
    for (x, y), w in ((pts[0], w0), (pts[-1], w1)):
        c.d.ellipse(P(x - w / 2, y - w / 2, x + w / 2, y + w / 2), fill=col)


def curve(p0, p1, p2, n=9):
    """kurva kuadratik p0 -> p2 dengan titik kendali p1"""
    return [((1 - t) ** 2 * p0[0] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * p1[0] + t * t * p2[0],
             (1 - t) ** 2 * p0[1] + 2 * (1 - t) * t * p1[1] + t * t * p2[1]) for t in [k / (n - 1) for k in range(n)]]


def head_mask(dr, inset=OL / 2):
    dr.ellipse(P(HX - HR + inset, HY - HR + inset, HX + HR - inset, HY + HR - inset), fill=255)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ wajah
EXPR = {  # nama: (mata, alis, mulut)
    "neutral": ("open", "calm", "flat"), "blink": ("closed", "calm", "flat"), "talk": ("open", "calm", "open"),
    "smile": ("happy", "soft", "smile"), "smiletalk": ("happy", "soft", "opensmile"),
    "sad": ("sedih", "sad", "frown"), "sadtalk": ("sedih", "sad", "open"),
    "sleepy": ("ngantuk", "calm", "flat"), "sleepytalk": ("ngantuk", "calm", "open"),
    "angry": ("open", "angry", "frown"), "angrytalk": ("open", "angry", "open"),
    "surprised": ("wide", "raised", "flat"), "surprisedtalk": ("wide", "raised", "open"),
    "look": ("look", "soft", "flat"), "whistle": ("squint", "soft", "whistle"),
}


WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)


def face_detail(c, skin, tua=False, yo=0, tx=0):
    """hidung, kerutan halus (tokoh tua) -- digambar di lapisan 'wajah'"""
    line = darker(skin, 0.68)
    if tx:                                                                           # 3/4: hidung menonjol ke arah hadap
        taper(c, curve((500 + tx + 18, 470 + yo), (500 + tx + 42, 512 + yo), (500 + tx + 14, 524 + yo)), 5, 8, line)
    else:
        taper(c, curve((482, 512 + yo), (500, 530 + yo), (520, 510 + yo)), 5, 8, line)   # hidung: lengkung bawah
    if tua:
        soft = darker(skin, 0.74)
        for k, (w, dy) in enumerate(((70, 0), (52, 26))):                           # dahi
            taper(c, curve((500 + tx - w, 300 + dy + yo), (500 + tx, 288 + dy + yo), (500 + tx + w, 300 + dy + yo)), 3, 6, soft)
        for sx in ((1,) if tx else (-1, 1)):
            x0 = 500 + tx + sx * 150                                                 # kerut ujung mata
            taper(c, [(x0, 438 + yo), (x0 + sx * 22, 430 + yo)], 5, 2, soft)
            taper(c, [(x0, 452 + yo), (x0 + sx * 22, 458 + yo)], 5, 2, soft)
            taper(c, curve((500 + tx + sx * 58, 540 + yo), (500 + tx + sx * 80, 575 + yo), (500 + tx + sx * 70, 610 + yo)), 6, 2, soft)  # garis senyum


def eye_shape(c, x, y, eyes, sq=1.0, sx=1):
    """satu mata. Phase 4.1: keadaan mata dibedakan dengan jelas:
      open/look  : bulat penuh, kilau atas            closed : garis (kedip)
      happy      : lengkung (senyum mata tertutup)    squint : chevron
      bawah      : FOKUS ke bawah - mata tetap terbuka (~80% tinggi), kelopak atas lurus tipis memotong puncak,
                   kilau di BAWAH (pupil menatap turun). Bukan mengantuk.
      ngantuk    : kelopak berat menutup setengah, garis kelopak melengkung turun, tanpa kilau
      sedih      : terbuka, kelopak atas miring turun ke sisi luar (sx = sisi luar), kilau kecil di bawah"""
    if eyes in ("open", "look"):
        c.ellipse(x, y, 30 * sq, 40, INK, outline=False)
        c.ellipse(x + 10 * sq, y - 15, 11 * sq, 12, WHITE, outline=False); c.ellipse(x - 11 * sq, y + 16, 5, 5, WHITE, outline=False)
    elif eyes == "wide":
        c.ellipse(x, y, 38 * sq, 48, INK, outline=False)
        c.ellipse(x + 12 * sq, y - 18, 14 * sq, 15, WHITE, outline=False); c.ellipse(x - 14 * sq, y + 20, 6, 6, WHITE, outline=False)
    elif eyes == "closed":
        c.line([(x - 30 * sq, y + 5), (x + 30 * sq, y + 5)], 10)
    elif eyes == "happy":
        c.arc(x, y + 23, 34 * sq, 34, 200, 340, 11)
    elif eyes == "squint":
        c.line([(x - 30 * sq, y - 10), (x + 28 * sq, y + 7), (x - 30 * sq, y + 23)], 10)
    elif eyes == "bawah":
        _lid_eye(c, x, y + 6, 30 * sq, 40, (x - 31 * sq, y - 22), (x + 31 * sq, y - 22))   # ~85% terbuka, puncak terpotong
        c.line([(x - 32 * sq, y - 22), (x + 32 * sq, y - 22)], 6)                         # kelopak atas tipis, lurus
        c.ellipse(x + 7 * sq, y + 28, 9 * sq, 7, WHITE, outline=False)                    # kilau BAWAH = menatap turun
    elif eyes == "ngantuk":
        _lid_eye(c, x, y + 4, 30 * sq, 40, (x - 31 * sq, y + 6), (x + 31 * sq, y + 6))      # setengah tertutup
        c.line(curve((x - 34 * sq, y + 2), (x, y + 12), (x + 34 * sq, y + 2)), 11)         # kelopak berat melengkung
    elif eyes == "sedih":
        _lid_eye(c, x, y + 4, 28 * sq, 38, (x - sx * 32 * sq, y - 28), (x + sx * 32 * sq, y - 4))
        c.line([(x - sx * 33 * sq, y - 29), (x + sx * 33 * sq, y - 5)], 7)                # kelopak miring turun ke luar
        c.ellipse(x - 4 * sq, y + 22, 6, 6, WHITE, outline=False)


def _lid_eye(c, x, y, rx, ry, l0, l1, n=40):
    """elips (mata) yang bagian atasnya dipotong garis kelopak l0-l1 (kanvas y ke bawah)"""
    (ax, ay), (bx, by) = l0, l1
    pts = []
    for k in range(n):
        t = 2 * math.pi * k / n
        px_, py_ = x + rx * math.cos(t), y + ry * math.sin(t)
        ly = ay + (by - ay) * ((px_ - ax) / (bx - ax) if bx != ax else 0.0)
        pts.append((px_, max(py_, ly)))
    c.poly(pts, INK, outline=False)


PROFILE_TURN = 2                                                                    # face(turn=2) = tampak profil


def skin_mask(sp, view):
    """Phase 4.2-B: mask KULIT wajah per tampak ('front' | 'q' | 's'): piksel berwarna kulit pada kepala tampak yang
    sama SETELAH rambut/hijab/topi/helm digambar, diciutkan setengah garis tepi. Lapisan khusus kulit (blush) dipotong
    mask ini sehingga tidak bisa melewati batas kulit (mis. hijab, helm). -> PIL 'L' (ukuran kanvas)"""
    from PIL import ImageFilter
    import numpy as np
    c0 = Canvas()
    hair_and_head(c0, sp, view)
    hat(c0, sp, turn={"front": 0, "q": 1, "s": 2}[view])
    a = np.asarray(c0.im)
    sk = np.array(sp["kulit"][:3], dtype=np.int16)
    m = (np.abs(a[..., :3].astype(np.int16) - sk).max(axis=2) <= 6) & (a[..., 3] >= 250)
    im = Image.fromarray((m * 255).astype("uint8"), "L")
    # erosi ~setengah garis tepi: blur lalu ambang tinggi (cepat; MinFilter besar terlalu lambat di kanvas 3x)
    return im.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(OL * S / 2)).point(lambda v: 255 if v >= 250 else 0)


def _skin_only(c, detail, draw):
    """gambar 'draw' (lapisan khusus kulit) lalu potong dengan mask kulit tampak (detail['skin_mask']) bila ada"""
    m = (detail or {}).get("skin_mask")
    if m is None:
        draw(c)
        return
    t = Canvas()
    draw(t)
    from PIL import ImageChops
    al = ImageChops.multiply(t.im.getchannel("A"), m)
    t.im.putalpha(al)
    c.im.alpha_composite(t.im)


def face(c, expr, mustache=False, glasses=False, blush=True, yo=0, detail=None, turn=0):
    """turn: 0 = depan, 1 = tampak 3/4 menghadap kanan layar (fitur bergeser, mata jauh menyempit)"""
    eyes, brows, mouth = EXPR[expr]
    if turn >= PROFILE_TURN:
        return face_profile(c, eyes, brows, mouth, mustache, glasses, blush, yo, detail)
    look = 35 if eyes == "look" else 0
    tx = int(70 * turn)
    ex = (400 + look + tx + int(25 * turn), 600 + look + tx)
    if blush:
        _skin_only(c, detail, lambda cc: [cc.ellipse(x, 545 + yo, 52 * k, 30, (236, 120, 110, 150), outline=False)
                                          for x, k in ((330 + tx + int(35 * turn), 1 - 0.35 * turn),
                                                       (670 + int(tx * 0.45), 1 - 0.2 * turn))])
    if detail:
        face_detail(c, detail["kulit"], detail.get("tua"), yo, tx)
    for i, x in enumerate(ex):
        y = 455 + yo
        sq = 1 - 0.18 * turn if i == 0 else 1.0                                       # mata jauh sedikit menyempit
        eye_shape(c, x, y, eyes, sq, sx=-1 if i == 0 else 1)
    by = 380 + yo
    # alis meruncing: pangkal (dekat hidung) tebal -> ujung tipis; bentuk per ekspresi
    for sx, x in ((-1, ex[0]), (1, ex[1])):
        inner, outer = x - sx * 34, x + sx * 36
        if brows == "calm":
            pts = curve((inner, by + 2), (x, by - 8), (outer, by + 2))
        elif brows == "raised":
            pts = curve((inner, by - 4), (x, by - 34), (outer, by - 8))
        elif brows == "soft":
            pts = curve((inner, by - 8), (x, by - 10), (outer, by + 4))
        elif brows == "sad":
            pts = curve((inner, by - 16), (x - sx * 4, by - 10), (outer, by + 8))
        elif brows == "angry":
            pts = curve((inner, by + 14), (x, by + 2), (outer, by - 12))
        elif brows == "fokus":                                                     # konsentrasi: rendah & rata, pangkal sedikit turun
            pts = curve((inner, by + 10), (x, by + 2), (outer, by + 6))
        else:
            continue
        taper(c, pts, 17, 8)
    if glasses:
        for x in ex:
            c.ellipse(x, 455 + yo, 62, 55, None, ow=9)
        c.line([(ex[0] + 62, 450 + yo), (ex[1] - 62, 450 + yo)], 9)
    if mustache:                                          # kumis dua sisi, sedikit melengkung turun di ujung
        for sx in (-1, 1):
            taper(c, curve((502 + tx + sx * 2, 570 + yo), (500 + tx + sx * 36, 560 + yo), (500 + tx + sx * 66, 578 + yo)), 22, 8)
    my = (612 if mustache else 595) + yo
    mx = 500 + tx
    if mouth == "flat":
        c.line([(mx - 30, my), (mx + 30, my + 2)], 10)
    elif mouth == "smile":
        c.arc(mx, my - 25, 50, 32, 25, 155, 11)
    elif mouth == "frown":
        c.arc(mx, my + 22, 42, 26, 205, 335, 11)
    elif mouth == "open":
        c.ellipse(mx, my + 5, 26, 30, LIP, ow=9)
    elif mouth == "opensmile":
        c.d.pieslice(P(mx - 45, my - 22, mx + 45, my + 48), 0, 180, fill=LIP, outline=INK, width=9 * S)
        c.d.pieslice(P(mx - 22, my + 18, mx + 22, my + 44), 0, 180, fill=(230, 110, 110, 255))
    elif mouth == "whistle":
        c.ellipse(mx, my, 16, 16, LIP, ow=8)
    elif mouth in ("chew", "chew2"):                      # mengunyah: bibir terkatup bergelombang + pipi menggembung bergantian
        sx = 1 if mouth == "chew" else -1
        ox = sx * 6
        c.line(curve((mx - 28 + ox, my + 2), (mx + ox, my - 7 * sx), (mx + 28 + ox, my + 2)), 10)
        c.arc(mx + sx * 92, my - 22, 30, 34, (-70 if sx > 0 else 110), (70 if sx > 0 else 250), 7)


def brow_pts(brows, inner, x, outer, by, sx):
    if brows == "calm":
        return curve((inner, by + 2), (x, by - 8), (outer, by + 2))
    if brows == "raised":
        return curve((inner, by - 4), (x, by - 34), (outer, by - 8))
    if brows == "soft":
        return curve((inner, by - 8), (x, by - 10), (outer, by + 4))
    if brows == "sad":
        return curve((inner, by - 16), (x - sx * 4, by - 10), (outer, by + 8))
    if brows == "angry":
        return curve((inner, by + 14), (x, by + 2), (outer, by - 12))
    if brows == "fokus":
        return curve((inner, by + 10), (x, by + 2), (outer, by + 6))
    return None


PROFILE_EYE_X = 175                     # tampak profil (menghadap kanan layar): mata/mulut maju sekian px dari pusat kepala
PROFILE_MOUTH_X = 168


def face_profile(c, eyes, brows, mouth, mustache=False, glasses=False, blush=True, yo=0, detail=None):
    """Phase 4.1-B: wajah tampak PROFIL (90 derajat, menghadap kanan layar; kiri = cermin oleh rig).
    Satu mata, satu alis, mulut pendek di sisi depan, hidung menonjol keluar garis kepala (lapisan 'wajah')."""
    ex, y = HX + PROFILE_EYE_X, 455 + yo
    if blush:
        _skin_only(c, detail, lambda cc: cc.ellipse(HX + 112, 548 + yo, 40, 28, (236, 120, 110, 150), outline=False))
    if detail:
        skin = detail["kulit"]
        nx = HX + (252 if yo else 282)                                               # tepi depan wajah (hijab: oval wajah)
        c.ellipse(nx + 4, 505 + yo, 30, 34, skin)                                    # hidung menonjol
        c.ellipse(nx - 18, 505 + yo, 28, 44, skin, outline=False)                    # tutup garis kepala di pangkal hidung
        if detail.get("tua"):
            soft = darker(skin, 0.74)
            taper(c, [(ex + 40, 438 + yo), (ex + 62, 430 + yo)], 5, 2, soft)
    eye_shape(c, ex, y, eyes, 0.78, sx=-1)
    pts = brow_pts(brows, ex + 30, ex, ex - 34, 380 + yo, -1)
    if pts:
        taper(c, pts, 17, 8)
    if glasses:
        c.ellipse(ex, 455 + yo, 50, 55, None, ow=9)
        c.line([(ex - 50, 450 + yo), (HX - 30, 445 + yo)], 9)
    if mustache:
        taper(c, curve((HX + 214, 568 + yo), (HX + 180, 562 + yo), (HX + 150, 580 + yo)), 22, 8)
    my = (612 if mustache else 595) + yo
    mx = HX + PROFILE_MOUTH_X
    if mouth == "flat":
        c.line([(mx - 8, my), (mx + 30, my + 1)], 10)
    elif mouth == "smile":
        c.arc(mx + 6, my - 18, 30, 24, 25, 120, 11)
    elif mouth == "frown":
        c.arc(mx + 6, my + 18, 28, 20, 240, 330, 11)
    elif mouth == "open":
        c.ellipse(mx + 14, my + 5, 16, 26, LIP, ow=9)
    elif mouth == "opensmile":
        c.d.pieslice(P(mx - 16, my - 18, mx + 40, my + 40), 0, 180, fill=LIP, outline=INK, width=9 * S)
    elif mouth == "whistle":
        c.ellipse(mx + 30, my, 13, 15, LIP, ow=8)
    elif mouth in ("chew", "chew2"):
        k = 1 if mouth == "chew" else -1
        c.line(curve((mx - 6, my + 2), (mx + 12, my - 6 * k), (mx + 30, my + 2)), 10)
        if k > 0:
            c.arc(mx - 60, my - 22, 26, 30, -70, 70, 7)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ badan
LEGS = {"stand": (((440, 440), (560, 560)), (0, 0)), "walkA": (((470, 410), (530, 590)), (0, 0)),
        "walkB": (((470, 470), (530, 530)), (30, 0)), "walkC": (((470, 590), (530, 410)), (0, 0)),
        "walkD": (((470, 470), (530, 530)), (0, 30))}


def legs(c, kind, pants, shoes, floor=1216):
    if kind == "sitback":                                              # duduk, tampak belakang: pinggul melebar di dudukan
        c.rrect(362, 1024, 638, 1098, 44, pants if pants else INK)
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                             # tumit sepatu mengintip di kiri-kanan
            c.ellipse(500 + sx * 112, floor - 22, 30, 21, shoes, ow=8)
        return
    (xs, lift) = LEGS[kind]
    for (x0, x1), up in zip(xs, lift):
        c.limb([(x0, 1040), (x1, 1300 - up)], 32, pants if pants else INK, outline=bool(pants))
        c.ellipse(x1 + (12 if x1 >= 500 else -12), 1312 - up, 42, 24, shoes, ow=8)


def hand(c, hx, hy, side, skin, grip=False):
    """tangan kartun. Terbuka: telapak + empat jari rapat + ibu jari terpisah di sisi dalam.
    grip: kepalan (punggung jari dengan sela ruas) + ibu jari melingkar di depan -> benda terlihat digenggam."""
    sh = darker(skin, 0.68)
    if grip:
        c.rrect(hx - 30, hy - 26, hx + 30, hy + 28, 22, skin)
        for k in (-12, 1, 14):                                                    # sela jari
            taper(c, [(hx + side * 26, hy + k), (hx + side * 2, hy + k + 2)], 5, 2, sh)
        c.ellipse(hx - side * 14, hy - 8, 14, 20, skin, ow=8)                     # ibu jari di depan genggaman
        taper(c, [(hx - side * 14, hy - 22), (hx - side * 12, hy + 2)], 4, 1, sh)
        return
    c.ellipse(hx - side * 22, hy - 8, 12, 19, skin, ow=8)                         # ibu jari
    c.rrect(hx - 27, hy - 28, hx + 27, hy + 30, 22, skin)                         # telapak + jari rapat
    for k in (-9, 4):                                                             # sela jari
        taper(c, [(hx + side * k, hy + 6), (hx + side * k, hy + 26)], 4, 1, sh)


def arm(c, side, pose, sleeve, skin):
    sx = HX + side * 140
    pts = {"down": [(sx, 760), (sx + side * 40, 900), (sx + side * 30, 990)],
           "point": [(sx, 760), (sx + side * 150, 700), (sx + side * 300, 620)],
           "hip": [(sx, 760), (sx + side * 95, 860), (sx + side * 30, 950)],
           "up": [(sx, 760), (sx + side * 70, 760), (sx - side * 70, 640)],
           "wave": [(sx, 760), (sx + side * 150, 700), (sx + side * 170, 520)]}[pose]
    c.limb(pts, 44, sleeve)
    hx, hy = pts[-1]
    if pose == "point":
        c.limb([(hx, hy), (hx + side * 70, hy - 35)], 18, skin)
    hand(c, hx, hy, side, skin)


def stitch(c, pts, col, w=4, dash=16):
    """garis jahitan putus-putus"""
    import math
    for (x0, y0), (x1, y1) in zip(pts, pts[1:]):
        L = math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0); n = max(1, int(L // dash))
        for k in range(0, n, 2):
            a, b = k / n, min(1, (k + 1) / n)
            c.line([(x0 + (x1 - x0) * a, y0 + (y1 - y0) * a), (x0 + (x1 - x0) * b, y0 + (y1 - y0) * b)], w, col)


def torso(c, sp, view):
    if sp["dress"]:
        c.poly([(355, 730), (645, 730), (690, 1120), (310, 1120)], sp["dress"])
        return
    c.rrect(370, 1000, 630, 1080, 30, sp["celana"])
    top = sp["jaket"] or sp["baju"]
    c.rrect(345, 700, 655, 1050, 70, top)
    fold = darker(top, 0.8)
    for sx in (-1, 1):                                            # jahitan bahu + lipatan pinggang
        c.line([(500 + sx * 120, 712), (500 + sx * 150, 760)], 5, fold)
        taper(c, curve((500 + sx * 130, 1000), (500 + sx * 100, 985), (500 + sx * 70, 1000)), 6, 2, fold)
    if sp["jaket"] and view == "front":
        j = sp["jaket"]
        c.rrect(420, 684, 580, 728, 18, darker(j, 0.88))                              # kerah berdiri
        c.line([(500, 700), (500, 1040)], 10, darker(j, 0.55))                         # ritsleting
        for y in range(712, 1036, 14):
            c.line([(494, y), (506, y)], 3, (150, 150, 155, 255))
        c.rrect(484, 730, 516, 770, 6, (175, 175, 180, 255))                          # kepala ritsleting
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                              # saku miring + panel
            c.line([(500 + sx * 70, 900), (500 + sx * 115, 975)], 9, darker(j, 0.6))
            c.line([(500 + sx * 128, 735), (500 + sx * 128, 860)], 5, darker(j, 0.78))
            taper(c, curve((500 + sx * 40, 820), (500 + sx * 70, 840), (500 + sx * 95, 830)), 6, 2, darker(j, 0.75))
        c.rrect(350, 1012, 650, 1050, 16, darker(j, 0.85))                            # rib pinggang
    elif not sp["jaket"] and view == "front":                                          # kemeja: kerah + kancing + saku
        b = sp["baju"]
        for sx in (-1, 1):
            c.poly([(500, 735), (500 + sx * 78, 700), (500 + sx * 92, 742), (500 + sx * 26, 772)], b)
        for y in (800, 860, 920, 980):
            c.ellipse(500, y, 7, 7, darker(b, 0.7), outline=False)
        c.rrect(538, 800, 608, 860, 8, b); c.line([(540, 812), (606, 812)], 4, darker(b, 0.75))
    if sp["rompi"]:
        if view == "front":
            c.poly([(352, 720), (450, 720), (500, 830), (550, 720), (648, 720), (652, 1040), (348, 1040)], sp["rompi"])
        else:
            c.poly([(352, 720), (648, 720), (652, 1040), (348, 1040)], sp["rompi"])
        for y in (880, 960):
            c.d.rectangle(P(352, y, 648, y + 26), fill=(205, 212, 220, 255))
    if sp["celemek"] and view == "back":                                               # tali celemek di punggung: leher + simpul pinggang
        a = darker(sp["celemek"], 0.85)
        for sx in (-1, 1):
            c.line([(500 + sx * 62, 712), (500 + sx * 40, 760)], 11, a)
        c.line([(352, 912), (648, 905)], 12, a)
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                              # simpul pita
            c.poly([(500, 908), (500 + sx * 48, 885), (500 + sx * 52, 932)], sp["celemek"])
            c.line([(500 + sx * 6, 914), (500 + sx * 22, 975)], 10, a)
        c.ellipse(500, 908, 13, 12, a)
    if sp["celemek"] and view == "front":
        a = sp["celemek"]
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                              # tali leher
            c.line([(500 + sx * 92, 806), (500 + sx * 60, 712)], 12, darker(a, 0.8))
        c.poly([(400, 800), (600, 800), (630, 1060), (370, 1060)], a)
        stitch(c, [(412, 815), (588, 815), (614, 1046), (386, 1046), (412, 815)], darker(a, 0.62))
        c.rrect(445, 925, 555, 990, 10, darker(a, 0.9))                                # saku celemek
        c.line([(500, 928), (500, 988)], 5, darker(a, 0.62))
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                              # tali pinggang
            c.line([(500 + sx * 108, 905), (500 + sx * 150, 912)], 11, darker(a, 0.8))


def hair_and_head(c, sp, view):
    skin = sp["kulit"]
    style, hc = sp["rambut"]
    if sp["hijab"]:
        hj = sp["hijab"]
        c.poly([(230, 600), (770, 600), (800, 900), (200, 900)], hj)              # kain menjuntai ke dada
        c.ellipse(HX, HY + 10, HR + 40, HR + 45, hj)
        if view == "back":
            return
        if view == "s":                                                             # profil: oval wajah di sisi depan
            fx_, fw = HX + 92, 160
        else:
            fx_, fw = HX, 240
        c.ellipse(fx_, HY + 40, fw, 255, skin)                                     # wajah oval
        c.clipped(lambda d: d.rectangle(P(0, 0, W, 300), fill=hj),
                  lambda d: d.ellipse(P(fx_ - fw + 7, HY + 40 - 255 + 7, fx_ + fw - 7, HY + 40 + 255 - 7), fill=255))
        c.ellipse(fx_, HY + 40, fw, 255, None)
        return
    if style == "panjang":                                                         # rambut panjang di belakang kepala
        if view == "s":                                                            # profil: tergerai di BELAKANG (sisi kiri)
            c.rrect(HX - 330, HY - 150, HX + 60, HY + 430, 120, hc)
        else:
            c.rrect(HX - 320, HY - 150, HX + 320, HY + 330 if view == "front" else HY + 420, 120, hc)
    if style == "sanggul":
        c.ellipse(HX, HY - 300, 95, 80, hc)
    if view == "s":                                                                # profil: telinga di tengah-belakang kepala
        pass                                                                       # digambar SETELAH kepala (di atas kulit)
    elif view == "q":                                                              # 3/4: hanya telinga dekat (kiri layar)
        c.ellipse(HX - 262, 462, 46, 55, skin)
        c.ellipse(HX - 258, 465, 21, 30, darker(skin, 0.82), outline=False)
        c.arc(HX - 262, 462, 22, 26, 200, 340, 7)
    elif view == "front":
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                         # telinga + daun telinga dalam
            c.ellipse(HX + sx * 280, 455, 48, 55, skin)
            c.ellipse(HX + sx * 284, 458, 22, 30, darker(skin, 0.82), outline=False)
            c.arc(HX + sx * 280, 455, 22, 26, 200 if sx < 0 else -20, 340 if sx < 0 else 160, 7)
    else:
        for sx in (-1, 1):
            c.ellipse(HX + sx * 280, 455, 48, 55, skin)
    if style in ("cepak", "poni") and view in ("front", "q", "s"):               # siluet: jumput rambut di ubun-ubun (bukan lingkaran sempurna)
        q = {"q": 40, "s": 80}.get(view, 0)
        tufts = []
        for k, a in enumerate(range(-50, 51, 10)):                                 # cepak: tepi atas bergerigi lembut (satu siluet)
            h = 12 + (k % 3) * 4
            ang = math.radians(a - 90 + q * 0.25)
            tufts.append((ang, h))
        for pad, colr in ((OL * 0.55, INK), (0, hc)):                               # lapis tinta lalu isi -> garis luar menyatu
            for ang, h in tufts:
                pts = [(HX + math.cos(ang + t_) * (r_ + pad), HY + math.sin(ang + t_) * (r_ + pad))
                       for t_, r_ in ((-0.1, HR - 12), (-0.045, HR + h * 0.75), (0.0, HR + h), (0.045, HR + h * 0.75), (0.1, HR - 12))]
                c.poly(pts, colr, outline=False)
    c.ellipse(HX, HY, HR, HR, skin)
    if style != "botak":
        if view == "back":
            low = HY + 230 if style in ("panjang", "sanggul") else HY + 175   # rambut pendek: tengkuk (kulit) terlihat
            if sum(hc[:3]) / 3 < 70:                                           # rambut hitam dari belakang: cokelat-arang agar terbaca di malam hari
                hc = tuple(int(v * 0.4 + w * 0.6) for v, w in zip(hc[:3], (96, 82, 78))) + (255,)
            def shape(d):
                d.ellipse(P(HX - 330, HY - 360, HX + 330, low), fill=hc)
                if low < HY + 200:                                                 # garis rambut tengkuk meruncing (bukan potongan lurus)
                    d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX - 250, low - 120), (HX - 150, low + 15), (HX - 60, low + 38),
                                                           (HX + 60, low + 38), (HX + 150, low + 15), (HX + 250, low - 120)]], fill=hc)
        elif style == "cepak":
            q = {"q": 45, "s": 120}.get(view, 0)                                   # 3/4 / profil: garis rambut bergeser
            def shape(d):                                                          # garis rambut 'M' lembut + cambang
                d.ellipse(P(HX - 330, HY - 420, HX + 330, HY - 95), fill=hc)
                pts = [(HX - 300, HY - 60), (HX - 250 + q, HY - 120), (HX - 120 + q, HY - 150), (HX + q, HY - 132), (HX + 120 + q, HY - 150),
                       (HX + 250, HY - 120), (HX + 300, HY - 60), (HX + 300, HY - 300), (HX - 300, HY - 300)]
                if q:
                    pts[0] = (HX - 300, HY + 10)                                    # sisi belakang kepala lebih banyak rambut
                if view == "s":                                                    # profil: rambut menutup belakang kepala s/d tengkuk
                    d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX - 310, HY - 300), (HX + 30, HY - 300), (HX + 10, HY - 60),
                                                           (HX - 60, HY + 40), (HX - 150, HY + 170), (HX - 310, HY + 200)]], fill=hc)
                d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in pts], fill=hc)
                for sx in ((-1,) if q else (-1, 1)):
                    d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX + sx * 300, HY - 90), (HX + sx * 256, HY - 105),
                                                           (HX + sx * 266, HY - 25), (HX + sx * 300, HY - 25)]], fill=hc)
        elif style == "poni":
            def shape(d):
                d.ellipse(P(HX - 330, HY - 430, HX + 330, HY - 40), fill=hc)
                d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(240, 330), (330, 300), (300, 380), (420, 320), (400, 400), (520, 320), (540, 390),
                                                       (640, 310), (650, 380), (760, 330), (780, 250), (230, 250)]], fill=hc)
                if view == "s":
                    d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX - 310, HY - 200), (HX + 20, HY - 200), (HX - 40, HY + 60),
                                                           (HX - 150, HY + 190), (HX - 310, HY + 220)]], fill=hc)
        else:  # panjang / sanggul: belah samping
            def shape(d):
                d.ellipse(P(HX - 330, HY - 430, HX + 330, HY - 60), fill=hc)
                d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(210, 300), (420, 290), (300, 470), (210, 520)]], fill=hc)
                if view == "s":
                    d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX - 320, HY - 250), (HX + 40, HY - 250), (HX - 30, HY + 80),
                                                           (HX - 120, HY + 260), (HX - 320, HY + 260)]], fill=hc)
        c.clipped(shape, head_mask)
        if view == "back" and style != "botak":                                      # rambut belakang: helai & kilap -> terbaca sbg rambut, bukan lubang hitam
            lum = sum(hc[:3]) / 3
            tex = (tuple(min(255, int(v * 1.6 + 50)) for v in hc[:3]) if lum < 110 else darker(hc, 0.72)[:3]) + (170,)
            for k, x in enumerate(range(HX - 230, HX + 240, 38)):                    # helai lebih rapat & jelas
                bend = (x - HX) * 0.18
                taper(c, curve((x - bend * 0.3, HY - 130 + (k % 3) * 14), (x + bend * 0.2, HY + 10), (x + bend * 0.6, HY + 150 - abs(x - HX) * 0.35)), 6, 1.5, tex)
            sheen = tuple(min(255, int(v * 1.6 + 70)) for v in hc[:3])
            dark_h = lum < 110
            if sp["topi"][0] and sp["topi"][0] != "tidak":               # bertopi/helm: kilap di pita rambut yang terlihat (bawah pinggiran)
                c.arc(HX, HY + 110, 210, 85, 218, 322, 26, sheen + ((80,) if dark_h else (45,)))
            else:
                c.arc(HX - 40, HY - 10, 230, 120, 205, 255, 14, sheen + ((120,) if dark_h else (55,)))
        if style in ("cepak", "poni") and view in ("front", "q", "s"):             # tekstur & kilap rambut
            tex = darker(hc, 0.72)
            tex = tex[:3] + (150,)
            for k, x in enumerate(range(HX - 170, HX + 190, 85)):
                taper(c, curve((x, HY - 250 + (k % 2) * 14), (x + 14, HY - 225), (x + 6, HY - 200 + (k % 2) * 14)), 5, 2, tex)
            c.arc(HX - 60, HY - 160, 170, 95, 205, 245, 10, tuple(min(255, int(v * 1.2 + 25)) for v in hc[:3]) + (140,))
        c.ellipse(HX, HY, HR, HR, None)
        if style in ("cepak", "poni") and view in ("front", "q", "s"):             # rambut menyatu dgn jumput: hapus garis kepala di ubun-ubun
            qa = {"q": 10, "s": 20}.get(view, 0)                                   # sama dengan geser sudut jumput (q * 0.25)
            c.arc(HX, HY, HR, HR, 226 + qa, 314 + qa, OL + 3, hc)
    if view == "s":                                                                # telinga profil (di atas rambut/kulit)
        c.ellipse(HX - 40, 470, 44, 54, skin)
        c.ellipse(HX - 36, 473, 20, 28, darker(skin, 0.82), outline=False)
        c.arc(HX - 40, 470, 21, 25, 200, 340, 7)


def hat(c, sp, turn=0):
    """turn 1: tampak 3/4 (garis tengah & ventilasi bergeser ke arah hadap, tali dagu hanya sisi dekat)"""
    kind, col = sp["topi"]
    t = int(60 * turn)
    if not kind or kind == "tidak":
        return
    if kind == "topi":
        if turn >= 2:                                                              # profil: mahkota + pet menjorok ke DEPAN
            c.poly([(220, 300), (290, 170), (470, 135), (640, 175), (720, 300)], col)
            c.poly([(620, 285), (900, 300), (905, 325), (610, 322)], darker(col))
        else:
            c.poly([(250 + t, 300), (330 + t, 175), (500 + t, 140), (670 + t, 175), (750 + t, 300)], col)
            c.rrect(210 + t, 280, 790 + t, 320, 18, darker(col))
    elif kind == "peci":
        c.poly([(320, 300), (345, 150), (655, 150), (680, 300)], col)
    elif kind == "helm":                                                       # helm setengah muka
        for sx in ((-1,) if turn else (-1, 1)):                                    # tali dagu
            c.line([(HX + sx * 268, HY - 30), (HX + sx * 215, HY + 205), (HX + sx * 90 + t, HY + 268)], 9, (40, 40, 44, 255))
        c.d.pieslice(P(HX - 320, HY - 345, HX + 320, HY + 250), 180, 360, fill=col, outline=INK, width=OL * S)
        lite = tuple(min(255, int(v * 1.1 + 14)) for v in col[:3]) + (255,)
        c.clipped(lambda d: d.ellipse(P(HX - 250, HY - 330, HX + 160, HY - 160), fill=lite),
                  lambda d: d.pieslice(P(HX - 300, HY - 325, HX + 300, HY + 230), 180, 360, fill=255))   # kilap cangkang
        c.d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(HX - 30 + t, HY - 344), (HX + 30 + t, HY - 344), (HX + 24 + t, HY - 62), (HX - 24 + t, HY - 62)]],
                    fill=darker(col, 0.72))                                         # garis tengah
        c.line([(HX - 3 + t, HY - 330), (HX - 3 + t, HY - 70)], 5, (235, 235, 235, 255))
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                                         # ventilasi (celah memanjang)
            for k in range(2):
                vx = HX + t + sx * (70 + k * 40) * (1 - 0.3 * turn * (sx < 0))
                c.rrect(vx - 7, HY - 305 + k * 10, vx + 7, HY - 240 + k * 10, 6, darker(col, 0.55), outline=False)
        c.rrect(170, HY - 66, 830, HY - 16, 18, darker(col, 0.7))                  # bibir helm
        c.line([(205, HY - 41), (795, HY - 41)], 4, darker(col, 0.45))
    elif kind == "caping":
        c.poly([(110, 330), (500, 80), (890, 330)], col)
        for x in (300, 500, 700):
            c.line([(500, 95), (x, 320)], 5, darker(col, 0.8))
    elif kind == "kupluk":
        c.d.pieslice(P(HX - 300, HY - 320, HX + 300, HY + 200), 180, 360, fill=col, outline=INK, width=OL * S)
        c.rrect(200, HY - 90, 800, HY - 30, 20, darker(col))
        c.ellipse(HX, HY - 330, 45, 45, darker(col))


def draw_human(sp, v):
    """v: dict(view, expr, left, right, legs, fx)"""
    c = Canvas()
    view = v.get("view", "front")
    legs(c, v.get("legs", "stand"), None if sp["dress"] else sp["celana"], sp["sepatu"], v.get("floor", 1216))
    torso(c, sp, view)
    sleeve = sp["jaket"] or sp["dress"] or sp["baju"]
    if sp["alat"] == "peluit" and view == "front":
        c.line([(455, 735), (500, 860), (545, 735)], 6, (40, 90, 170, 255))
        if v.get("expr") != "whistle":
            c.rrect(478, 850, 528, 880, 10, (225, 225, 230, 255))
    if sp["tas"]:
        c.line([(380, 730), (620, 960)] if view == "back" else [(620, 730), (380, 960)], 14, darker(sp["tas"], 0.7))
    arm(c, -1, v.get("left", "down"), sleeve, sp["kulit"])
    right = v.get("right", sp["tangan"])
    if right != "wave":
        arm(c, 1, right, sleeve, sp["kulit"])
    if sp["tas"]:
        c.rrect(560, 930, 680, 1030, 18, sp["tas"]) if view == "back" else c.rrect(320, 930, 440, 1030, 18, sp["tas"])
    if sp["handuk"] and view == "front":
        c.poly([(560, 705), (640, 715), (625, 900), (580, 900)], sp["handuk"])
    c.shade(mask=c.im.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > 128 else 0), dx=45, dy=0, k=0.72, blur=10, light=0.12)
    hair_and_head(c, sp, view)
    if sp["hijab"]:
        c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: (d.ellipse(P(HX - HR - 40, HY + 10 - HR - 45, HX + HR + 40, HY + 10 + HR + 45), fill=255),
                                   d.polygon([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in [(230, 600), (770, 600), (800, 900), (200, 900)]], fill=255)),
                dx=70, dy=60, k=0.74, blur=16, light=0.15)
    else:
        c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: d.ellipse(P(HX - HR, HY - HR, HX + HR, HY + HR), fill=255), dx=75, dy=70, k=0.72, blur=18, light=0.15)
    if view == "front":
        yo = 30 if sp["hijab"] else 0
        face(c, v.get("expr", "neutral"), sp["kumis"], sp["kacamata"], yo=yo, detail={"kulit": sp["kulit"], "tua": sp["tua"]})
        if v.get("expr") == "whistle":
            c.rrect(470, 600, 555, 640, 12, (225, 225, 230, 255))
            c.ellipse(553, 620, 22, 26, (225, 225, 230, 255), ow=9)
            k = 0 if v.get("fx", 0) == 0 else 18
            for r in (70, 115, 160):
                c.arc(560, 620, r + k, r + k, -35, 35, 12, (255, 235, 90, 255))
    elif sp["rambut"][0] == "sanggul" and not sp["hijab"]:
        c.ellipse(HX, 225, 85, 70, sp["rambut"][1])
    hat(c, sp)
    if right == "wave":                                   # tangan melambai di depan rambut
        arm(c, 1, "wave", sleeve, sp["kulit"])
    return c


def normalize(spec):
    g = lambda k, d=None: spec.get(k, d)
    tangan = {"pinggang": "hip", "turun": "down", None: "down"}.get(g("tangan"), "down")
    return {
        "kulit": rgba(g("kulit"), "langsat"),
        "rambut": style_color(g("rambut"), "cepak", "hitam"),
        "hijab": rgba(g("hijab")),
        "topi": style_color(g("topi"), None, "biru") if g("topi") else (None, None),
        "baju": rgba(g("baju"), "putih"),
        "celana": rgba(g("celana"), (60, 62, 78)),
        "rompi": rgba(g("rompi")), "jaket": rgba(g("jaket")), "celemek": rgba(g("celemek")),
        "dress": rgba(g("dress")), "sepatu": rgba(g("sepatu"), (38, 30, 32)),
        "handuk": rgba(g("handuk")), "tas": rgba(g("tas")),
        "kumis": bool(g("kumis")), "kacamata": bool(g("kacamata")),
        "tangan": tangan, "alat": g("alat"),
        # tokoh tua (kerutan halus): umur: tua, atau otomatis bila rambut abu/putih
        "tua": g("umur") == "tua" or (g("umur") is None and _is_grey(style_color(g("rambut"), "cepak", "hitam")[1])),
    }


def _is_grey(c):
    return c is not None and max(c[:3]) - min(c[:3]) < 25 and max(c[:3]) > 90


def human_variants(sp):
    V = {e: {"expr": e} for e in EXPR if e != "whistle"}
    V.update({"point": {"expr": "look", "right": "point"}, "pointtalk": {"expr": "talk", "right": "point"},
              "wave": {"expr": "smile", "right": "wave"}, "wavetalk": {"expr": "smiletalk", "right": "wave"},
              "back": {"view": "back"}})
    for l in "ABCD":
        V["walk" + l] = {"expr": "neutral", "legs": "walk" + l}
        V["back_walk" + l] = {"view": "back", "legs": "walk" + l}
    if sp["alat"] == "peluit":
        V["whistle"] = {"expr": "whistle", "left": "up", "fx": 0}
        V["whistle2"] = {"expr": "whistle", "left": "up", "fx": 1}
    return V


def draw_cat(color, variant):
    c = Canvas()
    fur = rgba(color, "oranye"); dark = darker(fur, 0.8)
    lg = {"walkA": [(330, 290), (420, 450), (600, 560), (680, 720)],
          "walkB": [(330, 360), (420, 400), (600, 640), (680, 650)]}[variant]
    for x0, x1 in lg:
        c.limb([(x0, 1150), (x1, 1300)], 34, fur)
    c.limb([(300, 1080), (220, 980), (200, 860), (240, 800)], 34, fur)
    c.ellipse(500, 1100, 230, 110, fur)
    for x in (420, 500, 580):
        c.line([(x, 1000), (x - 15, 1060)], 16, dark)
    c.poly([(640, 860), (665, 760), (720, 840)], fur)
    c.poly([(780, 840), (840, 760), (860, 870)], fur)
    c.ellipse(750, 940, 130, 115, fur)
    c.ellipse(800, 925, 16, 22, INK, outline=False)
    c.ellipse(690, 925, 16, 22, INK, outline=False)
    c.ellipse(745, 975, 12, 8, (230, 110, 120, 255), outline=False)
    for dy in (-12, 12):
        c.line([(790, 985 + dy), (900, 975 + dy * 2)], 5)
    return c


def variant_names(cdef):
    if cdef.get("jenis") == "kucing":
        return ["walkA", "walkB"]
    return list(human_variants(normalize(cdef.get("rupa", {}))))


def uses_rig(cdef):
    return cdef.get("jenis", "manusia") != "kucing" and cdef.get("rig", True)


def run(story, only=None, log=print):
    """Buat gambar tokoh: lapisan rig (bawaan) atau PNG semua varian (rig: false / kucing)."""
    out = story.path("chars", "x").parent
    cache_f = out / "cache.json"
    cache = json.loads(cache_f.read_text()) if cache_f.exists() else {}
    for name, cdef in story.data.get("tokoh", {}).items():
        key = digest([VERSION, cdef])
        rig = uses_rig(cdef)
        done = (out / name / "rig.json").exists() if rig else all((out / f"{name}_{v}.png").exists() for v in variant_names(cdef))
        if cache.get(name) == key and done:
            continue
        log(f"  gambar tokoh: {name}" + (" (rig berlapis)" if rig else ""))
        if cdef.get("jenis") == "kucing":
            for v in ("walkA", "walkB"):
                draw_cat(cdef.get("warna", "oranye"), v).save(out / f"{name}_{v}.png")
        elif rig:
            from . import rig2d
            rig2d.write(normalize(cdef.get("rupa", {})), out / name)
        else:
            sp = normalize(cdef.get("rupa", {}))
            for v, opts in human_variants(sp).items():
                draw_human(sp, opts).save(out / f"{name}_{v}.png")
        cache[name] = key
    cache_f.write_text(json.dumps(cache, indent=1))

"""Render di GPU Google Colab lewat Colab CLI (`colab`, sudah login di mesin ini).

Alur: scene.blend lokal -> salinan ter-pack (semua gambar di dalam file) -> unggah -> Blender 4.5.16 di VM
(EGL diarahkan ke driver NVIDIA) -> frame dikemas tar.gz -> unduh -> ekstrak ke folder lokal.

Sesi VM sengaja dibiarkan hidup antar perintah (Blender & cache shader sudah siap, render berikutnya cepat).
Hentikan dengan:  make.py --colab-stop   (VM menganggur tetap memakai compute unit)."""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import tarfile
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SESSION = os.environ.get("COLAB_SESSION", "anim2d")
GPUS = tuple(os.environ.get("COLAB_GPUS", "G4,L4,A100,T4").split(","))   # terbaik -> terendah untuk Eevee (raster); H100 minim unit grafis
REMOTE = "/content/job"
BLENDER = "/content/blender/blender"
NV_ENV = {"__EGL_VENDOR_LIBRARY_FILENAMES": "/usr/share/glvnd/egl_vendor.d/10_nvidia.json",
          "LD_LIBRARY_PATH": "/usr/lib64-nvidia"}


class ColabError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _colab():
    exe = shutil.which("colab") or str(Path.home() / ".local/bin/colab")
    if not Path(exe).exists():
        raise ColabError("Colab CLI tidak ditemukan (uv tool install google-colab-cli)")
    return exe


def _cli(*args, stdin=None, timeout=None):
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([_colab(), *args], input=stdin, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, cwd="/tmp")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:                 # jangan crash: pemanggil memutuskan (ensure -> stop sesi)
        r = subprocess.CompletedProcess(e.cmd, 124, (e.stdout or b"").decode(errors="replace") if isinstance(e.stdout, bytes) else (e.stdout or ""),
                                        f"timeout {timeout} dtk")
    r.out = "\n".join(l for l in (r.stdout + r.stderr).splitlines()
                      if "new version" not in l and "colab update" not in l and "silence this" not in l)
    return r


def _py(code, timeout=600, what="perintah", retries=0):
    """jalankan kode Python di kernel VM; kode harus mencetak '@@OK@@' bila berhasil.
    retries: ulangi bila 'colab exec' macet (hanya untuk perintah idempoten)."""
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".py", delete=False) as f:
        f.write(code)
    try:
        for k in range(retries + 1):
            r = _cli("exec", "-s", SESSION, "--timeout", str(timeout), "-f", f.name, timeout=timeout + 60)
            if "@@OK@@" in r.out or r.returncode != 124:
                break
    finally:
        os.unlink(f.name)
    if "@@OK@@" not in r.out:
        raise ColabError(f"{what} di Colab gagal:\n{r.out[-2500:]}")
    return r.out


def _py_bg(code, timeout, what="perintah", poll=20):
    """jalankan kode panjang sebagai proses LATAR di VM lalu pantau dengan exec singkat.
    ('colab exec' bisa tidak pernah kembali bila eksekusinya lama tanpa keluaran, walau VM sudah selesai.)"""
    import base64
    b64 = base64.b64encode(code.encode()).decode()
    _py(f"import base64, subprocess, os\n"
        f"for p in ('{REMOTE}/bg.done', '{REMOTE}/bg.log'):\n    os.path.exists(p) and os.remove(p)\n"
        f"open('{REMOTE}/bg.py', 'w').write(base64.b64decode('{b64}').decode())\n"
        f"subprocess.Popen(['bash', '-c', 'python3 {REMOTE}/bg.py > {REMOTE}/bg.log 2>&1; touch {REMOTE}/bg.done'], start_new_session=True)\n"
        f"print('@@OK@@')", 180, what + " (mulai)")
    t0 = time.time()
    while time.time() - t0 < timeout:
        time.sleep(poll)
        try:
            out = _py(f"import os\nif os.path.exists('{REMOTE}/bg.done'):\n    print('@@DONE@@'); print(open('{REMOTE}/bg.log').read()[-3000:])\n"
                      f"print('@@OK@@')", 60, what + " (cek)")
        except ColabError:
            continue                                    # cek gagal sesaat: coba lagi
        if "@@DONE@@" in out:
            body = out.split("@@DONE@@", 1)[1].rsplit("@@OK@@", 1)[0]    # buang penanda milik skrip cek
            if "@@OK@@" not in body:
                raise ColabError(f"{what} di Colab gagal:\n{body[-2500:]}")
            return body
    raise ColabError(f"{what}: lewat batas waktu {timeout} dtk")


def alive():
    r = _cli("status", "-s", SESSION, timeout=120)
    return "Status:" in r.out and "not found" not in r.out


_HW = {"hw": None}                                         # perangkat keras sesi (dicatat di provenance)


def ensure(log=print):
    """pastikan sesi GPU hidup & Blender terpasang"""
    if not alive():
        for g in GPUS:
            log(f"  [colab] meminta GPU {g} ...")
            r = _cli("new", "-s", SESSION, "--gpu", g, timeout=900)
            if alive():
                break
            log(f"  [colab] {g} tidak tersedia ({(r.out.strip().splitlines() or ['?'])[-1][:90]})")
            _cli("stop", "-s", SESSION, timeout=120)        # lepas alokasi yang mungkin datang terlambat (cegah VM yatim)
        else:
            raise ColabError("tidak ada GPU Colab yang tersedia saat ini; coba lagi nanti atau render lokal")
    st = _cli("status", "-s", SESSION, timeout=120).out
    hw = next((l for l in st.splitlines() if "Hardware" in l), "")
    if "BUSY" in st:                                       # sisa perintah lama (mis. render yang dihentikan dari laptop)
        log("  [colab] kernel masih sibuk perintah lama -> restart kernel")
        _cli("restart-kernel", "-s", SESSION, timeout=300)
    try:                                                   # kernel bisa IDLE tapi tidak menerima exec lagi
        _py("print('@@OK@@')", 45, "ping", retries=1)
    except ColabError:
        log("  [colab] kernel tidak merespons -> restart kernel")
        _cli("restart-kernel", "-s", SESSION, timeout=300)
        _py("print('@@OK@@')", 60, "ping setelah restart", retries=2)
    _py(f"import os; os.makedirs('{REMOTE}', exist_ok=True)\n"
        f"if os.path.exists('{BLENDER}') and os.path.exists('/usr/share/glvnd/egl_vendor.d/10_nvidia.json'):\n"
        f"    print('@@OK@@'); raise SystemExit\n" +
        (HERE / "setup_vm.py").read_text().replace("@@SETUP_OK@@", "@@OK@@"), 900, "persiapan VM")
    _HW["hw"] = hw.split('|')[1].strip() if '|' in hw else hw.strip()
    log(f"  [colab] siap: {_HW['hw']}")


def stop(log=print):
    r = _cli("stop", "-s", SESSION, timeout=300)
    log(f"  [colab] sesi '{SESSION}' dihentikan" if r.returncode == 0 else f"  [colab] stop: {r.out[-300:]}")


def _packed_copy(blend, blender_bin, env):
    """salinan .blend dengan semua gambar ter-pack (diperlukan di VM)"""
    dst = blend.with_name(blend.stem + "_packed.blend")
    expr = (f"import bpy; bpy.ops.file.pack_all(); "
            f"bpy.ops.wm.save_as_mainfile(filepath=r'{dst}', copy=True, compress=True)")
    r = subprocess.run([blender_bin, "-b", str(blend), "--python-expr", expr], capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
    if not dst.exists():
        raise ColabError("gagal mem-pack scene.blend:\n" + (r.stdout + r.stderr)[-1500:])
    return dst


def _upload(local, remote):
    for k in range(3):
        r = _cli("upload", "-s", SESSION, str(local), remote, timeout=1800)
        if r.returncode == 0 and "Upload failed" not in r.out:
            return
        time.sleep(5)
    raise ColabError(f"unggah {local.name} gagal: {r.out[-400:]}")


def _download(remote, local):
    for k in range(3):
        r = _cli("download", "-s", SESSION, remote, str(local), timeout=3600)
        if Path(local).exists() and Path(local).stat().st_size > 0:
            return
        time.sleep(5)
    raise ColabError(f"unduh {remote} gagal: {r.out[-400:]}")


def render(blend, out_dir, frames, pct, samples, blender_bin, local_env, prefix="f_", names=None, log=print,
           chunk=None, workers=None):
    """render 'frames' (daftar nomor frame) di Colab -> out_dir/<prefix><nomor 4 digit>.png
    (atau out_dir/<names[i]> bila 'names' diberikan). Dikerjakan per potongan (chunk) agar progres terlihat
    dan kegagalan di tengah tidak mengulang semuanya; frame yang sudah ada di out_dir dilewati."""
    out_dir = Path(out_dir); out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # beberapa proses Blender sekaligus di satu GPU: Eevee tidak memenuhi GPU sendirian (sinkron adegan, simpan PNG)
    workers = int(workers or os.environ.get("COLAB_WORKERS", 3))
    chunk = int(chunk or 40 * workers)
    names = names or [f"{prefix}{f:04d}.png" for f in frames]
    todo = [(f, n) for f, n in zip(frames, names) if not (out_dir / n).exists() or (out_dir / n).stat().st_size == 0]
    if not todo:
        return
    ensure(log)
    t0 = time.time()
    packed = _packed_copy(Path(blend), blender_bin, local_env)
    log(f"  [colab] unggah adegan ({packed.stat().st_size / 1e6:.1f} MB)")
    _py(f"import shutil, os, subprocess; subprocess.run(['pkill', '-f', '{BLENDER}']); "    # sisa render lama di VM
        f"shutil.rmtree('{REMOTE}/out', ignore_errors=True); os.makedirs('{REMOTE}/out'); print('@@OK@@')", 90, "bersihkan VM", retries=3)
    _upload(packed, f"{REMOTE}/scene.blend")
    packed.unlink()
    done = 0
    have = lambda n: (out_dir / n).exists() and (out_dir / n).stat().st_size > 0
    for i in range(0, len(todo), chunk):
        part = [(f, n) for f, n in todo[i:i + chunk] if not have(n)]   # bisa sudah dibuat render lokal paralel
        if not part:
            done += len(todo[i:i + chunk]); continue
        job = [[f, n] for f, n in part]
        code = f'''
import subprocess, os, json, tarfile, glob
env = {{**os.environ, **{json.dumps(NV_ENV)}}}
env["LD_LIBRARY_PATH"] = env["LD_LIBRARY_PATH"] + ":" + os.environ.get("LD_LIBRARY_PATH", "")
job = {json.dumps(job)}
for p in glob.glob("{REMOTE}/out/*") + glob.glob("{REMOTE}/out.tar"):   # out.tar lama tidak boleh tertukar
    os.remove(p)
def expr(sub):
    return ("import bpy, json; s=bpy.context.scene; s.render.resolution_percentage={pct}; s.eevee.taa_render_samples={samples}\\n"
            "for f, n in json.loads(%r):\\n    s.frame_set(f); s.render.filepath='{REMOTE}/out/'+n; bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)" % json.dumps(sub))
W = {workers}
ps = [subprocess.Popen(["{BLENDER}", "-b", "{REMOTE}/scene.blend", "--python-expr", expr(job[k::W])],
                       stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, env=env) for k in range(W) if job[k::W]]
logs = [p.communicate()[0] for p in ps]
got = sorted(os.listdir("{REMOTE}/out"))
if len(got) < len(job):
    print("\\n".join(l[-1500:] for l in logs))
else:
    with tarfile.open("{REMOTE}/out.tar", "w") as t:
        for n in got:
            t.add("{REMOTE}/out/" + n, arcname=n)
    print("@@OK@@", len(got))
'''
        tar = out_dir / ".colab_part.tar"
        for attempt in range(2):                           # exec kadang macet walau VM sudah IDLE: ambil hasil yg sudah jadi / ulang
            try:
                _py_bg(code, timeout=max(900, len(part) * 30), what=f"render frame {part[0][0]}-{part[-1][0]}")
                _download(f"{REMOTE}/out.tar", tar)
                break
            except Exception as e:                         # noqa: BLE001
                log(f"    [colab] potongan {part[0][0]}-{part[-1][0]} bermasalah ({str(e).strip().splitlines()[-1][:80] if str(e).strip() else e}); coba ambil hasil")
                try:
                    _download(f"{REMOTE}/out.tar", tar)
                    with tarfile.open(tar) as t:
                        if {m.name for m in t.getmembers()} >= {n for _, n in part}:   # isi harus frame potongan ini
                            break
                except Exception:                          # noqa: BLE001
                    pass
                if attempt == 1:
                    raise
        with tarfile.open(tar) as t:
            mem = [m for m in t.getmembers() if not have(m.name)]
            t.extractall(out_dir, members=mem, filter="data")
        tar.unlink()
        try:                                               # Phase 4.1-E: metadata provenance potongan ini
            from engine import provenance as PV
            got = {m.name for m in mem}
            PV.record_chunk(out_dir, blend, [(f, n) for f, n in part if n in got],
                            {"engine": "EEVEE", "resolution": [1080 * pct // 100, 1920 * pct // 100], "samples": samples,
                             "alpha_tokoh": local_env.get("ANIM_ALPHA", "scene") if hasattr(local_env, "get") else "scene",
                             "glare": local_env.get("ANIM_GLARE", "scene") if hasattr(local_env, "get") else "scene",
                             "colab": True},
                            f"colab:{os.environ.get('COLAB_SESSION', 'utama')}",
                            {"gpu": _HW.get("hw"), "blender_remote": BLENDER, "workers": workers,
                             "catatan": "scene.blend lokal di-pack (packed copy) sebelum diunggah; checksum = berkas lokal"})
        except Exception as e:                             # noqa: BLE001
            log(f"    [colab] PERINGATAN: metadata provenance gagal ditulis ({e})")
        done += len(todo[i:i + chunk])
        el = time.time() - t0
        log(f"    [colab] {done}/{len(todo)} frame, {el / done:.1f} dtk/frame, sisa ~{(len(todo) - done) * el / done / 60:.0f} menit")

# dijalankan DI VM Colab (colab exec -f): pasang Blender 4.5.16 sekali per sesi
import os, subprocess, pathlib, time
VER = "4.5.16"
DST = pathlib.Path("/content/blender")
t = time.time()
if not (DST / "blender").exists():
    subprocess.run("apt-get -qq update && apt-get -qq install -y libxi6 libxkbcommon0 libxkbcommon-x11-0 libsm6 libxrender1 "
                   "libxxf86vm1 libxfixes3 libgl1 libegl1 libglx-mesa0 xz-utils > /dev/null", shell=True, check=True)
    url = f"https://download.blender.org/release/Blender{VER.rsplit('.', 1)[0]}/blender-{VER}-linux-x64.tar.xz"
    subprocess.run(f"wget -q {url} -O /content/b.tar.xz && tar xf /content/b.tar.xz -C /content && rm /content/b.tar.xz "
                   f"&& mv /content/blender-{VER}-linux-x64 {DST}", shell=True, check=True)
# Eevee butuh OpenGL: arahkan EGL (glvnd) ke driver NVIDIA, bukan Mesa llvmpipe (CPU, sangat lambat)

vend.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
vend.write_text('{"file_format_version": "1.0.0", "ICD": {"library_path": "/usr/lib64-nvidia/libEGL_nvidia.so.0"}}')
r = subprocess.run([str(DST / "blender"), "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True)
print(r.stdout.splitlines()[0] if r.stdout else r.stderr[-500:])
print(subprocess.run("nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,driver_version --format=csv,noheader", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout)
print(f"@@SETUP_OK@@ {time.time() - t:.0f}s")

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/common.py
# sha256: 00c89012d2c73786a444736cb74f50a284ec0ec6249d260ad5d24fce25980e38
==============================================================================================================
"""Hal-hal umum: membaca story.yaml, lokasi folder, dan pembaca 'cue' waktu.

Cue adalah cara menunjuk waktu di narasi tanpa menulis detik:
    "s2"              -> awal baris narasi s2
    "s2:meniup"       -> saat kata "meniup" mulai diucapkan di baris s2
    "s3:tidak#2"      -> kemunculan ke-2 kata "tidak" di baris s3
    "s5:itu.end"      -> saat kata "itu" selesai diucapkan
    "s2:pak-0.1"      -> 0,1 detik sebelum kata "pak"
    "end"             -> akhir video
    12.5              -> detik ke-12,5 (angka biasa)
"""
import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FPS = 30


class StoryError(Exception):
    pass


class Story:
    def __init__(self, story_dir):
        self.dir = Path(story_dir).resolve()
        f = self.dir / "story.yaml"
        if not f.exists():
            raise StoryError(f"Tidak ada {f}")
        self.data = yaml.safe_load(f.read_text()) or {}
        self.slug = self.dir.name
        self.build = self.dir / "build"
        self.out = self.dir / "output"
        d = self.data
        self.voice = d.get("suara", "id-ID-ArdiNeural")
        self.lines = []
        for i, l in enumerate(d.get("narasi", [])):
            if isinstance(l, str):
                l = {"teks": l}
            self.lines.append({"id": str(l.get("id", f"n{i + 1}")), "text": l.get("teks", l.get("text", "")),
                               "voice": l.get("suara", self.voice), "rate": l.get("kecepatan", d.get("kecepatan", "+0%")),
                               "pitch": l.get("nada", d.get("nada", "+0Hz")), "pause": float(l.get("jeda", d.get("jeda", 0.45)))})
        if not self.lines:
            raise StoryError("story.yaml belum punya 'narasi'")

    def path(self, *parts):
        p = self.build.joinpath(*parts)
        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return p

    @property
    def timeline(self):
        f = self.build / "audio" / "timeline.json"
        if not f.exists():
            raise StoryError("Narasi belum dibuat. Jalankan langkah 'tts' dulu.")
        return normalize_timeline(json.loads(f.read_text()))


def to_ms(t):
    """detik (float) -> milidetik integer (pembulatan setengah ke atas, deterministik)"""
    return int(math.floor(float(t) * 1000 + 0.5))


def normalize_timeline(tl):
    """Phase 4A: representasi waktu tunggal = milidetik integer.
    Tiap baris mendapat start_ms / end_ms dengan aturan batas eksplisit: interval SETENGAH-TERBUKA [start_ms, end_ms),
    dan end_ms baris n == start_ms baris n+1 (tidak ada celah / tumpang tindih akibat pembulatan float).
    Kata mendapat t0_ms / t1_ms. Field detik (start, dur, t0, t1, total) tetap ada untuk konsumen lama (audio, subtitle).
    Timeline lama (tanpa *_ms) dinormalisasi di sini, jadi build lama tetap bisa dipakai."""
    lines = tl["lines"]
    for l in lines:
        l["start_ms"] = to_ms(l["start"]) if "start_ms" not in l else int(l["start_ms"])
    for i, l in enumerate(lines):
        nxt = lines[i + 1]["start_ms"] if i + 1 < len(lines) else None
        own_end = int(l["end_ms"]) if "end_ms" in l else to_ms(l["start"] + l["dur"])
        l["end_ms"] = nxt if nxt is not None else own_end           # rapat ke baris berikutnya
        if l["end_ms"] <= l["start_ms"]:
            raise StoryError(f"timeline: baris {l['id']} berdurasi nol/negatif ({l['start_ms']}..{l['end_ms']} ms)")
        for w in l["words"]:
            w["t0_ms"], w["t1_ms"] = to_ms(w["t0"]), to_ms(w["t1"])
    tl["total_ms"] = int(tl.get("total_ms") or to_ms(tl["total"]))
    if lines:
        tl["total_ms"] = max(tl["total_ms"], lines[-1]["end_ms"])
    tl["time_unit"] = "ms"
    return tl


def norm_word(w):
    return re.sub(r"[^\w]", "", w.lower())


CUE_RE = re.compile(r"^(?P<line>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)(?::(?P<word>[^#+\-.]+?)(?:#(?P<n>\d+))?(?P<end>\.end)?)?"
                    r"\s*(?P<off>[+-]\s*\d+(?:\.\d+)?)?$")


class Cues:
    def __init__(self, timeline):
        self.tl = timeline if "total_ms" in timeline and all("end_ms" in l for l in timeline["lines"]) \
            else normalize_timeline(timeline)
        self.lines = {l["id"]: l for l in self.tl["lines"]}
        self.total = self.tl["total"]

    def line_of(self, cue):
        """ID baris yang DIRUJUK cue ('n4', 'n4:kata', 'n4:kata+0.2'); None bila cue berupa angka/awal/akhir.
        Dipakai untuk aksi bicara: baris ditentukan oleh ID, bukan dengan mencari interval waktu."""
        if cue is None or isinstance(cue, (int, float)):
            return None
        m = CUE_RE.match(str(cue).strip())
        if not m or m["line"] not in self.lines:
            return None
        return m["line"]

    def line_at(self, t):
        """baris yang aktif pada waktu t (detik), interval setengah-terbuka [start_ms, end_ms) dalam ms integer"""
        ms = to_ms(t)
        for l in self.tl["lines"]:
            if l["start_ms"] <= ms < l["end_ms"]:
                return l
        return self.tl["lines"][-1] if self.tl["lines"] and ms >= self.tl["lines"][-1]["start_ms"] else None

    def __call__(self, cue):
        if cue is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(cue, (int, float)):
            return float(cue)
        cue = str(cue).strip()
        if cue in ("end", "akhir"):
            return self.total
        if cue in ("start", "awal"):
            return 0.0
        m = CUE_RE.match(cue)
        if not m:
            raise StoryError(f"Cue '{cue}' tidak dikenali (contoh: s2, s2:kata, s2:kata#2, s2:kata.end, s2:kata+0.3)")
        line = self.lines.get(m["line"])
        if line is None:
            raise StoryError(f"Cue '{cue}': baris '{m['line']}' tidak ada. Baris yang ada: {', '.join(self.lines)}")
        if m["word"] is None:
            t = line["start"]
        else:
            w = norm_word(m["word"])
            hits = [x for x in line["words"] if norm_word(x["w"]) == w]
            n = int(m["n"] or 1)
            if len(hits) < n:
                words = " ".join(x["w"] for x in line["words"])
                raise StoryError(f"Cue '{cue}': kata '{m['word']}' (ke-{n}) tidak ada di baris {m['line']}: \"{words}\"")
            t = hits[n - 1]["t1" if m["end"] else "t0"]
        if m["off"]:
            t += float(m["off"].replace(" ", ""))
        return max(0.0, min(self.total, t))

    def line_end(self, line_id):
        """akhir UCAPAN baris (akhir kata terakhir), bukan akhir jeda"""
        l = self.lines[line_id]
        return l["words"][-1]["t1_ms"] / 1000 if l["words"] else l["end_ms"] / 1000

    def words_between(self, t0, t1):
        """kata yang MULAI di [t0, t1) — dibandingkan dalam ms integer (tanpa toleransi float)"""
        a, b = to_ms(t0), to_ms(t1)
        return [w for l in self.tl["lines"] for w in l["words"] if a <= w["t0_ms"] < b]


def frame(t):
    """detik -> nomor frame (frame pertama = 1). Lewat ms integer, pembulatan setengah ke atas (aturan batas eksplisit:
    kejadian pada ms m jatuh di frame floor(m*FPS/1000 + 0.5) + 1)."""
    ms = to_ms(t)
    return (ms * FPS + 500) // 1000 + 1


def digest(obj):
    return hashlib.sha1(json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/grade.py
# sha256: 9ea24bd65bdcabe254252cad72ddb5c822ba0b560505b26169ce94fcab8e4f0a
==============================================================================================================
"""Grading per shot (Phase 4D-D3): look sinematik global + koreksi per shot.

Urutan penggabungan (belakang menang):  warna (story, global)  <  koreksi otomatis shot  <  shot.warna (story, manual)

Koreksi otomatis memakai metrik luminans yang SAMA di semua tempat (still, video, laporan):
  hitam  = porsi piksel dengan luma Rec.709 < 0.03   (black crush)
  gelap  = porsi luma < 0.08
  terbakar = porsi luma > 0.97                        (highlight clipping)
Aturan: grading boleh menambah 'hitam' paling banyak MAX_CRUSH_ADD dibanding RAW (suasana malam tetap gelap,
tetapi grading tidak boleh menelan detail yang sudah ada di RAW). Bila lebih, vinyet & kontras dikurangi dan titik
hitam diangkat bertahap (maks 4 iterasi). Metrik subjek dihitung juga pada kotak subjek (bila diberikan)."""
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

MAX_CRUSH_ADD = 0.02
LUMA = np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])


def metrics(img, box=None):
    a = np.asarray(img.convert("RGB"), dtype=np.float32) / 255
    if box:
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = (int(v) for v in box)
        a = a[max(0, y0):y1, max(0, x0):x1]
    y = a @ LUMA
    if y.size == 0:
        return None
    return {"hitam": round(float((y < 0.03).mean()), 4), "gelap": round(float((y < 0.08).mean()), 4),
            "terbakar": round(float((y > 0.97).mean()), 5), "median": round(float(np.median(y)), 4),
            "p95": round(float(np.percentile(y, 95)), 4)}


def apply(src, filters):
    """grading ffmpeg satu gambar -> PIL.Image"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        out = Path(td) / "g.png"
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", str(src), "-vf", ",".join(filters), "-frames:v", "1",
                        "-update", "1", str(out)], check=True)
        return Image.open(out).copy()


def auto_correct(src, filters_fn, base_override=None, box=None):
    """-> (override, laporan). filters_fn(override) -> daftar filter ffmpeg"""
    raw = metrics(Image.open(src))
    ov = dict(base_override or {})
    steps = []
    for it in range(5):
        g = metrics(apply(src, filters_fn(ov)))
        steps.append({"override": dict(ov), "graded": g})
        if g["hitam"] - raw["hitam"] <= MAX_CRUSH_ADD or it == 4:
            break
        # kurangi penyebab crush: vinyet (sudut) -> kontras -> angkat titik hitam
        ov["vinyet"] = round(float(ov.get("vinyet", 0.3)) * 0.6, 3)
        ov["kontras"] = round(max(1.0, float(ov.get("kontras", 1.05)) - 0.02), 3)
        ov["angkat_hitam"] = round(float(ov.get("angkat_hitam", 0.025)) + 0.012, 3)
    rep = {"raw": raw, "langkah": steps, "akhir": steps[-1]["graded"], "override": ov,
           "lolos": steps[-1]["graded"]["hitam"] - raw["hitam"] <= MAX_CRUSH_ADD}
    if box:
        rep["subjek_raw"] = metrics(Image.open(src), box)
    return ov, rep


class ShotGrades:
    """cache koreksi per shot di build/grade_auto.json (kunci: build ID + nomor shot)"""

    def __init__(self, story, build_id):
        self.f = story.build / "grade_auto.json"
        self.bid = build_id
        d = json.loads(self.f.read_text()) if self.f.exists() else {}
        self.d = d if d.get("build_id") == build_id else {"build_id": build_id, "shots": {}}

    def get(self, n):
        return (self.d["shots"].get(str(n)) or {}).get("override")

    def put(self, n, ov, rep, src):
        self.d["shots"][str(n)] = {"override": ov, "laporan": rep, "sumber": str(src)}
        self.f.write_text(json.dumps(self.d, indent=1))

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/grip.py
# sha256: f34f737009f69be8ffa6f317162ee66659786bea44199ce3b45163f74da7f491
==============================================================================================================
"""Grip Profile (Phase 4B-B1): cara memegang ditentukan TIPE BENDA (atau metadata aset), bukan nama tokoh.

Tiap profil mendeskripsikan:
  kelas        plate | utensil | cup | flat | box | helmet | bag
  gaya         gaya tangan 2D berlapis (sprite rig2d):  power | pinch | wrap | palm | hook
               - lapisan BELAKANG (telapak/jari di balik benda) menempel di pergelangan, di belakang benda
               - lapisan DEPAN (ibu jari / jari melingkar) disajikan di DEPAN benda (presentasi sadar-kamera)
  titik        titik genggam semantik di ruang lokal benda (m): 'rim' (tepi piring), 'handle' (gagang), 'body', ...
  sumbu_tangan arah lokal benda yang harus sejajar sumbu tangan di layar:
               power -> sumbu gagang melintang kepalan; pinch -> dari tepi ke pusat piring (jari di bawah piring)
  kontak       palm | fingers | thumb_over | wrap  (dokumentasi + validator)
  dims         ukuran benda (m), dipakai jangkauan & batas presentasi
  tangan_pref  'dominant' | 'nondominant' | 'any' (tangan bebas bila tangan dominan sibuk)
  jarak        posisi pegang ideal relatif badan: fwd (m, ke depan), lat (m, ke sisi tangan), py (y kanvas setinggi)
  maju_min     jarak minimum benda di depan bidang tokoh (arah kamera) sebelum skala presentasi dipakai
  presentasi   batas koreksi visual: skala_min (Phase 4.1: piring 1,0 = ukuran fisik tidak boleh berubah; di bawah
               batas -> FAIL validator), lompat_maks (m per frame)
  presentasi.geser_maks (Phase 4.2-A2) jarak MAKSIMUM (m) geser presentasi sepanjang sinar kamera (pengganti skala
               untuk benda berukuran tetap). Di atas batas -> WARN validator (pose/blocking harus diperbaiki, bukan
               geser tak terbatas). 4 cm = sekitar sepertiga jari-jari piring makan.
  toleransi_orientasi  (Phase 4.1) galat orientasi tangan maksimum (derajat) terhadap sumbu benda di layar.
               Benda kaku (utensil) 10° = target engineering; piring/gelas lebih longgar karena simetris.
  dof          (Phase 4.1) derajat kebebasan genggam yang boleh dipilih solver agar orientasi tangan tercapai tanpa
               menekuk pergelangan berlebihan: 'arah_gagang' (utensil, ujung tetap), 'sudut_tepi' (piring, pusat
               tetap), 'sudut_badan' (gelas), None (titik genggam tetap)
  rotasi_lengan (Phase 4.2-A1) varian rotasi lengan bawah yang sah untuk genggam power: ["pronasi"] dan/atau
               "supinasi". Ujung alat SELALU keluar di sisi ibu jari. Pada sprite 2D, supinasi = lapisan tangan
               DICERMINKAN pada sumbu lengan bawah (ibu jari pindah sisi) sehingga target sudut tangan berputar 180
               derajat SECARA KONSISTEN dengan gambar. Membalik target TANPA mencerminkan tangan (Phase 4.1) = inversi
               tidak sah -> dilaporkan 'terbalik_tanpa_izin' (FAIL validator). Galat orientasi diukur (a) terhadap
               varian yang dirender (ibu jari sprite) dan (b) terhadap genggam pronasi yang dimaksud desain.
Metadata aset (aset.json 'grip': {...}) boleh menimpa kunci mana pun -> aset baru tidak butuh kode baru."""
import json
