_ASSET_GRIPS = None


def _asset_grips():
    """aset.json: {"<id>": {..., "grip": {"kelas": "box", "dims": {...}, ...}}}"""
    global _ASSET_GRIPS
    if _ASSET_GRIPS is None:
        _ASSET_GRIPS = {}
        f = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "external" / "aset.json"
        try:
            for k, v in json.loads(f.read_text()).items():
                if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(v.get("grip"), dict):
                    _ASSET_GRIPS[k] = v["grip"]
        except Exception:
            pass
    return _ASSET_GRIPS


def profile(kind):
    """profil lengkap untuk tipe benda 'kind' (tipe bawaan atau id aset ber-metadata 'grip')"""
    spec = dict(PROPS.get(kind) or _asset_grips().get(kind) or {"kelas": "flat", "dims": {}})
    base = _BASE[spec["kelas"]]
    out = json.loads(json.dumps(base))
    for k, v in spec.items():
        if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(out.get(k), dict):
            out[k] = {**out[k], **v}
        else:
            out[k] = v
    out["tipe"] = kind
    return out


def style_of(kind):
    return profile(kind)["gaya"]


def hand_angle(axis_screen, style, side=None):
    """sudut tangan (derajat, konvensi rotasi Y Blender pada bidang sprite: + = searah jarum jam di layar)
    agar sumbu tangan sesuai sumbu benda di layar.
      axis_screen: (dx, dy) arah di kanvas (y ke bawah)
      power: gagang melintang kepalan -> sumbu-x tangan sejajar gagang (tangan tegak lurus gagang);
             dengan 'side' (sisi sprite) bertanda: axis_screen = arah ke UJUNG alat, keluar di sisi ibu jari
      pinch: jari menunjuk dari tepi ke pusat piring -> sumbu-y tangan (ke bawah) sejajar axis
      wrap:  (Phase 4.2) axis = sumbu badan gelas; sumbu-y tangan sejajar (tanda dipilih bl_interact._grip_ik)"""
    # rotasi kanvas (y ke bawah) sebesar t searah jarum jam: (x, y) -> (x cos t - y sin t, x sin t + y cos t)
    dx, dy = axis_screen
    if abs(dx) + abs(dy) < 1e-9:
        return 0.0
    if style == "power":                              # sumbu-x tangan (1, 0) -> (cos t, sin t) sejajar gagang
        a = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        if side is not None:                          # Phase 4.1: BERTANDA - ujung alat keluar di sisi ibu jari
            return wrap_deg(a if side < 0 else a + 180)   # ibu jari sprite di x = -side (sisi dalam)
        return (a + 90) % 180 - 90                    # tanpa sisi: gagang tanpa arah -> [-90, 90)
    return math.degrees(math.atan2(-dx, dy))          # sumbu-y tangan (0, 1) -> (-sin t, cos t) = arah ke benda


def thumb_dir(theta, side, supinated=False):
    """Phase 4.2-A1: arah sisi IBU JARI tangan sprite di layar (kanvas, y ke bawah) untuk sudut tangan theta.
    Ibu jari sprite di x lokal = -side; supinasi (tangan dicerminkan) memindahkannya ke +side."""
    return rot_canvas((side if supinated else -side, 0.0), theta)


def rot_canvas(v, deg):
    t = math.radians(deg)
    return (v[0] * math.cos(t) - v[1] * math.sin(t), v[0] * math.sin(t) + v[1] * math.cos(t))


def wrap_deg(a):
    return (a + 180) % 360 - 180


WRIST_LIMIT = 75.0                                    # batas tekuk pergelangan relatif lengan bawah (derajat)
WRIST_COMFORT = 30.0                                  # Phase 4.1: tekuk di atas ini dihindari solver (kepalan 'menempel' di samping lengan)

==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/manifest.py
# sha256: 5c70400322bc4c825c278337065807ccea861c646af2b62d25b9ea5e31f2ef6f
==============================================================================================================
"""Provenance build (Phase 4A-A2).

Setiap build adegan mendapat BUILD ID = hash dari semua masukan yang menentukan gambar:
  source (engine/**/*.py, make.py, validator), story.yaml, timeline narasi, tokoh (VERSION + rig.json + semua PNG
  lapisan), manifest aset eksternal (aset.json), plan.json.
build/manifest.json mencatat masukan, build ID, waktu, dan semua keluaran (still, video, laporan, validator) dengan
frame, shot, pengaturan render, dan checksum SHA-256. PNG distempel metadata teks (build_id, frame, shot), MP4 lewat
tag 'comment'. Paket review hanya menerima berkas yang build ID-nya sama (verify()).

Proyek bukan repo git: 'git_commit' diisi bila ada, selain itu None dan source_hash dipakai."""
import hashlib
import json
import platform
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

from .common import ROOT

SOURCE_GLOBS = ["engine/*.py", "engine/sets/*.py", "engine/colab/*.py", "make.py"]     # menentukan adegan/render -> build ID
TOOL_GLOBS = ["scripts/dev/*.py", "tests/*.py"]   # alat analisis (validator, paket, uji): dicatat, TIDAK mengubah build ID


def sha256_file(p, chunk=1 << 20):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(chunk), b""):
            h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()


def _hash_files(files, base):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for f in sorted(files):
        h.update(str(Path(f).relative_to(base)).encode()); h.update(b"\0")
        h.update(sha256_file(f).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()


def source_hash(globs=None):
    files = [p for g in (globs or SOURCE_GLOBS) for p in ROOT.glob(g) if p.is_file()]
    return _hash_files(files, ROOT), len(files)


def git_commit():
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(ROOT), "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
        return r.stdout.strip() if r.returncode == 0 else None
    except Exception:
        return None


def char_info(story):
    from . import chars as C
    out = {}
    for name, cdef in story.data.get("tokoh", {}).items():
        d = story.build / "chars" / name
        files = [p for p in d.glob("*") if p.suffix in (".png", ".json")] if d.exists() else \
            list((story.build / "chars").glob(f"{name}_*.png"))
        out[name] = {"chars_version": C.VERSION, "spec_hash": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(cdef, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
                     "rig_hash": _hash_files(files, story.build)[:16] if files else None, "n_files": len(files)}
    return out


def inputs(story):
    src, n_src = source_hash()
    asset_f = ROOT / "assets" / "external" / "aset.json"
    plan_f = story.build / "plan.json"
    tl_f = story.build / "audio" / "timeline.json"
    return {"git_commit": git_commit(), "source_hash": src, "source_files": n_src,
            "story_hash": sha256_file(story.dir / "story.yaml"),
            "timeline_hash": sha256_file(tl_f) if tl_f.exists() else None,
            "characters": char_info(story),
            "asset_manifest_hash": sha256_file(asset_f) if asset_f.exists() else None,
            "plan_hash": sha256_file(plan_f) if plan_f.exists() else None,
            "tools_hash": source_hash(TOOL_GLOBS)[0]}


def build_id_of(inp):
    key = json.dumps({k: v for k, v in inp.items() if k not in ("source_files", "tools_hash")}, sort_keys=True)
    return "P4-" + hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest()[:10]


def _path(story):
    return story.build / "manifest.json"


def load(story):
    p = _path(story)
    return json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else None


def begin_scene(story):
    """dipanggil SEBELUM Blender membangun adegan: hitung build ID dari masukan saat ini"""
    inp = inputs(story)
    bid = build_id_of(inp)
    old = load(story)
    doc = {"build_id": bid, "story": story.slug, "inputs": inp,
           "scene_build": {"dimulai": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), "blender": None, "selesai": None},
           "host": {"python": platform.python_version(), "os": platform.platform()},
           "outputs": [o for o in (old or {}).get("outputs", []) if o.get("build_id") == bid]}
    _path(story).write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
    return bid


def end_scene(story, blender_version=None, seconds=None):
    doc = load(story)
    doc["scene_build"].update({"selesai": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), "blender": blender_version,
                               "durasi_dtk": round(seconds, 1) if seconds else None})
    blend = story.build / "scene.blend"
    if blend.exists():
        doc["scene_build"]["scene_blend_sha256"] = sha256_file(blend)
    _path(story).write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))


def current_id(story):
    doc = load(story)
    return doc["build_id"] if doc else None


def stamp_png(path, meta):
    """tulis metadata teks ke PNG (tanpa mengubah piksel)"""
    from PIL import Image, PngImagePlugin
    im = Image.open(path)
    im.load()
    info = PngImagePlugin.PngInfo()
    for k, v in (im.text or {}).items():
        if k not in meta:
            info.add_text(k, str(v))
    for k, v in meta.items():
        info.add_text(k, json.dumps(v) if not isinstance(v, str) else v)
    im.save(path, pnginfo=info)


def read_png_meta(path):
    from PIL import Image
    try:
        return dict(Image.open(path).text or {})
    except Exception:
        return {}


def shot_of(plan, f):
    for s in plan.get("shots", []):
        if s["f0"] <= f <= s["f1"]:
            return f"shot{s['n']:02d}_{s['kamera']}" + (f"_{s['subjek']}" if s.get("subjek") else "")
    return None


def register(story, path, kind, frame=None, shot=None, settings=None, stamp=True, extra=None):
    """catat keluaran ke manifest (+ stempel PNG). kind: still_raw | still_graded | video | klip | laporan | validasi | ..."""
    doc = load(story)
    if doc is None:
        raise RuntimeError("manifest.json belum ada: jalankan langkah 'adegan' dulu")
    path = Path(path)
    if stamp and path.suffix.lower() == ".png":
        meta = {"build_id": doc["build_id"], "kind": kind}
        if frame is not None:
            meta["frame"] = str(frame)
        if shot:
            meta["shot"] = shot
        stamp_png(path, meta)
    rec = {"file": str(path.relative_to(ROOT)) if path.is_relative_to(ROOT) else str(path), "kind": kind,
           "build_id": doc["build_id"], "frame": frame, "shot": shot, "settings": settings or {},
           "sha256": sha256_file(path), "dibuat": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), **(extra or {})}
    doc["outputs"] = [o for o in doc["outputs"] if o["file"] != rec["file"]] + [rec]
    _path(story).write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
    return rec


def verify(story, paths):
    """-> [(berkas, status, build_id_berkas)]; status: OK | BEDA_BUILD | TIDAK_TERCATAT | CHECKSUM_BEDA"""
    doc = load(story) or {}
    bid = doc.get("build_id")
    recs = {o["file"]: o for o in doc.get("outputs", [])}
    out = []
    for p in paths:
        p = Path(p)
        key = str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) if p.is_relative_to(ROOT) else str(p)
        r = recs.get(key)
        if r is None:
            fb = read_png_meta(p).get("build_id") if p.suffix.lower() == ".png" else None
            out.append((key, "TIDAK_TERCATAT" if fb != bid else "OK_STEMPEL", fb)); continue
        if r["build_id"] != bid:
            out.append((key, "BEDA_BUILD", r["build_id"])); continue
        out.append((key, "OK" if sha256_file(p) == r["sha256"] else "CHECKSUM_BEDA", r["build_id"]))
    return out

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/palette.py
# sha256: 830004ebd43cadf64cf07625d6f9a931f08cd1d40d767b305c30a21dd3e18086

"""Nama warna yang bisa dipakai di story.yaml (tanpa dependensi, dipakai juga di dalam Blender)."""
COLORS = {"putih": (240, 240, 232), "hitam": (30, 28, 34), "abu": (110, 108, 115), "abu_muda": (190, 190, 195),
          "merah": (200, 40, 40), "merah_tua": (130, 25, 30), "biru": (40, 80, 160), "biru_tua": (30, 45, 95),
          "biru_muda": (120, 180, 230), "hijau": (60, 150, 80), "hijau_tua": (30, 90, 50), "kuning": (240, 200, 40),
          "oranye": (240, 110, 30), "coklat": (130, 85, 55), "coklat_tua": (80, 50, 35), "krem": (230, 210, 170),
          "tosca": (90, 190, 190), "ungu": (120, 70, 160), "pink": (240, 140, 170), "emas": (220, 170, 60),
          "ojol": (0, 160, 80), "navy": (25, 35, 70)}
SKINS = {"terang": (236, 184, 130), "langsat": (232, 176, 120), "sawo": (222, 150, 88), "gelap": (170, 105, 60)}


def to_rgb(v, default=None):
    """'merah' | '#rrggbb' | [r, g, b] -> (r, g, b) 0-255, atau None"""
    if v is None or v is False:
        v = default
    if v is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
        return tuple(int(x) for x in v[:3])
    v = str(v).strip()
    if v.startswith("#"):
        return tuple(int(v[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (1, 3, 5))
    table = {**COLORS, **SKINS}
    if v not in table:
        raise ValueError(f"warna '{v}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(sorted(table))}, atau #rrggbb")
    return table[v]


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/performance.py
# sha256: 58e6b1c32a5d95fce26cfd5dc22a07706bbbc3d1ab33a85f327880e609cc056b
==============================================================================================================
"""Sistem performa tokoh (Phase 4C). Murni Python, dipanggil plan.py setelah event & dialog diketahui.

Kanal yang DIPISAH (tidak lagi dikendalikan satu nama sprite ekspresi):
  emosi        dari events (neutral/smile/surprised/...; tetap kompatibel dengan story.yaml lama)
  mata         override bentuk mata: kedip otomatis di SEMUA emosi bermata terbuka; senyum bervariasi
               mata tertutup (happy) <-> terbuka (open) pada tahan panjang
  alis         micro-expression: alis naik singkat saat mendengar awal kalimat lawan bicara / kaget kecil
  kepala_miring sudut kepala (derajat): pembicara berganti per frasa, pendengar condong ke pembicara
  angguk       impuls anggukan (amplitudo px kanvas): pendengar di jeda frasa pembicara, pembicara di tekanan kata
  gestur       tangan BEBAS (tidak memegang/beraksi) saat bicara: dada | terbuka | angkat_kecil per frasa
Semua variasi ber-seed (reproducible). Keluaran: plan.characters[i]['perf']."""
import random

FPS = 30
OPEN_EYES = {"open", "wide"}
PHRASE_GAP = 0.22          # jeda antar kata (detik) yang dianggap batas frasa


def _variant_runs(events, end):
    """[(f0, f1, varian)] dari daftar event terurut"""
    out = []
    for (f, v), nxt in zip(events, events[1:] + [(end + 1, None)]):
        if nxt[0] > f:
            out.append((f, nxt[0], v))
    return out


def _phrases(d):
    """kata dialog -> frasa [(f0, f1, [kata])], dipisah jeda > PHRASE_GAP"""
    ws = d["kata"]
    out, cur = [], []
    for i, (w, w0, w1) in enumerate(ws):
        if cur and (w0 - cur[-1][2]) / FPS > PHRASE_GAP:
            out.append(cur); cur = []
        cur.append((w, w0, w1))
    if cur:
        out.append(cur)
    return [(p[0][1], p[-1][2], [w for w, _, _ in p]) for p in out]


def build(chars, dialogue, variants, end, seed, busy_hands=None):
    """chars: [{name, events, rig, cat}]; variants: {nama_tokoh: {varian: {eyes, brows, mouth}}}
    busy_hands: {nama_tokoh: [(f0, f1, hs)]} rentang tangan sibuk (memegang/aksi) -> tidak digestur"""
    busy_hands = busy_hands or {}
    out = {}
    for ci, c in enumerate(chars):
        if not c.get("rig") or c.get("cat"):
            continue
        rnd = random.Random(seed * 7919 + ci * 104729 + sum(map(ord, c["name"])))
        V = variants.get(c["name"], {})
        runs = _variant_runs(sorted(c["events"]), end)
        mata, alis, tilt, angguk, gestur = [], [], [], [], []
        # --- mata: kedip di semua emosi bermata terbuka + variasi senyum
        for f0, f1, v in runs:
            if v in ("none", "back") or v not in V:
                continue
            eyes = V[v].get("eyes")
            if eyes in OPEN_EYES:
                f = f0 + rnd.randint(25, 75)
                while f + 4 < f1:
                    mata += [[f, "closed"], [f + 3, None]]
                    if rnd.random() < 0.15 and f + 9 < f1:                 # kedip ganda sesekali
                        mata += [[f + 6, "closed"], [f + 9, None]]
                    f += rnd.randint(60, 135)
            elif eyes == "happy" and f1 - f0 > 75:                         # senyum panjang: mata terbuka bergantian
                f = f0 + rnd.randint(35, 60)
                while f + 20 < f1:
                    dur = rnd.randint(28, 55)
                    g1 = min(f + dur, f1 - 1)
                    mata += [[f, "open"], [g1, None]]
                    b = f + rnd.randint(8, max(9, g1 - f - 6))                # kedip di tengah fase mata terbuka
                    if b + 3 < g1:
                        mata += [[b, "closed"], [b + 3, "open"]]
                    f = g1 + rnd.randint(40, 80)
        # --- dialog: pembicara (miring per frasa, angguk tekanan, gestur) & pendengar (alis, angguk, condong)
        for d in dialogue:
            phr = _phrases(d)
            if d["tokoh"] == c["name"]:
                for i, (p0, p1, words) in enumerate(phr):
                    tilt.append([p0, round(rnd.uniform(-5, 5), 1)])
                    if i > 0 or rnd.random() < 0.5:
                        angguk.append([p0 + 2, round(rnd.uniform(4, 8), 1)])
                    sides = [hs for hs in (-1, 1) if not any(a <= p0 and p1 <= b and h == hs for a, b, h in busy_hands.get(c["name"], []))]
                    if sides and p1 - p0 >= 18:
                        hs = rnd.choice(sides)
                        g = rnd.choice(["dada", "terbuka", "angkat_kecil"])
                        gestur.append([p0, min(p1 + 6, end), hs, g])
                tilt.append([d["f1"] + 4, 0.0])
            else:
                alis += [[d["f0"] + 4, "raised"], [d["f0"] + 22, None]]
                tilt.append([d["f0"] + 6, round(rnd.choice([-1, 1]) * rnd.uniform(3, 6), 1)])
                for p0, p1, _ in phr[:-1]:
                    if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                        angguk.append([p1 + 3, round(rnd.uniform(3, 6), 1)])
                angguk.append([d["f1"] + 3, round(rnd.uniform(5, 8), 1)])
                tilt.append([d["f1"] + 12, 0.0])
        out[c["name"]] = {"mata": sorted(mata, key=lambda x: x[0]), "alis": sorted(alis, key=lambda x: x[0]),
                          "kepala_miring": sorted(tilt, key=lambda x: x[0]), "angguk": sorted(angguk, key=lambda x: x[0]),
                          "gestur": sorted(gestur, key=lambda x: x[0]), "seed": seed}
    return out


def override_at(track, f):
    """nilai override terakhir <= f pada track [[f, v|None], ...] (None = tanpa override)"""
    cur = None
    for ff, v in track:
        if ff <= f:
            cur = v
        else:
            break
    return cur


def nod_at(track, f, dur=9):
    """amplitudo anggukan (px kanvas) pada frame f: tiap impuls = lengkung turun-naik selama 'dur' frame"""
    import math
    a = 0.0
    for f0, amp in track:
        if f0 <= f < f0 + dur:
            a = max(a, amp * math.sin(math.pi * (f - f0) / dur))
    return a

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/plan.py
