"""Menerjemahkan story.yaml + timeline narasi -> build/plan.json (semua dalam frame & koordinat).
plan.json kemudian dibaca blender_build.py di dalam Blender."""
import json
import math
import random

from .chars import uses_rig, variant_names
from . import locations, performance, schedule, seats
from .common import FPS, Cues, StoryError, frame, to_ms
from .locations import PROP_ANCHOR_Z, PROP_SIZE

# --------------------------------------------------------------- nama-nama yang boleh dipakai di story.yaml
DO = {"diam": "neutral", "netral": "neutral", "senyum": "smile", "sedih": "sad", "marah": "angry", "kaget": "surprised", "ngantuk": "sleepy",
      "menoleh": "look", "menunjuk": "point", "melambai": "wave", "meniup": "whistle", "meniup_peluit": "whistle",
      "bicara": "talk", "jalan": "walk", "sembunyi": "hide", "muncul": "show", "kedip": "blink",
      # khusus rig berlapis (bisa bersamaan dengan bicara/ekspresi)
      "duduk": "sit", "pegang": "hold", "memegang": "hold", "makan": "eat", "minum": "eat", "masak": "cook",
      "memasak": "cook", "mengaduk": "cook", "melirik": "glance",
      # interaksi benda (Phase 3): ambil dari jangkar, taruh ke jangkar, serahkan ke tokoh lain, lepas (jatuh)
      "ambil": "pick", "mengambil": "pick", "taruh": "put", "menaruh": "put", "letakkan": "put",
      "serahkan": "give", "memberikan": "give", "lepas": "release", "melepas": "release"}
RIG_ACTS = {"sit", "hold", "eat", "cook", "glance", "pick", "put", "give", "release"}
HOLDABLE = ["gelas", "teh", "kopi", "piring", "nasi_goreng", "sutil", "hp", "kantong", "mangkok", "helm", "bungkus", "sendok"]
TALK = {"normal": ("talk", "neutral"), "senyum": ("smiletalk", "smile"), "sedih": ("sadtalk", "sad"),
        "ngantuk": ("sleepytalk", "sleepy"),
        "marah": ("angrytalk", "angry"), "kaget": ("surprisedtalk", "surprised"),
        "menunjuk": ("pointtalk", "point"), "melambai": ("wavetalk", "wave")}
CAM = {"pembuka": "establishing", "penutup": "ending", "dekat": "closeup", "sedang": "medium", "penuh": "full",
       "lebar": "wide", "bawah": "low", "atas": "high", "detail": "insert", "lewat_bahu": "ots", "bebas": "custom",
       "sedang_dekat": "mcu", "sangat_dekat": "ecu", "ikuti": "tracking", "dolly": "dolly"}
# sisi tangan internal: +1 = arah Z x hadap = sisi KIRI tokoh (= sprite sisi +1 pada tampak depan tanpa cermin).
# (sebelum Phase 3 'kanan' dipetakan ke +1 sehingga semua aksi "tangan kanan" memakai tangan kiri tokoh)
HAND_SIDE = {"kanan": -1, "kiri": 1}
MOVE = {"maju": "push", "mundur": "pull", "geser_kiri": "pan_left", "geser_kanan": "pan_right", "naik": "rise",
        "diam": "static", "guncang": "shake", "crane_naik": "crane_up", "crane_turun": "crane_down"}
DEFAULT_MOVE = {"closeup": ["push"], "medium": ["push"], "full": ["push"], "wide": ["pan_right"], "low": ["rise"],
                "high": ["static"], "insert": ["push"], "ots": ["push"], "mcu": ["push"], "ecu": ["static"],
                "tracking": [], "dolly": []}
LOC = locations.get("jalan_kota")                               # diisi ulang di build_plan()
OTS_WARN = []


def ground_z(x):
    return LOC["lantai"](x)


# --------------------------------------------------------------- vektor kecil (tanpa numpy)
def add(a, b): return tuple(x + y for x, y in zip(a, b))
def sub(a, b): return tuple(x - y for x, y in zip(a, b))
def mul(a, k): return tuple(x * k for x in a)
def length(a): return math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in a))
def lerp(a, b, t): return tuple(x + (y - x) * t for x, y in zip(a, b))


class Subject:
    """Sesuatu yang bisa dibidik kamera: tokoh atau properti."""
    def __init__(self, name, xy_fn, head_h, mid_h, size=1.0):
        self.name, self._xy, self.head_h, self.mid_h, self.size = name, xy_fn, head_h, mid_h, size
        self.facing = []                                            # [[frame, [x, y] | None]] (tokoh ber-rig)

    def facing_at(self, f):
        t = None
        for kf, v in self.facing:
            if kf <= f:
                t = v
        return t

    def xy(self, f):
        return self._xy(f)

    def ground(self, f):
        return ground_z(self.xy(f)[0])

    def head(self, f):
        x, y = self.xy(f); return (x, y, self.ground(f) + self.head_h)

    def mid(self, f):
        x, y = self.xy(f); return (x, y, self.ground(f) + self.mid_h)


class Char:
    def __init__(self, name, cdef):
        if "posisi" not in cdef:
            raise StoryError(f"Tokoh '{name}' belum punya 'posisi: [x, y]'")
        self.name, self.cdef = name, cdef
        self.kind = cdef.get("jenis", "manusia")
        self.cat = self.kind == "kucing"
        self.height = float(cdef.get("tinggi", 1.1 if self.cat else 1.75))
        self.base = tuple(cdef["posisi"][:2])
        self.valid = set(variant_names(cdef))
        self.rig = uses_rig(cdef)
        self.idle = "walkB" if self.cat else DO.get(cdef.get("diam", "neutral"), cdef.get("diam", "neutral"))
        self.walks, self.events, self.pops, self.shakes, self.rig_acts = [], [], [], [], []
        # arah badan (dunia): [(frame, target)]; target = nama tokoh/properti, [x, y], sudut (derajat), atau 'kamera'
        self.facing = [(1, cdef["hadap"])] if "hadap" in cdef else []

        hh, mh = (0.33, 0.2) if self.cat else ((0.645, 0.41) if self.rig else (0.69, 0.45))   # rig: kaki menapak tanah
        self.subject = Subject(name, self.pos, self.height * hh, self.height * mh)

    def pos(self, f):
        cur = self.base
        for f0, f1, a, b in self.walks:
            if f < f0:
                return cur
            if f <= f1:
                return lerp(a, b, (f - f0) / max(1, f1 - f0))
            cur = b
        return cur

    def check(self, v):
        if v != "none" and v not in self.valid:
            raise StoryError(f"Tokoh '{self.name}' tidak punya varian '{v}'. Yang ada: {', '.join(sorted(self.valid))}")
        return v


def vibrate(f0, f1, a, b, step):
    return [(f, a if ((f - f0) // step) % 2 == 0 else b) for f in range(f0, f1, step)]


def build_plan(story, mode="full", seed=7):
    global LOC
    d = story.data
    tl = story.timeline
    latar = d.get("latar", {})
    try:
        LOC = locations.get(locations.location_name(latar))
    except KeyError as e:
        raise StoryError(str(e))
    for key, allowed in (("waktu", locations.TIMES), ("cuaca", locations.WEATHERS)):
        if latar.get(key) and latar[key] not in allowed:
            raise StoryError(f"latar {key} '{latar[key]}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(allowed)}")
    cue = Cues(tl)
    END = frame(tl["total"])
    rnd = random.Random(seed)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- tokoh & aksi
    chars = {n: Char(n, c) for n, c in d.get("tokoh", {}).items()}
    dialogue = []                                               # Phase 4A: rentang bicara per tokoh (untuk validasi)
    acts = []
    for a in d.get("aksi", []):
        if a.get("tokoh") not in chars:
            raise StoryError(f"Aksi {a}: tokoh '{a.get('tokoh')}' tidak ada di bagian 'tokoh'")
        acts.append((cue(a.get("dari", 0)), a))
    acts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for ch in chars.values():
        ch.events.append((1, ch.idle))
    for t0, a in acts:
        ch = chars[a["tokoh"]]
        f0 = frame(t0)
        if "hadap" in a:
            ch.facing.append((f0, a["hadap"]))
        if a["lakukan"] in ("hadap", "menghadap"):
            continue
        do = DO.get(a["lakukan"], a["lakukan"])
        t1 = cue(a["sampai"]) if "sampai" in a else None
        f1 = frame(t1) if t1 is not None else None
        after = DO.get(a.get("lalu"), a.get("lalu"))
        if do in RIG_ACTS:
            if not ch.rig:
                raise StoryError(f"Aksi '{a['lakukan']}' butuh tokoh ber-rig (hapus 'rig: false' pada '{ch.name}')")
            side = HAND_SIDE.get(a.get("tangan", "kanan"))
            if side is None:
                raise StoryError(f"tangan '{a.get('tangan')}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: kanan, kiri")
            benda = a.get("benda", {"minum": "gelas"}.get(a["lakukan"]))
            if benda and benda not in HOLDABLE:
                raise StoryError(f"benda '{benda}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(HOLDABLE)}")
            ra = {"type": "look" if do == "glance" else do, "f0": f0, "f1": f1 or END + 1, "side": side, "tangan_eksplisit": "tangan" in a,
                  "dir": -1 if a.get("arah") == "kiri" else 1}
            if benda and do != "glance":
                ra["benda"] = benda
            if do in ("pick", "put", "give", "release") and not benda:
                raise StoryError(f"Aksi '{a['lakukan']}' butuh 'benda'")
            if do == "give":
                tgt = a.get("ke")
                if tgt not in chars:
                    raise StoryError(f"serahkan: 'ke' harus nama tokoh. Pilihan: {', '.join(chars)}")
                ra["side_ke"] = HAND_SIDE.get(a.get("tangan_ke", "kanan"), -1)
                ra["tangan_ke_eksplisit"] = "tangan_ke" in a
            for k_src, k_dst in (("kursi", "kursi"), ("warna_kursi", "warna_kursi"), ("tempat", "tempat"), ("ke", "ke")):
                if k_src in a:
                    ra[k_dst] = a[k_src]
            if do == "sit" and isinstance(a.get("kursi"), str) and a["kursi"] not in ("dingklik", "tidak"):
                # Phase 4B-B5: duduk di jangkar dudukan dunia ('<properti>.duduk[k]'); tokoh menyesuaikan diri ke kursi
                seat = seats.find(latar.get("properti", []), a["kursi"], ground_z)
                if seat is None:
                    names = [s["name"] for p in latar.get("properti", []) for s in seats.anchors_for(p)]
                    raise StoryError(f"kursi '{a['kursi']}' tidak ada. Jangkar dudukan: {', '.join(names) or '(tidak ada)'}")
                ra["kursi_jangkar"] = seat
                if ch.walks:
                    raise StoryError(f"'{ch.name}' berjalan lalu duduk di jangkar: belum didukung (letakkan 'posisi' di kursi)")
                ch.base = tuple(seat["pos"][:2])
                if not ch.facing and seat["maju"]:
                    ch.facing = [(1, [seat["pos"][0] + seat["maju"][0], seat["pos"][1] + seat["maju"][1]])]
            ch.rig_acts.append(ra)
            continue
        if do == "talk":
            mood = a.get("ekspresi", "normal")
            if mood not in TALK:
                raise StoryError(f"ekspresi bicara '{mood}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(TALK)}")
            op, cl = TALK[mood]
            # Phase 4A: baris dialog ditentukan dari ID pada cue 'dari' (n4, n4:kata), BUKAN dengan mencari interval
            # float (bug Phase 3: 9.38 + 5.854 = 15.234000000000002 -> n4 terbaca sebagai n3, tanpa event mulut).
            line_id = cue.line_of(a.get("dari"))
            if line_id is None:
                ln = cue.line_at(t0)
                line_id = ln["id"] if ln else None
            if t1 is None:
                if line_id is None:
                    raise StoryError(f"Aksi bicara {a}: tidak ada baris narasi pada t={t0:.3f}s; beri 'sampai'")
                t1 = cue.line_end(line_id); f1 = frame(t1)
            if not t1 > t0:
                raise StoryError(f"Aksi bicara '{ch.name}' dari {a.get('dari')}: akhir dialog ({t1:.3f}s) tidak setelah "
                                 f"awal ({t0:.3f}s). Periksa 'dari'/'sampai'.")
            words = cue.words_between(t0, t1)
            if not words:
                raise StoryError(f"Aksi bicara '{ch.name}' dari {a.get('dari')} ({t0:.3f}-{t1:.3f}s): tidak ada kata diucapkan")
            ch.events.append((f0, ch.check(cl)))
            n_open = 0
            spans = []
            for w in words:
                w0 = frame(w["t0"]); w1 = max(frame(w["t1"]), w0 + 4)
                vib = vibrate(w0, w1, ch.check(op), cl, 4)
                n_open += sum(1 for _, v in vib if v == op)
                ch.events += vib + [(w1, cl)]
                spans.append([w["w"], w0, w1])
            ch.events.append((f1, ch.check(after or cl)))
            ch.pops.append(f0)
            dialogue.append({"tokoh": ch.name, "baris": line_id, "f0": f0, "f1": f1, "t0_ms": to_ms(t0), "t1_ms": to_ms(t1),
                             "buka": op, "tutup": cl, "lalu": after or cl, "kata": spans, "n_event_buka": n_open})
        elif do == "whistle":
            f1 = f1 or f0 + 45
            ch.events += vibrate(f0, f1, ch.check("whistle"), ch.check("whistle2"), 3)
            ch.events.append((f1, ch.check(after or ch.idle)))
            ch.pops.append(f0); ch.shakes.append((f0, f1))
        elif do == "walk":
            if "ke" not in a:
                raise StoryError(f"Aksi jalan untuk '{ch.name}' butuh 'ke: [x, y]'")
            f1 = f1 or END
            start = ch.pos(f0)
            ch.walks.append((f0, f1, start, tuple(a["ke"][:2])))
            ch.walks.sort()
            if ch.cat:
                cyc, step = ["walkA", "walkB"], 5
            else:
                back = a.get("arah", "depan") in ("belakang", "back")
                cyc, step = (["back_walk" + l for l in "ABCD"] if back else ["walk" + l for l in "ABCD"]), 6
            ch.events += [(f, ch.check(cyc[((f - f0) // step) % len(cyc)])) for f in range(f0, f1, step)]
            default_after = "walkB" if ch.cat else ("back" if a.get("arah") in ("belakang", "back") else ch.idle)
            ch.events.append((f1, ch.check(after or default_after)))
        elif do == "hide":
            ch.events.append((f0, "none"))
            if f1:
                ch.events.append((f1, ch.check(after or ch.idle)))
        elif do == "show":
            ch.events.append((f0, ch.check(after or ch.idle)))
        else:
            ch.events.append((f0, ch.check(do)))
            if f1:
                ch.events.append((f1, ch.check(after or ch.idle)))
            ch.pops.append(f0)

    prop_pos = {p.get("nama", p["jenis"]): tuple(p["posisi"][:2]) for p in latar.get("properti", [])}

    def resolve_facing(ch):
        out = []
        for f, t in sorted(ch.facing, key=lambda k: k[0]):
            px, py = ch.pos(f)
            if t in (None, "kamera", "depan"):
                out.append([f, None])
            elif isinstance(t, (int, float)):                       # sudut: 0 = ke arah -Y (kamera bawaan)
                out.append([f, [px + math.sin(math.radians(t)), py - math.cos(math.radians(t))]])
            elif isinstance(t, (list, tuple)):
                out.append([f, [float(t[0]), float(t[1])]])
            elif t in chars:
                out.append([f, list(chars[t].pos(f))])
            elif t in prop_pos:
                out.append([f, list(prop_pos[t])])
            else:
                raise StoryError(f"hadap '{t}' untuk '{ch.name}' tidak dikenal (nama tokoh/properti, [x, y], sudut, atau 'kamera')")
        return out

    out_chars = []
    for ch in chars.values():
        ev = sorted(ch.events, key=lambda e: e[0])                # urutan stabil: aksi belakangan menang
        if "tampil" in ch.cdef:                                    # hanya terlihat di rentang waktu tertentu
            r0, r1 = (frame(cue(x)) for x in ch.cdef["tampil"])
            s0 = ch.idle
            for fe, v in ev:
                if fe <= r0:
                    s0 = v
            ev = [(1, "none"), (r0, s0)] + [(f, v) for f, v in ev if r0 < f < r1] + [(r1, "none")]
        # kedip otomatis: tokoh ber-rig -> kanal mata terpisah (performance.py, semua emosi); tanpa rig -> cara lama
        if "blink" in ch.valid and not ch.rig:
            extra = []
            for (fa, v), (fb, _) in zip(ev, ev[1:] + [(END + 1, None)]):
                if v == "neutral":
                    f = fa + rnd.randint(20, 85)
                    while f + 4 < fb:
                        extra += [(f, "blink"), (f + 4, "neutral")]
                        f += 85 + rnd.randint(-25, 25)
            ev = sorted(ev + extra, key=lambda e: e[0])
        final = {}
        for f, v in ev:
            final[max(1, f)] = v
        loc_keys = [(1, *ch.base)]
        for f0, f1, a, b in ch.walks:
            loc_keys += [(f0, *a), (f1, *b)]
        out_chars.append({"name": ch.name, "height": ch.height, "cat": ch.cat, "rig": ch.rig, "rig_acts": ch.rig_acts,
                          "variants": sorted({v for v in final.values() if v != "none"}),
                          "events": sorted(final.items()), "loc": [(f, x, y, ground_z(x)) for f, x, y in loc_keys],
                          "pops": ch.pops, "shakes": ch.shakes, "walking": [(f0, f1) for f0, f1, _, _ in ch.walks],
                          "facing": resolve_facing(ch) if ch.rig else []})
        ch.subject.facing = out_chars[-1]["facing"]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- properti (subjek kamera)
    subjects = {n: c.subject for n, c in chars.items()}
    for i, p in enumerate(latar.get("properti", [])):
        name = p.get("nama", p["jenis"] if p["jenis"] not in subjects else f"{p['jenis']}{i}")
        x, y = p["posisi"][:2]
        hz = PROP_ANCHOR_Z.get(p["jenis"], 0.8)
        side = -1 if x > 0 else 1                                  # muka gerobak menghadap jalan
        off = side * 0.4 if p["jenis"] == "gerobak" else 0.0
        subjects[name] = Subject(name, lambda f, x=x + off, y=y: (x, y), hz, hz * 0.6, PROP_SIZE.get(p["jenis"], 1.0))
        if p["jenis"] == "gerobak_makan":                          # titik wajan untuk shot detail
            rz = math.radians(p.get("arah", 0)) + (0 if side == -1 else math.pi)
            lx, ly = float(p.get("kompor_x", 0.12)), -0.5               # sama dengan kit.gerobak_makan (kompor di sisi penjual)
            wx, wy = x + lx * math.cos(rz) - ly * math.sin(rz), y + lx * math.sin(rz) + ly * math.cos(rz)
            hh = float(p.get("tinggi_meja", 0.45)) + 0.15
            subjects[name + ".wajan"] = Subject(name + ".wajan", lambda f, wx=wx, wy=wy: (wx, wy), hh, hh, 0.55)
        if p["jenis"] == "gerobak" and p.get("jam"):
            subjects[name + ".jam"] = Subject(name + ".jam", lambda f, x=x + side * 0.43, y=y + side * 0.35: (x, y), 0.47, 0.47)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- kamera per shot
    shots = d.get("shot", [])
    if not shots:
        shots = [{"mulai": 0, "kamera": "establishing"}]
    starts = [cue(s.get("mulai", 0)) for s in shots]
    order = sorted(range(len(shots)), key=lambda i: starts[i])
    shots = [shots[i] for i in order]; starts = [starts[i] for i in order]
    starts[0] = 0.0
    cam_keys, zoom_keys, cam_shakes, shot_list, warnings = [], [], [], [], []
    poles = LOC["tiang"](latar)
    for k, s in enumerate(shots):
        f0 = frame(starts[k])
        f1 = (frame(starts[k + 1]) - 1) if k + 1 < len(shots) else END
        if f1 <= f0:
            raise StoryError(f"Shot ke-{k + 1} ({s.get('mulai')}) terlalu pendek / bertabrakan dengan shot berikutnya")
        typ = CAM.get(s.get("kamera", "medium"), s.get("kamera", "medium"))
        if typ not in set(CAM.values()):
            raise StoryError(f"kamera '{s.get('kamera')}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(sorted(CAM))}")
        moves = s.get("gerak", DEFAULT_MOVE.get(typ, []))
        moves = [MOVE.get(m, m) for m in (moves if isinstance(moves, list) else [moves])]
        subj = subjects.get(s["subjek"]) if s.get("subjek") else None
        if s.get("subjek") and subj is None:
            raise StoryError(f"Shot ke-{k + 1}: subjek '{s['subjek']}' tidak ada. Pilihan: {', '.join(subjects)}")
        if typ not in ("establishing", "ending", "custom") and subj is None:
            raise StoryError(f"Shot ke-{k + 1}: kamera '{typ}' butuh 'subjek'")
        OTS_WARN.clear()
        keys = camera_shot(typ, moves, subj, subjects.get(s.get("ke_arah")) if s.get("ke_arah") else None, f0, f1, s)
        lens = s.get("lensa")
        if lens is not None:
            l0, l1 = (lens, lens) if not isinstance(lens, list) else lens
            keys[0][3], keys[-1][3] = l0, l1
        if s.get("fstop"):
            for kk in keys:
                kk[4] = s["fstop"]
        warnings += [f"Shot ke-{k + 1} ({s.get('mulai')}): {w}" for w in OTS_WARN]
        cam_keys += [[f, list(p), list(t), l, fs] for f, p, t, l, fs in keys]
        for f, p, t, l, fs in keys:
            for pole in poles:
                if blocks(p, t, pole):
                    warnings.append(f"Shot ke-{k + 1} ({s.get('mulai')}): tiang di {pole} menghalangi kamera. "
                                    f"Geser tokoh/subjek atau atur 'sudut'.")
                    break
        if "zoom" in moves:
            zoom_keys.append((f0 + 8, keys[-1][3]))
        if "shake" in moves:
            cam_shakes.append((f0, f1))
        shot_list.append({"n": k + 1, "f0": f0, "f1": f1, "kamera": typ, "subjek": s.get("subjek"), "mulai": s.get("mulai"),
                          "ke_arah": s.get("ke_arah"), **({"warna": s["warna"]} if s.get("warna") else {}),
                          **({"komposisi": s["komposisi"]} if s.get("komposisi") is not None else {})})

    # Phase 4B-B6/4C: jadwal aksi sadar-potongan. Makan: siklus bervariasi (ber-seed) yang berakhir tepat sebelum fase
    # 'taruh sendok' (PLACE + RETRACT), dan fase itu seluruhnya terlihat di satu shot (tidak disembunyikan oleh cut).
    cuts = [s["f0"] for s in shot_list]
    sched_notes = []
    for c in out_chars:
        for i, a in enumerate(c["rig_acts"]):
            if a["type"] != "eat":
                continue
            f_end = min(a["f1"], END + 1)
            if a.get("benda") in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok") and a["f1"] <= END:
                p0, p1, note = schedule.place_window(f_end, schedule.PLACE + schedule.RETRACT, cuts, END)
                a["taruh"] = [p0, p0 + schedule.PLACE, p1]           # turun, lepas, lengan kembali selesai
                sched_notes.append(f"{c['name']} taruh sendok f{p0}-{p1} ({note})")
                f_eat = p0
            else:
                f_eat = f_end
            # Phase 4.2: suapan/tegukan harus terlihat di shot yang membingkai pemakan (keterlihatan aksi)
            wins = [(s_["f0"], s_["f1"]) for s_ in shot_list
                    if c["name"] in (s_.get("subjek"), s_.get("ke_arah")) and s_["kamera"] in ("medium", "mcu", "closeup", "full")
                    and s_["f1"] > a["f0"] and s_["f0"] < f_eat]
            a["siklus"], nts = schedule.eat_cycles_visible(a["f0"], f_eat, seed * 1000 + i + sum(map(ord, c["name"])), wins)
            sched_notes += [f"{c['name']} {a.get('benda')}: {n_}" for n_ in nts]

    # Phase 4B: jangkauan lengan untuk ambil/taruh di koordinat dunia. Engine TIDAK memindahkan tokoh diam-diam;
    # di luar jangkauan -> peringatan (benda akan 'melompat' ke tangan; residual tercatat di interaksi.json).
    reach_warn = []
    for c in out_chars:
        for a in c["rig_acts"]:
            t = a.get("tempat")
            if a["type"] in ("pick", "put") and isinstance(t, str):   # Phase 4.2-D: jangkar bernama '<properti>.x'
                pr = next((q for q in latar.get("properti", []) or [] if q.get("nama") == t.split(".")[0]), None)
                if pr and "posisi" in pr:
                    t = (pr["posisi"][0], pr["posisi"][1], 0.0)   # pusat properti (perkiraan; residual tetap dicatat)
            if a["type"] in ("pick", "put") and isinstance(t, (list, tuple)) and len(t) == 3:
                loc = [l for l in c["loc"] if l[0] <= a["f0"]] or c["loc"][:1]
                d = ((t[0] - loc[-1][1]) ** 2 + (t[1] - loc[-1][2]) ** 2) ** 0.5
                lim = schedule.REACH * c["height"]
                if d > lim:
                    reach_warn.append(f"{c['name']} {a['type']} f{a['f0']}: tempat {d:.2f} m dari badan > jangkauan {lim:.2f} m")
    for w in reach_warn:
        print("  PERINGATAN jangkauan:", w)
    sched_notes += [f"PERINGATAN jangkauan: {w}" for w in reach_warn]

    # lewat bahu: pemilik bahu terlihat dari belakang selama shot itu
    by_name = {c["name"]: c for c in out_chars}
    for sh in shot_list:
        c = by_name.get(sh["subjek"]) if sh["kamera"] == "ots" else None
        if c is None or c["cat"] or "back" not in chars[c["name"]].valid:
            continue
        ev = c["events"]
        at_end = next((v for f, v in reversed(ev) if f <= sh["f1"] + 1), ev[0][1])
        hidden = next((v for f, v in reversed(ev) if f <= sh["f0"]), ev[0][1]) == "none"
        if hidden:
            continue
        c["events"] = [e for e in ev if not (sh["f0"] <= e[0] <= sh["f1"] + 1)] + [(sh["f0"], "back"), (sh["f1"] + 1, at_end)]
        c["events"].sort(key=lambda e: e[0])
        if "back" not in c["variants"]:
            c["variants"] = sorted(set(c["variants"]) | {"back"})

    # Phase 4C: kanal performa terpisah (mata, alis, kepala, angguk, gestur) per tokoh ber-rig
    from .rig2d import decode_variants
    from .chars import normalize
    variants = {c["name"]: decode_variants(normalize(chars[c["name"]].cdef.get("rupa", {}))) for c in out_chars if c["rig"]}
    busy = {}
    for c in out_chars:
        for a in c["rig_acts"]:                                  # sama dengan bl_rig._normalize_acts: pegang piring
            if a["type"] == "hold" and a.get("benda") in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok"):   # setelah makan
                e = next((e for e in c["rig_acts"] if e["type"] == "eat" and e.get("benda") == a["benda"]
                          and abs(e["f1"] - a["f0"]) <= 2), None)
                if e:
                    a["side"] = -e["side"]
        for a in c["rig_acts"]:
            if a["type"] in ("hold", "pick", "put", "give", "release", "eat", "cook"):
                f1 = a.get("taruh", [0, a["f1"]])[1] if a["type"] == "eat" else a["f1"]
                sides = (-1, 1) if a["type"] in ("eat", "cook") else (a["side"],)
                for hs in sides:
                    busy.setdefault(c["name"], []).append((a["f0"], f1, hs))
                if a["type"] == "eat" and a.get("benda") in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok"):
                    busy[c["name"]].append((a["f0"], END + 1, -a["side"]))       # tangan piring
    perf = performance.build(out_chars, dialogue, variants, END, seed, busy)
    for c in out_chars:
        if c["name"] in perf:
            c["perf"] = perf[c["name"]]

    # guncangan kamera otomatis saat tokoh meniup peluit lama
    for c in out_chars:
        cam_shakes += [(a, b) for a, b in c["shakes"] if b - a > 30]

    return {"fps": FPS, "end": END, "total": tl["total"], "mode": mode, "seed": seed,
            "latar": latar, "characters": out_chars, "camera": cam_keys, "zoom": zoom_keys,
            "cam_shakes": cam_shakes, "shots": shot_list, "dialogue": dialogue, "jadwal": sched_notes,
            "chars_dir": str(story.build / "chars"), "warnings": sorted(set(warnings))}


def blocks(p, t, pole, r=0.3):
    """apakah tiang (x, y) berada di antara kamera p dan target t (dilihat dari atas)?"""
    px, py = p[0], p[1]; dx, dy = t[0] - px, t[1] - py
    L2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if L2 < 1e-6:
        return False
    u = ((pole[0] - px) * dx + (pole[1] - py) * dy) / L2
    if not (0.05 < u < 0.9):
        return False
    cx, cy = px + u * dx, py + u * dy
    return math.hypot(pole[0] - cx, pole[1] - cy) < r


def view_dir(xy, angle=0.0, back=False, lateral=0.55):
    """arah dari subjek ke kamera: condong ke tengah jalan & ke belakang (y negatif)"""
    t = LOC["tepi"]
    s = 1 if xy[0] < -t else (-1 if xy[0] > t else 0.35)
    dx, dy = lateral * s, (1.0 if back else -1.0)
    a = math.radians(angle)
    dx, dy = dx * math.cos(a) - dy * math.sin(a), dx * math.sin(a) + dy * math.cos(a)
    n = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    return dx / n, dy / n


def clamp_cam(p):
    b = LOC["batas_x"]
    return (max(-b, min(b, p[0])), max(LOC["y_min"], p[1]), max(0.15, min(LOC["z_max"], p[2])))


def facing_dir(subj, f, extra=0.0, base_off=24.0):
    """arah subjek->kamera untuk tokoh yang menghadap sesuatu: di depan wajahnya, digeser base_off derajat ke sisi
    'penonton' (sisi tengah jalan / depan ruangan) sehingga semua shot tetap di satu sisi garis aksi (aturan 180 derajat)"""
    if subj is None or not getattr(subj, "facing", None):
        return None
    tgt = subj.facing_at(f)
    if tgt is None:
        return None
    x, y = subj.xy(f)
    vx, vy = tgt[0] - x, tgt[1] - y
    n = math.hypot(vx, vy)
    if n < 1e-3:
        return None
    vx, vy = vx / n, vy / n
    ref = (x, y - 5.0) if LOC["dalam"] else (0.0, y - 1.0)
    side = 1 if vx * (ref[1] - y) - vy * (ref[0] - x) >= 0 else -1
    a = math.radians(side * (base_off + extra))                  # 'sudut' positif = makin ke sisi penonton
    return vx * math.cos(a) - vy * math.sin(a), vx * math.sin(a) + vy * math.cos(a)


def two_shot(typ, moves, a, b, f0, f1, s):
    """dua subjek dalam satu bingkai: kamera tegak lurus garis a-b di sisi penonton (aturan 180 derajat),
    bidik titik tengah; keduanya terlihat seimbang (tidak ada kepala raksasa di latar depan)"""
    keys = []
    dist0 = {"full": 3.6, "medium": 2.8, "wide": 6.5, "ending": 3.2}[typ] * float(s.get("jarak", 1.0))
    for i, f in enumerate((f0, f1)):
        (ax, ay), (bx, by) = a.xy(f), b.xy(f)
        mx, my = (ax + bx) / 2, (ay + by) / 2
        vx, vy = bx - ax, by - ay; sep = math.hypot(vx, vy) or 1.0; vx, vy = vx / sep, vy / sep
        nx, ny = -vy, vx
        ref = (mx, my - 5.0) if LOC["dalam"] else (0.0, my - 1.0)
        if nx * (ref[0] - mx) + ny * (ref[1] - my) < 0:
            nx, ny = -nx, -ny
        ang = math.radians(float(s.get("sudut", 40)))              # serong: kedua tokoh bertumpuk kedalaman (9:16)
        nx, ny = nx * math.cos(ang) - ny * math.sin(ang), nx * math.sin(ang) + ny * math.cos(ang)
        dist = max(dist0, sep * 1.8)
        hz = (a.head(f)[2] + b.head(f)[2]) / 2
        tz = hz - (0.25 if typ != "medium" else 0.12)
        lens = 32 if typ != "wide" else 24
        if typ == "ending" and i == 1:                       # penutup: tahan, lalu mundur & naik pelan
            fh = f0 + int((f1 - f0) * float(s.get("tahan", 0.6)))
            keys.append([fh, keys[0][1], keys[0][2], lens, 3.2])
            dist *= 2.4
            keys.append([f, clamp_cam((mx + nx * dist, my + ny * dist, hz + 2.2)), (mx, my, tz), 30, 4.0])
            break
        k = {"push": (1.1, 0.95), "pull": (0.95, 1.1)}.get(next((m for m in moves if m in ("push", "pull")), ""), (1.0, 0.97))[i]
        pos = (mx + nx * dist * k, my + ny * dist * k, hz + float(s.get("tinggi", 0)) - 0.05)
        keys.append([f, clamp_cam(pos), (mx, my, tz), lens, 2.8])
    return keys


def camera_shot(typ, moves, subj, other, f0, f1, s):
    """-> [[frame, pos, target, lens, fstop], [..akhir..]]"""
    if typ == "custom":
        pos, tgt = s["posisi_kamera"], s["target"]
        pos = pos if isinstance(pos[0], list) else [pos, pos]
        tgt = tgt if isinstance(tgt[0], list) else [tgt, tgt]
        return [[f0, tuple(pos[0]), tuple(tgt[0]), 35, 2.8], [f1, tuple(pos[1]), tuple(tgt[1]), 35, 2.8]]
    if typ == "establishing" and LOC["dalam"]:
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f1) if subj else (0.0, 2.0)
        t1 = subj.head(f1) if subj else (0, 2, 1.2)
        return [[f0, clamp_cam((-sx * 0.3, sy - 9.0, 2.6)), (0.0, sy + 3.0, 1.4), 24, 5.6],
                [f1, clamp_cam((sx * 0.5 + 0.6, sy - 5.0, 1.6)), t1, 28, 4.0]]
    if typ == "ending" and LOC["dalam"]:
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f0) if subj else (0.0, 2.0)
        t0 = subj.head(f0) if subj else (0, 2, 1.2)
        return [[f0, clamp_cam((sx + 0.8, sy - 3.2, 1.35)), t0, 40, 2.8],
                [f1, clamp_cam((0.0, sy - 9.5, 2.6)), (sx * 0.5, sy, 1.0), 24, 5.6]]
    if typ == "establishing":                                   # dari atas jalan, turun & mendarat menatap subjek
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f1) if subj else (-4.9, 1.2)
        tgt = subj.mid(f1) if subj else (sx * 0.2, sy + 8.8, 1.6)
        d = view_dir((sx, sy), float(s.get("sudut", 0)))
        end = clamp_cam((sx + d[0] * 6.5, sy + d[1] * 6.5, (subj.ground(f1) if subj else 0) + 2.2))
        return [[f0, clamp_cam((sx * 0.25, sy - 11.0, 4.2)), (sx * 0.7, sy + 4.0, 1.6), 26, 5.6],
                [f1, end, tgt, 30, 4.0]]
    if other is not None and typ in ("ending", "full", "medium", "wide"):
        return two_shot(typ, moves, subj, other, f0, f1, s)
    if typ == "ending":
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f0) if subj else (0.0, 0.0)
        d = view_dir((sx, sy), float(s.get("sudut", 0)))
        g = subj.ground(f0) if subj else 0
        p0 = (sx + d[0] * 3.4, sy + d[1] * 3.4, g + 1.25)
        p1 = (sx + d[0] * 3.1, sy + d[1] * 3.1, g + 1.22)                        # tahan pada kedua tokoh, dorong pelan
        t0 = subj.mid(f0) if subj else (0, 10, 1.5)
        fh = f0 + int((f1 - f0) * float(s.get("tahan", 0.6)))
        p2 = clamp_cam((sx + d[0] * 9.0, sy + d[1] * 9.0 - 2.0, g + 4.0))      # lalu menjauh & naik (tidak sampai langit)
        return [[f0, clamp_cam(p0), t0, 38, 3.2], [fh, clamp_cam(p1), t0, 38, 3.2], [f1, p2, add(t0, (0, 1.0, 0.2)), 30, 4.0]]
    if typ == "ots":
        if other is None:
            raise StoryError("Kamera lewat_bahu (ots) butuh 'ke_arah: <tokoh/properti>'")
        # kamera di belakang bahu 'subjek', menatap 'ke_arah'; subjek tampil membelakangi kamera (lihat build_plan)
        ax, ay = subj.xy(f0); bx, by = other.xy(f0)
        vx, vy = bx - ax, by - ay; n = math.hypot(vx, vy) or 1.0; vx, vy = vx / n, vy / n
        side = float(s.get("bahu", 1))                            # 1 = bahu kanan (dari sudut kamera), -1 = kiri
        px, py = -vy * side, vx * side
        back = 1.9 * float(s.get("jarak", 1.0))
        hz = subj.head(f0)[2]
        lat = float(s.get("geser", 1.05))                         # makin besar: kepala depan makin ke tepi bingkai
        p0 = (ax - vx * back + px * lat, ay - vy * back + py * lat, hz + 0.18)
        p1 = (ax - vx * (back - 0.3) + px * lat, ay - vy * (back - 0.3) + py * lat, hz + 0.18) if "push" in moves else p0
        if length(sub(clamp_cam(p0), p0)) > 0.3:
            OTS_WARN.append(f"kamera lewat_bahu di belakang '{subj.name}' terjepit tembok/batas lokasi; "
                            f"coba 'bahu: {-int(side)}' atau tukar subjek & ke_arah")
        return [[f0, clamp_cam(p0), add(other.head(f0), (0, 0, -0.1)), 40, 2.2], [f1, clamp_cam(p1), add(other.head(f1), (0, 0, -0.1)), 40, 2.2]]

    # preset umum: jarak, tinggi kamera, titik bidik, lensa, f-stop
    def spec(f):
        g = subj.ground(f); hd = subj.head(f); md = subj.mid(f)
        return {"closeup": (1.75, hd[2] - 0.05, add(hd, (0, 0, -0.1)), 45, 1.6),   # kepala + dada, ada konteks
                "medium": (2.9, hd[2] - 0.05, hd, 40, 1.6),
                "full": (4.5, g + 1.3, md, 35, 2.0),
                "wide": (8.0, g + 0.6, add(md, (0, 0, 0.4)), 24, 3.2),
                "low": (2.2, g + 0.3, add(hd, (0, 0, 1.2)), 22, 2.8),
                "high": (5.5, g + 4.0, (md[0], md[1], g + 0.2), 30, 4.0),
                "insert": (1.3, hd[2] + 0.1, hd, 45, 2.0),
                "mcu": (2.0, hd[2] - 0.12, add(hd, (0, 0, -0.16)), 45, 1.6),        # kepala + dada
                "ecu": (0.95, hd[2], hd, 60, 1.4),                                  # wajah memenuhi layar
                "tracking": (3.4, hd[2] - 0.2, md, 35, 2.0),
                "dolly": (2.8, hd[2] - 0.1, add(hd, (0, 0, -0.1)), 40, 1.8)}[typ]

    angle = float(s.get("sudut", 0)); back = s.get("dari_belakang", False)
    distk = {"push": (1.2, 0.92), "pull": (0.92, 1.2), "static": (1.03, 1.0)}
    dk = next((distk[m] for m in moves if m in distk), (1.0, 1.0))
    lat = (-0.6, 0.6) if "pan_right" in moves else ((0.6, -0.6) if "pan_left" in moves else (0, 0))
    dz = (0, 0.45) if "rise" in moves else (0, 0)
    if typ == "tracking":                                       # kamera mengikuti subjek yang bergerak
        keys = []
        frames = list(range(f0, f1, 12)) + [f1]
        for f in frames:
            dist, cz, tgt, lens, fs = spec(f)
            x, y = subj.xy(f)
            dx, dy = (not s.get("dari_belakang") and facing_dir(subj, f, float(s.get("sudut", 0)), 70)) or \
                view_dir((x, y), float(s.get("sudut", 0)), s.get("dari_belakang", False))
            dist *= float(s.get("jarak", 1.0))
            keys.append([f, clamp_cam((x + dx * dist, y + dy * dist, cz + float(s.get("tinggi", 0)))), tgt, lens, fs])
        return keys
    if typ == "dolly":                                          # kamera bergeser menyamping sejajar subjek
        lat = (-1.0, 1.0) if s.get("arah", "kanan") == "kanan" else (1.0, -1.0)
    keys = []
    for i, f in enumerate((f0, f1)):
        dist, cz, tgt, lens, fs = spec(f)
        dist *= float(s.get("jarak", 1.0)) * (subj.size if typ == "insert" else 1.0)
        x, y = subj.xy(f)
        fd = None if (back or typ == "insert") else facing_dir(subj, f, angle)
        dx, dy = fd or view_dir((x, y), angle, back, 1.6 if typ == "insert" else 0.55)
        px, py = -dy, dx
        pos = (x + dx * dist * dk[i] + px * lat[i], y + dy * dist * dk[i] + py * lat[i], cz + dz[i] + float(s.get("tinggi", 0)))
        keys.append([f, clamp_cam(pos), tgt, lens, fs])
    if "crane_up" in moves:
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f1)
        keys[1][1], keys[1][2], keys[1][3] = (0.0, sy - 12.0, 7.5), (-1.0, sy + 19.0, 5.0), 30
    if "crane_down" in moves:
        sx, sy = subj.xy(f0)
        keys[0][1], keys[0][2] = (0.0, sy - 15.0, 9.0), (3.0, sy + 58.0, 16.0)
    return keys


def run(story, mode="full", log=print):
    plan = build_plan(story, mode)
    story.path("plan.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=1))
    log(f"  {len(plan['shots'])} shot, {len(plan['characters'])} tokoh, {plan['end']} frame")
    for w in plan["warnings"]:
        log(f"  PERINGATAN: {w}")
    return plan


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/provenance.py
# sha256: 238cd49cf9e993ece17e690aa2a0ba3e05a43d94556f8f6b9c6a7adc9298694d
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.1-E: provenance render per potongan (chunk) + validasi sebelum perakitan video.

Setiap potongan render (lokal atau Colab, termasuk sesi Colab tambahan) menulis satu berkas
    build/frames_<mode>/chunks/<a>-<b>_<waktu>_<lokasi>.json
berisi: build ID, checksum scene.blend yang dirender, source hash, config hash, rentang & daftar frame,
setelan render, lokasi/GPU, versi Blender (bila diketahui) dan SHA-256 setiap PNG keluaran.

Indeks frame (frames.json, skema 2) menyimpan per frame: build_id, status, chunk, sha256, dan catatan.
Status:
  terverifikasi_chunk  : frame ditulis oleh potongan bermetadata; checksum cocok saat diindeks
  warisan_tanpa_bukti  : indeks lama (sebelum Phase 4.1) / dibuat manual -> TIDAK ada bukti identitas build
Validasi (`validate`) dijalankan sebelum perakitan klip/video; FAIL menghentikan perakitan kecuali
ANIM_IZINKAN_TANPA_PROVENANCE=1 (hasilnya tetap dicatat sebagai FAIL di laporan & manifest)."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

SKEMA = 2


def sha256_file(p, chunk=1 << 20):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as fh:
        for b in iter(lambda: fh.read(chunk), b""):
            h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()


def _build_dir(frames_dir):
    return Path(frames_dir).parent


def _manifest(build):
    p = Path(build) / "manifest.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else {}


def config_hash(build, settings):
    """hash konfigurasi: story.yaml + plan.json + setelan render"""
    build = Path(build)
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for p in (build.parent / "story.yaml", build / "plan.json"):
        h.update(p.read_bytes() if p.exists() else b"-")
    h.update(json.dumps(settings, sort_keys=True).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()


def record_chunk(frames_dir, blend, files, settings, lokasi, extra=None):
    """files = [(frame, nama_png)] yang BARU ditulis potongan ini (yang ada di disk)"""
    frames_dir = Path(frames_dir)
    build = _build_dir(frames_dir)
    man = _manifest(build)
    files = [(int(f), n) for f, n in files if (frames_dir / n).exists() and (frames_dir / n).stat().st_size > 0]
    if not files:
        return None
    fr = sorted(f for f, _ in files)
    rec = {
        "skema": SKEMA,
        "build_id": man.get("build_id"),
        "scene_blend": str(blend),
        "scene_blend_sha256": sha256_file(blend) if Path(blend).exists() else None,
        "scene_blend_sha256_manifest": (man.get("scene_build") or {}).get("scene_blend_sha256"),
        "source_hash": (man.get("inputs") or {}).get("source_hash"),
        "config_hash": config_hash(build, settings),
        "rentang": [fr[0], fr[-1]],
        "frames": fr,
        "setelan_render": settings,
        "lokasi": lokasi,
        "dibuat": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"),
        "keluaran_sha256": {str(f): sha256_file(frames_dir / n) for f, n in files},
        **(extra or {}),
    }
    d = frames_dir / "chunks"
    d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    p = d / f"{fr[0]:04d}-{fr[-1]:04d}_{time.strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{lokasi.split(':')[0]}_{os.getpid()}.json"
    p.write_text(json.dumps(rec, indent=1))
    return p


def load_chunks(frames_dir, overlaps=None):
    """frame -> (berkas chunk, rekaman) yang DIPILIH. Phase 4.2: bila beberapa potongan menulis frame yang sama, dipilih
    potongan yang checksum-nya cocok dengan PNG di disk (bila ada), selain itu yang terbaru; semua kandidat dicatat di
    'overlaps' {frame: [nama chunk ...]} bila diberikan."""
    out, cand = {}, {}
    d = Path(frames_dir) / "chunks"
    if not d.exists():
        return out
    for p in sorted(d.glob("*.json"), key=lambda q: q.stat().st_mtime):
        try:
            rec = json.loads(p.read_text())
        except Exception:                                   # noqa: BLE001
            continue
        for f in rec.get("frames", []):
            cand.setdefault(int(f), []).append((p.name, rec))
    sums = {}
    for f, lst in cand.items():
        if len(lst) > 1:
            png = Path(frames_dir) / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
            sh = sums.get(f) or (sha256_file(png) if png.exists() else None)
            match = [c for c in lst if c[1].get("keluaran_sha256", {}).get(str(f)) == sh]
            out[f] = (match or lst)[-1]
            if overlaps is not None:
                overlaps[f] = {"kandidat": [c[0] for c in lst], "dipilih": out[f][0],
                               "alasan": "checksum cocok" if match else "terbaru (tidak ada yang cocok)"}
        else:
            out[f] = lst[0]
    return out


def read_index(frames_dir):
    """frames.json -> {frame(str): {build_id, status, ...}} (skema lama datar = warisan_tanpa_bukti)"""
    p = Path(frames_dir) / "frames.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return {}
    d = json.loads(p.read_text())
    if isinstance(d, dict) and d.get("skema") == SKEMA:
        return d.get("frame", {})
    return {k: {"build_id": v, "status": "warisan_tanpa_bukti",
                "catatan": "indeks skema lama (sebelum Phase 4.1): hanya label build ID, tanpa checksum/metadata potongan"}
            for k, v in d.items()}


def write_index(frames_dir, frames, bid):
    """perbarui indeks untuk 'frames' yang ada di disk; status dari bukti chunk (bukan klaim)"""
    frames_dir = Path(frames_dir)
    idx = read_index(frames_dir)
    ch = load_chunks(frames_dir)
    for f in frames:
        png = frames_dir / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
        if not png.exists():
            continue
        c = ch.get(f)
        if c and c[1].get("build_id") == bid and c[1]["keluaran_sha256"].get(str(f)) == sha256_file(png):
            idx[str(f)] = {"build_id": bid, "status": "terverifikasi_chunk", "chunk": c[0],
                           "sha256": c[1]["keluaran_sha256"][str(f)]}
        elif str(f) not in idx or idx[str(f)].get("build_id") != bid:
            idx[str(f)] = {"build_id": bid, "status": "warisan_tanpa_bukti",
                           "catatan": "frame ada di disk tanpa metadata potongan yang cocok; build ID = label, bukan bukti"}
    (frames_dir / "frames.json").write_text(json.dumps(
        {"skema": SKEMA, "catatan": "status per frame: terverifikasi_chunk = checksum cocok dgn metadata potongan; "
                                    "warisan_tanpa_bukti = tidak ada bukti identitas build",
         "frame": idx}, sort_keys=True, indent=0))
    return idx


def build_ids(frames_dir, a, b):
    idx = read_index(frames_dir)
    return sorted({(idx.get(str(f)) or {}).get("build_id", "TANPA_ID") for f in range(a, b + 1)})


def validate(frames_dir, a, b, bid=None):
    """validasi provenance frame a..b sebelum perakitan -> laporan (status PASS/FAIL per cek)"""
    frames_dir = Path(frames_dir)
    build = _build_dir(frames_dir)
    man = _manifest(build)
    bid = bid or man.get("build_id")
    scene_sha = (man.get("scene_build") or {}).get("scene_blend_sha256")
    src = (man.get("inputs") or {}).get("source_hash")
    ov = {}
    ch = load_chunks(frames_dir, ov)
    miss, nometa, badsum, wrong_bid, wrong_scene, wrong_src = [], [], [], [], [], []
    wrong_cfg, noid = [], []
    cfg_cache = {}
    settings = set()
    for f in range(a, b + 1):
        png = frames_dir / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
        if not png.exists() or png.stat().st_size == 0:
            miss.append(f); continue
        c = ch.get(f)
        if not c:
            nometa.append(f); continue
        rec = c[1]
        if rec["keluaran_sha256"].get(str(f)) != sha256_file(png):
            badsum.append(f)
        if rec.get("build_id") != bid:
            wrong_bid.append(f)
        if scene_sha and rec.get("scene_blend_sha256") != scene_sha:
            wrong_scene.append(f)
        if src and rec.get("source_hash") != src:
            wrong_src.append(f)
        if not rec.get("build_id") or not rec.get("config_hash") or not rec.get("scene_blend_sha256"):
            noid.append(f)                                  # Phase 4.2: identitas kritis hilang -> FAIL
        key = json.dumps(rec.get("setelan_render", {}), sort_keys=True)
        if key not in cfg_cache:
            cfg_cache[key] = config_hash(build, rec.get("setelan_render", {}))
        if rec.get("config_hash") and rec["config_hash"] != cfg_cache[key]:
            wrong_cfg.append(f)                             # story.yaml / plan.json berubah sesudah frame dirender
        s = rec.get("setelan_render", {})
        settings.add(json.dumps({k: s.get(k) for k in ("resolution", "samples", "engine", "alpha_tokoh", "glare")}, sort_keys=True))

    def st(lst):
        return {"status": "PASS" if not lst else "FAIL", "jumlah": len(lst), "contoh": lst[:10]}
    cek = {"frame_lengkap": st(miss), "metadata_potongan": st(nometa), "checksum_keluaran": st(badsum),
           "build_id_sama": st(wrong_bid), "checksum_adegan_sama": st(wrong_scene), "source_hash_sama": st(wrong_src),
           "setelan_render_seragam": {"status": "PASS" if len(settings) <= 1 else "FAIL", "varian": sorted(settings)}}
    cek["config_hash_sama"] = st(wrong_cfg)
    cek["identitas_potongan_lengkap"] = st(noid)
    # Phase 4.2: identitas build kritis yang HILANG = FAIL (bukan NOT_TESTED/PASS tanpa syarat)
    for k_, v_, why in (("checksum_adegan_sama", scene_sha, "manifest tanpa scene_blend_sha256"),
                        ("source_hash_sama", src, "manifest tanpa source_hash"),
                        ("build_id_sama", bid, "build ID tidak diketahui")):
        if not v_:
            cek[k_] = {"status": "FAIL", "alasan": why + " (identitas kritis hilang)"}
    ok = all(v["status"] == "PASS" for v in cek.values())
    used = sorted({ch[f][0] for f in range(a, b + 1) if f in ch})
    recov = []
    d = frames_dir / "chunks"
    for nm in used:
        try:
            r_ = json.loads((d / nm).read_text())
        except Exception:                                   # noqa: BLE001
            continue
        if r_.get("peristiwa") or "manual" in str(r_.get("catatan", "")):
            recov.append({"chunk": nm, "peristiwa": r_.get("peristiwa") or "pemulihan_manual (dari catatan)",
                          "catatan": r_.get("catatan"), "frames": r_.get("rentang")})
    rep = {"rentang": [a, b], "build_id": bid, "status": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", "cek": cek,
           "chunks": used, "potongan_tumpang_tindih": {str(k): v for k, v in sorted(ov.items()) if a <= k <= b},
           "pemulihan_manual": recov,
           "catatan": "PASS hanya bila SEMUA cek PASS; pemulihan manual tidak menggagalkan bila checksum & identitas cocok, "
                      "tetapi selalu dilaporkan."}
    (build / f"provenance_{a}_{b}.json").write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=1))
    return rep


def record_video(build, path, jenis, sumber=None, catatan=None):
    """Phase 4.2: checksum video ASLI (hasil rakit) dan DISTRIBUSI (re-encode / salinan paket) -> build/video_checksum.json"""
    build = Path(build)
    p = build / "video_checksum.json"
    d = json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else {"video": []}
    man = _manifest(build)
    d["video"].append({"jenis": jenis, "berkas": str(path), "sha256": sha256_file(path), "ukuran": Path(path).stat().st_size,
                       "build_id": man.get("build_id"), "sumber_sha256": sha256_file(sumber) if sumber else None,
                       "sumber": str(sumber) if sumber else None, "catatan": catatan,
                       "dibuat": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z")})
    p.write_text(json.dumps(d, indent=1))
    return d["video"][-1]


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/qa_rules.py
# sha256: b874d8db83ed91904c2c27b23c4760b3924ea8f6bfc0281488eac2871db38f5d
==============================================================================================================
"""Aturan QA murni Python (Phase 4.1-A4), dipakai validator Blender (scripts/dev/validasi_interaksi.py) DAN regression
test, sehingga kasus gagal yang diketahui (orientasi 40°, piring 0,74×, serah-terima 0,148 m) dapat direproduksi tanpa
Blender.

Status:
  PASS            diuji, dalam toleransi
  WARN            diuji, di luar toleransi pada sebagian kecil sampel
  FAIL            diuji, di luar toleransi
  NOT_TESTED      relevan, tetapi BELUM dijalankan (mis. cek render di luar Blender, rentang frame tidak lengkap)
  NOT_APPLICABLE  tidak relevan untuk cerita/rentang ini (mis. tidak ada sutil, tidak ada serah-terima)

Baris per_frame interaksi.json: [f, residual_m, residual_px, k_presentasi, mode, galat_orientasi_derajat, (ujung_m)]"""
STATUSES = ("PASS", "WARN", "FAIL", "NOT_TESTED", "NOT_APPLICABLE")
HELD = ("hand", "carry", "place")


def _pct(v, q):
    v = sorted(v)
    return v[min(len(v) - 1, int(q * (len(v) - 1) + 0.5))] if v else 0.0


def orientation(rows, tol, frames=None):
    """galat orientasi tangan saat benda dipegang (frame terlihat bila 'frames' diberikan).
    FAIL: p95 > tol atau maks > 2*tol;  WARN: maks > tol (p95 masih dalam toleransi)."""
    v = [r[5] for r in rows if r[4] in HELD and (frames is None or r[0] in frames)]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "benda tidak dipegang di frame terlihat"}
    p95, mx = _pct(v, 0.95), max(v)
    st = "FAIL" if (p95 > tol or mx > 2 * tol) else ("WARN" if mx > tol else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "toleransi_derajat": tol, "p95": round(p95, 1), "maks": round(mx, 1),
            "median": round(_pct(v, 0.5), 1), "di_atas_toleransi": sum(1 for x in v if x > tol), "sampel": len(v)}


def physical_scale(rows, skala_min, frames=None):
    """skala presentasi benda yang dipegang. Ukuran fisik harus konsisten: k < skala_min -> FAIL."""
    v = [(r[0], r[3]) for r in rows if r[4] in HELD and (frames is None or r[0] in frames)]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "benda tidak dipegang di frame terlihat"}
    bad = [f for f, k in v if k < skala_min - 1e-3]
    kmin = min(k for _, k in v)
    return {"status": "FAIL" if bad else "PASS", "skala_min_profil": skala_min, "skala_min_terukur": round(kmin, 4),
            "frame_di_bawah": bad[:40], "jumlah_di_bawah": len(bad), "sampel": len(v)}


def handover(events, tol_px=6.0, tol_jump_m=0.02, tol_res_m=0.02):
    """events: [{'benda', 'frame', 'residual_pemberi_px', 'residual_penerima_px', 'lompatan_m', 'residual_maks_m'}]
    FAIL bila tangan tidak sampai (residual > tol_px) atau benda melompat/lepas kontak (> tol_jump_m / tol_res_m)."""
    if not events:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada serah-terima"}
    out, worst = [], "PASS"
    for e in events:
        why = []
        if max(e.get("residual_pemberi_px", 0), e.get("residual_penerima_px", 0)) > tol_px:
            why.append("tangan tidak menjangkau titik serah")
        if e.get("lompatan_m", 0) > tol_jump_m:
            why.append(f"benda melompat {e['lompatan_m']:.3f} m di frame perpindahan")
        if e.get("residual_maks_m", 0) > tol_res_m:
            why.append(f"kontak hilang {e['residual_maks_m']:.3f} m")
        st = "FAIL" if why else "PASS"
        worst = "FAIL" if st == "FAIL" else worst
        out.append({**e, "status": st, "alasan": why})
    return {"status": worst, "serah": out, "toleransi": {"px": tol_px, "lompatan_m": tol_jump_m, "residual_m": tol_res_m}}


def contact_window(rows, f0, f1):
    """residual genggam maksimum (m) di rentang [f0, f1)"""
    v = [r[1] for r in rows if f0 <= r[0] < f1 and r[4] in HELD]
    return max(v) if v else 0.0


def jump(phys_pos, f):
    """lompatan posisi fisik benda (m) antara f-1 dan f; phys_pos: {f: (x, y, z)}"""
    a, b = phys_pos.get(f - 1), phys_pos.get(f)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return 0.0
    return sum((x - y) ** 2 for x, y in zip(a, b)) ** 0.5


def summarize(status):
    return {k: sum(1 for v in status.values() if v.get("status") == k) for k in STATUSES}


def wrist(aw_by_frame, frames=None, comfort=30.0, limit=45.0):
    """tekuk pergelangan relatif lengan bawah (derajat) saat menggenggam. Di atas 'limit' kepalan tampak terlepas dari
    lengan bawah. FAIL: p95 > limit; WARN: ada frame > limit."""
    v = [abs(a) for f, a in aw_by_frame.items() if frames is None or int(f) in frames]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada genggam di frame terlihat"}
    p95, mx = _pct(v, 0.95), max(v)
    st = "FAIL" if p95 > limit else ("WARN" if mx > limit else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "nyaman_derajat": comfort, "batas_derajat": limit, "p95": round(p95, 1), "maks": round(mx, 1),
            "di_atas_batas": sum(1 for x in v if x > limit), "sampel": len(v)}


def transitions(events, frames=None, tol_jump_m=0.02, tol_px=6.0):
    """lompatan benda di setiap pergantian pemegang/dunia (ambil, taruh, serah, lepas)"""
    ev = [e for e in events if frames is None or e["frame"] in frames or e["frame"] - 1 in frames]
    if not ev:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada transisi kepemilikan yang terlihat"}
    bad = [e for e in ev if e.get("lompatan_m", 0) > tol_jump_m or e.get("residual_maks_px", 0) > tol_px]
    return {"status": "FAIL" if bad else "PASS", "gagal": bad, "sampel": len(ev),
            "lompatan_maks_m": max(e.get("lompatan_m", 0) for e in ev),
            "toleransi": {"lompatan_m": tol_jump_m, "px": tol_px}}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Phase 4.2 (gerbang A)
def grip_inversion(bad_frames, frames=None):
    """inversi genggam TIDAK SAH: ujung alat keluar di sisi kelingking sprite yang dirender, atau supinasi (tangan
    dicerminkan) pada benda yang profilnya tidak mengizinkan. Satu frame pun = FAIL."""
    v = [int(f) for f in bad_frames if frames is None or int(f) in frames]
    return {"status": "FAIL" if v else "PASS", "frame": v[:40], "jumlah": len(v)}


def mouth_contact(by_frame, frames=None, ratio=0.25, fail_ratio=1.0):
    """jarak pusat cekung sendok / bibir gelas ke anchor mulut pada fase 'kontak', relatif lebar acuan benda
    (lebar cekung / diameter gelas). by_frame: {f: [fase, jarak_px, acuan_px]}.
    WARN: ada frame > ratio*acuan; FAIL: ada frame > fail_ratio*acuan (alat tidak sampai mulut)."""
    v = [(int(f), x[1], x[2]) for f, x in by_frame.items() if x[0] == "kontak" and (frames is None or int(f) in frames)]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada fase kontak mulut yang terlihat"}
    rel = [(f, d / max(1e-6, ref)) for f, d, ref in v]
    mx = max(r for _, r in rel)
    st = "FAIL" if mx > fail_ratio else ("WARN" if mx > ratio else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "rasio_maks_thd_lebar": round(mx, 3), "batas_rasio": ratio,
            "frame_di_atas": [f for f, r in rel if r > ratio][:40], "sampel": len(v)}


def presentation_shift(by_frame, limit, frames=None):
    """geser presentasi sepanjang sinar kamera (pengganti skala benda berukuran tetap). by_frame: {f: [m, tampak]}.
    WARN bila ada frame > limit; FAIL bila p95 > limit (pose/blocking salah secara sistematis)."""
    if limit is None:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "profil tanpa geser_maks"}
    v = [(int(f), x[0]) for f, x in by_frame.items() if frames is None or int(f) in frames]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "PASS", "maks_m": 0.0, "batas_m": limit, "sampel": 0}
    d = [x for _, x in v]
    p95, mx = _pct(d, 0.95), max(d)
    st = "FAIL" if p95 > limit else ("WARN" if mx > limit else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "batas_m": limit, "p95_m": round(p95, 4), "maks_m": round(mx, 4),
            "frame_di_atas": [f for f, x in v if x > limit][:40], "sampel": len(v)}


def world_collision(by_frame, frames=None, tol_m=0.005):
    """benda dipegang vs proxy dunia. by_frame: {f: [jarak_m, proxy]} (hanya frame < 5 mm dicatat).
    FAIL: tembus > tol_m; WARN: tembus 0..tol_m; mepet (>= 0) = PASS dengan catatan."""
    v = [(int(f), x[0], x[1]) for f, x in by_frame.items() if frames is None or int(f) in frames]
    pen = [x for x in v if x[1] < -tol_m]
    touch = [x for x in v if -tol_m <= x[1] < 0]
    st = "FAIL" if pen else ("WARN" if touch else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "toleransi_tembus_m": tol_m, "tembus": pen[:20], "sentuh": touch[:20],
            "mepet_frame": len(v) - len(pen) - len(touch), "terdalam_m": round(min([x[1] for x in v] or [0.0]), 4)}


def contact_wrist(aw_by_frame, phase_by_frame, phases=("kontak", "dekat_mulut"), limit=45.0, hard=60.0, frames=None):
    """tekuk pergelangan HANYA di fase kontak kritis (mulut / serah-terima). WARN > limit, FAIL > hard."""
    v = [abs(a) for f, a in aw_by_frame.items()
         if (phase_by_frame.get(f) or phase_by_frame.get(str(f)) or [None])[0] in phases
         and (frames is None or int(f) in frames)]
    if not v:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada fase kontak kritis"}
    mx = max(v)
    return {"status": "FAIL" if mx > hard else ("WARN" if mx > limit else "PASS"), "maks": round(mx, 1),
            "batas_derajat": limit, "batas_keras_derajat": hard, "sampel": len(v)}


def elbow_branch(events, frames=None):
    """pergantian cabang siku (IK meloncat > 90°) selama benda dipegang — dibaurkan, tetapi tetap dilaporkan.
    WARN bila ada yang terlihat; PASS bila tidak ada."""
    v = [e for e in events if frames is None or int(e[1]) in frames]
    return {"status": "WARN" if v else "PASS", "kejadian": v[:20], "jumlah": len(v)}


def handover_collision(events):
    """titik serah & lintasan raih bebas tabrakan proxy dunia (Phase 4.2-A4)"""
    if not events:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada serah-terima"}
    bad = [e for e in events if e.get("status_tabrakan") == "tembus"]
    near = [e for e in events if e.get("status_tabrakan") == "mepet"]
    if any("status_tabrakan" not in e for e in events):
        return {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "laporan serah-terima tanpa uji tabrakan (build lama)"}
    return {"status": "FAIL" if bad else ("WARN" if near else "PASS"),
            "serah": [{k: e.get(k) for k in ("benda", "frame", "jarak_bebas_titik_m", "jarak_bebas_lintasan_m",
                                             "proxy_terdekat", "status_tabrakan")} for e in events]}


def temporal_flicker(means, cuts=(), warn=1.0, fail=2.0):
    """Phase 4.2-C: kedip cahaya = komponen frekuensi tinggi luminansi rata-rata per frame (0..255) DI DALAM shot:
    e[i] = |m[i] - (m[i-1] + m[i+1]) / 2|. Ramp halus (gerak kamera, fade) ~0; potongan shot diabaikan.
    means: {frame: luma}; cuts: frame awal tiap shot."""
    fr = sorted(means)
    cut = set(cuts)
    e = {}
    for a, b, c in zip(fr, fr[1:], fr[2:]):
        if c - a != 2 or b in cut or c in cut:
            continue
        e[b] = abs(means[b] - (means[a] + means[c]) / 2)
    if not e:
        return {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "kurang dari 3 frame berurutan"}
    worst = max(e, key=e.get)
    n_w = sum(1 for v in e.values() if v > warn)
    st = "FAIL" if e[worst] > fail else ("WARN" if n_w else "PASS")
    return {"status": st, "maks": round(e[worst], 2), "frame_maks": worst, "frame_di_atas_warn": n_w,
            "batas": {"warn": warn, "fail": fail}, "frame_diuji": len(e)}


def composition_bands(comp_rows):
    """Phase 4.2-C: komposisi per kunci kamera (kamera.json -> 'komposisi'): nilai AKHIR di dalam pita tipe shot
    ('pita' dicatat solver). Di luar pita <= 0,05 -> WARN; lebih -> FAIL. Kunci tanpa 'pita' -> NOT_TESTED."""
    out, worst = [], "PASS"
    rank = {"PASS": 0, "NOT_TESTED": 0, "WARN": 1, "FAIL": 2}
    tested = 0
    for r in comp_rows:
        band, m = r.get("pita"), r.get("akhir") or r.get("awal")
        if not band or not m:
            continue
        tested += 1
        for k, (lo, hi) in band.items():
            if k not in m or not isinstance(m[k], (int, float)):
                continue
            dv = max(0.0, lo - m[k], m[k] - hi)
            if dv > 0:
                st = "WARN" if dv <= 0.05 else "FAIL"
                why = None
                xs, hr = m.get("x"), m.get("hr_ndc")
                wtot = r.get("lebar_total_ndc") or ((max(xs) - min(xs) + 2 * hr) if xs and hr is not None else 0.0)
                if k == "tinggi" and m[k] < lo and wtot >= 1.6:
                    # bingkai potret 9:16: kelompok sudah selebar bingkai (semua kepala di dalam tepi aman) -> okupansi
                    # tinggi tidak bisa dinaikkan tanpa memotong tokoh. Dilaporkan sebagai WARN beralasan, bukan lolos.
                    st, why = "WARN", "dibatasi_lebar_kelompok (lebar %.2f NDC, termasuk tokoh lain di bingkai)" % wtot
                out.append({"shot": r["shot"], "frame": r["frame"], "metrik": k, "nilai": round(m[k], 3), "pita": [lo, hi],
                            "status": st, **({"alasan": why} if why else {})})
                worst = st if rank[st] > rank[worst] else worst
    if not tested:
        return {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "laporan komposisi tanpa pita"}
    return {"status": worst, "kunci_diuji": tested, "di_luar_pita": out}


def subject_face(shot_rows, face_shots=("medium", "mcu", "closeup"), away_max=110.0, occ_warn=0.1, occ_fail=0.25):
    """Phase 4.2-D: wajah subjek pada shot wajah: berpaling dari kamera (> away_max derajat) -> FAIL;
    tertutup (sinar ke wajah terhalang) > occ_warn -> WARN, > occ_fail -> FAIL."""
    out, worst, tested = [], "PASS", 0
    rank = {"PASS": 0, "WARN": 1, "FAIL": 2}
    for r in shot_rows:
        if r.get("kamera") not in face_shots:
            continue
        a = r.get("akhir") or {}
        tested += 1
        st = "PASS"
        aw = a.get("arah_wajah_thd_kamera_derajat")
        if aw is not None and aw > away_max:
            st = "FAIL"
        occ = a.get("wajah_terhalang", 0.0)
        if occ > occ_fail:
            st = "FAIL"
        elif occ > occ_warn and st == "PASS":
            st = "WARN"
        if st != "PASS":
            out.append({"shot": r["shot"], "subjek": r.get("subjek"), "arah_wajah_derajat": aw, "wajah_terhalang": occ,
                        "penghalang": a.get("penghalang"), "status": st})
            worst = st if rank[st] > rank[worst] else worst
    if not tested:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada shot wajah"}
    if any(r.get("akhir", {}).get("arah_wajah_thd_kamera_derajat") is None for r in shot_rows
           if r.get("kamera") in face_shots):
        return {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "laporan kamera tanpa arah wajah (build sebelum Phase 4.2-D)"}
    return {"status": worst, "shot_diuji": tested, "masalah": out}


def action_in_shot(events, shots, wide=("establishing", "wide", "full", "ending")):
    """Phase 4.2-D (heuristik, BUKAN uji proyeksi): aksi dua tokoh (serah-terima) jatuh di shot yang membingkai
    keduanya (tipe lebar, atau subjek + ke_arah = kedua tokoh). Selain itu WARN 'mungkin di luar bingkai'."""
    if not events:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada aksi dua tokoh"}
    out, worst = [], "PASS"
    for ev in events:
        sh = next((s for s in shots if s["f0"] <= ev["frame"] <= s["f1"]), None)
        who = {ev["pemberi"], ev["penerima"]}
        ok = sh is not None and (sh["kamera"] in wide or who <= {sh.get("subjek"), sh.get("ke_arah")})
        st = "PASS" if ok else "WARN"
        out.append({"frame": ev["frame"], "benda": ev.get("benda"), "shot": sh and sh["n"], "kamera": sh and sh["kamera"],
                    "status": st})
        worst = "WARN" if st == "WARN" else worst
    return {"status": worst, "aksi": out, "catatan": "heuristik berbasis daftar shot; bukti visual = still/klip"}


def eat_contact_in_shot(eaters, shots, kinds=("medium", "mcu", "closeup", "full"), margin=3):
    """Phase 4.2: suapan/tegukan TERLIHAT di shot yang membingkai pemakan. eaters = [{nama, f0, f1, kontak:[frame]}].
    Shot ber-subjek pemakan yang tumpang tindih dgn jendela makan >= 24 frame tanpa frame kontak -> WARN."""
    out, tested = [], 0
    for e in eaters:
        for s in shots:
            if e["nama"] not in (s.get("subjek"), s.get("ke_arah")) or s["kamera"] not in kinds:
                continue
            a, b = max(s["f0"] + margin, e["f0"]), min(s["f1"] - margin, e["f1"])
            if b - a < 24:
                continue
            tested += 1
            if not any(a <= f <= b for f in e["kontak"]):
                out.append({"tokoh": e["nama"], "benda": e.get("benda"), "shot": s["n"], "rentang": [a, b]})
    if not tested:
        return {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "tidak ada shot pemakan yang tumpang tindih dgn makan/minum"}
    return {"status": "WARN" if out else "PASS", "shot_diuji": tested, "tanpa_kontak": out}
