"""Rig 2D berlapis (layered sprites) untuk tokoh manusia.

Alih-alih satu gambar utuh per pose, tokoh dipecah jadi lapisan yang digambar di kanvas yang sama
(1000 x 1400) sehingga semuanya pas ditumpuk:

  kaki_<pose>      berdiri / jalanA-D / duduk
  badan            torso, rompi, celemek, tas, handuk
  lengan_kiri/kanan (+ _tunjuk)   lengan lurus ke bawah; diputar di bahu (pivot) oleh Blender
  kepala           telinga, kepala, rambut, hijab, topi; diputar di leher (miring kepala)
  wajah            pipi merah, kacamata
  mata_<bentuk>    open / closed / happy / wide / squint  (melirik = lapisan 'open' digeser)
  alis_<bentuk>    calm / raised / soft / sad / angry
  mulut_<bentuk>   flat / smile / frown / open / opensmile / whistle (+ kumis)
  fx_peluit_0/1    gelombang bunyi peluit
  belakang*        tampak belakang (gambar utuh, dipakai saat membelakangi kamera)

Gaya gambar sama persis dengan chars.draw_human (fungsi gambarnya dipakai ulang).
rig.json menjelaskan pivot, sudut pose lengan, dan cara menerjemahkan nama varian lama
(talk, smile, point, wave, walkA, back, ...) ke kombinasi lapisan, sehingga plan.json tidak berubah."""
import json
import math

from . import chars as C
from .chars import EXPR, HX, Canvas

# Phase 4.1-B: bawah = FOKUS menatap ke bawah (mata terbuka, kilau bawah) -- dibedakan dari ngantuk/sedih/closed/happy
EYES = ["open", "closed", "happy", "wide", "squint", "bawah", "ngantuk", "sedih"]
BROWS = ["calm", "raised", "soft", "sad", "angry", "fokus"]
MOUTHS = ["flat", "smile", "frown", "open", "opensmile", "whistle", "chew", "chew2"]
LEGS = ["stand", "walkA", "walkB", "walkC", "walkD", "sit"]
FOOT_Y = 1336                        # dasar sepatu saat berdiri (y kanvas) -> diletakkan tepat di tanah
SIT_DROP = 120                       # duduk di dingklik pendek: badan turun 120 px; lutut setinggi pinggul, betis tegak
SEAT_Y = 1088                        # garis pantat (y kanvas): tinggi dudukan = (FOOT_Y - SIT_DROP - SEAT_Y) px
SIT_FLOOR = FOOT_Y - SIT_DROP        # lantai (y kanvas) saat duduk
MOUTH = (500, 612)                   # titik mulut (y + yo bila berhijab): tujuan sendok
EYE = (500, 455)                     # pusat mata: titik acuan arah pandang

SHOULDER = {-1: (360, 760), 1: (640, 760)}
NECK = (500, 720)
HAND = (12, 228)                     # ujung tangan relatif bahu saat lengan lurus ke bawah (x dikali sisi)
WRIST = (12, 198)                    # pergelangan relatif bahu (Phase 4B: tangan genggam berputar di sini)
# tangan genggam berlapis (Phase 4B): titik genggam relatif pergelangan saat tangan menggantung (px kanvas)
GRIP_HAND = {"power": (0, 42), "pinch": (0, 30), "wrap": (0, 46), "palm": (0, 34), "hook": (0, 50)}
ELBOW = (16, 116)                    # siku relatif bahu (x dikali sisi)
TURN_PX = 70                         # tampak 3/4: fitur wajah bergeser sekian px ke arah hadap
PROFILE_SQUASH = 0.66                # tampak profil (Phase 4.1-B): badan lebih ramping lagi
Q_SQUASH = 0.88                      # tampak 3/4: badan sedikit lebih ramping
SHOULDER_Q = {-1: (382, 760), 1: (610, 760)}
SHOULDER_S = {-1: (452, 760), 1: (560, 760)}   # tampak profil (Phase 4.1-B): kedua bahu mendekati garis tengah badan ramping   # bahu pada tampak 3/4 (menghadap kanan layar; kiri = cermin)
LOOK_PX = 30                         # geseran mata saat melirik


def _face_part(c, eyes="-", brows="-", mouth="-", mustache=False, glasses=False, blush=False, yo=0, detail=None, turn=0):
    EXPR["__part"] = (eyes, brows, mouth)
    try:
        C.face(c, "__part", mustache, glasses, blush, yo, detail, turn)
    finally:
        del EXPR["__part"]


def _segment(c, a, b, w, fill, cap_a="round", cap_b="round", side=1):
    """ruas lengan berlapis dengan ujung sendi yang tidak menimbulkan garis sambungan saat diputar.
    cap: round = garis tepi melingkar penuh | outer = garis tepi hanya di setengah sisi luar (bahu menempel badan)
    | none = garis tepi berhenti datar di pusat sendi (ruas yang menumpuk di atas ruas lain).
    Isi ujung selalu bulat, jadi sendi tetap mulus ketika ditekuk."""
    S, OL, INK = C.S, C.OL, C.INK
    d = c.d
    pa, pb = (a[0] * S, a[1] * S), (b[0] * S, b[1] * S)
    ro = (w / 2 + OL) * S
    d.line([pa, pb], fill=INK, width=int(2 * ro))
    for (x, y), cap in ((pa, cap_a), (pb, cap_b)):
        box = [x - ro, y - ro, x + ro, y + ro]
        if cap == "round":
            d.ellipse(box, fill=INK)
        elif cap == "outer":                                   # setengah lingkaran sisi luar + atas
            d.pieslice(box, 230, 410, fill=INK) if side > 0 else d.pieslice(box, 130, 310, fill=INK)
    d.line([pa, pb], fill=fill, width=int(w * S))
    for x, y in (pa, pb):
        r = w / 2 * S
        d.ellipse([x - r, y - r, x + r, y + r], fill=fill)


def _upper_arm(c, side, sleeve):
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    ex, ey = sx + side * ELBOW[0], sy + ELBOW[1]
    _segment(c, (sx, sy), (ex, ey), 46, sleeve, cap_a="outer", cap_b="round", side=side)
    C.taper(c, [(sx + side * 4, sy + 92), (ex + side * 16, ey + 2)], 5, 2, C.darker(sleeve, 0.72))     # lipatan siku
    C.taper(c, [(sx - side * 10, sy + 30), (sx - side * 14, sy + 80)], 4, 1, C.darker(sleeve, 0.8))   # lipatan lengan dalam


def _fore_arm(c, side, sleeve, skin, hand_kind="open"):
    """lengan bawah dari siku ke tangan; hand_kind: open | point | grip.
    Ujung siku tanpa garis tepi (menumpuk di atas lengan atas) -> sendi mulus saat ditekuk."""
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    ex, ey = sx + side * ELBOW[0], sy + ELBOW[1]
    hx, hy = sx + side * HAND[0], sy + HAND[1]
    _segment(c, (ex, ey), (hx, hy - 8), 42, sleeve, cap_a="none", cap_b="round", side=side)
    c.line([(hx - 23, hy - 34), (hx + 23, hy - 34)], 9, C.darker(sleeve, 0.7))                # manset
    if hand_kind == "point":
        c.limb([(hx, hy), (hx + side * 4, hy + 72)], 18, skin)
    C.hand(c, hx, hy, side, skin, grip=hand_kind == "grip")


def _fore_arm_bare(c, side, sleeve):
    """lengan bawah sampai pergelangan, tanpa tangan (tangan = lapisan terpisah yang berputar di pergelangan)"""
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    ex, ey = sx + side * ELBOW[0], sy + ELBOW[1]
    wx, wy = sx + side * WRIST[0], sy + WRIST[1]
    _segment(c, (ex, ey), (wx, wy - 6), 42, sleeve, cap_a="none", cap_b="round", side=side)
    c.line([(wx - 23, wy - 6), (wx + 23, wy - 6)], 9, C.darker(sleeve, 0.7))                # manset


def _finger_gaps(c, xs, y0, y1, sh):
    for x in xs:
        C.taper(c, [(x, y0), (x, y1)], 4, 1, sh)


def grip_hand(c, side, skin, style, layer):
    """tangan genggam berlapis, menggantung dari pergelangan (wx, wy); ibu jari di sisi dalam (-side).
    layer 'b' = BELAKANG benda (pergelangan, telapak, jari di bawah/balik benda)
    layer 'f' = DEPAN benda (jari melingkar / ibu jari di atas tepi) -> benda terlihat DI DALAM genggaman"""
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    wx, wy = sx + side * WRIST[0], sy + WRIST[1]
    sh = C.darker(skin, 0.68)
    if layer == "b":
        c.limb([(wx, wy - 10), (wx, wy + 8)], 30, skin)                                    # pangkal pergelangan
        if style == "pinch":                                                              # telapak + jari lurus ke pusat piring
            c.rrect(wx - 27, wy + 2, wx + 27, wy + 68, 22, skin)
            _finger_gaps(c, (wx - 9, wx + 9), wy + 42, wy + 64, sh)
        elif style == "palm":
            c.rrect(wx - 27, wy + 2, wx + 27, wy + 64, 22, skin)
            _finger_gaps(c, (wx - 9, wx + 9), wy + 40, wy + 60, sh)
        else:                                                                             # telapak kepalan
            c.rrect(wx - 30, wy + 2, wx + 30, wy + 58, 22, skin)
        return
    FO = 8                                                                                # garis tepi jari depan: lebih tipis dari badan
    if style == "power":                                                                  # jari melingkari gagang + ibu jari
        c.rrect(wx - 29, wy + 27, wx + 29, wy + 58, 13, skin, ow=FO)
        _finger_gaps(c, (wx - 14, wx, wx + 14), wy + 31, wy + 46, sh)
        c.ellipse(wx - side * 20, wy + 22, 10, 15, skin, ow=FO)
        C.taper(c, [(wx - side * 20, wy + 13), (wx - side * 18, wy + 30)], 3, 1, sh)
    elif style == "pinch":                                                                # ibu jari di ATAS tepi piring
        c.limb([(wx - side * 18, wy + 14), (wx - side * 9, wy + 40)], 13, skin, ow=FO)
        C.taper(c, [(wx - side * 15, wy + 30), (wx - side * 9, wy + 33)], 3, 1, sh)       # lipatan buku ibu jari
    elif style == "wrap":                                                                 # jari di depan badan gelas
        c.rrect(wx - 29, wy + 38, wx + 29, wy + 64, 11, skin, ow=FO)
        for y in (wy + 47, wy + 56):
            C.taper(c, [(wx - 23, y), (wx + 23, y)], 3, 2, sh)
        c.ellipse(wx - side * 21, wy + 26, 9, 16, skin, ow=FO)
    elif style == "palm":                                                                 # ibu jari menahan sisi benda
        c.limb([(wx - side * 24, wy + 14), (wx - side * 22, wy + 44)], 14, skin, ow=FO)
    elif style == "hook":                                                                 # jari menekuk mengait tali/tepi
        c.rrect(wx - 29, wy + 34, wx + 29, wy + 60, 12, skin, ow=FO)
        _finger_gaps(c, (wx - 14, wx, wx + 14), wy + 38, wy + 52, sh)


def _arm(c, side, sleeve, skin, finger=False):
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    hx, hy = sx + side * HAND[0], sy + HAND[1]
    c.limb([(sx, sy), (sx + side * 18, sy + 120), (hx, hy - 5)], 44, sleeve)
    c.line([(sx + side * 4, sy + 110), (sx + side * 30, sy + 126)], 5, C.darker(sleeve, 0.75))   # lipatan siku
    c.line([(hx - 24, hy - 34), (hx + 24, hy - 34)], 9, C.darker(sleeve, 0.7))                  # manset
    if finger:
        c.limb([(hx, hy), (hx + side * 4, hy + 72)], 18, skin)
    C.hand(c, hx, hy, side, skin)


def _legs(c, kind, pants, shoes):
    """kaki_<kind>. Duduk (dingklik pendek): kaki_sit = betis + sepatu (di belakang badan),
    lutut_sit = paha memendek + lutut (di depan badan, mengarah ke kamera), lutut_sit_q = kaki tampak 3/4."""
    if kind not in ("sit", "lutut_sit", "lutut_sit_q"):
        C.legs(c, kind, pants, shoes)
        return
    col = pants if pants else C.INK
    ol = bool(pants)
    G = SIT_FLOOR
    if kind == "sit":
        for s in (-1, 1):
            c.limb([(500 + s * 98, 1060), (500 + s * 102, G - 44)], 34, col, outline=ol)
            c.ellipse(500 + s * 106, G - 24, 40, 25, shoes, ow=8)
            c.arc(500 + s * 106, G - 30, 26, 12, 200, 340, 4, C.darker(shoes, 1.6))           # kilap ujung sepatu
    elif kind == "lutut_sit":
        for s in (-1, 1):
            knee = (500 + s * 96, 1040)
            c.limb([(500 + s * 46, 1066), knee], 58, col, outline=ol)                       # paha (memendek)
            c.ellipse(knee[0], knee[1], 39, 35, col, outline=ol)
            C.taper(c, [(knee[0] - s * 22, knee[1] + 14), (knee[0] + s * 8, knee[1] + 26)], 5, 2, C.darker(col, 0.7))
    else:                                                                                    # 3/4 menghadap kanan layar
        c.ellipse(452, 1058, 78, 40, col, outline=ol)                                        # pinggul di dudukan
        for dx, dy, k in ((16, -12, 0.78), (0, 0, 1.0)):                                     # kaki jauh lalu kaki dekat
            cc = C.darker(col, k) if k < 1 else col
            hip, knee, ankle = (500 + dx, 1058 + dy), (648 + dx, 1046 + dy), (654 + dx, G - 44 + dy)
            c.limb([hip, knee], 50, cc, outline=ol)
            c.limb([knee, ankle], 34, cc, outline=ol)
            c.ellipse(knee[0], knee[1], 30, 29, cc, outline=ol)
            c.ellipse(ankle[0] + 24, G - 24 + dy, 50, 24, shoes, ow=8)
        C.taper(c, [(560, 1036), (620, 1032)], 5, 2, C.darker(col, 0.7))                    # lipatan paha


def _shade_body(c):
    c.shade(mask=c.im.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > 128 else 0), dx=45, dy=0, k=0.72, blur=10, light=0.12)


def draw_layers(sp):
    """-> {nama_lapisan: Canvas}"""
    L = {}
    yo = 30 if sp["hijab"] else 0
    for kind in LEGS + ["lutut_sit", "lutut_sit_q"]:
        c = Canvas(); _legs(c, kind, None if sp["dress"] else sp["celana"], sp["sepatu"]); _shade_body(c)
        L[kind if kind.startswith("lutut") else f"kaki_{kind}"] = c
    c = Canvas()
    C.torso(c, sp, "front")
    if sp["alat"] == "peluit":
        c.line([(455, 735), (500, 860), (545, 735)], 6, (40, 90, 170, 255))
    if sp["tas"]:
        c.line([(620, 730), (380, 960)], 14, C.darker(sp["tas"], 0.7))
        c.rrect(320, 930, 440, 1030, 18, sp["tas"])
    if sp["handuk"]:
        c.poly([(560, 705), (640, 715), (625, 900), (580, 900)], sp["handuk"])
    _shade_body(c); L["badan"] = c
    sleeve = sp["jaket"] or sp["dress"] or sp["baju"]
    for side, nm in ((-1, "kiri"), (1, "kanan")):
        c = Canvas(); _upper_arm(c, side, sleeve); _shade_body(c); L[f"lengan_atas_{nm}"] = c
        for kind, sfx in (("open", ""), ("point", "_tunjuk"), ("grip", "_genggam")):
            c = Canvas(); _fore_arm(c, side, sleeve, sp["kulit"], kind); _shade_body(c)
            L[f"lengan_bawah_{nm}{sfx}"] = c
        c = Canvas(); _fore_arm_bare(c, side, sleeve); _shade_body(c); L[f"lengan_bawah_{nm}_lepas"] = c
        for style in GRIP_HAND:
            for layer in ("b", "f"):
                c = Canvas(); grip_hand(c, side, sp["kulit"], style, layer)
                L[f"tangan_{style}_{nm}_{layer}"] = c
    # tampak 3/4 (menghadap ke kanan layar): badan ramping, wajah bergeser, satu telinga
    body = L["badan"].im
    q = Canvas(); w = int(body.width * Q_SQUASH)
    q.im.paste(body.resize((w, body.height), C.Image.LANCZOS), ((body.width - w) // 2 + 12 * C.S, 0)); L["badan_q"] = q
    c = Canvas(); C.hair_and_head(c, sp, "q"); c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: d.ellipse(C.P(HX - C.HR, C.HY - C.HR, HX + C.HR, C.HY + C.HR), fill=255),
                                                     dx=-60, dy=70, k=0.72, blur=18, light=0.15)
    C.hat(c, sp, turn=1); L["kepala_q"] = c
    c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, blush=True, glasses=sp["kacamata"], yo=yo, detail={"kulit": sp["kulit"], "tua": sp["tua"], "skin_mask": C.skin_mask(sp, "q")}, turn=1); L["wajah_q"] = c
    for e in EYES:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, eyes=e, yo=yo, turn=1); L[f"mata_{e}_q"] = c
    for b in BROWS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, brows=b, yo=yo, turn=1); L[f"alis_{b}_q"] = c
    for m in MOUTHS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, mouth=m, mustache=sp["kumis"], yo=yo, turn=1); L[f"mulut_{m}_q"] = c
    # tampak PROFIL (Phase 4.1-B, menghadap kanan layar): badan ramping, kepala & wajah profil; lengan = jalur 3/4
    s_ = Canvas(); w = int(body.width * PROFILE_SQUASH)
    s_.im.paste(body.resize((w, body.height), C.Image.LANCZOS), ((body.width - w) // 2 + 18 * C.S, 0)); L["badan_s"] = s_
    c = Canvas(); C.hair_and_head(c, sp, "s"); c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: d.ellipse(C.P(HX - C.HR, C.HY - C.HR, HX + C.HR, C.HY + C.HR), fill=255),
                                                     dx=-70, dy=70, k=0.72, blur=18, light=0.15)
    C.hat(c, sp, turn=2); L["kepala_s"] = c
    c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, blush=True, glasses=sp["kacamata"], yo=yo, detail={"kulit": sp["kulit"], "tua": sp["tua"], "skin_mask": C.skin_mask(sp, "s")}, turn=2); L["wajah_s"] = c
    for e in EYES:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, eyes=e, yo=yo, turn=2); L[f"mata_{e}_s"] = c
    for b in BROWS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, brows=b, yo=yo, turn=2); L[f"alis_{b}_s"] = c
    for m in MOUTHS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, mouth=m, mustache=sp["kumis"], yo=yo, turn=2); L[f"mulut_{m}_s"] = c
    c = Canvas()
    C.hair_and_head(c, sp, "front")
    if sp["hijab"]:
        c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: (d.ellipse(C.P(HX - C.HR - 40, C.HY + 10 - C.HR - 45, HX + C.HR + 40, C.HY + 10 + C.HR + 45), fill=255),
                                   d.polygon([(x * C.S, y * C.S) for x, y in [(230, 600), (770, 600), (800, 900), (200, 900)]], fill=255)),
                dx=70, dy=60, k=0.74, blur=16, light=0.15)
    else:
        c.shade(mask_fn=lambda d: d.ellipse(C.P(HX - C.HR, C.HY - C.HR, HX + C.HR, C.HY + C.HR), fill=255), dx=75, dy=70, k=0.72, blur=18, light=0.15)
    C.hat(c, sp)
    L["kepala"] = c
    c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, blush=True, glasses=sp["kacamata"], yo=yo, detail={"kulit": sp["kulit"], "tua": sp["tua"], "skin_mask": C.skin_mask(sp, "front")}); L["wajah"] = c
    for e in EYES:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, eyes=e, yo=yo); L[f"mata_{e}"] = c
    for b in BROWS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, brows=b, yo=yo); L[f"alis_{b}"] = c
    for m in MOUTHS:
        c = Canvas(); _face_part(c, mouth=m, mustache=sp["kumis"], yo=yo)
        if m == "whistle":
            c.rrect(470, 600, 555, 640, 12, (225, 225, 230, 255))
            c.ellipse(553, 620, 22, 26, (225, 225, 230, 255), ow=9)
        L[f"mulut_{m}"] = c
    for k in (0, 1):
        c = Canvas()
        for r in (70, 115, 160):
            c.arc(560, 620, r + 18 * k, r + 18 * k, -35, 35, 12, (255, 235, 90, 255))
        L[f"fx_peluit_{k}"] = c
    L["belakang"] = C.draw_human(sp, {"view": "back"})
    L["belakang_sit"] = C.draw_human(sp, {"view": "back", "legs": "sitback", "floor": SIT_FLOOR})
    for l in "ABCD":
        L[f"belakang_walk{l}"] = C.draw_human(sp, {"view": "back", "legs": "walk" + l})
    return L


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pose lengan
def arm_angle(side, target):
    """sudut putar (derajat, konvensi sumbu Y Blender) agar ujung tangan mengarah ke titik kanvas target"""
    sx, sy = SHOULDER[side]
    v0 = (side * HAND[0], -HAND[1])                       # (x, z) dengan z ke atas
    t = (target[0] - sx, -(target[1] - sy))
    a = math.atan2(t[1], t[0]) - math.atan2(v0[1], v0[0])
    return -math.degrees(math.atan2(math.sin(a), math.cos(a)))


def out(side, deg):
    """lengan diangkat 'deg' derajat ke arah luar badan"""
    return -side * deg


def ik(side, target, shoulder=None):
    """IK dua ruas di kanvas -> (sudut_bahu, tekuk_siku) dalam konvensi Blender (derajat, + = searah jarum jam).
    Siku dipilih yang mengarah ke luar badan. Target di luar jangkauan -> lengan lurus menunjuk target."""
    sx, sy = shoulder or SHOULDER[side]
    e0 = (side * ELBOW[0], -ELBOW[1]); h0 = (side * HAND[0], -HAND[1])                  # (x, z) z ke atas
    f0 = (h0[0] - e0[0], h0[1] - e0[1])
    L1, L2 = math.hypot(*e0), math.hypot(*f0)
    tx, tz = target[0] - sx, -(target[1] - sy)
    d = max(1e-6, min(math.hypot(tx, tz), L1 + L2 - 1e-3))
    base = math.atan2(tz, tx)
    cos_a = max(-1, min(1, (L1 * L1 + d * d - L2 * L2) / (2 * L1 * d)))
    best = None
    for sgn in (1, -1):
        a1 = base + sgn * math.acos(cos_a)
        ex, ez = L1 * math.cos(a1), L1 * math.sin(a1)
        score = side * ex - 0.3 * ez                                                     # ke luar & ke bawah
        if best is None or score > best[0]:
            best = (score, a1, ex, ez)
    _, a1, ex, ez = best
    a2 = math.atan2(tz - ez, tx - ex) if math.hypot(tx, tz) > 1e-6 else a1
    r1 = a1 - math.atan2(e0[1], e0[0])
    r2 = (a2 - a1) - (math.atan2(f0[1], f0[0]) - math.atan2(e0[1], e0[0]))
    wrap = lambda a: math.atan2(math.sin(a), math.cos(a))
    return [-math.degrees(wrap(r1)), -math.degrees(wrap(r2))]


def pose_table(sp):
    """pose lengan: [sudut_bahu, tekuk_siku]"""
    yo = 30 if sp["hijab"] else 0
    mouth = (500, 600 + yo)
    T = {}
    for side in (-1, 1):
        T[side] = {"down": [out(side, 3), 0], "point": [out(side, 95), 0], "angkat": [out(side, 70), 0],
                   "hip": ik(side, (500 + side * 120, 975)), "wave": ik(side, (500 + side * 250, 470)),
                   "up": ik(side, (500 + side * 10, 615 + yo)), "mulut": ik(side, (500 + side * 10, 625 + yo)),
                   "pegang": ik(side, (500 + side * 55, 905)), "dada": ik(side, (500 + side * 20, 830)),
                   "masak": ik(side, (500 + side * 170, 800)),
                   # gestur dialog (Phase 4C)
                   "terbuka": ik(side, (500 + side * 175, 900)), "angkat_kecil": ik(side, (500 + side * 120, 815))}
    return {str(k): v for k, v in T.items()}


def decode_variants(sp):
    """nama varian lama -> keadaan rig"""
    D = {}
    for v, o in C.human_variants(sp).items():
        if o.get("view") == "back":
            D[v] = {"view": "back", "sprite": "belakang" + ("_" + o["legs"] if o.get("legs") else "")}
            continue
        eyes, brows, mouth = EXPR[o.get("expr", "neutral")]
        D[v] = {"view": "front", "eyes": "open" if eyes == "look" else eyes, "look": 1 if eyes == "look" else 0,
                "brows": brows, "mouth": mouth, "legs": o.get("legs", "stand"),
                "left": o.get("left", "down"), "right": o.get("right", sp["tangan"]),
                "fx": o.get("fx") if v.startswith("whistle") else None}
    return D


RES = C.S                            # simpan lapisan pada resolusi gambar asli (3x kanvas): tajam sampai close-up
SIL = {"kepala": 6, "kepala_q": 6, "kepala_s": 6, "badan": 5, "badan_q": 5, "badan_s": 5, "belakang": 6, "kaki": 4, "lutut": 4}   # tebal tambahan siluet (px kanvas)


def _silhouette(im, px):
    """garis luar siluet lebih tebal dari garis dalam (hierarki ketebalan garis): alfa dilebarkan, diisi tinta, di bawah lapisan"""
    from PIL import ImageFilter
    bb = im.getchannel("A").getbbox()
    if not bb:
        return im
    r = max(1, int(px * C.S))
    pad = r * 3
    box = (max(0, bb[0] - pad), max(0, bb[1] - pad), min(im.width, bb[2] + pad), min(im.height, bb[3] + pad))
    a = im.crop(box).getchannel("A")
    a = a.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(r * 0.55)).point(lambda v: 255 if v > 6 else v * 40)   # dilasi ~r (blur + ambang)
    a = a.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(C.S * 0.5))
    ink = C.Image.new("RGBA", im.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    blk = C.Image.new("RGBA", a.size, (*C.INK[:3], 255)); blk.putalpha(a)
    ink.paste(blk, box[:2])
    return C.Image.alpha_composite(ink, im)


def write(sp, out_dir):
    """Tiap lapisan disimpan resolusi tinggi (RES x kanvas) dan dipotong sesuai isinya; 'crops' = kotak (kanvas px)."""
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for old in out_dir.glob("*.png"):
        old.unlink()
    layers = draw_layers(sp)
    crops = {}
    for n, c in layers.items():
        big = c.im if RES == C.S else c.im.resize((C.W * RES, C.H * RES), C.Image.LANCZOS)
        sil = SIL.get(n) or next((v for k, v in SIL.items() if n.startswith(k + "_")), 0)
        if sil:
            big = _silhouette(big, sil)
        bb = big.getchannel("A").getbbox() or (0, 0, 2, 2)
        pad = 4 * RES
        x0, y0 = max(0, bb[0] - pad) // RES * RES, max(0, bb[1] - pad) // RES * RES
        x1, y1 = min(big.width, bb[2] + pad), min(big.height, bb[3] + pad)
        x1, y1 = x0 + -(-(x1 - x0) // RES) * RES, y0 + -(-(y1 - y0) // RES) * RES
        big.crop((x0, y0, x1, y1)).save(out_dir / f"{n}.png", optimize=False, compress_level=3)
        crops[n] = [x0 / RES, y0 / RES, x1 / RES, y1 / RES]
    meta = {"canvas": [C.W, C.H], "res": RES, "crops": crops, "elbow": ELBOW, "arm2": True, "turn_px": TURN_PX,
            "shoulder_q": {str(k): v for k, v in SHOULDER_Q.items()}, "shoulder_s": {str(k): v for k, v in SHOULDER_S.items()}, "shoulder": {str(k): v for k, v in SHOULDER.items()}, "neck": NECK, "hand": HAND,
            "wrist": WRIST, "grip_hand": {k: list(v) for k, v in GRIP_HAND.items()},
            "look_px": LOOK_PX, "profile_px": C.PROFILE_EYE_X, "profile_mouth_px": C.PROFILE_MOUTH_X, "mouth": [MOUTH[0], MOUTH[1] + (30 if sp["hijab"] else 0)],
            "eye": [EYE[0], EYE[1] + (30 if sp["hijab"] else 0)], "sit_drop": SIT_DROP, "seat_y": SEAT_Y, "foot_y": FOOT_Y, "poses": pose_table(sp), "variants": decode_variants(sp), "layers": sorted(layers)}
    (out_dir / "rig.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1))


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/schedule.py
# sha256: 800bacb23e0dc9d591325692857452704c4dbf45b748b4e082b79fee4d9b2fb8
==============================================================================================================
"""Penjadwalan aksi (Phase 4B-B6 + 4C). Murni Python (dipakai plan.py; hasilnya dibaca bl_rig & bl_interact).

Prinsip: perpindahan benda TIDAK boleh disembunyikan potongan kamera. Fase reach / grip / lift / carry / place /
release / retract dijadwalkan agar seluruhnya terlihat di satu shot (dengan jarak 'margin' frame dari potongan).
Bila fase menabrak potongan, digeser lebih awal (bila shot sebelumnya cukup panjang) atau sesudah potongan.

Siklus makan: panjang tiap suapan bervariasi (+-VAR, ber-seed, reproducible) dan disesuaikan agar suapan terakhir
selesai tepat sebelum fase 'taruh sendok'."""
import random

EAT_BASE = 48
EAT_VAR = 0.15
PLACE = 14          # sendok/benda turun ke tempatnya (frame)
RETRACT = 10        # lengan kembali
MARGIN = 4          # jarak minimum fase ke potongan kamera
REACH = 0.30        # jangkauan horizontal tangan dari pusat badan, x tinggi tokoh (rig 2D: bahu->tangan ~0.16 H + lebar bahu)


def shot_bounds(cuts, f, end):
    """(awal, akhir) shot yang memuat frame f; cuts = frame awal tiap shot"""
    cs = sorted(set(cuts) | {1})
    a = max(c for c in cs if c <= f)
    nxt = [c for c in cs if c > f]
    return a, (nxt[0] - 1 if nxt else end)


def place_window(f_end, dur, cuts, end, margin=MARGIN):
    """fase berdurasi 'dur' yang IDEALNYA selesai di f_end. -> (f0, f1, catatan). Tidak memotong / menyentuh potongan."""
    f0 = f_end - dur
    s0, s1 = shot_bounds(cuts, max(1, f0), end)
    if f0 >= s0 + margin and f_end <= s1 + 1 - margin:
        return f0, f_end, "tetap"
    # geser lebih awal di shot yang memuat f0
    nf1 = min(f_end, s1 + 1 - margin)
    nf0 = nf1 - dur
    if nf0 >= s0 + margin:
        return nf0, nf1, f"digeser {nf1 - f_end:+d} f (sebelum potongan f{s1 + 1})"
    # tidak muat: setelah potongan berikutnya
    nf0 = s1 + 1 + margin
    return nf0, nf0 + dur, f"digeser {nf0 + dur - f_end:+d} f (sesudah potongan f{s1 + 1})"


def eat_cycles(f0, f_end, seed, base=EAT_BASE, var=EAT_VAR):
    """-> [[awal, panjang], ...] menutup [f0, f_end) persis; panjang bervariasi +-var (ber-seed)"""
    span = max(1, f_end - f0)
    rnd = random.Random(seed)
    n = max(1, round(span / base))
    raw = [base * (1 + rnd.uniform(-var, var)) for _ in range(n)]
    k = span / sum(raw)
    L = [max(24, int(round(x * k))) for x in raw]
    L[-1] = span - sum(L[:-1])
    if L[-1] < 24 and len(L) > 1:                     # gabung sisa pendek ke siklus sebelumnya
        L[-2] += L[-1]; L.pop()
    out, c = [], f0
    for x in L:
        out.append([c, x]); c += x
    return out


CONTACT_K = (18, 23)          # fase 'kontak' pada templat 48 frame (sama dengan actions.EAT_PHASES)


def contact_in(cycles, a, b):
    """True bila ada frame fase kontak di [a, b]"""
    for c0, L in cycles:
        for f in range(max(a, c0), min(b, c0 + L - 1) + 1):
            k = (f - c0) * EAT_BASE / L
            if CONTACT_K[0] <= k < CONTACT_K[1]:
                return True
    return False


def eat_cycles_visible(f0, f_end, seed, windows, margin=6):
    """Phase 4.2: siklus makan sadar-shot. windows = [(a, b)] rentang shot yang MEMBINGKAI pemakan; bila sebuah shot
    tidak memuat fase kontak (suapan tak terlihat di shot makan), satu siklus dijangkarkan agar kontaknya jatuh di
    tengah shot. Sisa siklus tetap bervariasi (ber-seed). -> (siklus, catatan)"""
    cyc = eat_cycles(f0, f_end, seed)
    breaks, notes = [], []
    for a, b in sorted(windows):
        a2, b2 = max(a + margin, f0), min(b - margin, f_end - 1)
        if b2 - a2 < 12 or contact_in(cyc, a2, b2):
            continue
        t = (a2 + b2) // 2
        cs = int(round(t - (CONTACT_K[0] + CONTACT_K[1]) / 2))   # awal siklus ~48 frame -> kontak di t
        if cs > f_end - EAT_BASE:
            # tidak ada ruang untuk siklus penuh sebelum f_end: siklus TERAKHIR dipendekkan (>= 32 frame) agar kontaknya
            # jatuh sedini mungkin di dalam shot
            km = (CONTACT_K[0] + CONTACT_K[1]) / 2 / EAT_BASE
            for t2 in range(a2 + 2, b2 + 1):
                c2 = int(round((t2 - km * f_end) / (1 - km)))
                if f_end - c2 >= 32 and contact_in([[c2, f_end - c2]], a2, b2):
                    cs = c2
                    break
        if f0 + 24 <= cs <= f_end - 32 and all(abs(cs - x) >= 24 for x in breaks):
            breaks.append(cs)
            pts = [f0] + sorted(breaks) + [f_end]
            cyc = []
            for j, (u, v) in enumerate(zip(pts, pts[1:])):
                cyc += eat_cycles(u, v, seed + 17 * j)
            notes.append(f"kontak dijangkarkan ke shot f{a}-{b} (siklus mulai f{cs})")
        else:
            notes.append(f"shot f{a}-{b}: tidak ada ruang untuk menjangkarkan kontak")
    return cyc, notes


def cycle_at(f, cycles):
    """-> (k48, panjang): fase siklus dinormalisasi ke templat 48 frame, atau None di luar siklus"""
    for c0, L in cycles:
        if c0 <= f < c0 + L:
            return (f - c0) * EAT_BASE / L, L
    return None


def fit_phase(f0, dur, cuts, end, margin=MARGIN):
    """fase [f0, f0+dur] yang dimulai di f0 (waktu dari cerita). Bila menabrak potongan: mulai sesudah potongan bila
    potongan dekat awal fase, selain itu selesai sebelum potongan. -> (f0 baru, catatan)"""
    cs = sorted(c for c in set(cuts) if c > 1)
    hit = [c for c in cs if f0 - margin < c <= f0 + dur + margin]
    if not hit:
        return f0, "tetap"
    c = hit[0]
    if c - f0 < dur / 2 or c - dur - margin < 1:
        nf = c + margin
        return nf, f"digeser {nf - f0:+d} f (sesudah potongan f{c})"
    nf = c - margin - dur
    prev = max([x for x in cs if x <= nf] or [1])
    if nf < prev + margin:
        nf = c + margin
        return nf, f"digeser {nf - f0:+d} f (sesudah potongan f{c})"
    return nf, f"digeser {nf - f0:+d} f (sebelum potongan f{c})"


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/seats.py
# sha256: 5a0b2fb2a4d07a5cae71531db8e8d4bf756b5f52f57152d2a8025e4889339611
==============================================================================================================
"""Jangkar tempat duduk dunia (Phase 4B-B5). Murni Python: dipakai plan.py (posisi tokoh) DAN kit.py (jangkar di
Blender), sehingga geometri dudukan hanya didefinisikan sekali.

Tiap jenis properti tempat duduk: daftar slot (x, y lokal properti), tinggi permukaan dudukan (m), arah hadap
orang yang duduk (vektor lokal; None = bebas/ikut 'hadap' tokoh), dan setengah lebar dudukan (m; untuk presentasi).
Nama jangkar: '<nama_properti>.duduk' (slot pertama) atau '<nama_properti>.duduk<k>'."""
import math

SEATS = {
    "dingklik": {"slots": [(0.0, 0.0)], "h": lambda p: float(p.get("tinggi", 0.26)), "maju": None, "lebar": 0.17},
    "bangku":   {"slots": [(0.0, -0.45), (0.0, 0.0), (0.0, 0.45)], "h": lambda p: 0.475, "maju": None, "lebar": 0.2,
                 "kotak": (0.2, 0.75)},
    "kursi":    {"slots": [(0.0, 0.0)], "h": lambda p: 0.475, "maju": (-1.0, 0.0), "lebar": 0.23},
    "sofa":     {"slots": [(-0.05, -0.55), (-0.05, 0.0), (-0.05, 0.55)], "h": lambda p: 0.5, "maju": (-1.0, 0.0), "lebar": 0.42},
    "motor":    {"slots": [(0.0, -0.25)], "h": lambda p: 0.89, "maju": (0.0, 1.0), "lebar": 0.15},
}


def anchors_for(p, floor_z=0.0):
    """properti story (dict: jenis, posisi, arah, nama) -> [{name, pos(x,y,z), maju(xy dunia)|None, lebar, tipe}]"""
    kind = p.get("jenis")
    spec = SEATS.get(kind)
    if not spec:
        return []
    x, y = p["posisi"][:2]
    rot = math.radians(p.get("arah", 0))
    c, s = math.cos(rot), math.sin(rot)
    nm = p.get("nama", kind)
    h = spec["h"](p)
    out = []
    for k, (lx, ly) in enumerate(spec["slots"]):
        wx, wy = x + lx * c - ly * s, y + lx * s + ly * c
        fw = None
        if spec["maju"]:
            fx, fy = spec["maju"]
            fw = (fx * c - fy * s, fx * s + fy * c)
        rec = {"name": f"{nm}.duduk" + (str(k) if k else ""), "pos": (wx, wy, floor_z + h), "maju": fw,
               "lebar": spec["lebar"], "tipe": kind, "tinggi": h}
        if spec.get("kotak"):                       # Phase 4.2-D: setengah ukuran jejak dudukan (lokal x, y) relatif SLOT
            hx, hy = spec["kotak"]
            rec["setengah"] = (hx + abs(lx), hy + abs(ly))      # slot di ujung bangku: jejak terjauh = setengah + offset
            rec["arah"] = float(p.get("arah", 0))
        out.append(rec)
    return out


def find(props, name, floor_fn=None):
    for p in props:
        for a in anchors_for(p, floor_fn(p["posisi"][0]) if floor_fn else 0.0):
            if a["name"] == name:
                return a
    return None

"""Lapisan waktu & cuaca — berlaku untuk semua lokasi.
waktu: pagi | siang | senja | malam      cuaca: cerah | mendung | hujan | habis_hujan | kabut
preset cahaya (latar.cahaya) menimpa pilihan otomatis: lihat PRESETS.

                         sky_s=1.2, amb=0.85, fog=0.0035, fog_c=(1.0, 0.65, 0.5), lamps=True),
    # malam: ambient biru dingin + lampu praktis hangat; bulan sebagai cahaya pinggir (rim) dari belakang
    "malam_cerah":  dict(top=(0.02, 0.035, 0.08), hor=(0.10, 0.045, 0.16), sun=(0.5, 0.62, 1.0), sun_s=0.45, elev=35, azim=170,
                         sky_s=2.5, amb=0.75, fog=0.004, fog_c=(0.7, 0.8, 1.0), lamps=True),

                         sky_s=2.0, amb=0.8, fog=0.012, fog_c=(0.6, 0.7, 0.9), lamps=True),

                         sky_s=1.5, amb=0.4, fog=0.001, fog_c=(1.0, 0.85, 0.7), lamps=True, lamp_color=(1.0, 0.75, 0.48)),
    "dalam_sejuk":  dict(top=(0.3, 0.45, 0.7), hor=(0.7, 0.8, 0.95), sun=(0.9, 0.95, 1.0), sun_s=2.0, elev=45, azim=-30,

TOKOH_LOOK = {   # ambien = terang minimum (RGB, bertint suasana); gain = kepekaan thd lampu; batas = terang maksimum
    "pagi_cerah":    {"ambien": (0.62, 0.6, 0.58), "gain": 1.2, "batas": 1.0, "tint_lampu": 0.35},

}


def preset_name(waktu, cuaca, interior=False):
    if interior and waktu in ("malam", "senja"):
        return "dalam_hangat"
    if waktu == "malam":
        return "malam_hujan" if cuaca in ("hujan", "habis_hujan", "mendung", "kabut") else "malam_cerah"
    if waktu == "senja":
        return "senja_emas"
    if cuaca in ("mendung", "hujan", "habis_hujan"):
        return "siang_mendung"
    return "pagi_cerah" if waktu == "pagi" else "siang_cerah"


def mix(a, b, t):
    return tuple(x + (y - x) * t for x, y in zip(a, b))


def build(cfg, interior=False):
    waktu = cfg.get("waktu", "malam")
    cuaca = cfg.get("cuaca", "cerah")
    name = cfg.get("cahaya") or preset_name(waktu, cuaca, interior)
    if name not in PRESETS:
        raise SystemExit(f"preset cahaya '{name}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(PRESETS)}")
    pr = dict(PRESETS[name])
    top, hor, sun_c, sun_s, elev, azim, sky_s, fog, fog_c = (pr[k] for k in ("top", "hor", "sun", "sun_s", "elev", "azim", "sky_s", "fog", "fog_c"))
    night = pr["lamps"] or waktu in ("malam", "senja")
    grey = (0.42, 0.44, 0.48) if waktu != "malam" else (0.03, 0.035, 0.045)
    if cuaca == "hujan" and waktu != "malam":
        fog *= 2.0
    if cuaca == "kabut":
        top, hor = mix(top, grey, 0.5), mix(hor, grey, 0.5)
        fog = max(fog * 6, 0.02); sun_s *= 0.5
    if cuaca == "habis_hujan":                                  # udara lembap: sedikit berkabut
        fog = max(fog, 0.008)
    if interior:
        fog *= 0.3
    if "kabut_tebal" in cfg:
        fog = float(cfg["kabut_tebal"])
    if "kabut" in cfg and isinstance(cfg["kabut"], (int, float)):
        fog = float(cfg["kabut"])
    if cfg.get("warna_langit"):
        from palette import to_rgb
        top = B.srgb(to_rgb(cfg["warna_langit"]))
    if cfg.get("warna_cakrawala"):
        from palette import to_rgb
        hor = B.srgb(to_rgb(cfg["warna_cakrawala"]))

    # --- langit gradasi (+ bintang malam)
    world = bpy.data.worlds.new("langit"); B.scene().world = world; world.use_nodes = True
    wn = world.node_tree.nodes; wl = world.node_tree.links.new
    bg = wn["Background"]
    tc = wn.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
    sep = wn.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ"); wl(tc.outputs["Generated"], sep.inputs[0])
    ramp = wn.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position, ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = 0.0, (*hor, 1)
    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position, ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = 0.45, (*top, 1)
    wl(sep.outputs["Z"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
    out = ramp.outputs["Color"]
    if waktu == "malam" and cuaca == "cerah" and cfg.get("bintang", True):
        vor = wn.new("ShaderNodeTexVoronoi"); vor.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 260
        wl(tc.outputs["Generated"], vor.inputs["Vector"])
        st = wn.new("ShaderNodeMath"); st.operation = "LESS_THAN"; st.inputs[1].default_value = 0.035
        wl(vor.outputs["Distance"], st.inputs[0])
        sz = wn.new("ShaderNodeMath"); sz.operation = "MULTIPLY"; wl(st.outputs[0], sz.inputs[0]); wl(sep.outputs["Z"], sz.inputs[1])
        sc = wn.new("ShaderNodeMath"); sc.operation = "MULTIPLY"; sc.inputs[1].default_value = 3.0; wl(sz.outputs[0], sc.inputs[0])
        addn = wn.new("ShaderNodeMixRGB"); addn.blend_type = "ADD"; addn.inputs[0].default_value = 1
        wl(out, addn.inputs[1]); wl(sc.outputs[0], addn.inputs[2]); out = addn.outputs[0]
    wl(out, bg.inputs["Color"]); bg.inputs["Strength"].default_value = sky_s
    # langit terlihat terang di kamera, tapi cahaya sekitarnya lebih redup -> bayangan lebih dalam & kontras
    amb = wn.new("ShaderNodeBackground"); wl(out, amb.inputs["Color"])
    amb.inputs["Strength"].default_value = float(cfg.get("cahaya_langit", pr["amb"]))
    lp = wn.new("ShaderNodeLightPath"); mx = wn.new("ShaderNodeMixShader")
    wl(lp.outputs["Is Camera Ray"], mx.inputs[0]); wl(amb.outputs[0], mx.inputs[1]); wl(bg.outputs[0], mx.inputs[2])
    wl(mx.outputs[0], wn["World Output"].inputs["Surface"])
    # kabut: kotak volume besar (volume di 'world' menyerap seluruh cahaya matahari di Eevee Next)
    if fog > 0:
        fm = bpy.data.materials.new("kabut"); fm.use_nodes = True
        fn = fm.node_tree.nodes; fn.remove(fn["Principled BSDF"])
        vol = fn.new("ShaderNodeVolumeScatter")
        vol.inputs["Density"].default_value = fog; vol.inputs["Anisotropy"].default_value = 0.5
        vol.inputs["Color"].default_value = (*fog_c, 1)
        fm.node_tree.links.new(vol.outputs[0], fn["Material Output"].inputs["Volume"])
        size, center = ((12, 22, 3.2), (0, -3.5, 1.6)) if interior else ((160, 240, 50), (0, 50, 24))
        B.box("kabut", center, size, fm)

    # --- matahari / bulan
    rot = (math.radians(90 - elev), 0, math.radians(azim))
    sun = B.light("SUN", (0, 0, 30), sun_s, sun_c, rot=rot)
    sun.data.angle = math.radians(1.5 if cuaca == "cerah" else 15)
    d = Vector((0, 0, -1)); d.rotate(sun.rotation_euler)            # arah cahaya
    if cuaca == "cerah" and not interior:
        if waktu == "malam" and cfg.get("bulan", True):
            B.ball("bulan", (22, 150, 52), 5.5, B.glow("bulan", (1.0, 0.95, 0.8), 8), 3)
        elif waktu in ("pagi", "senja"):
            p = -d * 260 + Vector((0, 0, 8))
            B.ball("matahari", p, 9, B.glow("matahari", sun_c, 25), 3)
    if waktu != "malam" and not interior and cfg.get("awan", True):
        clouds(waktu, cuaca)
    env = {"waktu": waktu, "cuaca": cuaca, "malam": night, "gelap": waktu == "malam", "preset": name,
           "warna_lampu": pr.get("lamp_color", (1.0, 0.7, 0.4)),
           "basah": float(cfg.get("basah", 0.95 if cuaca == "hujan" else 0.85 if cuaca == "habis_hujan" else
                                  {"malam": 0.5, "senja": 0.25, "pagi": 0.1}.get(waktu, 0.0) + (0.25 if cuaca in ("mendung", "kabut") else 0))),
           "hujan": cuaca == "hujan", "emisi_tokoh": 0.4 if waktu == "malam" else (0.3 if waktu == "senja" else 0.12)}
    # shader tokoh (bl_utils.char_mat): lantai ambien bertint, kepekaan cahaya, plafon terang
    look = dict(TOKOH_LOOK.get(name, TOKOH_LOOK["siang_cerah"]))
    look.update(cfg.get("tokoh_cahaya", {}) or {})
    look["ambien"] = tuple(look["ambien"])
    env["tokoh_look"] = look
    B.scene().view_settings.exposure = {"pagi": -0.2, "siang": -0.4, "senja": 0.0, "malam": 0.0}[waktu]
    return env


def clouds(waktu, cuaca):
    rnd = random.Random(5)
    col = {"pagi": (1.0, 0.9, 0.85), "siang": (1, 1, 1), "senja": (1.0, 0.6, 0.5)}[waktu]
    if cuaca != "cerah":
        col = (0.6, 0.62, 0.66)
    m = B.mat("awan", col, 1.0, col, 0.6 if waktu != "senja" else 1.2)
    for i in range(14 if cuaca == "cerah" else 26):
        cx, cy, cz = rnd.uniform(-120, 120), rnd.uniform(110, 260), rnd.uniform(35, 75)
        for k in range(4):
            o = B.ball("awan", (cx + rnd.uniform(-10, 10), cy + rnd.uniform(-4, 4), cz + rnd.uniform(-2, 3)), rnd.uniform(5, 10), m, 2)
            o.scale = (1.6, 1.0, 0.45)


def rain(area, end, dens=1.0):
    """hujan: partikel garis tipis jatuh di area (x0, x1, y0, y1)"""
    x0, x1, y0, y1 = area
    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(vertices=4, radius=0.006, depth=0.8, location=(0, 0, -100))   # tegak (sumbu Z)
    drop = bpy.context.object; drop.name = "tetes_hujan"
    drop.data.materials.append(B.mat("air_hujan", (0.75, 0.82, 0.9), 0.1, (0.7, 0.8, 0.95), 0.7))
    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=1, location=((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, 9))
    em = bpy.context.object; em.name = "awan_hujan"; em.scale = (x1 - x0, y1 - y0, 1)
    bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(scale=True)
    em.show_instancer_for_render = False; em.show_instancer_for_viewport = False
    ps = em.modifiers.new("hujan", "PARTICLE_SYSTEM").particle_system
    s = ps.settings
    area_m2 = (x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0)
    s.frame_start, s.frame_end, s.lifetime = -40, end, 22          # 12 m/s dari ketinggian 9 m ~ 22 frame
    s.count = int(min(400000, area_m2 * float(dens) * 12 * (end + 40) / 22))
    s.emit_from = "FACE"; s.normal_factor = -12.0
    s.effector_weights.gravity = 0.0
    s.render_type = "OBJECT"; s.instance_object = drop
    s.particle_size = 1.0; s.size_random = 0.4
    s.use_rotations = False
    return em

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/dalam_rumah.py
# sha256: a5e650dd09a4bedb57adbb42c4e0cfc1f9aa94a011643bee2d41d84cbf9100f0

"""Lokasi 'dalam_rumah': ruangan dengan jendela (pemandangan luar ikut waktu & cuaca), pintu,
hiasan dinding, dan perabot sesuai 'isi': ruang_tamu | dapur | kamar | kosong.
Koordinat: lantai x=-4.2..4.2, y=-12..5 (dinding belakang di y=5, jendela di dinding itu), tinggi 3 m.
Kamera otomatis dibatasi di dalam ruangan."""
import math
import random

import bl_utils as B
import kit
from palette import to_rgb

W, YB, YF, H = 4.2, 5.0, -12.0, 3.0
WIN_X, WIN_W, WIN_Z0, WIN_Z1 = 1.4, 1.8, 0.9, 2.2

PRESETS = {
    "ruang_tamu": [{"jenis": "sofa", "posisi": [-3.6, 2.6], "arah": 180},
                   {"jenis": "meja", "posisi": [-2.3, 2.6], "ukuran": [1.0, 0.6]},
                   {"jenis": "kursi", "posisi": [-2.3, 1.3], "arah": 90},
                   {"jenis": "tv", "posisi": [3.85, 1.5]},
                   {"jenis": "karpet", "posisi": [-1.5, 2.2]},
                   {"jenis": "pot_tanaman", "posisi": [3.6, 4.4]},
                   {"jenis": "lemari", "posisi": [3.85, -1.5]}],
    "dapur": [{"jenis": "kompor", "posisi": [-3.85, 3.0]},
              {"jenis": "rak", "posisi": [-3.9, 0.8]},
              {"jenis": "kulkas", "posisi": [3.75, 4.3]},
              {"jenis": "galon", "posisi": [3.9, 2.9]},
              {"jenis": "meja", "posisi": [0.8, 1.5], "ukuran": [1.6, 0.9]},
              {"jenis": "kursi", "posisi": [1.5, 1.1], "arah": 0},
              {"jenis": "kursi", "posisi": [1.5, 1.9], "arah": 0}],
    "kamar": [{"jenis": "kasur", "posisi": [-3.4, 3.8]},
              {"jenis": "lemari", "posisi": [3.85, 3.6]},
              {"jenis": "meja", "posisi": [3.7, 0.5], "ukuran": [1.2, 0.6], "isi": False},
              {"jenis": "lampu_meja", "posisi": [3.75, 0.8]},
              {"jenis": "kursi", "posisi": [3.1, 0.5], "arah": 180},
              {"jenis": "karpet", "posisi": [-1.0, 1.5], "warna": "biru_tua"}],
    "kosong": [],
}


# Phase 4.2-C: art direction interior rumah Indonesia. Palet terarah per gaya (bisa ditimpa warna_* di story.yaml).
GAYA = {
    "hangat": {"dinding": (232, 220, 192), "dinding_bawah": (176, 132, 92), "lis": (120, 76, 42), "kusen": (112, 70, 38),
               "plafon": (242, 238, 228), "gorden": (150, 46, 48), "vitrase": (214, 206, 186), "aksen": (60, 96, 74),
               "keramik": (214, 206, 190)},
    "sejuk": {"dinding": (206, 222, 214), "dinding_bawah": (122, 150, 140), "lis": (236, 236, 228), "kusen": (236, 236, 228),
              "plafon": (244, 244, 240), "gorden": (60, 92, 140), "vitrase": (246, 246, 244), "aksen": (190, 120, 60),
              "keramik": (224, 222, 216)},
}
WAINSCOT = 0.95                     # tinggi pelapis dinding bawah (m)


def _c(cfg, key, pal):
    return B.srgb(to_rgb(cfg.get("warna_" + key), pal[key]))


def _roster(name, cx, z, w, y, sx, m, mdark):
    """lubang angin (roster beton berpola) di atas pintu / jendela — khas rumah Indonesia"""
    n = max(3, int(w / 0.2))
    B.box(name + "_bingkai", (cx, y, z), (w + 0.08, 0.16, 0.26), m)
    for k in range(n):
        x = cx - w / 2 + (k + 0.5) * w / n
        B.box(name + "_lubang", (x, y + sx * 0.06, z), (w / n * 0.62, 0.05, 0.16), mdark, bevel=0)


def _wall_decor(cfg, pal, M, env, info, rnd):
    y = YB - 0.03
    wood = B.mat("bingkai_jati", B.srgb((96, 60, 32)), 0.45)
    # foto keluarga (bingkai besar) di atas sofa + dua foto kecil
    for i, (hx, hz, hw, hh) in enumerate([(-3.35, 1.85, 0.9, 0.62), (-2.6, 2.15, 0.34, 0.44), (-2.6, 1.6, 0.34, 0.34)]):
        B.box("bingkai_foto", (hx, y, hz), (hw, 0.035, hh), wood)
        B.box("pasfoto", (hx, y - 0.02, hz), (hw - 0.1, 0.006, hh - 0.1), B.mat(f"foto_rumah{i}", kit.PASTEL[(i * 3 + 1) % 8], 0.55))
        for j in range(2 if i == 0 else 1):                                    # siluet orang di foto (kesan foto keluarga)
            B.ball("figur_foto", (hx - 0.12 + j * 0.24 if i == 0 else hx, y - 0.025, hz + 0.05), 0.07 if i == 0 else 0.06,
                   B.mat("figur_foto", (0.22, 0.17, 0.14), 0.6))
    # kalender dinding: kepala merah + kisi tanggal
    kx, kz = 0.05, 1.75
    B.box("kalender", (kx, y, kz), (0.42, 0.012, 0.6), B.mat("kertas_kalender", (0.96, 0.95, 0.92), 0.8))
    B.box("kalender_kepala", (kx, y - 0.008, kz + 0.2), (0.42, 0.006, 0.2), B.mat("kalender_merah", (0.72, 0.1, 0.1), 0.6))
    for r_ in range(4):
        for c_ in range(6):
            B.box("tanggal", (kx - 0.17 + c_ * 0.068, y - 0.008, kz + 0.04 - r_ * 0.07), (0.04, 0.004, 0.035),
                  B.mat("tinta_kalender", (0.75, 0.12, 0.1) if c_ == 0 else (0.18, 0.18, 0.2), 0.7), bevel=0)
    # saklar & stop kontak dekat pintu
    plate = B.mat("saklar", (0.93, 0.92, 0.88), 0.35)
    B.box("saklar", (-1.45, y, 1.3), (0.08, 0.015, 0.12), plate)
    B.box("stop_kontak", (-1.45, y, 0.35), (0.08, 0.015, 0.08), plate)
    B.box("kabel_saklar", (-1.45, y + 0.005, 2.2), (0.012, 0.01, 1.7), plate, bevel=0)


def _ceiling_fan(z, M):
    root = B.empty("kipas_plafon", (0.0, 1.2, z))
    P = dict(parent=root)
    B.cyl("batang_kipas", (0, 0, -0.18), 0.015, 0.36, M.chrome, **P)
    B.cyl("motor_kipas", (0, 0, -0.4), 0.11, 0.12, B.mat("kipas_putih", (0.92, 0.92, 0.9), 0.35), verts=20, **P)
    blade = B.mat("baling_kayu", B.srgb((120, 82, 50)), 0.5)
    for k in range(3):
        a = k * 2 * math.pi / 3
        B.box("baling", (math.cos(a) * 0.42, math.sin(a) * 0.42, -0.42), (0.6, 0.13, 0.012), blade,
              rot=(0, math.radians(6), a), **P)
    root.rotation_euler.z = 0.3
    return root


def build(cfg, env, M, avoid):
    rnd = random.Random(cfg.get("acak", 7))
    info = {"lamps": [], "steam": [], "bulbs": []}
    pal = GAYA.get(cfg.get("gaya_rumah", "hangat"), GAYA["hangat"])
    wall_c = B.srgb(to_rgb(cfg.get("warna_dinding"), pal["dinding"]))
    wall = kit.wall_mat("dinding_rumah", wall_c, grime=0.12, pbr=None)
    low_c = _c(cfg, "dinding_bawah", pal)
    wall_low = kit.wall_mat("dinding_bawah_rumah", low_c, grime=0.25, pbr=None)
    lis = B.mat("lis_dinding", _c(cfg, "lis", pal), 0.45)
    kusen = B.mat("kusen_jati", _c(cfg, "kusen", pal), 0.45)
    lantai = cfg.get("lantai", "keramik")
    if lantai == "kayu":
        floor = kit.ground_mat("lantai_kayu", (0.45, 0.28, 0.15), 0.0, var=0.1, bump=0.02, pattern="kayu")
    else:
        floor = kit.ground_mat("lantai_keramik", tuple(c / 255 for c in pal["keramik"]), 0.0, var=0.05, bump=0.01, pattern="keramik")
    for m in (floor,):
        m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"].inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.22
    cy = (YB + YF) / 2
    B.box("lantai", (0, cy, -0.05), (2 * W + 0.4, YB - YF, 0.1), floor)
    plaf = B.mat("plafon_gipsum", _c(cfg, "plafon", pal), 0.9)
    B.box("plafon", (0, cy, H + 0.05), (2 * W + 0.4, YB - YF, 0.1), plaf)
    # plafon gantung: bingkai turun di tengah ruang (kedalaman & garis) + lis plafon di pertemuan dinding
    B.box("plafon_turun", (0, 1.2, H - 0.06), (4.6, 4.2, 0.02), plaf, bevel=0)
    for (bx, by, bw, bd) in ((0, 1.2 + 2.1, 4.6, 0.06), (0, 1.2 - 2.1, 4.6, 0.06), (-2.3, 1.2, 0.06, 4.2), (2.3, 1.2, 0.06, 4.2)):
        B.box("tepi_plafon_turun", (bx, by, H - 0.1), (bw, bd, 0.1), plaf)
    cornice = B.mat("lis_plafon", B.srgb((248, 246, 240)), 0.6)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("lis_plafon", (sx * (W - 0.04), cy, H - 0.05), (0.08, YB - YF, 0.1), cornice)
    B.box("lis_plafon", (0, YB - 0.04, H - 0.05), (2 * W, 0.08, 0.1), cornice)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("dinding_samping", (sx * (W + 0.1), cy, H / 2), (0.2, YB - YF, H), wall)
        B.box("dinding_samping_bawah", (sx * (W - 0.005), cy, WAINSCOT / 2), (0.02, YB - YF, WAINSCOT), wall_low, bevel=0)
        B.box("lis_samping", (sx * (W - 0.02), cy, WAINSCOT), (0.04, YB - YF, 0.04), lis)
        B.box("plint", (sx * (W - 0.015), cy, 0.05), (0.03, YB - YF, 0.1), lis)
    B.box("dinding_depan", (0, YF - 0.1, H / 2), (2 * W + 0.4, 0.2, H), wall)
    # dinding belakang dengan lubang jendela + pelapis bawah + lis
    x0, x1 = WIN_X - WIN_W / 2, WIN_X + WIN_W / 2
    B.box("dinding_bl", ((-W + x0) / 2, YB + 0.1, H / 2), (x0 + W, 0.2, H), wall)
    B.box("dinding_br", ((W + x1) / 2, YB + 0.1, H / 2), (W - x1, 0.2, H), wall)
    B.box("dinding_bawah", (WIN_X, YB + 0.1, WIN_Z0 / 2), (WIN_W, 0.2, WIN_Z0), wall)
    B.box("dinding_atas", (WIN_X, YB + 0.1, (WIN_Z1 + H) / 2), (WIN_W, 0.2, H - WIN_Z1), wall)
    DX0, DX1 = -2.2 - 0.5, -2.2 + 0.5                                 # bukaan pintu (pelapis bawah tidak melewati pintu)
    for (a_, b_) in ((-W, DX0), (DX1, W)):
        B.box("pelapis_bawah", ((a_ + b_) / 2, YB - 0.005, WAINSCOT / 2), (b_ - a_, 0.02, WAINSCOT), wall_low, bevel=0)
        B.box("lis_belakang", ((a_ + b_) / 2, YB - 0.02, WAINSCOT), (b_ - a_, 0.04, 0.04), lis)
        B.box("plint_b", ((a_ + b_) / 2, YB - 0.015, 0.05), (b_ - a_, 0.03, 0.1), lis)
    # jendela: kusen jati, kaca, teralis besi, vitrase + gorden berlipit
    for (fx, fz, sx_, sz_) in ((WIN_X, WIN_Z0, WIN_W + 0.1, 0.08), (WIN_X, WIN_Z1, WIN_W + 0.1, 0.08), (x0, (WIN_Z0 + WIN_Z1) / 2, 0.08, WIN_Z1 - WIN_Z0),
                               (x1, (WIN_Z0 + WIN_Z1) / 2, 0.08, WIN_Z1 - WIN_Z0), (WIN_X, (WIN_Z0 + WIN_Z1) / 2, 0.06, WIN_Z1 - WIN_Z0),
                               (WIN_X, WIN_Z1 - 0.45, WIN_W, 0.05)):
        B.box("kusen", (fx, YB + 0.02, fz), (sx_, 0.12, sz_), kusen)
    B.box("ambang_jendela", (WIN_X, YB - 0.06, WIN_Z0 - 0.02), (WIN_W + 0.3, 0.14, 0.04), kusen)
    besi = B.mat("teralis", (0.06, 0.06, 0.07), 0.45, metal=0.6)
    for k in range(9):
        tx = x0 + 0.12 + k * (WIN_W - 0.24) / 8
        B.cyl("teralis", (tx, YB + 0.1, (WIN_Z0 + WIN_Z1) / 2), 0.008, WIN_Z1 - WIN_Z0, besi, verts=6)
    for tz in (WIN_Z0 + 0.35, WIN_Z1 - 0.35):
        B.box("teralis_datar", (WIN_X, YB + 0.1, tz), (WIN_W - 0.1, 0.012, 0.012), besi, bevel=0)
    curtain = B.mat("gorden", _c(cfg, "gorden", pal), 0.95)
    sheer = B.mat("vitrase", _c(cfg, "vitrase", pal), 0.95)
    B.cyl("rel_gorden", (WIN_X, YB - 0.14, WIN_Z1 + 0.32), 0.012, WIN_W + 1.3, M.chrome, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=8)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        for k in range(5):                                                      # lipatan gorden (silinder bergelombang)
            gx = WIN_X + sx * (WIN_W / 2 + 0.08 + k * 0.1)
            o = B.cyl("gorden", (gx, YB - 0.12 + (k % 2) * 0.03, (WIN_Z0 - 0.2 + WIN_Z1 + 0.3) / 2), 0.06,
                      WIN_Z1 + 0.5 - WIN_Z0 + 0.2, curtain, verts=10)
            B.noise(o, "rotation_euler", 1, 0.01, 50)
    for sx in (-1, 1):                                                          # vitrase tipis di kedua sisi (pemandangan tetap terlihat)
        B.box("vitrase", (WIN_X + sx * WIN_W * 0.3, YB - 0.05, (WIN_Z0 + WIN_Z1) / 2 + 0.1), (WIN_W * 0.3, 0.01, WIN_Z1 - WIN_Z0 - 0.1),
              sheer, bevel=0)
    _roster("roster_jendela", WIN_X, WIN_Z1 + 0.42, WIN_W - 0.2, YB + 0.05, -1, B.mat("roster", (0.9, 0.88, 0.82), 0.8),
            B.mat("roster_gelap", (0.08, 0.07, 0.06), 0.9))
    # pintu panel jati + kusen + roster
    B.box("pintu", (-2.2, YB - 0.02, 1.05), (0.9, 0.05, 2.1), kusen)
    for pz in (0.55, 1.5):
        B.box("panel_pintu", (-2.2, YB - 0.05, pz), (0.62, 0.02, 0.7 if pz > 1 else 0.6), B.mat("panel_jati", B.srgb((92, 56, 30)), 0.5))
    for (fx, fz, sx_, sz_) in ((-2.2, 2.14, 1.08, 0.08), (DX0 - 0.0, 1.07, 0.08, 2.14), (DX1, 1.07, 0.08, 2.14)):
        B.box("kusen_pintu", (fx, YB - 0.03, fz), (sx_, 0.1, sz_), kusen)
    B.ball("gagang", (-1.88, YB - 0.08, 1.0), 0.035, M.chrome)
    B.box("plat_gagang", (-1.88, YB - 0.055, 0.98), (0.05, 0.01, 0.18), M.chrome)
    _roster("roster_pintu", -2.2, 2.45, 0.9, YB + 0.05, -1, B.mat("roster", (0.9, 0.88, 0.82), 0.8),
            B.mat("roster_gelap", (0.08, 0.07, 0.06), 0.9))
    _wall_decor(cfg, pal, M, env, info, rnd)
    kit.build_prop({"jenis": "jam_dinding", "posisi": [-W + 0.02, 0.5], "tinggi": 2.3}, M, env, info)
    if cfg.get("kipas_plafon", True):
        _ceiling_fan(H, M)

    # pemandangan luar jendela
    out_floor = kit.ground_mat("halaman", (0.15, 0.35, 0.1), env["basah"] * 0.4)
    B.box("halaman", (0, YB + 12, -0.06), (40, 24, 0.1), out_floor)
    B.box("pagar_halaman", (0, YB + 3.2, 0.5), (14, 0.15, 1.0), B.mat("pagar_tembok", (0.85, 0.82, 0.74), 0.8))
    for i in range(5):
        kit.tree(M, rnd.uniform(-8, 10), rnd.uniform(YB + 5, YB + 14), rnd.uniform(1.0, 1.6), rnd)
    kit.house(M, env, rnd, -3, YB + 16, 6, 1, storeys=1)
    kit.house(M, env, rnd, 9, YB + 14, 5, -1, storeys=1)

    # perabot
    isi = cfg.get("isi", "ruang_tamu")
    for p in PRESETS.get(isi, []):
        if any(abs(p["posisi"][0] - a[0]) < 0.6 and abs(p["posisi"][1] - a[1]) < 0.6 for a in avoid):
            continue
        kit.build_prop(dict(p), M, env, info)
    # lampu gantung + cahaya pengisi
    kit.build_prop({"jenis": "lampu_gantung", "posisi": [1.6, 1.5], "tinggi": H}, M, env, info)
    if env["malam"]:
        info["bulbs"].append((1.6, 1.5, H - 0.7))
    B.light("AREA", (0, -2, H - 0.05), 400 if not env["gelap"] else 120, (1.0, 0.92, 0.82), rot=(0, 0, 0)).data.size = 4
    if not env["gelap"]:
        # cahaya matahari sore masuk lewat jendela: bidang terang hangat + bayangan teralis di lantai (motivasi kunci)
        B.light("AREA", (WIN_X, YB + 0.6, 1.6), 160, (1.0, 0.95, 0.85), rot=(math.radians(-90), 0, 0)).data.size = 1.6
        if cfg.get("sinar_jendela", True):
            sun = B.light("SUN", (WIN_X, YB + 3, 3.5), float(cfg.get("sinar_kuat", 2.2)), (1.0, 0.86, 0.66),
                          rot=(math.radians(-58), 0, math.radians(200)))
            sun.data.angle = math.radians(1.5)
    return info


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/desa_sawah.py
# sha256: 0102a5258a3263b5ef66e3ce9634af64eb8b02110185f9594d3243bc3303123d
==============================================================================================================
"""Lokasi 'desa_sawah': jalan tanah lurus di tengah sawah, pematang, pohon kelapa, gubuk,
tiang listrik, gunung di kejauhan.
Koordinat: jalan tanah x=-1.6..1.6, rumput tepi sampai ±2.4, sawah di luar itu, jalan sepanjang Y."""
import math
import random

import bpy

import bl_utils as B
import kit

Y0, Y1 = -40, 160


def rice_clump(color):
    """satu rumpun padi: beberapa helai daun tipis yang mekar ke segala arah"""
    verts, faces = [], []
    for k in range(7):
        a = k / 7 * math.tau
        px, py = -math.sin(a) * 0.012, math.cos(a) * 0.012          # lebar pangkal helai
        tip = (0.75 + 0.15 * math.sin(k * 3.7), math.cos(a) * 0.22, math.sin(a) * 0.22)
        i = len(verts)
        verts += [(0, px, py), (0, -px, -py), tip]
        faces.append((i, i + 1, i + 2))
    me = bpy.data.meshes.new("rumpun_padi"); me.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)
    o = bpy.data.objects.new("rumpun_padi", me); B.scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    o.location = (0, 0, -200)
    me.materials.append(B.mat("padi", color, 0.7))
    return o


def build(cfg, env, M, avoid):
    rnd = random.Random(cfg.get("acak", 7))
    info = {"lamps": [], "steam": [], "bulbs": [], "kunang": []}
    road = kit.ground_mat("jalan_tanah", (0.36, 0.19, 0.08), env["basah"], var=0.25, bump=0.2)
    grass = kit.ground_mat("rumput", (0.12, 0.32, 0.07), env["basah"] * 0.3, var=0.35)
    water = kit.ground_mat("air_sawah", (0.07, 0.16, 0.05), 0.6, scale=0.5, var=0.3, bump=0.02)
    mud = kit.ground_mat("pematang", (0.28, 0.22, 0.12), env["basah"] * 0.5, var=0.2)
    B.box("jalan_tanah", (0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.05), (3.2, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), road)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("rumput_tepi", (sx * 2.0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.06), (0.9, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), grass)
        B.box("sawah", (sx * 52, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.2), (100, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), water)
        for k in range(1, 8):                                      # pematang memanjang
            B.box("pematang", (sx * (2.45 + k * 9), (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.1), (0.4, Y1 - Y0, 0.2), mud)
        for y in range(Y0, Y1, 12):                                 # pematang melintang
            B.box("pematang", (sx * 40, y, -0.1), (75, 0.4, 0.2), mud)

    # padi: ribuan rumpun lewat partikel
    phase = cfg.get("padi", "hijau")
    pc = {"hijau": (0.2, 0.55, 0.08), "kuning": (0.75, 0.62, 0.12), "muda": (0.35, 0.7, 0.15)}.get(phase, (0.2, 0.55, 0.08))
    if phase != "tidak":
        clump = rice_clump(pc)
        for sx in (-1, 1):
            bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=1, location=(sx * 17, 35, -0.15))
            f = bpy.context.object; f.scale = (29, 110, 1); bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(scale=True)
            f.data.materials.append(water)
            f.hide_render = False
            ps = f.modifiers.new("padi", "PARTICLE_SYSTEM").particle_system
            s = ps.settings
            s.type = "HAIR"; s.count = int(cfg.get("kepadatan_padi", 45000)); s.hair_length = 0.5
            s.render_type = "OBJECT"; s.instance_object = clump
            s.particle_size = 1.0; s.size_random = 0.4
            s.use_rotations = True; s.rotation_mode = "NOR"; s.phase_factor_random = 2.0
            s.emit_from = "FACE"; s.use_emit_random = True
            f.show_instancer_for_render = True

    # pohon kelapa & pohon
    for i in range(int(cfg.get("jumlah_kelapa", 14))):
        sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1))
        x = sx * rnd.uniform(2.6, 3.4) if i < 6 else sx * rnd.uniform(6, 45)
        y = rnd.uniform(-10, 120)
        if any(abs(x - a[0]) < 1.5 and abs(y - a[1]) < 2 for a in avoid):
            continue
        kit.coconut(M, x, y, rnd.uniform(6, 9), rnd=rnd)
    for i in range(10):
        kit.tree(M, rnd.choice((-1, 1)) * rnd.uniform(30, 70), rnd.uniform(60, 160), rnd.uniform(1.5, 2.5), rnd)
    if cfg.get("gubuk", True):
        kit.build_prop({"jenis": "gubuk", "posisi": [-12, 22]}, M, env, info)
        kit.build_prop({"jenis": "orang_orangan", "posisi": [9, 12]}, M, env, info)
    if cfg.get("rumah_desa", True):
        for i in range(4):
            sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1))
            kit.house(M, env, rnd, sx * rnd.uniform(25, 40), rnd.uniform(70, 130), 6, -sx, storeys=1)
    if cfg.get("tiang_listrik", True):
        tops = [kit.power_pole(M, -2.1, y) for y in range(-10, Y1, 30)]
        for a, b in zip(tops, tops[1:]):
            for i in range(3):
                kit.cable(M, a[i], b[i], 1.2)
        if env["malam"]:                                             # lampu bohlam di tiang
            for t in tops:
                p = t[1].copy(); p.z -= 1.2; p.x += 0.5
                B.ball("bohlam_tiang", p, 0.08, M.bulb)
                B.light("POINT", p, 150, (1.0, 0.75, 0.45), 0.05)
                info["lamps"].append(tuple(p))
    kit.mountains(260, rnd, (0.25, 0.38, 0.45) if not env["gelap"] else (0.03, 0.04, 0.06))
    if env["gelap"] and cfg.get("kunang", True):
        info["kunang"] = [(rnd.uniform(-15, 15), rnd.uniform(-5, 40), rnd.uniform(0.3, 1.5)) for _ in range(60)]
    return info


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/gang_kampung.py
# sha256: 3bb75c137c50c29c45c7767331ac0994f5351b6cf82c76025b4bffe58e9b1158
==============================================================================================================
"""Lokasi 'gang_kampung': gang sempit di antara rumah petak warna-warni, selokan, tiang listrik
dengan kabel semrawut, jemuran melintang, lampu dinding.
Koordinat: lorong x=-1.8..1.8, muka rumah x=±2.05, gang sepanjang Y."""
import random

from mathutils import Vector

import bl_utils as B
import kit

Y0, Y1 = -25, 80


def build(cfg, env, M, avoid):
    rnd = random.Random(cfg.get("acak", 7))
    info = {"lamps": [], "steam": [], "bulbs": []}
    floor = kit.ground_mat("cor_semen", (0.42, 0.41, 0.38), env["basah"], var=0.3)
    B.box("lantai_gang", (0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.05), (3.7, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), floor)
    gutter = B.mat("selokan", (0.04, 0.05, 0.04), 0.1)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("selokan", (sx * 1.93, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.04), (0.22, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), gutter)

    # rumah-rumah di kiri & kanan
    houses = []
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        y = Y0
        while y < Y1:
            w = rnd.uniform(3.2, 5.5)
            h = kit.house(M, env, rnd, sx * 2.05, y, w, -sx, avoid=avoid)
            houses.append((sx, y, w, h))
            if rnd.random() < 0.5:
                kit.wall_lamp(M, env, sx * 2.05, y + w * 0.5, 2.5, -sx)
                if env["malam"]:
                    info["lamps"].append((sx * 1.75, y + w * 0.5, 2.4))
            if cfg.get("hiasan", True) and rnd.random() < 0.55:
                px, py = sx * 1.75, y + w * 0.85
                if not any(abs(px - a[0]) < 1.0 and abs(py - a[1]) < 1.2 for a in avoid):
                    kit.build_prop({"jenis": rnd.choice(["pot_tanaman", "pot_tanaman", "ember", "sepeda"]), "posisi": [px, py],
                                    "warna": rnd.choice(["biru", "merah", "hijau", "kuning"])}, M, env, info)
            y += w
    # Phase 4.2: sedikit dimundurkan/dikecilkan agar tidak sebidang dgn dinding rumah terakhir (cek_sebidang: z-fighting)
    B.box("ujung_gang", (0, Y1 + 1.02, 2.49), (5.96, 1, 4.98), kit.wall_cached((0.85, 0.75, 0.6)))
    grate = B.mat("jeruji", (0.08, 0.08, 0.08), 0.5, metal=0.6)
    for sx in (-1, 1):                                              # tutup selokan berjeruji di beberapa titik
        for y in range(Y0, Y1, 7):
            for k in range(8):
                B.box("jeruji", (sx * 1.93, y + k * 0.1, 0.0), (0.22, 0.025, 0.02), grate)
    # barang-barang warga di tepi gang
    if cfg.get("hiasan", True):
        chair = [B.mat(f"kursi_plastik{i}", c, 0.4) for i, c in enumerate([(0.7, 0.05, 0.05), (0.05, 0.25, 0.65), (0.1, 0.5, 0.2)])]
        for i in range(26):
            sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1)); y = rnd.uniform(-8, 45); x = sx * rnd.uniform(1.35, 1.6)
            if any(abs(x - a[0]) < 0.9 and abs(y - a[1]) < 1.3 for a in avoid):
                continue
            kind = rnd.random()
            if kind < 0.18:
                kit.build_prop({"jenis": "motor", "posisi": [sx * 1.3, y], "arah": rnd.uniform(-15, 15),
                                "warna": rnd.choice(["merah", "biru", "hitam", "putih"])}, M, env, info)
            elif kind < 0.4:                                         # kursi plastik
                cm = rnd.choice(chair)
                B.box("kursi_plastik", (x, y, 0.42), (0.42, 0.42, 0.04), cm)
                B.box("sandaran", (x + sx * 0.2, y, 0.68), (0.04, 0.42, 0.5), cm)
                for dx in (-0.17, 0.17):
                    for dy in (-0.17, 0.17):
                        B.cyl("kaki", (x + dx, y + dy, 0.21), 0.02, 0.42, cm)
            elif kind < 0.55:
                kit.build_prop({"jenis": "ember", "posisi": [x, y], "warna": rnd.choice(["biru", "merah", "hijau", "kuning"])}, M, env, info)
            elif kind < 0.7:                                         # sepasang sandal
                sm = B.mat(f"sandal{rnd.randint(0, 2)}", rnd.choice([(0.05, 0.2, 0.6), (0.6, 0.05, 0.05), (0.05, 0.05, 0.05)]), 0.6)
                for dd in (-0.06, 0.06):
                    B.box("sandal", (x, y + dd, 0.01), (0.25, 0.09, 0.02), sm, rot=(0, 0, rnd.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)))
            elif kind < 0.82:
                kit.build_prop({"jenis": "galon", "posisi": [x, y]}, M, env, info)
            else:
                kit.potted(M, x, y, rnd)

    # tiang listrik + kabel semrawut
    if cfg.get("tiang_listrik", True):
        tops = [kit.power_pole(M, 1.7, y) for y in range(-6, Y1, 14)]
        for a, b in zip(tops, tops[1:]):
            for i in range(3):
                kit.cable(M, a[i], b[i], rnd.uniform(0.5, 1.0))
        for t in tops:                                          # kabel ke rumah
            for k in range(2):
                sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1))
                kit.cable(M, t[rnd.randint(0, 2)], Vector((sx * 2.05, t[0].y + rnd.uniform(-4, 4), 3.0)), 0.4, 8)

    # jemuran melintang gang
    jm = cfg.get("jemuran", True)
    for y in (jm if isinstance(jm, list) else ([5, 19, 33] if jm else [])):
        kit.jemuran(M, -2.0, 2.0, y, 2.9, rnd)
    if cfg.get("umbul_umbul"):
        for y in (cfg["umbul_umbul"] if isinstance(cfg["umbul_umbul"], list) else [10, 26]):
            kit.bunting(M, y, 3.6, -2.05, 2.05, 0.3, 10)
    if cfg.get("lampu_hias"):
        for y in (cfg["lampu_hias"] if isinstance(cfg["lampu_hias"], list) else [8, 22]):
            kit.string_lights(M, env, y, 3.8, -2.05, 2.05, 0.35, 14)
    return info


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/jalan_kota.py
# sha256: b5d2da3221dfae877994e22649d3ad8d5f1da3b74d9bfaf3a500b024d1b3127c
==============================================================================================================
"""Lokasi 'jalan_kota': jalan raya dengan trotoar, deretan ruko warna-warni, papan neon, lampu jalan.
Koordinat: aspal x=-4..4, trotoar x=±4..±7 (tinggi 0.15), muka ruko x=±7.5, jalan sepanjang Y."""
import math
import random

import bl_utils as B
import kit

NEON = [(1.0, 0.1, 0.55), (0.1, 0.85, 1.0), (0.2, 1.0, 0.3), (1.0, 0.75, 0.1), (0.6, 0.3, 1.0)]
SHOP_NAMES = ["KOPI", "APOTEK", "LAUNDRY", "BAKSO", "SERVIS HP", "FOTOKOPI", "WARNET", "SALON", "BENGKEL",
              "SEMBAKO", "MIE AYAM", "JAMU", "PULSA", "SATE", "MARTABAK", "BAKERY"]
Y0, Y1 = -25, 90


_CANOPY = {}


def _canopy_mat(c):
    if c not in _CANOPY:
        _CANOPY[c] = kit.corrugated_mat(f"kanopi_{len(_CANOPY)}", c, rust=0.3)
    return _CANOPY[c]


def build(cfg, env, M, avoid):
    rnd = random.Random(cfg.get("acak", 7))
    info = {"lamps": [], "steam": [], "bulbs": []}
    night = env["malam"]
    asphalt = kit.ground_mat("aspal", (0.03, 0.03, 0.035) if night else (0.09, 0.09, 0.1), env["basah"], pbr="asphalt_02", tile=2.2)
    side_m = kit.ground_mat("trotoar", (0.32, 0.31, 0.3) if night else (0.5, 0.5, 0.48), env["basah"] * 0.6, var=0.1, pattern="paving", cracks=False,
                            pbr="concrete_pavers_02", tile=1.6)
    curb = [B.mat("kanstin", (0.55, 0.52, 0.2), 0.6), B.mat("kanstin2", (0.05, 0.05, 0.05), 0.6)]
    walls = [kit.wall_cached(c, 1.0, brick=(i == 4)) for i, c in enumerate(
        [(0.55, 0.32, 0.22), (0.2, 0.42, 0.48), (0.62, 0.52, 0.3), (0.45, 0.28, 0.42), (0.3, 0.45, 0.3), (0.7, 0.66, 0.55)])]
    shutters = [kit.shutter_mat(f"rolling{i}", c, i * 1.7) for i, c in enumerate(
        [(0.08, 0.45, 0.55), (0.6, 0.12, 0.1), (0.75, 0.55, 0.08), (0.12, 0.42, 0.18), (0.18, 0.22, 0.6), (0.3, 0.3, 0.32)])]
    if not cfg.get("warna_warni", True):
        walls = [kit.wall_cached((0.25, 0.24, 0.23), 1.0)]
        shutters = [B.mat("rolling_polos", (0.25, 0.26, 0.28), 0.45, metal=0.3)]
    names = list(cfg.get("nama_toko", SHOP_NAMES)); rnd.shuffle(names)

    B.box("aspal", (0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.05), (8, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), asphalt)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("trotoar", (sx * 5.5, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, 0.075), (3, Y1 - Y0, 0.15), side_m)
        for i, y in enumerate(range(Y0, Y1)):
            B.box("kanstin", (sx * 4.05, y + 0.5, 0.09), (0.12, 1.0, 0.18), curb[i % 2])
    for y in range(Y0, Y1, 5):
        B.box("marka", (0, y, 0.003), (0.15, 2.4, 0.01), M.paint)

    shop_i = 0
    rnd_wall_trim = B.mat("lis_fasad_beton", (0.62, 0.6, 0.56), 0.8)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        y = Y0
        while y < Y1:
            w = rnd.uniform(4, 7); h = rnd.uniform(4.5, 10); cy = y + w / 2
            B.box("ruko", (sx * 10.5, cy, h / 2), (6, w - 0.15, h), rnd.choice(walls))
            fx, nx = sx * 7.5, -sx
            dw = w * rnd.uniform(0.55, 0.75)                           # lebar & bukaan pintu bervariasi tiap ruko
            opn = rnd.choice([0, 0, 0, 0.35, 0.8]) if night else rnd.choice([0, 1.6, 2.1])
            dh = 2.5 - opn
            if opn > 0:                                               # celah terbuka: ruang dalam gelap/remang
                B.box("dalam_ruko", (fx - nx * 0.3, cy, opn / 2), (0.05, dw, opn), B.mat("ruang_gelap", (0.02, 0.018, 0.015), 0.9))
            B.box("rolling_door", (fx + nx * 0.02, cy, opn + dh / 2 + 0.1), (0.05, dw, dh), rnd.choice(shutters))
            B.box("kotak_gulung", (fx + nx * 0.12, cy, 2.72), (0.22, dw + 0.1, 0.26), B.mat("kotak_gulung", (0.2, 0.2, 0.21), 0.5, metal=0.4))
            for dy_ in (-1, 1):                                        # rel samping
                B.box("rel_rolling", (fx + nx * 0.05, cy + dy_ * dw / 2, 1.35), (0.06, 0.05, 2.6), B.mat("rel", (0.25, 0.25, 0.26), 0.4, metal=0.6))
            B.box("gembok", (fx + nx * 0.06, cy, opn + 0.2), (0.04, 0.06, 0.08), B.mat("gembok", (0.55, 0.45, 0.2), 0.3, metal=0.8))
            B.box("lis_lantai", (fx + nx * 0.02, cy, 2.95), (0.12, w - 0.15, 0.25), M.white)
            dr = random.Random(int(cy * 997) + sx * 31 + 5)          # RNG dekorasi terpisah: tata letak ruko tidak berubah
            if dr.random() < 0.55:                                    # tanaman pot di depan ruko (model CC0)
                for k in range(dr.randint(1, 2)):
                    py = cy + dr.uniform(-w * 0.45, -dw / 2 - 0.1) if dr.random() < 0.5 else cy + dr.uniform(dw / 2 + 0.1, w * 0.45)
                    if kit.ext_model(dr.choice(["potted_plant_01", "potted_plant_02", "potted_plant_04"]),
                                     (fx + nx * 0.35, py, 0.15), dr.uniform(0, 6.28), height=dr.uniform(0.55, 1.0)) is None:
                        kit.potted(M, fx + nx * 0.35, py, dr, 0.15)
            if rnd.random() < 0.6:                                    # kanopi bergaris
                cA, cB = rnd.choice([((0.7, 0.05, 0.05), (0.9, 0.9, 0.88)), ((0.05, 0.3, 0.6), (0.9, 0.9, 0.88)),
                                     ((0.1, 0.45, 0.2), (0.9, 0.85, 0.3)), ((0.85, 0.4, 0.05), (0.95, 0.9, 0.8))])
                n = max(4, int(w * 0.75 / 0.4))
                for k in range(n):
                    B.box("kanopi", (fx + nx * 0.45, cy - w * 0.37 + (k + 0.5) * w * 0.74 / n, 2.75), (0.9, w * 0.74 / n, 0.03),
                          _canopy_mat(cA if k % 2 else cB), rot=(0, nx * math.radians(-18), 0))
            ac_old = h > 6 and rnd.random() < 0.5                      # (pemakaian rnd lama dipertahankan)
            ay0 = y + rnd.uniform(0.8, w - 0.8) if ac_old else None
            if (ac_old or (h > 5.5 and dr.random() < 0.5)):           # unit AC (model CC0) + siku penyangga + pipa
                for k in range(dr.randint(1, 2)):
                    ay = ay0 if (k == 0 and ay0 is not None) else y + dr.uniform(0.9, w - 0.9)
                    az = dr.uniform(3.7, min(h - 0.8, 6.5))
                    o = kit.ext_model("exterior_aircon_unit", (fx + nx * 0.24, ay, az), math.radians(90) * nx, width=0.82)
                    if o is None:
                        B.box("ac", (fx + nx * 0.2, ay, az + 0.25), (0.3, 0.75, 0.5), B.mat("ac", (0.85, 0.85, 0.82), 0.4))
                    for dy_ in (-0.28, 0.28):
                        B.box("siku_ac", (fx + nx * 0.22, ay + dy_, az - 0.03), (0.42, 0.03, 0.03), M.pole)
                    B.cyl("pipa_ac", (fx + nx * 0.06, ay + 0.45, az - 0.6), 0.02, 1.3, B.mat("pipa_ac", (0.85, 0.85, 0.82), 0.5))
            B.box("parapet", (sx * 10.5, cy, h + 0.25), (6.1, w - 0.1, 0.5), rnd.choice(walls))
            if rnd.random() < 0.35:
                B.cyl("tandon", (sx * 9.5, cy, h + 1.0), 0.6, 1.3, B.mat("tandon", (0.2, 0.35, 0.65), 0.4), verts=16)
            if cfg.get("neon", True) and rnd.random() < 0.6 and -8 < y < 70:
                c = rnd.choice(NEON)
                B.box("papan", (fx + nx * 0.06, cy, 3.1), (0.06, min(w * 0.75, 3.6), 0.75), M.board)
                lit = night and rnd.random() < float(cfg.get("toko_buka", 1.0))      # toko tutup -> papan mati
                k_neon = float(cfg.get("terang_neon", 1.0))
                tm = B.glow(f"neon{shop_i}", c, 12 * k_neon) if lit else B.mat(f"huruf{shop_i}", tuple(v * 0.6 for v in c), 0.5)
                B.text(names[shop_i % len(names)], (fx + nx * 0.11, cy, 3.1), 0.48, tm, nx)
                if lit:
                    B.light("POINT", (fx + nx * 0.9, cy, 3.0), 70 * k_neon, c, 0.4)
                shop_i += 1
            for fl in range(1, int(h // 3)):
                for k in range(max(1, int(w // 2))):
                    wy = y + 1.0 + k * 2.0
                    if wy > y + w - 0.8:
                        continue
                    on = night and rnd.random() < float(cfg.get("jendela_nyala", 0.45))
                    B.box("jendela", (fx + nx * 0.03, wy, fl * 3 + 1.4), (0.05, 1.0, 1.3), rnd.choice(M.win_on) if on else M.win_off)
                    for dz_ in (-0.68, 0.68):                             # Phase 4.1-C2: kusen + ambang jendela
                        B.box("kusen_jendela", (fx + nx * 0.06, wy, fl * 3 + 1.4 + dz_), (0.08, 1.12, 0.06), M.white)
                    for dy__ in (-0.53, 0.53):
                        B.box("kusen_jendela", (fx + nx * 0.06, wy + dy__, fl * 3 + 1.4), (0.08, 0.06, 1.36), M.white)
                    B.box("ambang_jendela", (fx + nx * 0.12, wy, fl * 3 + 0.7), (0.16, 1.22, 0.05), M.white)
            for fl in range(1, int(h // 3) + 1):                         # Phase 4.1-C2: lis lantai & pilaster tiap ruko
                if fl * 3 < h - 0.3:
                    B.box("lis_fasad", (fx + nx * 0.06, cy, fl * 3 - 0.05), (0.14, w - 0.15, 0.12), rnd_wall_trim)
            for yy in (y + 0.1, y + w - 0.1):
                B.box("pilaster", (fx + nx * 0.07, yy, h / 2), (0.16, 0.22, h), rnd_wall_trim)
            y += w
    B.box("ujung_jalan", (0, Y1 + 2, 5), (30, 2, 10), walls[0])
    if night and cfg.get("lampu_jauh", True):                        # Phase 4.1-C1: practical jauh -> bokeh & kedalaman
        bk = random.Random(int(cfg.get("acak", 7)) * 13 + 1)
        pal = [(1.0, 0.62, 0.3), (1.0, 0.78, 0.5), (0.55, 0.75, 1.0), (1.0, 0.45, 0.25), (0.6, 1.0, 0.7)]
        mats = [B.glow(f"bokeh_jauh{i}", c, 6.0) for i, c in enumerate(pal)]
        for end, sgn in ((Y1 + 0.9, 1), (Y0 - 0.9, -1)):              # jendela & lampu di gedung ujung jalan
            for k in range(46):
                B.box("jendela_jauh", (bk.uniform(-13, 13), end, bk.uniform(1.2, 9.5)), (0.5, 0.05, 0.45) if bk.random() < 0.7 else (0.08, 0.05, 0.08),
                      mats[bk.randrange(len(mats))], bevel=0)
        dark = B.mat("gedung_jauh", (0.025, 0.03, 0.04), 0.9)
        for sx in (-1, 1):                                          # gedung lebih jauh di balik deretan ruko: siluet + jendela
            yy = Y0
            while yy < Y1:
                w_, h_ = bk.uniform(6, 14), bk.uniform(9, 22)
                gx = sx * bk.uniform(17, 24)
                B.box("gedung_jauh", (gx, yy + w_ / 2, h_ / 2), (4, w_ - 0.5, h_), dark, bevel=0)
                for k in range(int(w_ * h_ / 9)):
                    if bk.random() < 0.35:
                        B.box("jendela_jauh", (gx - sx * 2.03, yy + bk.uniform(0.6, w_ - 0.6), bk.uniform(1.5, h_ - 0.8)), (0.05, 0.7, 0.6),
                              mats[bk.randrange(len(mats))], bevel=0)
                yy += w_

    flick = cfg.get("lampu_berkedip", True)
    for i, y in enumerate(cfg.get("lampu_kiri", [1.5, 15, 29, 43, 57, 71])):
        info["lamps"].append(kit.street_lamp(M, env, -4.3, y, -1, warm=i % 2 == 0))
    for i, y in enumerate(cfg.get("lampu_kanan", [8, 22, 36, 50, 64])):
        info["lamps"].append(kit.street_lamp(M, env, 4.3, y, 1, warm=i % 2 == 1, flicker=flick and i == 1))

    lh = cfg.get("lampu_hias", True)
    if lh:
        for y in (lh if isinstance(lh, list) else [6.5, 19, 33, 47]):
            kit.string_lights(M, env, y)
    uu = cfg.get("umbul_umbul", True)
    if uu:
        for j, y in enumerate(uu if isinstance(uu, list) else [12.5, 26, 40]):
            kit.bunting(M, y, colorful=j % 2 == 1)

    if cfg.get("hiasan", True):
        # motor parkir di tepi trotoar, kantong sampah, kerucut
        for i in range(10):
            sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1)); y = rnd.uniform(-6, 40)
            if any(abs(sx * 3.4 - a[0]) < 1.4 and abs(y - a[1]) < 1.8 for a in avoid):
                continue
            kit.build_prop({"jenis": "motor", "posisi": [sx * 3.4, y], "arah": rnd.choice([0, 180]) + rnd.uniform(-20, 20),
                            "warna": rnd.choice(["merah", "biru", "hitam", "putih", "abu"])}, M, env, info)
        for i in range(8):
            sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1)); y = rnd.uniform(-6, 40)
            if any(abs(sx * 7.0 - a[0]) < 1.2 and abs(y - a[1]) < 1.2 for a in avoid):
                continue
            for k in range(rnd.randint(1, 3)):
                o = B.ball("kantong_sampah", (sx * 7.1 + rnd.uniform(-0.2, 0.2), y + k * 0.45, 0.35), 0.28, B.mat("plastik_hitam", (0.02, 0.02, 0.025), 0.25), 2)
                o.scale = (1, 1, 0.8)
        for i in range(12):
            sx = rnd.choice((-1, 1)); y = rnd.uniform(-8, 30)
            if any(abs(sx * 6.7 - a[0]) < 1.5 and abs(y - a[1]) < 1.5 for a in avoid):
                continue
            kit.build_prop({"jenis": rnd.choice(["tong_sampah", "pot_tanaman", "pot_tanaman"]), "posisi": [sx * 6.7, y]}, M, env, info, lambda x: 0.15)
    return info

Semua fungsi menerima env (dari atmosfer.build) supaya lampu/jendela menyala hanya saat malam/senja."""
