          (0.78, 0.7, 0.92), (0.98, 0.75, 0.5), (0.75, 0.9, 0.6)]
FRUIT = [(0.95, 0.45, 0.05), (0.85, 0.08, 0.08), (0.25, 0.65, 0.15), (0.95, 0.85, 0.1), (0.45, 0.15, 0.45), (0.9, 0.6, 0.2)]
TARP = [(0.1, 0.35, 0.8), (0.95, 0.45, 0.05), (0.8, 0.1, 0.1), (0.1, 0.6, 0.3), (0.95, 0.8, 0.1), (0.2, 0.7, 0.8)]


def col(v, default):
    return B.srgb(to_rgb(v, default))


class Mats:

def _n(N, kind, **inputs):
    node = N.new(kind)
    for k, v in inputs.items():
        if k.startswith("_"):
            setattr(node, k[1:], v)
        else:
            node.inputs[k].default_value = v
    return node


def _ramp(N, stops, interp="LINEAR"):
    r = N.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB"); r.color_ramp.interpolation = interp
    els = r.color_ramp.elements
    while len(els) < len(stops):
        els.new(0.5)
    for e, (pos, c) in zip(els, stops):
        e.position = pos; e.color = (*c, 1) if len(c) == 3 else c
    return r


def _mix(N, L, a, b, fac, mode="MIX"):
    m = N.new("ShaderNodeMix"); m.data_type = "RGBA"; m.blend_type = mode
    L(fac, m.inputs[0]) if not isinstance(fac, (int, float)) else setattr(m.inputs[0], "default_value", fac)
    L(a, m.inputs[6]) if not isinstance(a, tuple) else setattr(m.inputs[6], "default_value", (*a, 1))
    L(b, m.inputs[7]) if not isinstance(b, tuple) else setattr(m.inputs[7], "default_value", (*b, 1))
    return m.outputs[2]


EXT = __import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "assets" / "external" / "polyhaven"
_PBR_IMG = {}


def _pbr_img(aid, mp):
    """gambar tekstur Poly Haven (diff/nor/rough/arm) -> (image, rerata luminans) atau None bila belum diunduh"""
    key = (aid, mp)
    if key in _PBR_IMG:
        return _PBR_IMG[key]
    f = next(iter(sorted((EXT / aid).glob(mp + ".*"))), None) if (EXT / aid).exists() else None
    if f is None:
        _PBR_IMG[key] = None
        return None
    img = bpy.data.images.load(str(f), check_existing=True)
    img.colorspace_settings.name = "sRGB" if mp == "diff" else "Non-Color"
    mean = 1.0
    if mp == "diff":
        import numpy as np
        px = np.asarray(img.pixels[:], dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 4)[::97, :3]
        mean = float((px @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])).mean()) or 1.0
    _PBR_IMG[key] = (img, mean)
    return _PBR_IMG[key]


def _pbr(N, L, vec, aid, tile):
    """lapisan detail PBR (CC0): -> dict(lum = luminans ternormalisasi (rerata 1), rough, normal, ao) atau None.
    tile = ukuran satu ubin tekstur di dunia (m)."""
    d = _pbr_img(aid, "diff")
    if d is None:
        return None
    mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); L(vec, mp.inputs["Vector"]); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (1 / tile,) * 3
    out = {}
    tex = N.new("ShaderNodeTexImage"); tex.image = d[0]; L(mp.outputs["Vector"], tex.inputs["Vector"])
    bw = N.new("ShaderNodeRGBToBW"); L(tex.outputs["Color"], bw.inputs[0])
    k = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); k.operation = "MULTIPLY"; L(bw.outputs[0], k.inputs[0]); k.inputs[1].default_value = 1 / d[1]
    out["lum"] = k.outputs[0]
    for mp_name, key in (("rough", "rough"), ("nor", "normal"), ("arm", "ao")):
        im = _pbr_img(aid, mp_name)
        if im is None:
            continue
        t = N.new("ShaderNodeTexImage"); t.image = im[0]; L(mp.outputs["Vector"], t.inputs["Vector"])
        if key == "normal":
            nm = N.new("ShaderNodeNormalMap"); nm.inputs["Strength"].default_value = 0.9
            L(t.outputs["Color"], nm.inputs["Color"]); out["normal"] = nm.outputs["Normal"]
        elif key == "ao":
            sp = N.new("ShaderNodeSeparateColor"); L(t.outputs["Color"], sp.inputs[0]); out["ao"] = sp.outputs[0]
        else:
            out["rough"] = t.outputs["Color"]
    return out


_EXT_COLL = {}


def ext_model(aid, loc, rot_z=0.0, width=None, height=None, parent=None, name=None):
    """model glTF eksternal (Poly Haven, CC0; lihat assets/external/LISENSI_ASET.md) sebagai instance koleksi.
    Titik asal = tengah-bawah model. width/height (m) menskalakan model; tanpa keduanya = ukuran asli.
    -> objek instance, atau None bila aset belum diunduh (pemanggil memakai pengganti prosedural)."""
    d = EXT / aid
    f = next(iter(sorted(d.glob("*.gltf"))), None) if d.exists() else None
    if f is None:
        return None
    if aid not in _EXT_COLL:
        before = set(bpy.data.objects)
        bpy.ops.import_scene.gltf(filepath=str(f))
        new = [o for o in bpy.data.objects if o not in before]
        bpy.context.view_layer.update()
        pts = [o.matrix_world @ Vector(c) for o in new if o.type == "MESH" for c in o.bound_box]
        lo = Vector((min(p.x for p in pts), min(p.y for p in pts), min(p.z for p in pts)))
        hi = Vector((max(p.x for p in pts), max(p.y for p in pts), max(p.z for p in pts)))
        coll = bpy.data.collections.new("aset_" + aid)
        for o in new:
            for c in list(o.users_collection):
                c.objects.unlink(o)
            coll.objects.link(o)
        coll.instance_offset = ((lo.x + hi.x) / 2, (lo.y + hi.y) / 2, lo.z)
        _EXT_COLL[aid] = (coll, hi - lo)
    coll, dims = _EXT_COLL[aid]
    sc_ = 1.0
    if width:
        sc_ = width / max(1e-6, dims.x)
    elif height:
        sc_ = height / max(1e-6, dims.z)
    o = bpy.data.objects.new(name or aid, None)
    o.instance_type = "COLLECTION"; o.instance_collection = coll
    o.location = loc; o.rotation_euler = (0, 0, rot_z); o.scale = (sc_,) * 3
    B.scene().collection.objects.link(o)
    if parent is not None:
        o.parent = parent
    return o


def corrugated_mat(name, color, rust=0.35):
    """seng/kanopi bergelombang dicat: normal & kekasaran dari tekstur CC0 'rusty_corrugated_iron', karat mengintip."""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    nt = m.node_tree; N = nt.nodes; L = nt.links.new
    b = N["Principled BSDF"]
    co = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord").outputs["Object"]
    P_ = _pbr(N, L, co, "rusty_corrugated_iron", 1.2)
    b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1)
    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.55
    b.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.25
    if P_:
        d = _pbr_img("rusty_corrugated_iron", "diff")[0]
        mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); L(co, mp.inputs["Vector"]); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (1 / 1.2,) * 3
        t = N.new("ShaderNodeTexImage"); t.image = d; L(mp.outputs["Vector"], t.inputs["Vector"])
        rn = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=1.6, Detail=6.0); L(co, rn.inputs["Vector"])
        rm = _ramp(N, [(0.62 - 0.2 * rust, (0, 0, 0)), (0.7 - 0.2 * rust, (1, 1, 1))]); L(rn.outputs["Fac"], rm.inputs["Fac"])
        lum = N.new("ShaderNodeCombineColor")
        for i in range(3):
            L(P_["lum"], lum.inputs[i])
        paint = _mix(N, L, (*color,), lum.outputs[0], 0.5, "MULTIPLY")
        L(_mix(N, L, paint, t.outputs["Color"], rm.outputs["Color"]), b.inputs["Base Color"])
        if "rough" in P_:
            L(P_["rough"], b.inputs["Roughness"])
        if "normal" in P_:
            L(P_["normal"], b.inputs["Normal"])
    return m


def ground_mat(name, color, wet, scale=0.18, var=0.25, bump=0.08, pattern=None, cracks=True, pbr=None, tile=2.0):
    """lantai/tanah kaya detail: variasi warna, noda, butiran, retakan, genangan bertepi tajam.
    wet 0..1 = seberapa banyak genangan."""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    nt = m.node_tree; N = nt.nodes; L = nt.links.new
    b = N["Principled BSDF"]
    tc = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord"); co = tc.outputs["Object"]
    dark = tuple(c * (1 - var) for c in color); light = tuple(min(1, c * (1 + var)) for c in color)
    # koordinat terdistorsi (domain warp): retakan & noda tidak berpola sel/berulang
    wn = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=0.9, Detail=3.0); L(co, wn.inputs["Vector"])
    wo = N.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath"); wo.operation = "MULTIPLY_ADD"; wo.inputs[1].default_value = (0.45, 0.45, 0.45)
    L(wn.outputs["Color"], wo.inputs[0]); L(co, wo.inputs[2]); cw_ = wo.outputs[0]
    # 1. variasi besar (petak warna) + noda kotor
    big = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=0.35, Detail=4.0); L(co, big.inputs["Vector"])
    base = _ramp(N, [(0.3, dark), (0.7, light)]); L(big.outputs["Fac"], base.inputs["Fac"])
    col = base.outputs["Color"]
    if pattern == "keramik":
        ck = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexBrick", Scale=1.0, **{"Mortar Size": 0.012, "Brick Width": 0.6, "Row Height": 0.6})
        ck.offset = 0.0
        ck.inputs["Color1"].default_value = (*color, 1); ck.inputs["Color2"].default_value = (*[c * 0.9 for c in color], 1)
        ck.inputs["Mortar"].default_value = (*[c * 0.55 for c in color], 1)
        L(co, ck.inputs["Vector"]); col = _mix(N, L, ck.outputs["Color"], col, 0.25)
    elif pattern == "kayu":
        wv = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexWave", Scale=2.2, Distortion=6.0, Detail=3.0); L(co, wv.inputs["Vector"])
        wr = _ramp(N, [(0.2, tuple(c * 0.65 for c in color)), (0.8, color)]); L(wv.outputs["Fac"], wr.inputs["Fac"])
        pl = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexBrick", Scale=1.0, **{"Mortar Size": 0.006, "Brick Width": 2.4, "Row Height": 0.18})
        pl.inputs["Mortar"].default_value = (0.05, 0.03, 0.02, 1); L(co, pl.inputs["Vector"])
        pl.inputs["Color1"].default_value = (1, 1, 1, 1); pl.inputs["Color2"].default_value = (0.85, 0.85, 0.85, 1)
        col = _mix(N, L, wr.outputs["Color"], pl.outputs["Color"], 1.0, "MULTIPLY")
    elif pattern == "paving" and not (pbr and _pbr_img(pbr, "diff")):   # paving prosedural (tanpa tekstur CC0)
        pv = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexBrick", Scale=1.0, **{"Mortar Size": 0.018, "Mortar Smooth": 0.35, "Bias": 0.0,
                                                        "Brick Width": 0.4, "Row Height": 0.2})
        pv.inputs["Color1"].default_value = (*[min(1, c * 1.12) for c in color], 1)
        pv.inputs["Color2"].default_value = (*[c * 0.72 for c in color], 1)
        pv.inputs["Mortar"].default_value = (0, 0, 0, 1); L(co, pv.inputs["Vector"])
        mortar = pv.outputs["Fac"]
        moss = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=2.5, Detail=5.0); L(co, moss.inputs["Vector"])
        mcol = _ramp(N, [(0.4, tuple(c * 0.3 for c in color)), (0.65, (0.05, 0.08, 0.03))]); L(moss.outputs["Fac"], mcol.inputs["Fac"])
        col = _mix(N, L, _mix(N, L, pv.outputs["Color"], col, 0.35), mcol.outputs["Color"], mortar)
        self_mortar = mortar
    P_ = _pbr(N, L, co if pattern == "paving" else cw_, pbr, tile) if pbr else None   # paving: blok harus lurus (tanpa warp)
    if P_:                                                    # detail nyata (CC0): luminans tekstur memodulasi warna prosedural
        lum = N.new("ShaderNodeCombineColor")
        for i in range(3):
            L(P_["lum"], lum.inputs[i])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, lum.outputs[0], 0.85, "MULTIPLY")
        if "ao" in P_ and pattern == "paving":               # nat paving (AO gelap) -> lumut/kotoran
            aoi = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); aoi.operation = "SUBTRACT"; aoi.inputs[0].default_value = 1.0; L(P_["ao"], aoi.inputs[1])
            aom = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); aom.operation = "MULTIPLY"; aom.use_clamp = True; L(aoi.outputs[0], aom.inputs[0]); aom.inputs[1].default_value = 3.0
            col = _mix(N, L, col, tuple(c * 0.35 for c in color), aom.outputs[0])
    stain = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=1.3, Detail=8.0, Roughness=0.65); L(cw_, stain.inputs["Vector"])
    oil = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexVoronoi", Scale=0.6, Randomness=1.0); L(cw_, oil.inputs["Vector"])       # noda oli/bekas ban
    om = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0.55, 0.53, 0.5)), (0.12, (1, 1, 1))]); L(oil.outputs["Distance"], om.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, col, om.outputs["Color"], 0.6, "MULTIPLY")
    sr = _ramp(N, [(0.45, (1, 1, 1)), (0.75, (0.55, 0.52, 0.48))]); L(stain.outputs["Fac"], sr.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, col, sr.outputs["Color"], 1.0, "MULTIPLY")
    # 2. butiran agregat (tajam, kecil)
    grain = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexVoronoi", Scale=55.0); L(co, grain.inputs["Vector"])
    gr = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0.8, 0.8, 0.8)), (0.35, (1, 1, 1)), (1.0, (1.12, 1.1, 1.05))]); L(grain.outputs["Distance"], gr.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, col, gr.outputs["Color"], 1.0, "MULTIPLY")
    agg = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexVoronoi", Scale=140.0); L(co, agg.inputs["Vector"])      # agregat: kerikil terang acak
    ag = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0, 0, 0)), (0.82, (0, 0, 0)), (0.9, (1, 1, 1))]); L(agg.outputs["Color"], ag.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, col, tuple(min(1, c * 2.2 + 0.03) for c in color), _mix(N, L, ag.outputs["Color"], (0, 0, 0), 0.4))
    mid = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=9.0, Detail=6.0, Roughness=0.7); L(co, mid.inputs["Vector"])   # bump skala menengah
    # 3. retakan
    crack_h = None
    if cracks and pattern not in ("keramik", "kayu"):
        cr = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexVoronoi", Scale=0.45, _feature="DISTANCE_TO_EDGE"); L(cw_, cr.inputs["Vector"])
        cw = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=3.0); L(co, cw.inputs["Vector"])
        crm = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0.3, 0.3, 0.3)), (0.008, (0.7, 0.7, 0.7)), (0.02, (1, 1, 1))]); L(cr.outputs["Distance"], crm.inputs["Fac"])
        keep = _ramp(N, [(0.55, (1, 1, 1)), (0.6, (0, 0, 0))]); L(cw.outputs["Fac"], keep.inputs["Fac"])   # retak hanya di sebagian
        crack = _mix(N, L, crm.outputs["Color"], (1, 1, 1), keep.outputs["Color"])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, crack, 0.6, "MULTIPLY")
        crack_h = crack
    # 4. genangan: bentuk dari noise besar, tepi tajam
    wet = max(0.0, min(1.0, wet))
    pud = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=0.22, Detail=5.0, Roughness=0.6); L(co, pud.inputs["Vector"])
    t = 0.74 - 0.26 * wet
    pm = _ramp(N, [(t, (0, 0, 0)), (t + 0.02, (1, 1, 1))]); L(pud.outputs["Fac"], pm.inputs["Fac"])
    pmask = pm.outputs["Color"]
    if wet < 0.2:
        pmask = None
    damp = None
    if pmask:
        dm = _ramp(N, [(t - 0.1, (0, 0, 0)), (t, (1, 1, 1))]); L(pud.outputs["Fac"], dm.inputs["Fac"])
        damp = dm.outputs["Color"]                                           # tepi lembap di sekitar genangan
        col = _mix(N, L, col, tuple(c * 0.6 for c in color), damp)
        col = _mix(N, L, col, tuple(c * 0.35 for c in color), pmask)          # tanah basah lebih gelap
    L(col, b.inputs["Base Color"])
    # kekasaran: kering kasar, basah agak licin, genangan seperti cermin
    dry_r = 0.85 - 0.35 * wet
    rough = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (dry_r - 0.15,) * 3), (1.0, (dry_r,) * 3)]); L(stain.outputs["Fac"], rough.inputs["Fac"])
    rcol = rough.outputs["Color"]
    if P_ and "rough" in P_:                                   # kekasaran alami dari tekstur, digeser ke tingkat kering/basah
        rr = N.new("ShaderNodeMapRange"); L(P_["rough"], rr.inputs["Value"])
        rr.inputs["To Min"].default_value, rr.inputs["To Max"].default_value = dry_r - 0.25, min(1.0, dry_r + 0.1)
        rcol = _mix(N, L, rcol, rr.outputs["Result"], 0.7)
    agr = _mix(N, L, rcol, (0.55, 0.55, 0.55), _mix(N, L, ag.outputs["Color"], (0, 0, 0), 0.5))
    rcol = agr
    if pmask:
        rcol = _mix(N, L, rcol, (0.35, 0.35, 0.35), damp)
        rcol = _mix(N, L, rcol, (0.09, 0.09, 0.09), pmask)                    # genangan mengkilap, tidak seperti cermin
    L(rcol, b.inputs["Roughness"])
    # bump: butiran + retakan (dimatikan di genangan)
    bh = _mix(N, L, grain.outputs["Distance"], mid.outputs["Fac"], 0.4)
    bh = _mix(N, L, bh, crack_h, 0.5) if crack_h else bh
    if pattern == "paving" and "self_mortar" in dir():
        bh = _mix(N, L, bh, (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), self_mortar)                     # nat lebih rendah dari blok
    if pmask:
        bh = _mix(N, L, bh, (0.5, 0.5, 0.5), pmask)
    bp = _n(N, "ShaderNodeBump", Strength=bump * 2.5, Distance=0.02); L(bh, bp.inputs["Height"]); L(bp.outputs["Normal"], b.inputs["Normal"])
    return m


def shutter_mat(name, color, seed=0.0):
    """rolling door: rusuk horizontal (bump), cat pudar, karat & kotor di bawah, coretan; tiap toko beda"""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    nt = m.node_tree; N = nt.nodes; L = nt.links.new
    b = N["Principled BSDF"]; b.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.35
    tc = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord"); co = tc.outputs["Object"]; gen = tc.outputs["Generated"]
    rib = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexWave", Scale=1.0, Distortion=0.0, _wave_type="BANDS", _bands_direction="Z")
    mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (1, 1, 5.8); L(co, mp.inputs["Vector"]); L(mp.outputs["Vector"], rib.inputs["Vector"])
    fade = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", _noise_dimensions="4D", Scale=1.2, Detail=5.0, W=seed); L(co, fade.inputs["Vector"])
    base = _ramp(N, [(0.3, tuple(c * 0.7 for c in color)), (0.7, tuple(min(1, c * 1.1 + 0.04) for c in color))])
    L(fade.outputs["Fac"], base.inputs["Fac"])
    rb = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0.72, 0.72, 0.72)), (0.5, (1, 1, 1)), (1.0, (0.85, 0.85, 0.85))]); L(rib.outputs["Fac"], rb.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, base.outputs["Color"], rb.outputs["Color"], 1.0, "MULTIPLY")
    sep = N.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ"); L(gen, sep.inputs[0])
    rust_n = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", _noise_dimensions="4D", Scale=6.0, Detail=8.0, W=seed + 3); L(co, rust_n.inputs["Vector"])
    low = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); low.operation = "SUBTRACT"; low.inputs[0].default_value = 1.0; L(sep.outputs["Z"], low.inputs[1])
    rm = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); rm.operation = "MULTIPLY"; L(low.outputs[0], rm.inputs[0]); L(rust_n.outputs["Fac"], rm.inputs[1])
    rr = _ramp(N, [(0.38, (0, 0, 0)), (0.55, (1, 1, 1))]); L(rm.outputs[0], rr.inputs["Fac"])
    col = _mix(N, L, col, (0.22, 0.1, 0.04), rr.outputs["Color"])
    L(col, b.inputs["Base Color"])
    rg = _ramp(N, [(0.0, (0.35, 0.35, 0.35)), (1.0, (0.8, 0.8, 0.8))]); L(rr.outputs["Color"], rg.inputs["Fac"]); L(rg.outputs["Color"], b.inputs["Roughness"])
    bp = _n(N, "ShaderNodeBump", Strength=0.35, Distance=0.02); L(rib.outputs["Fac"], bp.inputs["Height"]); L(bp.outputs["Normal"], b.inputs["Normal"])
    return m


def wall_mat(name, color, grime=1.0, brick=False, rough=0.85, pbr="damaged_plaster", tile=2.5):
    """tembok: plester tidak rata, kotor di bawah, bekas aliran air, noda lumut; opsional bata."""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    nt = m.node_tree; N = nt.nodes; L = nt.links.new
    b = N["Principled BSDF"]
    tc = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord"); co = tc.outputs["Object"]
    big = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=0.8, Detail=6.0, Roughness=0.6); L(co, big.inputs["Vector"])
    base = _ramp(N, [(0.35, tuple(c * 0.86 for c in color)), (0.7, tuple(min(1, c * 1.06) for c in color))])
    L(big.outputs["Fac"], base.inputs["Fac"]); col = base.outputs["Color"]
    if brick:
        bk = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexBrick", Scale=1.0, **{"Mortar Size": 0.012, "Brick Width": 0.24, "Row Height": 0.07})
        bk.inputs["Color1"].default_value = (*color, 1); bk.inputs["Color2"].default_value = (*[c * 0.75 for c in color], 1)
        bk.inputs["Mortar"].default_value = (0.5, 0.48, 0.45, 1)
        mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); L(co, mp.inputs["Vector"]); mp.inputs["Rotation"].default_value = (math.radians(90), 0, math.radians(90))
        L(mp.outputs["Vector"], bk.inputs["Vector"]); col = _mix(N, L, col, bk.outputs["Color"], 0.85, "MULTIPLY")
    if grime > 0:
        # aliran air: noise yang sangat memanjang ke bawah
        mp = N.new("ShaderNodeMapping"); L(co, mp.inputs["Vector"]); mp.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (6.0, 6.0, 0.35)
        st = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=1.5, Detail=4.0); L(mp.outputs["Vector"], st.inputs["Vector"])
        sr = _ramp(N, [(0.45, (1, 1, 1)), (0.7, (0.6, 0.57, 0.52))]); L(st.outputs["Fac"], sr.inputs["Fac"])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, sr.outputs["Color"], min(1.0, grime), "MULTIPLY")
        # kotor di bagian bawah (sepertiga meter pertama dari tanah, + noise)
        sep = N.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ"); L(tc.outputs["Generated"], sep.inputs[0])
        z = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); z.operation = "MULTIPLY"; L(sep.outputs["Z"], z.inputs[0]); z.inputs[1].default_value = 1.0
        bn = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=4.0); L(co, bn.inputs["Vector"])
        zz = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); zz.operation = "ADD"; L(z.outputs[0], zz.inputs[0])
        bn2 = N.new("ShaderNodeMath"); bn2.operation = "MULTIPLY"; L(bn.outputs["Fac"], bn2.inputs[0]); bn2.inputs[1].default_value = 0.08
        L(bn2.outputs[0], zz.inputs[1])
        br = _ramp(N, [(0.04, (0.45, 0.42, 0.36)), (0.16, (1, 1, 1))]); L(zz.outputs[0], br.inputs["Fac"])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, br.outputs["Color"], min(1.0, grime), "MULTIPLY")
        # noda lumut/jamur
        ms = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=2.5, Detail=8.0, Roughness=0.7); L(co, ms.inputs["Vector"])
        mr = _ramp(N, [(0.6, (1, 1, 1)), (0.72, (0.62, 0.68, 0.5))]); L(ms.outputs["Fac"], mr.inputs["Fac"])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, mr.outputs["Color"], min(1.0, grime * 0.8), "MULTIPLY")
    P_ = _pbr(N, L, co, pbr, tile) if (pbr and not brick) else None
    if P_:                                                    # plester lapuk nyata (CC0): pori, retak rambut, cat mengelupas
        lum = N.new("ShaderNodeCombineColor")
        for i in range(3):
            L(P_["lum"], lum.inputs[i])
        col = _mix(N, L, col, lum.outputs[0], 0.6, "MULTIPLY")
    L(col, b.inputs["Base Color"])
    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rough
    if P_ and "rough" in P_:
        rr = N.new("ShaderNodeMapRange"); L(P_["rough"], rr.inputs["Value"])
        rr.inputs["To Min"].default_value, rr.inputs["To Max"].default_value = rough - 0.2, min(1.0, rough + 0.1)
        L(rr.outputs["Result"], b.inputs["Roughness"])
    fine = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=25.0, Detail=6.0); L(co, fine.inputs["Vector"])
    bp = _n(N, "ShaderNodeBump", Strength=0.12, Distance=0.01); L(fine.outputs["Fac"], bp.inputs["Height"]); L(bp.outputs["Normal"], b.inputs["Normal"])
    if P_ and "normal" in P_:
        L(P_["normal"], bp.inputs["Normal"])
    return m


def roof_mat(name, color):
    """genteng: baris-baris bergelombang"""
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    nt = m.node_tree; N = nt.nodes; L = nt.links.new
    b = N["Principled BSDF"]; co = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord").outputs["Object"]
    bk = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexBrick", Scale=1.0, **{"Mortar Size": 0.008, "Brick Width": 0.3, "Row Height": 0.22, "Bias": 0.3})
    bk.inputs["Color1"].default_value = (*color, 1); bk.inputs["Color2"].default_value = (*[c * 0.7 for c in color], 1)
    bk.inputs["Mortar"].default_value = (*[c * 0.3 for c in color], 1)
    L(co, bk.inputs["Vector"])
    dirt = _n(N, "ShaderNodeTexNoise", Scale=1.2, Detail=6.0); L(co, dirt.inputs["Vector"])
    dr = _ramp(N, [(0.4, (1, 1, 1)), (0.7, (0.45, 0.42, 0.38))]); L(dirt.outputs["Fac"], dr.inputs["Fac"])
    L(_mix(N, L, bk.outputs["Color"], dr.outputs["Color"], 1.0, "MULTIPLY"), b.inputs["Base Color"])
    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.6
    bp = _n(N, "ShaderNodeBump", Strength=0.5); L(bk.outputs["Fac"], bp.inputs["Height"]); L(bp.outputs["Normal"], b.inputs["Normal"])
    return m


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ bangunan
_WALLS = {}


def wall_cached(color, grime=1.0, brick=False):
    key = (tuple(round(c, 3) for c in color), grime, brick)
    if key not in _WALLS:
        _WALLS[key] = wall_mat(f"tembok_{len(_WALLS)}", color, grime, brick)
    return _WALLS[key]


def roof_cached(color=(0.45, 0.13, 0.06)):
    key = ("atap",) + tuple(color)
    if key not in _WALLS:
        _WALLS[key] = roof_mat(f"genteng_{len(_WALLS)}", color)
    return _WALLS[key]


def potted(M, x, y, rnd, z=0.0):
    h = rnd.uniform(0.25, 0.45); r = rnd.uniform(0.13, 0.22)
    B.cyl("pot", (x, y, z + h / 2), r, h, rnd.choice([M.pot, B.mat("pot_hitam", (0.05, 0.05, 0.05), 0.6), B.mat("pot_putih", (0.8, 0.8, 0.78), 0.5)]), verts=12)
    kind = rnd.random()
    if kind < 0.5:
        for k in range(rnd.randint(2, 4)):
            B.ball("daun_pot", (x + rnd.uniform(-0.1, 0.1), y + rnd.uniform(-0.1, 0.1), z + h + rnd.uniform(0.1, 0.35)),
                   rnd.uniform(0.12, 0.22), rnd.choice([M.green, M.green2]), 1)
    else:                                                       # tanaman berdaun panjang (lidah mertua / palem kecil)
        for k in range(7):
            a = k / 7 * math.tau
            o = B.box("daun_panjang", (x + math.cos(a) * 0.08, y + math.sin(a) * 0.08, z + h + 0.25), (0.04, 0.09, 0.55), M.green2,
                      rot=(math.sin(a) * 0.4, -math.cos(a) * 0.4, a))
    if rnd.random() < 0.3:
        B.ball("bunga", (x, y, z + h + 0.3), 0.06, B.mat(f"bunga{rnd.randint(0, 3)}", rnd.choice(FRUIT), 0.6), 1)


def house(M, env, rnd, face_x, y0, width, nx, height=None, color=None, storeys=None, door=True, detail=True, avoid=()):
    """rumah sederhana penuh detail: muka di x=face_x menghadap arah nx (+1 = +X)."""
    storeys = storeys or (2 if rnd.random() < 0.25 else 1)
    h = height or (rnd.uniform(2.8, 3.4) * (1 if storeys == 1 else 1.9))
    depth = 5.0
    cy = y0 + width / 2
    fx = lambda d: face_x + nx * d                                    # posisi x di depan muka rumah
    wall = wall_cached(color or rnd.choice(PASTEL), 1.0, brick=rnd.random() < 0.15)
    B.box("rumah", (face_x - nx * depth / 2, cy, h / 2), (depth, width - 0.08, h), wall)
    B.box("lis_bawah", (fx(0.02), cy, 0.3), (0.06, width - 0.08, 0.6), wall_cached((0.32, 0.3, 0.3), 1.0))
    dy = y0 + width * 0.28
    if door:
        dc = rnd.choice([M.dark_wood, M.wood, B.mat("pintu_hijau", (0.1, 0.3, 0.2), 0.5), B.mat("pintu_biru", (0.1, 0.2, 0.4), 0.5)])
        B.box("kusen_pintu", (fx(0.03), dy, 1.08), (0.06, 1.05, 2.16), M.white)
        B.box("pintu", (fx(0.05), dy, 1.03), (0.06, 0.88, 2.04), dc)
        for k in range(2):
            B.box("panel", (fx(0.085), dy, 0.6 + k * 0.85), (0.02, 0.6, 0.6), B.mat("panel_gelap", (0.05, 0.03, 0.02), 0.6))
        B.ball("gagang", (fx(0.1), dy + 0.33, 1.0), 0.03, M.chrome)
        B.box("ventilasi", (fx(0.035), dy, 2.3), (0.04, 0.9, 0.18), M.white)
        if detail:
            B.box("keset", (fx(0.4), dy, 0.006), (0.45, 0.7, 0.012), B.mat(f"keset{rnd.randint(0, 3)}", rnd.choice(TARP), 0.95))
            if rnd.random() < 0.7:                                    # kanopi seng di atas pintu
                cm = B.mat(f"kanopi{rnd.randint(0, 4)}", rnd.choice([(0.55, 0.56, 0.58), (0.15, 0.35, 0.55), (0.6, 0.2, 0.1), (0.2, 0.45, 0.3)]), 0.4, metal=0.4)
                B.box("kanopi", (fx(0.5), dy + 0.3, 2.55), (1.1, 2.0, 0.04), cm, rot=(0, nx * math.radians(12), 0))
                for k in range(8):
                    B.box("gelombang", (fx(0.5), dy - 0.6 + k * 0.26, 2.57), (1.1, 0.02, 0.03), cm, rot=(0, nx * math.radians(12), 0))
            B.box("nomor", (fx(0.04), dy + 0.7, 1.9), (0.02, 0.22, 0.14), B.mat("plat_nomor", (0.05, 0.2, 0.5), 0.4))
            if rnd.random() < 0.8:
                B.box("meteran", (fx(0.06), y0 + width * 0.5, 1.7), (0.1, 0.25, 0.35), B.mat("kotak_meteran", (0.6, 0.62, 0.6), 0.5))
            for k in range(rnd.randint(0, 3)):
                px_, py_ = fx(0.35 + rnd.uniform(0, 0.15)), y0 + width * rnd.uniform(0.5, 0.95)
                if any(abs(px_ - a_[0]) < 0.9 and abs(py_ - a_[1]) < 1.0 for a_ in avoid):
                    continue          # Phase 4.2-D: tidak di tempat tokoh/properti cerita (tata letak berubah HANYA bila ada yang dilewati)
                potted(M, px_, py_, rnd)
            if rnd.random() < 0.5:                                    # poster / stiker sedot WC dsb.
                B.box("poster", (fx(0.035), y0 + width * rnd.uniform(0.5, 0.9), rnd.uniform(1.2, 1.7)),
                      (0.01, rnd.uniform(0.2, 0.4), rnd.uniform(0.25, 0.4)), B.mat(f"poster{rnd.randint(0, 5)}", rnd.choice(FRUIT + PASTEL), 0.7))
            B.cyl("talang", (fx(0.08), y0 + 0.1, h / 2), 0.04, h, B.mat("pipa", (0.35, 0.36, 0.38), 0.5))
    for st in range(storeys):
        for wy in ([y0 + width * 0.7] if st == 0 else [y0 + width * 0.3, y0 + width * 0.7]):
            z = 1.55 + st * 2.8
            B.box("kusen", (fx(0.03), wy, z), (0.06, 1.15, 1.05), M.white)
            on = env["malam"] and rnd.random() < 0.65
            B.box("kaca", (fx(0.05), wy, z), (0.04, 0.95, 0.85), rnd.choice(M.win_on) if on else M.win_off)
            B.box("ambang", (fx(0.1), wy, z - 0.56), (0.16, 1.25, 0.06), M.white)
            if detail and rnd.random() < 0.7:                          # teralis
                for k in range(6):
                    B.box("teralis", (fx(0.1), wy - 0.42 + k * 0.17, z), (0.015, 0.015, 0.85), M.pole)
                B.box("teralis_h", (fx(0.1), wy, z), (0.015, 0.95, 0.015), M.pole)
            if on and rnd.random() < 0.5:
                # Phase 4.2: muka depan gorden dulu TEPAT sebidang dgn muka depan kaca (keduanya fx(0.07)) -> z-fighting:
                # jendela berganti krem/hijau per frame mengikuti goyang kamera (kedip FAIL uji3 shot 2). Kini 2,5 mm di depan.
                B.box("gorden", (fx(0.08), wy - 0.3, z), (0.015, 0.35, 0.8), B.mat(f"gorden{rnd.randint(0, 3)}", rnd.choice(PASTEL), 0.9))
        if detail and st == storeys - 1 and rnd.random() < 0.35:     # unit AC
            ay = y0 + width * rnd.uniform(0.45, 0.6)
            az = 2.4 + st * 2.8
            B.box("ac", (fx(0.2), ay, az), (0.3, 0.75, 0.5), B.mat("ac", (0.85, 0.85, 0.82), 0.4))
            B.cyl("kipas_ac", (fx(0.36), ay - 0.1, az), 0.18, 0.02, M.black, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=16)
    # atap genteng menjorok + listplang
    roof = roof_cached(rnd.choice([(0.45, 0.13, 0.06), (0.5, 0.2, 0.08), (0.25, 0.25, 0.27), (0.35, 0.1, 0.08)]))
    B.box("atap", (fx(0.35), cy, h + 0.15), (1.5, width + 0.1, 0.08), roof, rot=(0, -nx * math.radians(22), 0))
    B.box("listplang", (fx(1.05), cy, h - 0.12), (0.03, width + 0.1, 0.22), M.white)
    B.box("atap_atas", (face_x - nx * depth / 2, cy, h + 0.05), (depth, width, 0.1), roof)
    if detail and rnd.random() < 0.3:                                 # tandon air di atap
        B.cyl("tandon", (face_x - nx * 2.5, cy, h + 0.6), 0.45, 1.0, B.mat("tandon", (0.2, 0.35, 0.65), 0.4), verts=16)
    if detail and rnd.random() < 0.35:                                # antena TV
        ax = face_x - nx * 1.5
        B.cyl("tiang_antena", (ax, cy, h + 1.2), 0.02, 2.4, M.chrome)
        for k in range(5):
            B.box("antena", (ax, cy, h + 1.6 + k * 0.15), (0.8 - k * 0.12, 0.02, 0.02), M.chrome)
    return h


def wall_lamp(M, env, x, y, z, nx):
    B.box("braket", (x + nx * 0.15, y, z), (0.3, 0.04, 0.04), M.pole)
    B.ball("bohlam_dinding", (x + nx * 0.3, y, z - 0.08), 0.06, M.bulb)
    if env["malam"]:
        B.light("POINT", (x + nx * 0.3, y, z - 0.15), 45, (1.0, 0.7, 0.4), 0.05)


def street_lamp(M, env, x, y, side, warm=True, flicker=False):
    """lampu jalan: tiang di x, lengan ke arah jalan (side=-1 tiang kiri)"""
    lamp_m = B.glow("lampu_hangat" if warm else "lampu_putih", (1.0, 0.62, 0.28) if warm else (0.8, 0.9, 1.0), 30) if env["malam"] else B.mat("kap_mati", (0.75, 0.75, 0.72), 0.3)
    B.cyl("tiang", (x, y, 2.9), 0.07, 5.6, M.pole)
    B.box("lengan", (x - side * 0.6, y, 5.6), (1.3, 0.08, 0.08), M.pole)
    B.box("kap", (x - side * 1.15, y, 5.5), (0.55, 0.3, 0.15), lamp_m)
    if env["malam"]:
        c = (1.0, 0.6, 0.3) if warm else (0.75, 0.85, 1.0)
        s = B.light("SPOT", (x - side * 1.15, y, 5.35), 1100, c, 0.15, spot=(math.radians(125), 0.7))
        B.light("POINT", (x - side * 1.15, y, 5.2), 50, c, 0.3)
        if flicker:
            B.noise(s.data, "energy", 0, 900, 2)
    return (x - side * 1.15, y, 5.4)


def power_pole(M, x, y, h=7.5):
    B.cyl("tiang_listrik", (x, y, h / 2), 0.11, h, M.concrete, verts=10)
    B.box("palang", (x, y, h - 0.4), (1.4, 0.08, 0.08), M.pole)
    tops = []
    for dx in (-0.6, 0.0, 0.6):
        B.cyl("isolator", (x + dx, y, h - 0.28), 0.03, 0.12, M.white)
        tops.append(Vector((x + dx, y, h - 0.25)))
    B.cyl("trafo", (x + 0.25, y, h - 1.6), 0.25, 0.8, B.mat("trafo", (0.4, 0.45, 0.42), 0.5)) if random.random() < 0.3 else None
    return tops


def cable(M, a, b, sag=0.6, n=12):
    pts = []
    for i in range(n + 1):
        t = i / n
        p = a.lerp(b, t); p.z -= sag * 4 * t * (1 - t)
        pts.append(p)
    B.wire(pts, 0.01, M.black)


def tree(M, x, y, s=1.0, rnd=None):
    rnd = rnd or random
    B.cyl("batang", (x, y, 1.2 * s), 0.14 * s, 2.4 * s, M.trunk, verts=8)
    for i in range(4):
        B.ball("daun", (x + rnd.uniform(-0.6, 0.6) * s, y + rnd.uniform(-0.6, 0.6) * s, (2.6 + rnd.uniform(0, 1.0)) * s),
               rnd.uniform(0.9, 1.3) * s, M.green if i % 2 else M.green2, 2)


def coconut(M, x, y, h=7.0, lean=None, rnd=None):
    rnd = rnd or random
    lean = lean if lean is not None else rnd.uniform(-0.25, 0.25)
    seg, p = 7, Vector((x, y, 0))
    for i in range(seg):
        q = p + Vector((lean * (i + 1) / seg * 0.5, 0, h / seg))
        mid = (p + q) / 2
        o = B.cyl("batang_kelapa", mid, 0.13 - i * 0.008, (q - p).length + 0.05, M.trunk, verts=8)
        o.rotation_euler = (0, math.atan2(q.x - p.x, q.z - p.z), 0)
        p = q
    fr = frond_mesh(M)
    for k in range(10):
        a = k / 10 * math.tau + rnd.uniform(-0.2, 0.2)
        o = bpy.data.objects.new("pelepah", fr); B.scene().collection.objects.link(o)
        o.location = p
        o.rotation_euler = (0, math.radians(rnd.uniform(-10, 25)), a)
        o.scale = (s := rnd.uniform(0.85, 1.15), s, s)
        B.noise(o, "rotation_euler", 1, 0.05, 30)
    for k in range(3):
        B.ball("buah_kelapa", p + Vector((math.cos(k * 2.1) * 0.2, math.sin(k * 2.1) * 0.2, -0.25)), 0.13, B.mat("kelapa", (0.3, 0.4, 0.1), 0.6), 1)


def frond_mesh(M):
    """pelepah kelapa: tulang daun melengkung ke bawah dengan anak daun di kiri-kanan"""
    me = bpy.data.meshes.get("pelepah")
    if me:
        return me
    verts, faces = [], []
    n = 10
    for i in range(n + 1):
        t = i / n
        x, z = 2.6 * t, 0.5 * t - 1.6 * t * t                # melengkung ke bawah
        w = 0.55 * math.sin(math.pi * min(1, t * 1.2)) + 0.02
        verts += [(x, 0, z), (x + 0.25, w, z - 0.25 * w), (x + 0.25, -w, z - 0.25 * w)]
    for i in range(n):
        a, b = i * 3, (i + 1) * 3
        faces += [(a, b, b + 1, a + 1), (a, a + 2, b + 2, b)]
    me = bpy.data.meshes.new("pelepah"); me.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)
    me.materials.append(M.green2)
    return me


def mountains(y=260, rnd=None, color=(0.25, 0.38, 0.45)):
    rnd = rnd or random
    m = B.mat("gunung", color, 0.95)
    for i in range(7):
        h = rnd.uniform(35, 80)
        o = B.cyl("gunung", (rnd.uniform(-200, 200), y + rnd.uniform(-30, 30), h / 2), rnd.uniform(60, 110), h, m, verts=7)
        o.data.vertices.foreach_set("co", [c for v in o.data.vertices for c in ((v.co.x * (0.05 if v.co.z > 0 else 1)), (v.co.y * (0.05 if v.co.z > 0 else 1)), v.co.z)])


def jemuran(M, x0, x1, y, z, rnd, flutter=True):
    a, b = Vector((x0, y, z)), Vector((x1, y, z))
    cable(M, a, b, 0.25, 10)
    n = int(abs(x1 - x0) / 0.55)
    for i in range(1, n):
        t = i / n
        p = a.lerp(b, t); p.z -= 0.25 * 4 * t * (1 - t)
        w, hh = rnd.uniform(0.3, 0.5), rnd.uniform(0.4, 0.75)
        o = B.box("baju_jemur", p - Vector((0, 0, hh / 2)), (w, 0.02, hh), B.mat(f"kain{i % 7}", rnd.choice(PASTEL + FRUIT), 0.9))
        if flutter:
            B.noise(o, "rotation_euler", 0, 0.25, 15)


def string_lights(M, env, y, z=6.6, x0=-7.4, x1=7.4, sag=0.9, n=30):
    cols = [(1, 0.15, 0.1), (1, 0.8, 0.1), (0.1, 1, 0.3), (0.15, 0.4, 1), (1, 0.2, 0.7)]
    mats = [B.glow(f"bohlam_hias{i}", c, 18) if env["malam"] else B.mat(f"bohlam_plastik{i}", c, 0.3) for i, c in enumerate(cols)]
    pts = []
    for i in range(n + 1):
        t = i / n
        pts.append(Vector((x0 + (x1 - x0) * t, y, z - sag * 4 * t * (1 - t))))
    B.wire(pts, 0.012, M.pole)
    for i, p in enumerate(pts[1:-1]):
        B.ball("bohlam_hias", p - Vector((0, 0, 0.07)), 0.055, mats[i % len(mats)])


def bunting(M, y, z=6.0, x0=-7.4, x1=7.4, sag=0.7, n=22, colorful=False):
    fm = [B.mat(f"bendera{i}", c, 0.7) for i, c in enumerate([(0.85, 0.05, 0.05), (0.95, 0.95, 0.95), (1, 0.75, 0.05), (0.1, 0.5, 0.9), (0.1, 0.7, 0.3)])]
    me = bpy.data.meshes.get("segitiga") or bpy.data.meshes.new("segitiga")
    if not me.vertices:
        me.from_pydata([(-0.17, 0, 0), (0.17, 0, 0), (0, 0, -0.42)], [], [(0, 1, 2)])
    pts = [Vector((x0 + (x1 - x0) * i / n, y, z - sag * 4 * (i / n) * (1 - i / n))) for i in range(n + 1)]
    B.wire(pts, 0.008, M.pole)
    for i, p in enumerate(pts[1:-1]):
        o = bpy.data.objects.new("bendera", me.copy()); B.scene().collection.objects.link(o)
        o.data.materials.append(fm[i % 5] if colorful else fm[i % 2])
        o.location = p
        B.noise(o, "rotation_euler", 0, 0.5, 18)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ properti
def floor_z(x, y, lantai):
    return lantai(x) if lantai else 0.0


def build_prop(p, M, env, info, lantai=None):
    kind = p["jenis"]
    x, y = p["posisi"][:2]
    gz = p.get("tinggi_lantai", floor_z(x, y, lantai))
    try:                                                        # Phase 4B-B5: jangkar dudukan dunia (engine/seats.py)
        import kit_anchors
        import seats
        for a in seats.anchors_for(p, gz):
            kit_anchors.add("kursi", a["name"], a["pos"], maju=a["maju"], lebar=a["lebar"], tipe=a["tipe"], tinggi=a["tinggi"])
    except ImportError:
        pass
    rot = math.radians(p.get("arah", 0))
    rnd = random.Random(hash((kind, x, y)) & 0xffff)
    root = B.empty(kind, (x, y, gz), rot)
    P = dict(parent=root)
    c = lambda d: col(p.get("warna"), d)
    if kind == "gerobak_makan":
        side = -1 if x > 0 else 1
        root.rotation_euler.z = math.radians(p.get("arah", 0)) + (0 if side == -1 else math.pi)
        gerobak_makan(p, M, env, info, root, x, y, gz, side)
        return
    if kind == "dingklik":
        m = B.mat("plastik_dingklik", c((200, 20, 20)), 0.3)
        h = float(p.get("tinggi", 0.26))
        B.cyl("dudukan", (0, 0, h - 0.02), 0.17, 0.04, m, verts=24, **P)
        for k in range(4):
            a = k * math.pi / 2 + math.pi / 4
            B.box("kaki_dingklik", (math.cos(a) * 0.12, math.sin(a) * 0.12, (h - 0.04) / 2), (0.04, 0.04, h - 0.04), m, **P)
        return
    if kind == "aset":                                          # model eksternal berlisensi (assets/external)
        o = ext_model(p["id"], (0, 0, float(p.get("z", 0.0))), 0.0, p.get("lebar"), p.get("tinggi"), parent=root, name=p.get("nama", p["id"]))
        if o is None:
            print(f"[aset] '{p['id']}' belum diunduh: jalankan scripts/aset_eksternal.py (dilewati)")
        return
    if kind == "meja_lipat":
        B.box("daun_meja", (0, 0, 0.45), (0.6, 1.0, 0.025), B.mat("meja_lipat", c((235, 235, 230)), 0.3), **P)
        import kit_anchors
        nm = p.get("nama", "meja_lipat")
        for k, dy in ((0, 0.0), (1, 0.3), (2, -0.3)):                 # titik taruh di permukaan meja (dunia)
            kit_anchors.add("meja", f"{nm}.meja" + (f"{k}" if k else ""),
                            (x - math.sin(rot) * dy, y + math.cos(rot) * dy, gz + 0.4625))
        for dx in (-0.25, 0.25):
            for dy in (-0.45, 0.45):
                B.cyl("kaki", (dx, dy, 0.22), 0.012, 0.44, M.chrome, verts=8, **P)
        for k in range(int(p.get("gelas", 2))):
            gx = rnd.uniform(-0.15, 0.15)
            B.cyl("gelas_teh", (gx, -0.3 + k * 0.3, 0.52), 0.035, 0.1, glass_mat(), verts=16, **P)
            B.cyl("teh", (gx, -0.3 + k * 0.3, 0.505), 0.03, 0.07, B.mat("teh_isi", (0.45, 0.12, 0.02), 0.1), verts=16, **P)
        return
    if kind == "gerobak":
        side = -1 if x > 0 else 1
        root.rotation_euler.z = 0 if side == -1 else math.pi
        body = B.mat("gerobak_" + str(p.get("warna", "tosca")), c((20, 150, 180)), 0.45)
        B.box("gerobak", (0, 0, 0.6), (0.8, 1.7, 0.9), body, **P)
        B.box("list", (-0.41, 0, 0.8), (0.02, 1.7, 0.12), M.red, **P)
        B.box("meja", (-0.05, 0, 1.07), (0.95, 1.85, 0.05), M.wood, **P)
        for dy in (-0.7, 0.7):
            B.cyl("roda", (-0.42, dy, 0.25), 0.25, 0.06, M.pole, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=16, **P)
        for dx, dy in ((-0.4, -0.85), (-0.4, 0.85), (0.4, -0.85), (0.4, 0.85)):
            B.cyl("tiang_atap", (dx, dy, 1.6), 0.025, 1.1, M.wood, **P)
        for i in range(8):
            B.box("atap", (0, -0.95 + i * 0.27 + 0.135, 2.17), (1.1, 0.27, 0.05), M.red if i % 2 == 0 else M.white, **P)
        if p.get("tulisan"):
            B.box("papan_gerobak", (-0.56, 0, 2.4), (0.04, 1.4, 0.3), M.board, **P)
            t = B.text(p["tulisan"], (x + side * 0.6, y, gz + 2.4), 0.2, B.glow("neon_gerobak", (1.0, 0.8, 0.1), 10 if env["malam"] else 2), side)
        for i, cc in enumerate(FRUIT * 2):
            B.box("sachet", (-0.52, -0.75 + i * 0.135, 1.9), (0.01, 0.11, 0.33), B.mat(f"sachet{i % 6}", cc, 0.5), **P)
        for dy in (-0.45, 0.0, 0.45):
            B.cyl("termos", (0.1, dy, 1.27), 0.09, 0.35, M.chrome, verts=16, **P)
        glass = (x + side * 0.3, y - side * 0.6 * (1 if side == -1 else -1), gz + 1.15)
        B.cyl("gelas", (-0.3, -0.6, 1.15), 0.04, 0.12, B.mat("gelas_kopi", (0.5, 0.3, 0.15), 0.1), **P)
        if p.get("uap", True):
            info["steam"].append((x + side * 0.3, y + (-0.6 if side == -1 else 0.6), gz + 1.15))
        B.ball("bohlam_gerobak", (-0.1, 0, 1.8), 0.07, M.bulb, **P)
        if env["malam"]:
            B.light("POINT", (-0.1, 0, 1.75), 90, (1.0, 0.65, 0.35), 0.05, parent=root)
            info["bulbs"].append((x + side * 0.1, y, gz + 1.85))
        B.box("bangku", (-0.9, 0.2, 0.3), (0.35, 1.5, 0.05), M.wood, **P)
        for dy in (-0.6, 0.6):
            B.box("kaki_bangku", (-0.9, 0.2 + dy, 0.12), (0.3, 0.05, 0.3), M.wood, **P)
        if p.get("jam"):
            B.box("jam", (-0.43, -0.35, 0.47), (0.06, 0.5, 0.2), M.board, **P)
            B.text(str(p["jam"]), (x + side * 0.47, y + (-0.35 if side == -1 else 0.35), gz + 0.47), 0.15, B.glow("digit", (0.2, 1.0, 0.5), 15), side)
    elif kind == "rambu_parkir":
        B.cyl("tiang_rambu", (0, 0, 1.25), 0.035, 2.5, M.pole, **P)
        B.box("rambu", (0, 0, 2.35), (0.04, 0.5, 0.5), B.mat("rambu_biru", (0.05, 0.2, 0.7), 0.4, (0.05, 0.2, 0.7), 0.6), **P)
        f = 1 if x < 0 else -1
        B.text("P", (x + f * 0.03, y, gz + 2.35), 0.42, B.glow("huruf_p", (1, 1, 1), 1.5), f)
    elif kind == "garis_parkir":
        for i in range(int(p.get("jumlah", 4))):
            B.box("garis_parkir", (0, i * 2.5, 0.003), (1.9, 0.1, 0.01), M.paint, **P)
    elif kind == "tong_sampah":
        B.cyl("tong_sampah", (0, 0, 0.4), 0.25, 0.8, B.mat("tong", c((40, 130, 60)), 0.6), verts=14, **P)
    elif kind == "pot_tanaman":
        B.cyl("pot", (0, 0, 0.25), 0.22, 0.5, M.pot, verts=12, **P)
        B.ball("tanaman", (0, 0, 0.8), 0.4, M.green, 2, **P)
    elif kind == "bangku":
        B.box("dudukan", (0, 0, 0.45), (0.4, 1.5, 0.05), M.wood, **P)
        for dy in (-0.6, 0.6):
            B.box("kaki", (0, dy, 0.22), (0.35, 0.05, 0.45), M.wood, **P)
    elif kind == "motor":
        body = B.mat("motor_" + str(p.get("warna", "merah")), c((200, 40, 40)), 0.35)
        for dy in (-0.62, 0.62):
            B.cyl("ban", (0, dy, 0.3), 0.3, 0.1, M.tire, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=20, **P)
            B.cyl("velg", (0, dy, 0.3), 0.15, 0.11, M.chrome, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=12, **P)
        B.box("bodi", (0, 0.0, 0.62), (0.3, 0.95, 0.32), body, **P)
        B.box("tameng", (0, 0.5, 0.75), (0.34, 0.12, 0.55), body, **P)
        B.box("jok", (0, -0.2, 0.84), (0.28, 0.65, 0.1), M.tire, **P)
        B.box("setang", (0, 0.55, 1.08), (0.7, 0.05, 0.05), M.pole, **P)
        B.ball("lampu", (0, 0.6, 0.95), 0.07, M.head, **P)
        B.box("lampu_rem", (0, -0.55, 0.75), (0.15, 0.03, 0.05), M.tail, **P)
        if p.get("lampu_nyala", False):
            B.light("SPOT", (0, 0.68, 0.95), 300, (1, 0.95, 0.85), 0.05, rot=(math.radians(-80), 0, 0), spot=(math.radians(50), 0.5), parent=root)
    elif kind == "mobil":
        body = B.mat("mobil_" + str(p.get("warna", "putih")), c((230, 230, 230)), 0.25, metal=0.3)
        for dx in (-0.8, 0.8):
            for dy in (-1.25, 1.25):
                B.cyl("ban", (dx, dy, 0.33), 0.33, 0.22, M.tire, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=20, **P)
        B.box("bodi", (0, 0, 0.6), (1.75, 3.9, 0.6), body, **P)
        B.box("kabin", (0, -0.25, 1.15), (1.55, 2.1, 0.55), M.glass_dark, **P)
        B.box("atap", (0, -0.25, 1.44), (1.5, 1.9, 0.05), body, **P)
        for dx in (-0.6, 0.6):
            B.box("lampu", (dx, 1.95, 0.7), (0.35, 0.03, 0.12), M.head, **P)
            B.box("lampu_rem", (dx, -1.95, 0.72), (0.3, 0.03, 0.1), M.tail, **P)
        if p.get("lampu_nyala", False):
            for dx in (-0.6, 0.6):
                B.light("SPOT", (dx, 2.0, 0.7), 400, (1, 0.95, 0.85), 0.05, rot=(math.radians(-85), 0, 0), spot=(math.radians(45), 0.5), parent=root)
    elif kind == "sepeda":
        frame = B.mat("sepeda_" + str(p.get("warna", "biru")), c((40, 80, 160)), 0.4)
        for dy in (-0.5, 0.5):
            o = B.cyl("ban", (0, dy, 0.33), 0.33, 0.04, M.tire, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=20, **P)
        B.box("rangka", (0, 0, 0.55), (0.04, 0.9, 0.04), frame, rot=(math.radians(10), 0, 0), **P)
        B.box("tiang_jok", (0, -0.15, 0.7), (0.04, 0.04, 0.4), frame, **P)
        B.box("jok", (0, -0.15, 0.92), (0.12, 0.25, 0.05), M.black, **P)
        B.box("setang", (0, 0.45, 0.95), (0.5, 0.04, 0.04), M.pole, **P)
    elif kind == "pohon":
        tree(M, x, y, float(p.get("ukuran", 1.0)), rnd)
    elif kind == "pohon_kelapa":
        coconut(M, x, y, float(p.get("tinggi", 7.0)), rnd=rnd)
    elif kind == "tiang_listrik":
        power_pole(M, x, y)
    elif kind == "jemuran":
        x1 = p.get("sampai_x", x + 3)
        jemuran(M, x, x1, y, gz + p.get("tinggi", 2.4), rnd)
    elif kind == "meja":
        top = B.mat("meja_" + str(p.get("warna", "kayu")), c((122, 78, 42)), 0.45)
        B.aus(top, variasi=0.06, kotor=0.1, gores=0.15, celah=0.2, serat=0.8, skala=2.0)
        w, d = p.get("ukuran", [1.2, 0.7])
        th = float(p.get("tinggi", 0.75))                     # Phase 4.1: tinggi meja (meja kopi, meja makan, ...)
        B.box("daun_meja", (0, 0, th), (d, w, 0.05), top, **P)
        B.box("rangka_meja", (0, 0, th - 0.06), (d - 0.08, w - 0.08, 0.07), M.dark_wood, **P)
        for dx in (-d / 2 + 0.06, d / 2 - 0.06):
            for dy in (-w / 2 + 0.06, w / 2 - 0.06):
                B.cyl("kaki", (dx, dy, (th - 0.03) / 2), 0.026, th - 0.03, M.dark_wood, verts=10, **P)
                B.cyl("kaki_bubut", (dx, dy, th * 0.55), 0.036, 0.06, M.dark_wood, verts=10, **P)
        if p.get("taplak", w >= 0.8):                             # Phase 4.2: taplak renda + kaca (khas meja tamu)
            B.box("taplak", (0, 0, th + 0.027), (d * 0.62, w * 0.75, 0.004), B.mat("taplak_renda", (0.82, 0.78, 0.68), 0.9), bevel=0, **P)
        if p.get("isi", True):
            B.cyl("gelas", (0.1, 0.2, th + 0.08), 0.04, 0.12, B.mat("teh", (0.6, 0.3, 0.1), 0.1), **P)

    elif kind == "kursi":

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ aset detail: gerobak makanan
def hemi(name, loc, r, mat, depth=0.45, parent=None, segments=24):
    """setengah bola (wajan, mangkok) — sisi cekung menghadap ke atas"""
    import bmesh
    bm = bmesh.new()
    bmesh.ops.create_uvsphere(bm, u_segments=segments, v_segments=12, radius=r)
    bmesh.ops.delete(bm, geom=[v for v in bm.verts if v.co.z > 1e-4], context="VERTS")
    for v in bm.verts:
        v.co.z *= depth
    bmesh.ops.solidify(bm, geom=list(bm.faces), thickness=0.006)
    o = B._finish(B._obj(name, bm, loc, (0, 0, 0), smooth=True), name, mat, parent)
    return o


def ring(name, loc, R, r, mat, parent=None, rot=(0, 0, 0), seg=32, tseg=8):
    """torus (bibir wajan/piring, pegangan, ban): R = jari-jari besar, r = tebal"""
    import bmesh
    bm = bmesh.new()
    vs = [[bm.verts.new(((R + r * math.cos(b)) * math.cos(a), (R + r * math.cos(b)) * math.sin(a), r * math.sin(b)))
           for b in (k * math.tau / tseg for k in range(tseg))] for a in (j * math.tau / seg for j in range(seg))]
    for j in range(seg):
        for k in range(tseg):
            bm.faces.new((vs[j][k], vs[(j + 1) % seg][k], vs[(j + 1) % seg][(k + 1) % tseg], vs[j][(k + 1) % tseg]))
    return B._finish(B._obj(name, bm, loc, rot, smooth=True), name, mat, parent)


# Phase 4.1-C2: preset keausan per JENIS material (kata kunci nama material; berlaku untuk semua cerita/lokasi).
WEAR = [  # (kata kunci, parameter bl_utils.aus)
    (("wajan",), dict(variasi=0.06, kotor=0.0, gores=0.18, celah=0.2, warna_gores=(0.13, 0.12, 0.115, 1), skala=2.0)),
    (("aluminium", "logam", "chrome", "rel", "tiang", "kotak_gulung", "siku"), dict(variasi=0.05, kotor=0.3, gores=0.45, celah=0.35)),
    (("gerobak_", "panel", "list_", "pintu_", "cat_", "motor_", "mobil_", "sepeda_", "tong"),
     dict(variasi=0.06, kotor=0.4, gores=0.35, celah=0.45, warna_gores=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1))),
    (("kayu", "meja_", "kursi_", "kusen", "lemari_", "papan", "bambu"),
     dict(variasi=0.18, kotor=0.25, gores=0.2, celah=0.5, warna_gores=(0.55, 0.4, 0.25, 1), skala=1.5, serat=0.8)),
    (("plastik", "dingklik", "ember", "termos", "tandon", "gas_melon", "pot"),
     dict(variasi=0.05, kotor=0.35, gores=0.25, celah=0.3, warna_gores=(0.85, 0.85, 0.82, 1), skala=1.5)),
    (("keramik", "piring"), dict(variasi=0.02, kotor=0.0, celah=0.2)),
    (("spanduk", "stiker", "kanopi", "payung_", "terpal"), dict(variasi=0.07, kotor=0.25, rembes=0.3, celah=0.2)),
    (("beton", "kanstin", "parapet", "lis_"), dict(variasi=0.1, kotor=0.45, rembes=0.35, celah=0.5)),
]


def wear_pass(latar):
    """beri variasi/aus pada material lingkungan & properti yang masih polos (warna tetap, tanpa tekstur).
    Dilewati: emisif, kaca/transmisi, BLENDED, material bertekstur, material tokoh (dibuat sesudahnya).
    latar.aus: false mematikan; latar.aus_kuat mengali kotor/gores."""
    if latar.get("aus", True) is False:
        return {"aktif": False}
    k = float(latar.get("aus_kuat", 1.0))
    done = []
    for m in list(bpy.data.materials):
        if not m.use_nodes or m.get("aus"):
            continue
        b = m.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
        if b is None or b.inputs["Base Color"].is_linked:
            continue
        if b.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value > 0 or getattr(m, "surface_render_method", "") == "BLENDED":
            continue
        if "Transmission Weight" in b.inputs and b.inputs["Transmission Weight"].default_value > 0.1:
            continue
        nm = m.name.lower()
        for keys, prm in WEAR:
            if any(kw in nm for kw in keys):
                prm = dict(prm)
                for kk in ("kotor", "gores", "rembes"):
                    if kk in prm:
                        prm[kk] *= k
                B.aus(m, **prm); done.append(m.name)
                break
    return {"aktif": True, "jumlah": len(done), "material": sorted(done)}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ makanan: nasi goreng (dipakai wajan & piring)
def _food_mat(name, color, rough=0.45, coat=0.0, var=0.0):
    """material makanan; var > 0: variasi warna per butir (Object Info Random)"""
    m = bpy.data.materials.get(name)
    if m:
        return m
    m = B.mat(name, color, rough)
    nt = m.node_tree; b = nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
    if coat and "Coat Weight" in b.inputs:                     # kilap minyak
        b.inputs["Coat Weight"].default_value = coat; b.inputs["Coat Roughness"].default_value = 0.25
    if var:
        oi = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeObjectInfo")
        hsv = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeHueSaturation")
        hsv.inputs["Color"].default_value = (*color, 1)
        mr = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeMapRange"); mr.inputs["To Min"].default_value = 1 - var; mr.inputs["To Max"].default_value = 1 + var
        nt.links.new(oi.outputs["Random"], mr.inputs["Value"]); nt.links.new(mr.outputs[0], hsv.inputs["Value"])
        nt.links.new(hsv.outputs[0], b.inputs["Base Color"])
    return m


def nasi_goreng(name, parent, loc, r, h, density=1.0):
    """gundukan nasi goreng: kubah dasar + ribuan butir nasi & topping (telur orak-arik, cabai, daun bawang, ayam)
    sebagai geometri nyata yang digabung per bahan (ringan, selalu ter-render, tanpa sistem partikel)"""
    import bmesh
    from mathutils import Matrix, Euler, Vector
    rnd = random.Random(sum(map(ord, name)))
    surf = lambda rho, th: h * max(0.0, 1 - (rho / r) ** 2) ** 0.6 * (1 + 0.12 * math.sin(3 * th + rho * 40))
    bm = bmesh.new()
    bmesh.ops.create_uvsphere(bm, u_segments=20, v_segments=10, radius=r)
    bmesh.ops.delete(bm, geom=[v for v in bm.verts if v.co.z < -1e-4], context="VERTS")
    for v in bm.verts:
        rho = math.hypot(v.co.x, v.co.y)
        v.co.z = surf(min(rho, r), math.atan2(v.co.y, v.co.x)) * 0.92
    base = B._finish(B._obj(name, bm, loc, (0, 0, 0), smooth=True), name, _food_mat("nasi_dasar", (0.5, 0.24, 0.07), 0.5, 0.3), parent)
    kinds = [  # (bahan, warna, bobot, pembuat geometri)
        ("butir_a", (0.62, 0.3, 0.08), 30, "grain"), ("butir_b", (0.42, 0.18, 0.05), 22, "grain"),     # nasi berkecap
        ("butir_c", (0.78, 0.46, 0.17), 18, "grain"), ("telur_orak", (1.0, 0.75, 0.15), 9, "egg"),
        ("cabai", (0.8, 0.05, 0.02), 5, "disc"), ("daun_bawang", (0.15, 0.55, 0.1), 7, "rod"),
        ("ayam", (0.85, 0.62, 0.38), 6, "cube")]
    tot = sum(k[2] for k in kinds)
    bms = {k[0]: bmesh.new() for k in kinds}
    n = int(1700 * (r / 0.12) ** 2 * density)
    for _ in range(n):
        x = rnd.random() * tot
        for kind in kinds:
            x -= kind[2]
            if x <= 0:
                break
        nm, col, _, shape = kind
        rho = r * math.sqrt(rnd.random()) * 0.96
        th = rnd.uniform(0, math.tau)
        p = Vector((math.cos(th) * rho, math.sin(th) * rho, surf(rho, th) * 0.92 + rnd.uniform(-0.002, 0.004)))
        rot = Euler((rnd.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rnd.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rnd.uniform(0, math.tau))).to_matrix().to_4x4()
        k = rnd.uniform(0.8, 1.25)
        b = bms[nm]
        if shape == "grain":
            M_ = Matrix.Translation(p) @ rot @ Matrix.Diagonal((0.0085 * k, 0.0042 * k, 0.0038 * k, 1))
            bmesh.ops.create_uvsphere(b, u_segments=6, v_segments=4, radius=1.0, matrix=M_)
        elif shape == "egg":
            M_ = Matrix.Translation(p) @ rot @ Matrix.Diagonal((0.012 * k, 0.009 * k, 0.005 * k, 1))
            bmesh.ops.create_icosphere(b, subdivisions=1, radius=1.0, matrix=M_)
        elif shape == "disc":
            M_ = Matrix.Translation(p) @ rot
            bmesh.ops.create_cone(b, cap_ends=True, segments=8, radius1=0.006 * k, radius2=0.006 * k, depth=0.002, matrix=M_)
        elif shape == "rod":
            M_ = Matrix.Translation(p) @ rot @ Matrix.Rotation(math.pi / 2, 4, "X")
            bmesh.ops.create_cone(b, cap_ends=True, segments=6, radius1=0.0028, radius2=0.0028, depth=0.011 * k, matrix=M_)
        else:
            M_ = Matrix.Translation(p) @ rot @ Matrix.Diagonal((1.6 * k, 0.7, 0.6, 1))
            bmesh.ops.create_cube(b, size=0.009, matrix=M_)
    for nm, col, _, shape in kinds:
        b = bms[nm]
        if not b.verts:
            b.free(); continue
        o = B._finish(B._obj(f"{name}_{nm}", b, (0, 0, 0), (0, 0, 0), smooth=shape in ("grain", "egg")), f"{name}_{nm}",
                      _food_mat("ng_" + nm, col, 0.38 if shape == "grain" else 0.45, 0.35, 0.15), base)
    return base


def glass_mat(name="kaca_bening", tint=(0.9, 0.95, 1.0), rough=0.03):
    m = bpy.data.materials.get(name)
    if m:
        return m
    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name); m.use_nodes = True
    b = m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
    b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*tint, 1); b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rough
    b.inputs["Alpha"].default_value = 0.18                     # kaca: tembus pandang + pantulan (cepat di Eevee)
    b.inputs["IOR"].default_value = 1.45
    if "Coat Weight" in b.inputs:
        b.inputs["Coat Weight"].default_value = 1.0; b.inputs["Coat Roughness"].default_value = 0.02
    try:
        m.surface_render_method = "BLENDED"
        m.use_transparency_overlap = False
    except AttributeError:
        pass
    return m


def flame_mat():
    """api kompor gas: inti biru terang di pangkal -> biru transparan -> ujung jingga tipis -> hilang.
    Gradien sepanjang tinggi lidah api (koordinat Generated Z), dicampur Transparent (BLENDED)."""
    m = bpy.data.materials.get("api_gas")
    if m:
        return m
    m = bpy.data.materials.new("api_gas"); m.use_nodes = True
    m.surface_render_method = "BLENDED"
    nt = m.node_tree; N, L = nt.nodes, nt.links
    for n in list(N):
        N.remove(n)
    out = N.new("ShaderNodeOutputMaterial")
    tc = N.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord"); sep = N.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ")
    L.new(tc.outputs["Generated"], sep.inputs[0])
    ramp = N.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
    cr = ramp.color_ramp
    cr.elements[0].position, cr.elements[0].color = 0.0, (0.55, 0.75, 1.0, 1.0)
    cr.elements[1].position, cr.elements[1].color = 1.0, (1.0, 0.45, 0.1, 1.0)
    e = cr.elements.new(0.35); e.color = (0.12, 0.3, 1.0, 1.0)
    e = cr.elements.new(0.8); e.color = (0.2, 0.3, 0.9, 1.0)
    L.new(sep.outputs["Z"], ramp.inputs[0])
    aramp = N.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")                         # alpha: pekat di pangkal, memudar ke ujung
    ar = aramp.color_ramp
    ar.elements[0].position, ar.elements[0].color = 0.0, (0.95, 0.95, 0.95, 1)
    ar.elements[1].position, ar.elements[1].color = 1.0, (0, 0, 0, 1)
    e = ar.elements.new(0.6); e.color = (0.45, 0.45, 0.45, 1)
    L.new(sep.outputs["Z"], aramp.inputs[0])
    em = N.new("ShaderNodeEmission"); em.inputs["Strength"].default_value = 14
    L.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], em.inputs["Color"])
    tr = N.new("ShaderNodeBsdfTransparent")
    mix = N.new("ShaderNodeMixShader")
    L.new(aramp.outputs["Color"], mix.inputs[0]); L.new(tr.outputs[0], mix.inputs[1]); L.new(em.outputs[0], mix.inputs[2])
    L.new(mix.outputs[0], out.inputs["Surface"])
    return m


def gerobak_makan(p, M, env, info, root, x, y, gz, side):
    """gerobak nasi goreng/bakso detail. Lokal: muka pembeli di -X, panjang di Y, tinggi meja 'tinggi_meja'."""
    P = dict(parent=root)
    rnd = random.Random(int(abs(x * 100 + y * 10)))
    H = float(p.get("tinggi_meja", 0.45))                         # skala kartun (tubuh pendek, kepala besar): meja setinggi pinggang
    Lg, D = 1.7, 0.7
    body = B.mat("gerobak_" + str(p.get("warna", "biru")), col(p.get("warna"), (30, 90, 170)), 0.35)
    trim = B.mat("list_merah", (0.7, 0.04, 0.03), 0.4)
    alu = B.mat("aluminium", (0.75, 0.76, 0.78), 0.25, metal=1.0)
    wood = B.mat("kayu_meja", (0.45, 0.27, 0.12), 0.5)
    # kabinet: rangka + panel menjorok + list
    B.box("kabinet", (0, 0, H / 2 + 0.06), (D, Lg, H - 0.12), body, **P)
    for k in range(3):
        B.box("panel", (-D / 2 - 0.005, -Lg / 3 + k * Lg / 3, H / 2 + 0.05), (0.02, Lg / 3 - 0.1, H - 0.3), B.mat("panel_terang", tuple(min(1, c * 1.35) for c in col(p.get("warna"), (30, 90, 170))), 0.3), **P)
    B.box("list_atas", (-D / 2 - 0.012, 0, H - 0.05), (0.025, Lg + 0.02, 0.06), trim, **P)
    for k in range(3):                                          # engsel & pegangan pintu panel (sisi pembeli)
        py = -Lg / 3 + k * Lg / 3
        B.box("pegangan_panel", (-D / 2 - 0.03, py + Lg / 6 - 0.12, H / 2 + 0.05), (0.02, 0.02, 0.1), alu, **P)
        for dz in (-0.12, 0.12):
            B.box("engsel", (-D / 2 - 0.022, py - Lg / 6 + 0.06, H / 2 + 0.05 + dz), (0.012, 0.025, 0.04), alu, **P)
    for dy in (-Lg / 2, Lg / 2):                                 # tiang sudut rangka (siku aluminium)
        for dx in (-D / 2, D / 2):
            B.box("siku_rangka", (dx, dy, H / 2 + 0.06), (0.03, 0.03, H - 0.1), alu, **P)
    B.box("list_bawah", (-D / 2 - 0.012, 0, 0.14), (0.025, Lg + 0.02, 0.05), trim, **P)
    # Phase 4.2: permukaan kerja = aluminium brushed (kasar 0,6, di atas batas raytracing EEVEE 0,5). Pada kasar 0,25
    # pantulan raytraced ruang-layar dari etalase/piring berbintik dan pada GPU Colab sesekali "menyala" sebagai bidang
    # terang satu frame (kedip benchmark f466/4.1, f478/f482/4.2). Kasar di atas batas -> pantulan probe yang stabil.
    alu_kerja = B.mat("aluminium_kerja", (0.32, 0.33, 0.35), 0.6, metal=1.0)
    B.box("meja", (-0.05, 0, H + 0.015), (D + 0.16, Lg + 0.12, 0.03), alu_kerja, **P)
    for dy in (-0.62, 0.62):                                   # roda sepeda
        for k in range(10):
            a = k / 10 * math.pi
            B.box("jari_roda", (D / 2 + 0.03, dy, 0.24), (0.006, 0.42, 0.006), alu, rot=(a, 0, 0), bevel=0, **P)
        ring("ban", (D / 2 + 0.03, dy, 0.24), 0.225, 0.02, M.tire, root, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), seg=36, tseg=8)
        ring("pelek", (D / 2 + 0.03, dy, 0.24), 0.205, 0.008, alu, root, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), seg=36, tseg=6)
        B.cyl("as_roda", (D / 2 + 0.03, dy, 0.24), 0.025, 0.06, alu, rot=(0, math.radians(90), 0), verts=12, **P)
    for dy in (-0.75, 0.75):
        B.box("kaki", (-D / 2 + 0.08, dy, 0.06), (0.05, 0.05, 0.12), alu, **P)
    B.box("pegangan", (0, Lg / 2 + 0.35, H - 0.05), (0.04, 0.6, 0.04), alu, **P)
    # etalase kaca
    gh, gd, gl = 0.28, 0.42, Lg * 0.5                         # etalase hanya di separuh belakang -> penjual terlihat
    ex, ey = -0.08, Lg / 4
    B.box("etalase_kaca", (ex, ey, H + 0.03 + gh / 2), (gd, gl, gh), glass_mat(), bevel=0.004, **P)
    for dx in (-gd / 2, gd / 2):
        for dz in (0.03, gh + 0.03):
            B.box("rangka_etalase", (ex + dx, ey, H + dz), (0.02, gl + 0.02, 0.02), alu, **P)
    for k in range(3):
        for dx in (-gd / 2, gd / 2):
            B.box("tiang_etalase", (ex + dx, ey - gl / 2 + k * gl / 2, H + 0.03 + gh / 2), (0.02, 0.02, gh), alu, **P)
    B.box("rak_etalase", (ex, ey, H + 0.03 + gh * 0.5), (gd - 0.02, gl - 0.02, 0.01), glass_mat(), bevel=0, **P)
    food = [((0.95, 0.85, 0.6), "telur"), ((0.95, 0.8, 0.35), "mi"), ((0.2, 0.55, 0.12), "sawi"), ((0.85, 0.15, 0.1), "sosis"),
            ((0.9, 0.2, 0.05), "cabai"), ((0.98, 0.95, 0.85), "bawang")]
    for k in range(12):
        c, kind = food[k % len(food)]
        fy = ey - gl / 2 + 0.1 + (k % 6) * (gl - 0.2) / 5
        fz = H + 0.06 if k < 6 else H + 0.05 + gh * 0.5
        if kind in ("telur", "bawang"):
            for j in range(5):
                o = B.ball(kind, (ex + rnd.uniform(-0.1, 0.1), fy + rnd.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), fz + 0.025), 0.025, B.mat(kind, c, 0.4), 1, **P)
                o.scale = (1, 1, 1.25)
        else:
            B.box("wadah", (ex, fy, fz + 0.02), (0.13, 0.12, 0.04), alu, **P)
            for j in range(6):
                B.box(kind, (ex + rnd.uniform(-0.08, 0.08), fy + rnd.uniform(-0.08, 0.08), fz + 0.05), (0.05, 0.02, 0.012), B.mat(kind, c, 0.5),
                      rot=(0, 0, rnd.uniform(0, 3.1)), **P)
    if env["malam"]:                                           # lampu di dalam etalase
        B.light("AREA", (ex, ey, H + gh - 0.01), 25, (1.0, 0.92, 0.75), rot=(0, 0, 0), parent=root).data.size = 0.3
    # kompor + wajan + api (sisi penjual, ujung depan)
    wy = -Lg / 2 + 0.35
    kx = float(p.get("kompor_x", 0.12))                        # kompor di sisi penjual (+x lokal): penjual berdiri di luar badan gerobak
    B.box("kompor", (kx, wy, H + 0.03), (0.3, 0.3, 0.06), M.black, **P)      # kompor rendah: wajan terjangkau tangan
    wmat = B.mat("wajan", (0.06, 0.06, 0.07), 0.35, metal=0.9)
    wok = hemi("wajan", (kx, wy, H + 0.18), 0.2, wmat, 0.5, root)
    ring("bibir_wajan", (kx, wy, H + 0.18), 0.2, 0.006, wmat, root)                     # bibir tergulung
    for sgn in (-1, 1):                                                                  # telinga wajan (besi lengkung)
        ring("telinga_wajan", (kx + sgn * 0.215, wy, H + 0.175), 0.028, 0.005, B.mat("besi_hitam", (0.04, 0.04, 0.045), 0.45, metal=0.8),
             root, rot=(0, 0, math.radians(90)), seg=16, tseg=6)
    B.cyl("gagang_wajan", (kx, wy - 0.27, H + 0.16), 0.015, 0.16, M.dark_wood, rot=(math.radians(90), 0, 0), **P)
    B.cyl("ring_gagang", (kx, wy - 0.195, H + 0.165), 0.018, 0.02, B.mat("besi_hitam", (0.04, 0.04, 0.045), 0.45, metal=0.8),
          rot=(math.radians(90), 0, 0), verts=10, **P)
    B.cyl("dudukan_kompor", (kx, wy, H + 0.075), 0.13, 0.012, B.mat("besi_hitam", (0.04, 0.04, 0.045), 0.45, metal=0.8), verts=20, **P)
    nasi_goreng("nasi_wajan", root, (kx, wy, H + 0.103), 0.12, 0.06, 1.2)            # gundukan nasi goreng di dasar wajan
    flame = flame_mat()
    import bmesh
    for k in range(18):                                          # lidah api gas: ring burner, condong ke dalam (ke dasar wajan)
        a = k / 18 * math.tau
        bm = bmesh.new(); bmesh.ops.create_cone(bm, cap_ends=False, segments=8, radius1=0.0085, radius2=0.0015, depth=0.034)
        o = B._finish(B._obj("api", bm, (kx + math.cos(a) * 0.072, wy + math.sin(a) * 0.072, H + 0.068 + 0.017),
                             (-math.sin(a) * 0.35, math.cos(a) * 0.35, 0), smooth=True), "api", flame, root)
        o.visible_shadow = False
        B.noise(o, "scale", 2, 0.18, 2 + k % 3)
    B.cyl("burner", (kx, wy, H + 0.064), 0.08, 0.008, M.black, verts=20, **P)
    fl = B.light("POINT", (kx, wy, H + 0.1), 60 if env["malam"] else 15, (1.0, 0.55, 0.2), 0.08, parent=root)
    B.noise(fl.data, "energy", 0, 8, 6)               # Phase 4.2-C: api gas stabil (dulu ±25 tiap 2 frame -> kedip OTS f459-473)
    rz = root.rotation_euler.z                                  # uap wajan (posisi dunia)
    lx, ly = kx, wy
    import kit_anchors
    toW = lambda a, b: (x + a * math.cos(rz) - b * math.sin(rz), y + a * math.sin(rz) + b * math.cos(rz))
    kit_anchors.add("wajan", p.get("nama", "gerobak") + ".wajan", (*toW(lx, ly), gz + H + 0.165), r=0.12)
    kit_anchors.add("meja", p.get("nama", "gerobak") + ".meja", (*toW(-0.25, 0.1), gz + H + 0.03))
    kit_anchors.add("serah", p.get("nama", "gerobak") + ".serah", (*toW(-D / 2 - 0.15, -0.2), gz + H + 0.15))
    info["steam"].append((x + lx * math.cos(rz) - ly * math.sin(rz), y + lx * math.sin(rz) + ly * math.cos(rz), gz + H + 0.2))
    # botol, piring, termos, tisu (sisi penjual, ujung belakang)
    for k, (cc, nm) in enumerate([((0.08, 0.03, 0.02), "kecap"), ((0.75, 0.05, 0.03), "saus"), ((0.9, 0.5, 0.05), "minyak")]):
        B.cyl("botol_" + nm, (-0.22, 0.25 + k * 0.1, H + 0.12), 0.03, 0.2, B.mat("botol_" + nm, cc, 0.15), verts=12, **P)
        B.cyl("tutup", (-0.22, 0.25 + k * 0.1, H + 0.235), 0.015, 0.03, M.white, verts=8, **P)
    for k in range(8):
        B.cyl("piring", (-0.2, 0.62, H + 0.04 + k * 0.012), 0.11, 0.01, B.mat("keramik_putih", (0.92, 0.92, 0.9), 0.15), verts=24, bevel=0, **P)
    # termos di bawah meja, tisu kecil di sisi dalam: tidak menghalangi wajah/tangan penjual & wajan
    B.cyl("termos", (0.12, 0.25, 0.13 + 0.13), 0.07, 0.26, B.mat("termos_merah", (0.75, 0.08, 0.06), 0.3), verts=16, **P)
    B.box("tisu", (-0.36, 0.1, H + 0.035), (0.09, 0.13, 0.06), B.mat("kotak_tisu", (0.95, 0.9, 0.75), 0.6), **P)
    # tabung gas melon & ember di bawah
    B.cyl("tabung_gas", (0.05, -0.2, 0.12 + 0.17), 0.13, 0.3, B.mat("gas_melon", (0.25, 0.6, 0.15), 0.35), verts=20, **P)
    B.box("ember_cuci", (-0.55, -0.6, 0.15), (0.3, 0.3, 0.3), B.mat("ember_hitam", (0.05, 0.05, 0.06), 0.4), **P) if p.get("ember", True) else None
    # kanopi bergaris + tiang
    th = H + 1.55                                                # kanopi tinggi: tidak memotong kepala tokoh di shot dekat
    for dx, dy in ((D / 2, -Lg / 2), (D / 2, Lg / 2)):          # tiang di sisi penjual saja; kanopi menjorok ke depan
        B.cyl("tiang_kanopi", (dx, dy, H + (th - H) / 2), 0.022, th - H, alu, verts=8, **P)
        B.box("lengan_kanopi", (dx - 0.4, dy, th - 0.02), (0.85, 0.025, 0.025), alu, rot=(0, math.radians(-8), 0), **P)
    cA, cB = M.red, M.white
    n = 9
    for k in range(n):
        B.box("kanopi", (-0.12, -Lg / 2 - 0.1 + (k + 0.5) * (Lg + 0.2) / n, th + 0.05), (D + 0.5, (Lg + 0.2) / n, 0.025),
              cA if k % 2 else cB, rot=(0, math.radians(-8), 0), **P)
    for k in range(n):                                           # rumbai tepi kanopi
        B.box("rumbai", (-D / 2 - 0.38, -Lg / 2 - 0.1 + (k + 0.5) * (Lg + 0.2) / n, th - 0.02), (0.01, (Lg + 0.2) / n, 0.16),
              cA if k % 2 else cB, **P)
    # spanduk nama
    tulisan = p.get("tulisan", "NASI GORENG")
    B.box("spanduk", (-D / 2 - 0.4, 0, th - 0.28), (0.015, Lg + 0.1, 0.3), B.mat("spanduk_kuning", (0.98, 0.8, 0.08), 0.6), bevel=0, **P)
    t = B.text(tulisan, (0, 0, 0), 0.19, B.mat("huruf_merah", (0.65, 0.02, 0.02), 0.5), -1)
    t.parent = root; t.location = (-D / 2 - 0.41, 0, th - 0.28); t.rotation_euler = (math.radians(90), 0, math.radians(-90))
    if env["malam"]:                                             # lampu sorot kecil di bawah kanopi -> spanduk terbaca
        sl = B.light("AREA", (-D / 2 - 0.75, 0, th - 0.62), 22, (1.0, 0.82, 0.6), rot=(0, math.radians(-124), 0), parent=root)
        sl.data.size = 0.5
    import kit_anchors
    rz_ = root.rotation_euler.z
    wpt = lambda a, b, c: (x + a * math.cos(rz_) - b * math.sin(rz_), y + a * math.sin(rz_) + b * math.cos(rz_), gz + c)
    kit_anchors.add("papan", p.get("nama", "gerobak") + ".papan", wpt(-D / 2 - 0.4, 0, th - 0.28),
                    corners=[wpt(-D / 2 - 0.41, sy_ * (Lg + 0.1) / 2, th - 0.28 + sz_ * 0.15) for sy_ in (-1, 1) for sz_ in (-1, 1)])
    # menu kecil di kaca etalase
    if p.get("menu", True):
        B.box("stiker_menu", (ex - gd / 2 - 0.004, ey, H + 0.17), (0.004, 0.26, 0.14), B.mat("stiker", (0.98, 0.95, 0.85), 0.6), bevel=0, **P)
    # kerupuk gantung
    bag = B.mat("plastik_kerupuk", (0.95, 0.95, 0.95), 0.12)            # plastik bening tipis (kaca/transmisi tampil hitam di Eevee)
    bag.surface_render_method = "BLENDED"
    bag.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"].inputs["Alpha"].default_value = 0.28
    for k in range(3):                                           # kerupuk gantung di ujung belakang (tidak menutupi wajah)
        kx = -0.3 + k * 0.25
        B.cyl("tali", (kx, Lg / 2 + 0.08, th - 0.1), 0.003, 0.1, M.black, verts=4, bevel=0, **P)   # digantung tinggi: di atas kepala
        o = B.box("kerupuk", (kx, Lg / 2 + 0.08, th - 0.27), (0.2, 0.05, 0.26), bag, **P)
        B.box("isi_kerupuk", (kx, Lg / 2 + 0.08, th - 0.29), (0.17, 0.035, 0.2), B.mat("kerupuk", (0.95, 0.72, 0.35), 0.6), **P)
        B.noise(o, "rotation_euler", 1, 0.05, 25)
    # bohlam utama: cahaya praktis hangat yang benar-benar menerangi tokoh & sekitar
    lc = env.get("warna_lampu", (1.0, 0.7, 0.4))
    for dy in (-0.45, 0.45):
        B.cyl("fiting", (-0.1, dy, th - 0.06), 0.02, 0.06, M.black, verts=8, **P)
        B.ball("bohlam_gerobak", (-0.1, dy, th - 0.14), 0.045, B.glow("bohlam_hangat", (1.0, 0.75, 0.45), 45 if env["malam"] else 4), 2, **P)
        if env["malam"]:
            B.light("POINT", (-0.1, dy, th - 0.2), 110, lc, 0.06, parent=root)
            bx, by = -0.1 * math.cos(root.rotation_euler.z) - dy * math.sin(root.rotation_euler.z), -0.1 * math.sin(root.rotation_euler.z) + dy * math.cos(root.rotation_euler.z)
            info["bulbs"].append((x + bx, y + by, gz + th - 0.14))
    # tempat duduk pembeli: bangku panjang di sisi pembeli
    if p.get("bangku", False):
        B.box("bangku", (-D / 2 - 0.6, 0, 0.42), (0.32, 1.4, 0.04), wood, **P)
        for dy in (-0.6, 0.6):
            B.box("kaki_bangku", (-D / 2 - 0.6, dy, 0.2), (0.28, 0.04, 0.4), wood, **P)


PROP_KINDS = ["aset", "gerobak", "gerobak_makan", "dingklik", "meja_lipat", "motor", "mobil", "sepeda", "rambu_parkir", "garis_parkir", "tong_sampah", "pot_tanaman", "bangku",
              "pohon", "pohon_kelapa", "tiang_listrik", "jemuran", "meja", "kursi", "sofa", "tv", "lemari", "kasur",
              "kompor", "kulkas", "galon", "rak", "karpet", "lampu_gantung", "lampu_meja", "jam_dinding", "warung",
              "lapak", "payung_pasar", "keranjang", "peti", "karung", "ember", "batu", "pagar", "orang_orangan",
              "gubuk", "pos_ronda"]


def lapak(M, env, x, y, nx, rnd, color=None, root=None):
    """lapak pasar: meja + dagangan + terpal. nx = arah hadap (ke lorong)."""
    tarp = B.mat(f"terpal{rnd.randint(0, 5)}", col(color, None) if color else rnd.choice(TARP), 0.7)
    B.box("meja_lapak", (x, y, 0.8), (1.4, 2.6, 0.06), M.wood)
    for dx in (-0.6, 0.6):
        for dy in (-1.2, 1.2):
            B.box("kaki", (x + dx, y + dy, 0.4), (0.06, 0.06, 0.8), M.wood)
    kind = rnd.random()
    for i in range(int(rnd.uniform(14, 22))):
        px, py = x + rnd.uniform(-0.55, 0.55), y + rnd.uniform(-1.15, 1.15)
        if kind < 0.6:
            B.ball("buah", (px, py, 0.9 + rnd.uniform(0, 0.12)), rnd.uniform(0.07, 0.11), B.mat(f"buah{i % 6}", rnd.choice(FRUIT), 0.5))
        else:
            B.box("barang", (px, py, 0.9), (0.15, 0.2, rnd.uniform(0.08, 0.2)), B.mat(f"kemasan{i % 6}", rnd.choice(FRUIT + TARP), 0.5))
    for dy in (-1.3, 1.3):
        B.cyl("tiang_terpal", (x - nx * 0.75, y + dy, 1.2), 0.03, 2.4, M.bamboo)
        B.cyl("tiang_terpal", (x + nx * 0.9, y + dy, 1.45), 0.03, 2.9, M.bamboo)
    o = B.box("terpal", (x + nx * 0.08, y, 2.65), (1.9, 2.9, 0.03), tarp, rot=(0, nx * math.radians(12), 0))
    B.box("keranjang", (x - nx * 0.4, y + rnd.uniform(-1, 1), 0.15), (0.45, 0.45, 0.3), B.mat("anyaman", (0.6, 0.45, 0.25), 0.9))
    if env["malam"]:
        B.ball("bohlam", (x - nx * 0.5, y, 2.3), 0.06, M.bulb)
        B.light("POINT", (x - nx * 0.5, y, 2.2), 60, (1.0, 0.7, 0.4), 0.05)


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/kit_anchors.py
# sha256: d2f194f624ba0a6e3e0832c9e8eecb76484a780f74255965a63402a12132595f
==============================================================================================================
"""Titik interaksi (anchor) dunia yang didaftarkan aset, dipakai rig untuk menargetkan tangan/benda.

Jenis: wajan (pusat permukaan masakan, r = jari-jari area aduk), meja (permukaan), kursi (dudukan),
serah (titik serah-terima di atas meja/etalase), piring (titik taruh piring)."""
import math

ANCHORS = []


def add(kind, name, pos, **extra):
    ANCHORS.append({"kind": kind, "name": name, "pos": tuple(pos), **extra})


def nearest(kind, xy, max_d=4.0):
    best, bd = None, max_d
    for a in ANCHORS:
        if a["kind"] != kind:
            continue
        d = math.hypot(a["pos"][0] - xy[0], a["pos"][1] - xy[1])
        if d < bd:
            best, bd = a, d
    return best


def get(name):
    return next((a for a in ANCHORS if a["name"] == name), None)

# FILE [BUILD]: engine/sets/pasar.py
# sha256: bc060c5375f43f53bda634cbd4ce3cbe016fee0c9eeafdefb1d39f261fafb751
==============================================================================================================
"""Lokasi 'pasar': lorong pasar tradisional dengan lapak di kiri-kanan (buah, sayur, kemasan),
terpal warna-warni, peti & karung, spanduk nama pasar.
Koordinat: lorong x=-2.5..2.5, meja lapak di x=±3.3, dinding pasar x=±4.8, lorong sepanjang Y."""
import random

import bl_utils as B
import kit

Y0, Y1 = -20, 60


def build(cfg, env, M, avoid):
    rnd = random.Random(cfg.get("acak", 7))
    info = {"lamps": [], "steam": [], "bulbs": []}
    floor = kit.ground_mat("lantai_pasar", (0.34, 0.33, 0.3), max(env["basah"], 0.4), var=0.35)
    B.box("lantai", (0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, -0.05), (10, Y1 - Y0, 0.1), floor)
    wall = kit.wall_cached((0.45, 0.55, 0.5), 1.0)
    roof = B.mat("atap_pasar", (0.35, 0.35, 0.37), 0.5, metal=0.5)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        B.box("dinding", (sx * 4.9, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, 2.5), (0.2, Y1 - Y0, 5), wall)
        for y in range(Y0, Y1, 6):
            B.cyl("tiang_pasar", (sx * 4.6, y, 2.6), 0.12, 5.2, M.concrete)
    B.box("atap", (0, (Y0 + Y1) / 2, 5.4), (10, Y1 - Y0, 0.15), roof)
    for y in range(Y0, Y1, 4):                                    # celah cahaya di atap
        B.box("rangka", (0, y, 5.25), (10, 0.12, 0.12), M.pole)
    for sx in (-1, 1):
        y = Y0 + 1.5
        while y < Y1 - 2:
            if not any(abs(sx * 3.3 - a[0]) < 1.4 and abs(y - a[1]) < 1.6 for a in avoid):
                kit.lapak(M, env, sx * 3.3, y, -sx, rnd)
            if rnd.random() < 0.5:
                kit.build_prop({"jenis": rnd.choice(["peti", "karung", "keranjang"]), "posisi": [sx * 2.35, y + 1.45],
                                "jumlah": rnd.randint(1, 3)}, M, env, info)
            y += 3.2
    nama = cfg.get("nama_pasar", "PASAR RAKYAT")
    for y in cfg.get("spanduk", [12, 34]):
        B.box("spanduk", (0, y, 4.2), (4.8, 0.03, 0.7), B.mat("kain_spanduk", (0.95, 0.85, 0.15), 0.8))
        t = B.text(nama, (0, y - 0.03, 4.2), 0.42, B.mat("huruf_spanduk", (0.7, 0.05, 0.05), 0.6), 1)
        t.rotation_euler = (1.5708, 0, 0)
    # lampu neon tabung di plafon (siang redup, malam terang)
    tube = B.glow("lampu_tl", (0.9, 0.95, 1.0), 15 if env["malam"] else 4)
    for y in range(Y0 + 3, Y1, 7):
        B.box("lampu_tl", (0, y, 5.2), (0.08, 1.2, 0.05), tube)
        B.light("POINT", (0, y, 4.9), 300 if env["malam"] else 120, (0.9, 0.95, 1.0), 0.4)
        info["lamps"].append((0, y, 5.0))
    if cfg.get("lampu_hias"):
        for y in (cfg["lampu_hias"] if isinstance(cfg["lampu_hias"], list) else [5, 20, 35]):
            kit.string_lights(M, env, y, 4.6, -4.7, 4.7, 0.5, 18)
    return info
