# FILE [BUILD]: engine/tts.py
# sha256: 958872a0de0ff572b8d1b5c4435b3566196dba66c2d119aee97c85be58f1f855
==============================================================================================================
"""Narasi per baris dengan edge-tts + timestamp per kata -> build/audio/timeline.json
Baris yang teks/suaranya tidak berubah tidak dibuat ulang (cache)."""
import asyncio
import json
import subprocess

import edge_tts

from .common import digest

PAD_BEFORE = 0.15


def _duration(p):
    return float(subprocess.check_output(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "csv=p=0", str(p)]))


async def _synth(text, voice, rate, pitch, path):
    words = []
    com = edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice, rate=rate, pitch=pitch, boundary="WordBoundary")
    with open(path, "wb") as f:
        async for ch in com.stream():
            if ch["type"] == "audio":
                f.write(ch["data"])
            elif ch["type"] == "WordBoundary":
                words.append({"w": ch["text"], "t0": ch["offset"] / 1e7, "t1": (ch["offset"] + ch["duration"]) / 1e7})
    return words


def run(story, log=print):
    cache_f = story.path("audio", "cache.json")
    cache = json.loads(cache_f.read_text()) if cache_f.exists() else {}
    # Waktu diakumulasi dalam MILIDETIK INTEGER (Phase 4A): start(n+1) == start(n) + dur(n) persis.
    # (Phase 3: round(start) dan round(dur) terpisah -> 9.38 + 5.854 = 15.234000000000002 > 15.234 -> n4 dianggap n3.)
    t_ms, lines = 0, []
    for line in story.lines:
        voice, rate, pitch = line["voice"], line["rate"], line["pitch"]
        key = digest([line["text"], voice, rate, pitch])
        mp3 = story.path("audio", f"{line['id']}.mp3")
        if cache.get(line["id"], {}).get("key") == key and mp3.exists():
            words = cache[line["id"]]["words"]
        else:
            log(f"  suara: {line['id']} ({voice})")
            words = asyncio.run(_synth(line["text"], voice, rate, pitch, mp3))
            cache[line["id"]] = {"key": key, "words": words}
        pause = line["pause"]
        dur_ms = int(round((_duration(mp3) + PAD_BEFORE + pause) * 1000))
        pad_ms = int(round(PAD_BEFORE * 1000))
        wl = []
        for w in words:
            w0 = t_ms + pad_ms + int(round(w["t0"] * 1000))
            w1 = max(w0 + 1, t_ms + pad_ms + int(round(w["t1"] * 1000)))
            wl.append({"w": w["w"], "t0": w0 / 1000, "t1": min(w1, t_ms + dur_ms) / 1000})
        lines.append({"id": line["id"], "text": line["text"], "start": t_ms / 1000, "dur": dur_ms / 1000,
                      "start_ms": t_ms, "end_ms": t_ms + dur_ms, "audio": str(mp3), "words": wl})
        t_ms += dur_ms
    cache_f.write_text(json.dumps(cache, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
    story.path("audio", "timeline.json").write_text(json.dumps({"total": t_ms / 1000, "total_ms": t_ms, "time_unit": "ms",
                                                                 "lines": lines}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
    log(f"  narasi total {t_ms / 1000:.1f} detik, {len(lines)} baris")

# FILE [BUILD]: make.py
# sha256: 1bbda82fa9b066e9b9c50fe687731c2331bb7a55c2099e8ac485f66bde3ccbd3
==============================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pembuat video animasi 2.5D dari story.yaml.

Pemakaian (jalankan dengan .venv/bin/python):
  make.py baru <nama>               buat cerita baru dari template di stories/<nama>/
  make.py stories/<nama>            buat video lengkap (1080x1920)  -> stories/<nama>/output/<nama>.mp4
  make.py stories/<nama> --cek      periksa story.yaml + buat contact sheet 1 frame per shot (cepat, ~1 menit)
  make.py stories/<nama> --pratinjau video cepat (540x960, kualitas rendah) untuk cek gerak & timing
  make.py stories/<nama> --langkah audio,subtitle,video   jalankan langkah tertentu saja
  make.py stories/<nama> --frame 120,300,450              render still frame tertentu (graded) -> output/still/
  tambahkan --colab agar render VIDEO dikerjakan GPU Google Colab (still/--cek tetap lokal);
  make.py --colab-stop  untuk mematikan VM-nya

Langkah: suara -> tokoh -> rencana -> adegan -> render -> audio -> subtitle -> video
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from engine import audio, captions, chars, manifest, plan as planner, tts  # noqa: E402
from engine.common import Story, StoryError, digest  # noqa: E402

USE_COLAB = False

STEPS = ["suara", "tokoh", "rencana", "adegan", "render", "audio", "subtitle", "video"]
GPU_ENV = {**os.environ, "__NV_PRIME_RENDER_OFFLOAD": "1", "__GLX_VENDOR_LIBRARY_NAME": "nvidia"}


def log(msg):
    print(msg, flush=True)


def blender():
    b = shutil.which("blender")
    if not b:
        raise StoryError("Blender tidak ditemukan di PATH")
    return b


def build_scene(story):
    blend = story.path("scene.blend")
    bid = manifest.begin_scene(story)                          # Phase 4A-A2: build ID dari semua masukan
    t0 = time.time()
    r = subprocess.run([blender(), "-b", "-P", str(ROOT / "engine" / "blender_build.py"), "--",
                        str(story.path("plan.json")), str(blend), bid], capture_output=True, text=True, env=GPU_ENV)
    if "SCENE_OK" not in r.stdout:
        err = [l for l in (r.stdout + r.stderr).splitlines() if "Error" in l or "Traceback" in l or "line " in l or "raise" in l]
        raise StoryError("Gagal membangun adegan di Blender:\n" + "\n".join(err[-15:] or (r.stdout + r.stderr).splitlines()[-15:]))
    ver = next((l for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith("Blender ")), None)
    manifest.end_scene(story, ver, time.time() - t0)
    log(f"  adegan Blender tersimpan  [build {bid}, {time.time() - t0:.0f} dtk]")
    return blend


def render_settings(pct, samples):
    """pengaturan render yang dicatat di manifest (selain yang tersimpan di scene.blend)"""
    return {"engine": "EEVEE", "resolution": [1080 * pct // 100, 1920 * pct // 100], "samples": samples,
            "alpha_tokoh": os.environ.get("ANIM_ALPHA", "scene"), "glare": os.environ.get("ANIM_GLARE", "scene"),
            "colab": USE_COLAB}


def render(story, plan, mode, only=None):
    """only = daftar frame (tes animasi pendek); None = semua frame"""
    frames = story.build / f"frames_{mode}"
    bid = manifest.current_id(story) or digest([story.path("plan.json").read_text(), mode])
    # Phase 4A: cache per frame -> build ID (frames.json). Frame dari build lain dihapus HANYA bila diminta dirender
    # ulang (semua frame, atau satu shot via --shot), sehingga render ulang per shot tidak membuang shot lain.
    frames.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    from engine import provenance as PV
    idx = PV.read_index(frames)
    (frames / ".versi").unlink(missing_ok=True)
    pct, samples = (50, 12) if mode == "pratinjau" else (100, int(os.environ.get("ANIM_SAMPLES", 32)))
    want0 = list(only) if only else list(range(1, plan["end"] + 1))
    stale = [f for f in want0 if (idx.get(str(f)) or {}).get("build_id") not in (bid,)]
    for f in stale:
        (frames / f"f_{f:04d}.png").unlink(missing_ok=True)
    if stale:
        log(f"  {len(stale)} frame perlu dirender (build {bid})")
    expr = ("import bpy; s=bpy.context.scene; s.render.use_overwrite=False; s.render.use_placeholder=True; "
            f"s.render.resolution_percentage={pct}; s.eevee.taa_render_samples={samples}; "
            f"s.render.filepath=r'{frames}/f_'")
    want = list(only) if only else list(range(1, plan["end"] + 1))
    done = len(list(frames.glob("f_*.png")))
    if only:
        log(f"  render klip frame {want[0]}-{want[-1]} ({len(want)} frame)" + (" [GPU Colab]" if USE_COLAB else ""))
    else:
        log(f"  render {plan['end']} frame ({'pratinjau' if mode == 'pratinjau' else 'penuh'}), sudah ada {done}"
            + (" [GPU Colab]" if USE_COLAB else ""))
    def _index():
        PV.write_index(frames, want, bid)
    if USE_COLAB:
        from engine.colab import remote
        try:
            remote.render(story.build / "scene.blend", frames, want, pct, samples, blender(), GPU_ENV,
                          prefix="f_", log=log)
        except remote.ColabError as e:
            _index()
            raise StoryError(str(e))
        _index()
        return frames
    t0 = time.time()
    if only:
        expr += f"; s.frame_start={want[0]}; s.frame_end={want[-1]}"
    p = subprocess.Popen([blender(), "-b", str(story.build / "scene.blend"), "--python", str(ROOT / "engine" / "bl_render_opts.py"),
                          "--python-expr", expr, "-a"],
                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, env=GPU_ENV)
    n = 0
    saved, bver = [], None
    for line in p.stdout:
        if line.startswith("Blender ") and bver is None:
            bver = line.strip()
        if line.startswith("Saved:"):
            nm = Path(line.split("'")[1] if "'" in line else line.split()[-1]).name
            if nm.startswith("f_"):
                saved.append((int(nm[2:6]), nm))
            n += 1
            if n % 60 == 0:
                el = time.time() - t0
                left = (plan["end"] - done - n) * el / n
                log(f"    {done + n}/{plan['end']} frame, sisa ~{left / 60:.0f} menit")
    rc = p.wait()
    if saved:                                                         # Phase 4.1-E: metadata potongan render lokal
        PV.record_chunk(frames, story.build / "scene.blend", saved, render_settings(pct, samples),
                        "lokal", {"blender": bver})
    if rc != 0:
        _index()
        raise StoryError("Render Blender gagal")
    _index()
    return frames


def frames_build_ids(story, mode, a, b):
    from engine import provenance as PV
    return PV.build_ids(story.build / f"frames_{mode}", a, b)


def check_provenance(story, frames, a, b):
    """Phase 4.1-E: validasi provenance sebelum merakit klip/video"""
    from engine import provenance as PV
    rep = PV.validate(frames, a, b, manifest.current_id(story))
    bad = {k: v for k, v in rep["cek"].items() if v["status"] == "FAIL"}
    if bad:
        msg = "; ".join(f"{k} ({v.get('jumlah', len(v.get('varian', [])))})" for k, v in bad.items())
        if os.environ.get("ANIM_IZINKAN_TANPA_PROVENANCE") == "1":
            log(f"  PERINGATAN provenance FAIL (diizinkan lewat ANIM_IZINKAN_TANPA_PROVENANCE=1): {msg}")
        else:
            raise StoryError(f"Provenance frame {a}-{b} gagal divalidasi: {msg} (laporan: build/provenance_{a}_{b}.json)")
    else:
        log(f"  provenance frame {a}-{b}: PASS ({len(rep['chunks'])} potongan bermetadata)")
    return rep


def render_stills(story, picks, out, pct, samples):
    """picks = [(nama_file, frame)] -> out/nama_file (lokal atau Colab)"""
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for n, _ in picks:
        (out / n).unlink(missing_ok=True)
    expr = (f"import bpy; s=bpy.context.scene; s.render.resolution_percentage={pct}; s.eevee.taa_render_samples={samples}\n"
            f"for n,f in {picks}:\n"
            f"    s.frame_set(f); s.render.filepath=r'{out}/'+n; bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)")
    r = subprocess.run([blender(), "-b", str(story.build / "scene.blend"), "--python", str(ROOT / "engine" / "bl_render_opts.py"),
                        "--python-expr", expr], capture_output=True, text=True, env=GPU_ENV)
    if r.returncode:
        raise StoryError("Render still gagal:\n" + r.stdout[-1500:])


def grade_still(story, src, dst, f=None, plan=None):
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", str(src), "-vf", ",".join(grade_filters(story, frame=f, plan=plan, src=src)),
                    "-frames:v", "1", "-update", "1", str(dst)], check=True)


def frame_stills(story, frames, pct=100, samples=64, plan=None):
    raw = story.build / "still_raw"
    picks = [(f"f_{f:04d}.png", f) for f in frames]
    t0 = time.time()
    render_stills(story, picks, raw, pct, samples)
    dt = (time.time() - t0) / max(1, len(picks))
    out = story.out / "still"; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    plan = plan or json.loads(story.path("plan.json").read_text())
    st = render_settings(pct, samples) | {"detik_per_still_rata2": round(dt, 1)}
    for n, f in picks:
        grade_still(story, raw / n, out / n, f, plan)
        shot = manifest.shot_of(plan, f)
        manifest.register(story, raw / n, "still_raw", f, shot, st)
        manifest.register(story, out / n, "still_graded", f, shot, st | {"grading": grade_filters(story, frame=f, plan=plan)})
    log(f"  still: {out} ({len(picks)} frame, ~{dt:.0f} dtk/still, build {manifest.current_id(story)})")


def stills(story, plan):
    out = story.build / "cek"
    picks = [(f"shot_{s['n']:02d}.png", (s["f0"] + s["f1"]) // 2) for s in plan["shots"]]
    render_stills(story, picks, out, 50, 16)
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
    for s in plan["shots"]:                                     # grading sama seperti video
        f = out / f"shot_{s['n']:02d}.png"
        g = out / f"graded_{s['n']:02d}.png"
        grade_still(story, f, g, (s["f0"] + s["f1"]) // 2, plan)
    font = ImageFont.truetype(str(ROOT / "assets/fonts/Poppins-Bold.ttf"), 22)
    tw, th, cols = 270, 480, 8
    ims = []
    for s in plan["shots"]:
        im = Image.open(out / f"graded_{s['n']:02d}.png").convert("RGB").resize((tw, th))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        label = f"{s['n']}. {s['kamera']}" + (f" {s['subjek']}" if s["subjek"] else "")
        d.rectangle((0, 0, tw, 56), fill=(0, 0, 0))
        d.text((8, 4), label, font=font, fill=(255, 212, 0))
        d.text((8, 30), f"{(s['f0'] - 1) / 30:.1f}s  {s['mulai']}", font=ImageFont.truetype(str(ROOT / "assets/fonts/Poppins-Bold.ttf"), 16), fill=(255, 255, 255))
        ims.append(im)
    rows = (len(ims) + cols - 1) // cols
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (tw * min(cols, len(ims)), th * rows), (20, 20, 20))
    for i, im in enumerate(ims):
        sheet.paste(im, ((i % cols) * tw, (i // cols) * th))
    story.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    f = story.out / f"{story.slug}_cek.png"
    sheet.save(f)
    log(f"  contact sheet: {f}")


def shot_at(plan, f):
    return next((s for s in plan.get("shots", []) if s["f0"] <= f <= s["f1"]), None) if plan and f else None


def shot_override(story, plan, f=None, src=None, shot=None):
    """Phase 4D-D3: koreksi grading untuk shot yang memuat frame f = otomatis (metrik crush) + manual (shot.warna).
    Koreksi otomatis dihitung sekali per build & shot dari gambar RAW 'src' (frame tengah shot untuk video)."""
    from engine import grade as G
    s = shot or shot_at(plan, f)
    if s is None:
        return {}
    cache = G.ShotGrades(story, manifest.current_id(story) or "tanpa_build")
    auto = cache.get(s["n"])
    if auto is None and src is not None and Path(src).exists():
        auto, rep = G.auto_correct(src, lambda ov: grade_filters(story, {**ov, **(s.get("warna") or {})}))
        cache.put(s["n"], auto, rep, src)
        log(f"  grading shot {s['n']}: hitam RAW {rep['raw']['hitam']:.1%} -> graded {rep['akhir']['hitam']:.1%}"
            + (f" (koreksi {auto})" if auto else ""))
    return {**(auto or {}), **(s.get("warna") or {})}


def grade_filters(story, override=None, frame=None, plan=None, src=None):
    """grading warna akhir (dipakai video, contact sheet, dan still). Kunci warna: saturasi, kontras, terang,
    tajam (unsharp 0..1), vinyet (sudut, radian; 0 = mati), oranye_teal (0..1 kekuatan pergeseran warna), grain.
    frame + plan -> ditambah koreksi per shot (shot_override)."""
    if frame is not None and plan is not None:
        override = {**shot_override(story, plan, frame, src), **(override or {})}
    grade = {**story.data.get("warna", {}), **(override or {})}
    waktu = story.data.get("latar", {}).get("waktu", "malam")
    sat = grade.get("saturasi", 1.1 if waktu == "malam" else 1.08)
    k = float(grade.get("oranye_teal", 0.7))
    # oranye-teal: bayangan kebiruan, highlight hangat
    base = {"malam": (-0.05, -0.01, 0.07, 0.06, 0.02, -0.05), "senja": (-0.02, 0, 0.05, 0.07, 0.02, -0.06),
            "pagi": (-0.02, 0, 0.04, 0.05, 0.02, -0.04), "siang": (-0.02, 0, 0.03, 0.03, 0.01, -0.03)}[waktu]
    bal = ":".join(f"{n}={v * k:.3f}" for n, v in zip(("rs", "gs", "bs", "rh", "gh", "bh"), base))
    f = [f"colorbalance={bal},eq=saturation={sat}:brightness={grade.get('terang', 0.0)}:contrast={grade.get('kontras', 1.05)}:gamma=0.98"]
    toe = float(grade.get("angkat_hitam", 0.025 if waktu == "malam" else 0.0))   # angkat titik hitam (bukan eksposur): cegah black crush
    if toe:
        f.append(f"curves=all='0/{toe:.3f} 0.1/{0.1 + toe * 0.6:.3f} 0.5/0.5 1/1'")
    if grade.get("tajam", 0.35):
        f.append(f"unsharp=5:5:{grade.get('tajam', 0.35)}:5:5:0.0")
    vin = grade.get("vinyet", 0.3 if waktu == "malam" else 0.45)   # malam: vinyet lebih ringan (sudut sudah gelap)
    if vin:
        f.append(f"vignette={vin}")
    return f


def graded_chain(story, plan, frames, a, b, label="v"):
    """filter_complex: frame a..b dipotong per shot, tiap potongan digrading dengan koreksi shot-nya, lalu disambung.
    Input 0 harus dimulai di frame a. -> (teks filter, nama label keluaran)"""
    parts, n = [], 0
    for s in plan["shots"]:
        s0, s1 = max(a, s["f0"]), min(b, s["f1"])
        if s0 > s1:
            continue
        mid = frames / f"f_{(s['f0'] + s['f1']) // 2:04d}.png"
        ov = shot_override(story, plan, shot=s, src=mid if mid.exists() else frames / f"f_{s0:04d}.png")
        parts.append(f"[0:v]trim=start_frame={s0 - a}:end_frame={s1 - a + 1},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                     + ",".join(grade_filters(story, ov)) + f"[g{n}]")
        n += 1
    chain = ";".join(parts) + ";" + "".join(f"[g{i}]" for i in range(n)) + f"concat=n={n}:v=1:a=0[{label}]"
    return chain, label


def assemble(story, plan, mode):
    frames = story.build / f"frames_{mode}"
    if not frames.exists() or len(list(frames.glob("f_*.png"))) < plan["end"]:
        raise StoryError("Frame belum lengkap; jalankan langkah 'render' dulu")
    ids = frames_build_ids(story, mode, 1, plan["end"])
    prov = check_provenance(story, frames, 1, plan["end"])
    if ids != [manifest.current_id(story)]:
        log(f"  PERINGATAN: frame berasal dari build {ids}; build adegan saat ini {manifest.current_id(story)}")
    story.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    name = story.slug + ("_pratinjau" if mode == "pratinjau" else "")
    out = story.out / f"{name}.mp4"
    total = plan["total"]
    grade = story.data.get("warna", {})
    chain, lab = graded_chain(story, plan, frames, 1, plan["end"])
    vf = []
    if mode == "pratinjau":
        vf.append("scale=1080:1920")
    ass = story.build / "captions.ass"
    if ass.exists() and ass.stat().st_size > 0:
        vf.append(f"subtitles={ass}:fontsdir={ROOT / 'assets/fonts'}")
    if grade.get("grain", True):
        vf.append("noise=c0s=5:c0f=t")                          # grain film halus seperti contoh
    vf += ["fade=t=in:st=0:d=0.5", f"fade=t=out:st={max(0, total - 0.9):.2f}:d=0.9"]
    fc = chain + f";[{lab}]" + ",".join(vf) + "[out]"
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-framerate", str(plan["fps"]), "-i", str(frames / "f_%04d.png"),
           "-i", str(story.build / "audio" / "mix.wav"), "-filter_complex", fc, "-map", "[out]", "-map", "1:a",
           "-af", "loudnorm=I=-14:TP=-1.5:LRA=11",
           "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium" if mode == "pratinjau" else "slow", "-crf", "18", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
           "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-ar", "48000", "-shortest", "-movflags", "+faststart",
           "-metadata", f"comment=build_id={manifest.current_id(story)} frames={','.join(ids)}", str(out)]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
    manifest.register(story, out, "video", settings={"frames_build_ids": ids, "mode": mode,
                                                     "provenance": {"status": prov["status"], "chunks": len(prov["chunks"])}})
    from engine import provenance as PV
    PV.record_video(story.build, out, "asli", catatan=f"rakitan make.py mode={mode}; provenance {prov['status']}")
    log(f"  VIDEO: {out}")
    return out


def assemble_clip(story, plan, a, b):
    """tes animasi pendek: frame a..b + potongan audio & subtitle yang sesuai, grading sama dengan video akhir"""
    frames = story.build / "frames_penuh"
    fps = plan["fps"]; t0 = (a - 1) / fps; dur = (b - a + 1) / fps
    out = story.out / f"{story.slug}_klip_{a}_{b}.mp4"
    ids = frames_build_ids(story, "penuh", a, b)
    prov = check_provenance(story, frames, a, b)
    chain, lab = graded_chain(story, plan, frames, a, b)
    vf = []
    ass = story.build / "captions.ass"
    if ass.exists() and ass.stat().st_size > 0:
        vf += [f"setpts=PTS+{t0:.4f}/TB", f"subtitles={ass}:fontsdir={ROOT / 'assets/fonts'}", "setpts=PTS-STARTPTS"]
    if story.data.get("warna", {}).get("grain", True):
        vf.append("noise=c0s=5:c0f=t")
    fc = chain + f";[{lab}]" + ",".join(vf or ["null"]) + "[out]"
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-framerate", str(fps), "-start_number", str(a), "-i", str(frames / "f_%04d.png"),
           "-ss", f"{t0:.3f}", "-t", f"{dur:.3f}", "-i", str(story.build / "audio" / "mix.wav"),
           "-filter_complex", fc, "-map", "[out]", "-map", "1:a", "-frames:v", str(b - a + 1),
           "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "slow", "-crf", "18",
           "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-ar", "48000", "-movflags", "+faststart",
           "-metadata", f"comment=build_id={manifest.current_id(story)} frames={','.join(ids)}", str(out)]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
    manifest.register(story, out, "klip", settings={"frames": [a, b], "frames_build_ids": ids,
                                                    "provenance": {"status": prov["status"], "chunks": len(prov["chunks"])}})
    log(f"  KLIP: {out}")
    return out


def new_story(name):
    dst = ROOT / "stories" / name
    if dst.exists():
        raise StoryError(f"{dst} sudah ada")
    dst.mkdir(parents=True)
    shutil.copy(ROOT / "stories" / "_template" / "story.yaml", dst / "story.yaml")
    log(f"Cerita baru: {dst / 'story.yaml'}\nEdit file itu, lalu jalankan:  .venv/bin/python make.py stories/{name} --cek")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("story", nargs="?", default="-", help="folder cerita (stories/<nama>) atau 'baru'")
    ap.add_argument("nama", nargs="?", help="nama cerita baru (untuk 'baru')")
    ap.add_argument("--cek", action="store_true", help="hanya contact sheet 1 frame per shot")
    ap.add_argument("--pratinjau", action="store_true", help="render cepat resolusi rendah")
    ap.add_argument("--langkah", help="jalankan langkah tertentu, mis. audio,subtitle,video")
    ap.add_argument("--frame", help="render still frame tertentu, mis. 120,300 (100%%, 64 sampel)")
    ap.add_argument("--colab", action="store_true", help="render video di GPU Google Colab (still tetap lokal)")
    ap.add_argument("--klip", help="tes animasi pendek, mis. 159-281 (video; pakai --colab untuk render di Colab)")
    ap.add_argument("--shot", help="render ulang frame shot tertentu saja (mis. 6 atau 6,9), lalu rakit video bila frame lengkap")
    ap.add_argument("--colab-stop", action="store_true", help="matikan VM Colab")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    global USE_COLAB
    USE_COLAB = a.colab
    if a.colab_stop:
        from engine.colab import remote
        remote.stop(log)
        if a.story in ("-", "stop"):
            return
    if a.story == "baru":
        return new_story(a.nama or "cerita_baru")
    story = Story(a.story)
    mode = "pratinjau" if a.pratinjau else "penuh"
    steps = a.langkah.split(",") if a.langkah else (["suara", "tokoh", "rencana", "adegan"] if (a.cek or a.frame or a.shot) else
                                                       (["suara", "tokoh", "rencana", "adegan", "audio", "subtitle"] if a.klip else STEPS))
    for s in steps:
        if s not in STEPS:
            raise StoryError(f"langkah '{s}' tidak dikenal. Pilihan: {', '.join(STEPS)}")
    t0 = time.time()
    log(f"== {story.data.get('judul', story.slug)} ==")
    plan = None
    for s in STEPS:
        if s not in steps:
            continue
        log(f"[{s}]")
        if s == "suara":
            tts.run(story, log)
        elif s == "tokoh":
            chars.run(story, log=log)
        elif s == "rencana":
            plan = planner.run(story, mode, log)
        else:
            plan = plan or planner.build_plan(story, mode)
            if s == "adegan":
                build_scene(story)
            elif s == "render":
                render(story, plan, mode)
            elif s == "audio":
                audio.run(story, plan, log)
            elif s == "subtitle":
                captions.run(story, log)
            elif s == "video":
                assemble(story, plan, mode)
    if a.cek:
        stills(story, plan or planner.build_plan(story, mode))
    if a.frame:
        frame_stills(story, [int(x) for x in a.frame.split(",")], samples=int(os.environ.get("ANIM_STILL_SAMPLES", 64)),
                     plan=plan)
    if a.shot:
        plan = plan or planner.build_plan(story, mode)
        want = sorted({f for n in a.shot.split(",") for s in plan["shots"] if s["n"] == int(n)
                       for f in range(s["f0"], s["f1"] + 1)})
        if not want:
            raise StoryError(f"shot {a.shot} tidak ada (1..{len(plan['shots'])})")
        for f in want:                                           # paksa render ulang walau build sama
            (story.build / f"frames_{mode}" / f"f_{f:04d}.png").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        render(story, plan, mode, want)
    if a.klip:
        k0, k1 = (int(x) for x in a.klip.split("-"))
        plan = plan or planner.build_plan(story, mode)
        render(story, plan, "penuh", range(k0, k1 + 1))
        assemble_clip(story, plan, k0, k1)
    log(f"selesai dalam {(time.time() - t0) / 60:.1f} menit")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except StoryError as e:
        print(f"\nKESALAHAN: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/ab_grading.py
# sha256: 2053cdbe8c9345e71da86aaedc411cb00508cb0a6831728576614e9e48c0ce67
==============================================================================================================
"""A/B grading global vs per-shot (Phase 4D-D3) + grain/unsharp on/off untuk satu still RAW.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/ab_grading.py <story> <frame> <keluaran_dir>
Tulis: global.png, pershot.png, pershot_tanpa_grain_tajam.png + metrik.json"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
import make  # noqa: E402
from engine import grade as G  # noqa: E402
from engine import plan as planner  # noqa: E402
from engine.common import Story  # noqa: E402

story = Story(sys.argv[1]); f = int(sys.argv[2]); out = Path(sys.argv[3]); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
plan = json.loads((story.build / "plan.json").read_text())
src = story.build / "still_raw" / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
from PIL import Image  # noqa: E402
raw = Image.open(src)
res = {"frame": f, "raw": G.metrics(raw)}
glob_f = make.grade_filters(story)
shot_f = make.grade_filters(story, frame=f, plan=plan, src=src)
plain = [x for x in shot_f if not x.startswith(("noise", "unsharp"))]
for name, flt in (("global", glob_f), ("pershot", shot_f), ("pershot_tanpa_grain_tajam", plain)):
    im = G.apply(src, flt)
    im.save(out / f"{name}_f{f:04d}.png")
    res[name] = G.metrics(im)
    res[name + "_filter"] = flt
(out / f"metrik_f{f:04d}.json").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1))
print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in res.items() if not k.endswith("_filter")}, indent=1))


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/banding_gambar.py
# sha256: 2f88e3c0ca8f2243c2341744c1b39f9b39b142149b683702f3d06fe2899f42f6
==============================================================================================================
"""Gabung beberapa gambar berdampingan dengan label (dipakai paket review).
  python banding_gambar.py <keluaran.jpg> <tinggi> "<label>=<path>[@x0,y0,x1,y1]" ...
Crop opsional dalam piksel gambar asli."""
import sys

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

out, H = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2])
tiles = []
for arg in sys.argv[3:]:
    lab, path = arg.split("=", 1)
    box = None
    if "@" in path:
        path, b = path.split("@")
        box = tuple(int(v) for v in b.split(","))
    im = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
    if box:
        im = im.crop(box)
    im = im.resize((int(im.width * H / im.height), H), Image.LANCZOS)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    try:
        font = ImageFont.truetype("DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", max(14, H // 32))
    except OSError:
        font = ImageFont.load_default()
    tw = d.textlength(lab, font=font)
    d.rectangle((0, 0, tw + 16, H // 32 + 14), fill=(0, 0, 0))
    d.text((8, 5), lab, fill=(255, 230, 80), font=font)
    tiles.append(im)
W = sum(t.width for t in tiles) + 6 * (len(tiles) - 1)
canvas = Image.new("RGB", (W, H), (20, 20, 20))
x = 0
for t in tiles:
    canvas.paste(t, (x, 0)); x += t.width + 6
canvas.save(out, quality=92)
print(out, canvas.size)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/banding_phase.py
# sha256: 91c7eceefeb9df0d0e59b99a4783d264097155d192af2256411a2c8e5d81f7cd
==============================================================================================================
"""Tahap C: lembar perbandingan berdampingan (referensi Phase 2 vs hasil baru) per frame benchmark.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/banding_phase.py <folder_ref> <folder_baru> <keluaran.jpg>"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

FR = [(60, "establishing"), (120, "two-shot"), (180, "medium Darto"), (212, "detail wajan"), (250, "interaksi makan"),
      (330, "MCU Jali"), (500, "OTS Jali->Darto"), (580, "MCU Darto"), (760, "penutup")]
ref, new, out = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
font = ImageFont.truetype(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "assets/fonts/Poppins-Bold.ttf"), 20)
tw, th = 300, 533
sheet = Image.new("RGB", (tw * 2 * 3 + 20 * 2, (th + 34) * 3), (18, 18, 18))
for i, (f, lab) in enumerate(FR):
    a = next(iter(ref.glob(f"*{f:04d}*.png")), None)
    b = new / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
    x0, y0 = (i % 3) * (tw * 2 + 20), (i // 3) * (th + 34)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
    d.text((x0 + 6, y0 + 4), f"f{f} {lab}:  Phase 2 | Phase 3", font=font, fill=(255, 212, 0))
    for k, p in enumerate((a, b)):
        if p and Path(p).exists():
            sheet.paste(Image.open(p).convert("RGB").resize((tw, th)), (x0 + k * tw, y0 + 34))
sheet.save(out, quality=88)
print(out)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/cek_sebidang.py
# sha256: d0160300640903e5aebe039486b0fcf71cb057697f2f965bdf98067e65a56db3
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.2: deteksi kandidat Z-FIGHTING: dua objek mesh berbeda yang punya muka sebidang (jarak bidang < tol) dan
proyeksinya saling tumpang tindih (luas > min_area). Pada render, permukaan seperti itu bergantian tampil per frame
mengikuti goyang kamera (mis. gorden vs kaca jendela uji3 shot 2).
  blender -b <scene.blend> -P scripts/dev/cek_sebidang.py -- <out.json> [tol_m=0.0008] [min_area_m2=0.0004]
Hanya poligon yang menghadap arah berlawanan dari dalam objeknya (muka luar) dan objek yang terlihat di render."""
import json
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

import bpy
from mathutils import Vector

argv = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:] if "--" in sys.argv else []
OUT = argv[0] if argv else "sebidang.json"
TOL = float(argv[1]) if len(argv) > 1 else 0.0008
MIN_AREA = float(argv[2]) if len(argv) > 2 else 0.0004
SKIP_PREFIX = ("tanah", "lantai", "jalan", "langit", "dunia")      # bidang besar dasar: ditangani terpisah

dg = bpy.context.evaluated_depsgraph_get()
faces = []                                   # (obj, normal(tuple bulat), d, poly2d bbox, area)
for ob in bpy.context.scene.objects:
    if ob.type != "MESH" or ob.hide_render or not ob.visible_get():
        continue
    if ob.name.lower().startswith(SKIP_PREFIX):
        continue
    ev = ob.evaluated_get(dg)
    me = ev.to_mesh()
    mw = ob.matrix_world
    for p in me.polygons:
        if p.area * (mw.to_scale().length / 1.732) ** 2 < MIN_AREA:
            continue
        n = (mw.to_3x3() @ p.normal).normalized()
        c = mw @ p.center
        ax = max(range(3), key=lambda i: abs(n[i]))
        if abs(n[ax]) < 0.999:               # hanya muka sejajar sumbu (kotak set dressing); cukup untuk kasus nyata
            continue
        sgn = 1 if n[ax] > 0 else -1
        if ax == 2 and sgn < 0:              # muka bawah (alas di tanah/meja): tidak terlihat kamera
            continue
        vs = [mw @ me.vertices[i].co for i in p.vertices]
        o = [i for i in range(3) if i != ax]
        lo = (min(v[o[0]] for v in vs), min(v[o[1]] for v in vs))
        hi = (max(v[o[0]] for v in vs), max(v[o[1]] for v in vs))
        faces.append((ob.name, ax, sgn, c[ax], lo, hi))
    ev.to_mesh_clear()

bucket = defaultdict(list)
for f in faces:
    bucket[(f[1], f[2], round(f[3] / TOL))].append(f)
hits = {}
keys = list(bucket)
for k in keys:
    group = bucket[k] + bucket.get((k[0], k[1], k[2] + 1), [])
    for i in range(len(group)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(group)):
            a, b = group[i], group[j]
            if a[0] == b[0] or abs(a[3] - b[3]) > TOL:
                continue
            w = min(a[5][0], b[5][0]) - max(a[4][0], b[4][0])
            h = min(a[5][1], b[5][1]) - max(a[4][1], b[4][1])
            if w > 0.01 and h > 0.01 and w * h > MIN_AREA:
                key = tuple(sorted((a[0], b[0])))
                hits[key] = max(hits.get(key, 0), round(w * h, 4))
res = sorted(({"objek": list(k), "luas_m2": v} for k, v in hits.items()), key=lambda r: -r["luas_m2"])
json.dump({"tol_m": TOL, "jumlah": len(res), "pasangan": res}, open(OUT, "w"), indent=1)
print("SEBIDANG", len(res), res[:25])

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/colab_ambil.py
# sha256: cf2317fc1d91b74ec63971c200a9ec4aaf30eb8293671d5583bfb2b02c6494cd
==============================================================================================================
"""ambil frame yang sudah jadi di VM Colab (render dihentikan) -> frames_penuh + metadata provenance.
   COLAB_SESSION=<sesi> .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/colab_ambil.py <story>"""
import os, sys, tarfile, json
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path("/home/ahmad-mafaza-afnani/Documents/2d-animation"); sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from engine.colab import remote as R
from engine import provenance as PV
story = ROOT / sys.argv[1]; out = story / "build" / "frames_penuh"; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
code = f'''
import subprocess, os, tarfile, struct, zlib
subprocess.run(["pkill", "-f", "{R.BLENDER}"])          # hentikan render: file yang sedang ditulis dibuang di bawah
import time; time.sleep(3)
def ok(p):                                              # PNG utuh: berakhir dengan chunk IEND
    try:
        b = open(p, "rb").read()
        return b[:8] == b"\\x89PNG\\r\\n\\x1a\\n" and b[-12:-8] == b"\\x00\\x00\\x00\\x00" and b[-8:-4] == b"IEND"
    except Exception:
        return False
d = "{R.REMOTE}/out"
got = sorted(n for n in (os.listdir(d) if os.path.isdir(d) else []) if n.endswith(".png") and ok(os.path.join(d, n)))
bad = sorted(n for n in (os.listdir(d) if os.path.isdir(d) else []) if n.endswith(".png") and n not in got)
with tarfile.open("{R.REMOTE}/ambil.tar", "w") as t:
    for n in got: t.add(os.path.join(d, n), arcname=n)
print("UTUH", len(got), "RUSAK", bad)
print("@@OK@@")
'''
print(R._py(code, 300, "kumpulkan frame").strip().splitlines()[-2])
tar = out / ".ambil.tar"
R._download(f"{R.REMOTE}/ambil.tar", tar)
with tarfile.open(tar) as t:
    mem = [m for m in t.getmembers() if not (out / m.name).exists()]
    t.extractall(out, members=mem, filter="data")
tar.unlink()
names = sorted(m.name for m in mem)
print("diekstrak", len(names), names[:1], names[-1:])
if names:
    p = PV.record_chunk(out, story / "build" / "scene.blend", [(int(n[2:6]), n) for n in names],
        {"engine": "EEVEE", "resolution": [1080, 1920], "samples": 32, "alpha_tokoh": "scene", "glare": "scene", "colab": True},
        f"colab:{os.environ.get('COLAB_SESSION', 'utama')}",
        {"gpu": "T4", "blender_remote": R.BLENDER, "workers": 3,
         "peristiwa": "pemulihan_manual",
         "catatan": "render dihentikan atas permintaan pengguna; frame utuh (PNG berakhir IEND) diunduh manual dari VM "
                    "sebelum potongan selesai. scene.blend lokal di-pack sebelum diunggah; checksum = berkas lokal"})
    print("provenance:", p.name)
    bid = json.loads((story / "build" / "manifest.json").read_text())["build_id"]
    PV.write_index(out, [int(n[2:6]) for n in names], bid)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/colab_bantu.py
# sha256: 1f7e56bf9a030254e56886b9682c751051241106d858ae660d88a3d6d6c62b4f
==============================================================================================================
"""Sesi Colab tambahan untuk mempercepat render video: merender frame dari BELAKANG (mundur) ke folder frame
yang sama, sementara `make.py --colab` maju dari depan. Tiap potongan melewati frame yang sudah ada.

    COLAB_SESSION=anim2d_b .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/colab_bantu.py stories/benchmark_warung
Sesi tambahan otomatis dihentikan setelah selesai."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
sys.argv = sys.argv[:2]
import make  # noqa: E402
from engine.colab import remote  # noqa: E402

story = ROOT / sys.argv[1]
plan = json.loads((story / "build" / "plan.json").read_text())
frames = story / "build" / "frames_penuh"
want = list(range(plan["end"], 0, -1))                       # mundur dari frame terakhir
try:
    remote.render(story / "build" / "scene.blend", frames, want, 100, 32, make.blender(), make.GPU_ENV,
                  prefix="f_", log=lambda m: print(m, flush=True))
finally:
    remote.stop(lambda m: print(m, flush=True))
print("@@BANTU_SELESAI@@", flush=True)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/colab_yatim.py
# sha256: 1ca913000efe8eefd0fa6e747f722a825f3ea36b65f47d01dfc716e97fa5ae3e
==============================================================================================================
"""Deteksi VM Colab 'yatim': alokasi GPU aktif di akun yang tidak tercatat sebagai sesi bernama di colab-cli
(mis. alokasi yang datang terlambat setelah permintaan GPU dilaporkan 'tidak tersedia'). VM yatim tetap memakai
compute unit. Default hanya MELAPORKAN; '--hentikan' melepas VM yatim (bukan sesi bernama).
  ~/.local/share/uv/tools/google-colab-cli/bin/python scripts/dev/colab_yatim.py [--hentikan]"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from colab_cli.common import State

st = State()
known = {v.get("endpoint") for v in json.loads((Path.home() / ".config/colab-cli/sessions.json").read_text()).values()}
aktif = [a.endpoint for a in st.client.list_assignments()]
yatim = [e for e in aktif if e not in known]
print(f"alokasi aktif {len(aktif)}, sesi bernama {len(known & set(aktif))}, yatim {len(yatim)}: {yatim}")
if yatim and "--hentikan" in sys.argv:
    for e in yatim:
        st.client.unassign(e)
        print("dilepas:", e)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/klip_diagnostik.py
# sha256: 94d6b88c419bca2f34b25ee21c7e58ff45d9cb0eba55b7608d2cd3440523e2e1
==============================================================================================================
"""Klip diagnostik Phase 4.2: CUPLIKAN dari video final (bukan render terpisah), diberi label cerita/build/rentang frame
dan nomor frame terbakar di pojok. Rentang diambil dari data build (interaksi.json/plan.json), bukan nomor tetap.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/klip_diagnostik.py
Hanya video yang build ID-nya (komentar metadata) cocok dengan manifest cerita yang diproses."""
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
OUT = ROOT / "output" / "phase42"
FONT = "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/noto/NotoSans-Regular.ttf"
STORIES = {"benchmark": "benchmark_warung", "generalisasi": "_uji_phase4_rumah", "uji3": "uji3_kopi_sore"}
FPS = 30


EXTRA = {"benchmark_warung": [("kedip_meja_gerobak_shot7", 458, 540)],     # titik kedip yang ditemukan QA video 4.2
         "uji3_kopi_sore": [("kedip_jendela_shot2", 61, 145)]}


def ranges(st):
    """-> [(nama, f0, f1)] dari data build: shot makan/minum (kontak mulut) dan serah-terima"""
    b = ROOT / "stories" / st / "build"
    inter = json.loads((b / "interaksi.json").read_text())
    plan = json.loads((b / "plan.json").read_text())
    out = []
    for h in inter.get("serah_terima", []):
        out.append((f"serah_terima_f{h['frame']}", max(1, h["frame"] - 45), min(plan["end"], h["frame"] + 30)))
    for n, p in inter.get("props", {}).items():
        k = sorted(int(f) for f, v in p.get("kontak_mulut_per_frame", {}).items() if v[0] == "kontak")
        if not k:
            continue
        # shot yang memuat kontak terakhir (suapan/tegukan di dalam shot pemakan), dipotong ke ≤ 3 detik di sekitarnya
        s = next(x for x in plan["shots"] if x["f0"] <= k[-1] <= x["f1"])
        f0, f1 = max(s["f0"], k[-1] - 50), min(s["f1"], k[-1] + 40)
        out.append((f"{n.rsplit('_', 2)[0]}_mulut_shot{s['n']}", f0, f1))
    return out + EXTRA.get(st, [])


def main():
    for key, st in STORIES.items():
        bid = json.loads((ROOT / "stories" / st / "build" / "manifest.json").read_text())["build_id"]
        vid = ROOT / "stories" / st / "output" / f"{st}.mp4"
        if not vid.exists():
            print(key, "tidak ada video"); continue
        meta = subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format_tags=comment", "-of",
                               "default=nw=1:nk=1", str(vid)], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        if f"build_id={bid}" not in meta:
            print(key, "DILEWATI: video bukan build", bid); continue
        for nm, f0, f1 in ranges(st):
            dst = OUT / f"09_klip_{key}_{nm}_f{f0}-{f1}_{bid}.mp4"
            lab = f"CUPLIKAN video final {key} {bid}  f{f0}-{f1}  ({nm})".replace(":", r"\:")
            vf = (f"select='between(n\\,{f0 - 1}\\,{f1 - 1})',setpts=N/{FPS}/TB,"
                  f"drawtext=fontfile={FONT}:text='{lab}':x=20:y=20:fontsize=30:fontcolor=white:box=1:boxcolor=black@0.6,"
                  f"drawtext=fontfile={FONT}:text='f%{{eif\\:n+{f0}\\:d}}':x=20:y=64:fontsize=40:fontcolor=yellow:box=1:boxcolor=black@0.6")
            r = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", str(vid), "-vf", vf, "-an", "-c:v", "libx264",
                                "-crf", "20", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(dst)], capture_output=True, text=True)
            print(key, dst.name, "OK" if r.returncode == 0 else r.stderr[-300:])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


==============================================================================================================
# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/lembar_mata_profil.py
# sha256: 419ebe189e733e793957c12a1d830a68420a955ebbfabf8a953ae954dd9b85c9
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.1-B: lembar diagnostik keadaan mata (fokus-bawah / ngantuk / sedih / kedip / senyum-tutup) dan tampak
profil untuk beberapa tokoh, disusun dari lapisan sprite rig (chars/<tokoh>/*.png + rig.json). Tanpa Blender.
Pakai: python3 scripts/dev/lembar_mata_profil.py <keluaran.png> <story_dir>[:tokoh,...] ..."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

STATES = [("open", "calm", "flat", "terbuka"), ("bawah", "fokus", "flat", "fokus ke bawah"),
          ("ngantuk", "calm", "flat", "ngantuk"), ("sedih", "sad", "frown", "sedih"),
          ("closed", "calm", "flat", "kedip"), ("happy", "soft", "smile", "senyum tutup")]


def compose(cdir, meta, names, box):
    W, H = meta["canvas"]
    res = meta["res"]
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    for n in names:
        p = cdir / f"{n}.png"
        if not p.exists():
            continue
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = meta["crops"][n]
        lay = Image.open(p).convert("RGBA").resize((int(x1 - x0), int(y1 - y0)), Image.LANCZOS)
        im.alpha_composite(lay, (int(x0), int(y0)))
    return im.crop(box)


def main():
    out = Path(sys.argv[1])
    rows = []
    for arg in sys.argv[2:]:
        sd, _, only = arg.partition(":")
        cdirs = sorted(p for p in (Path(sd) / "build" / "chars").iterdir() if (p / "rig.json").exists())
        for cd in cdirs:
            if only and cd.name not in only.split(","):
                continue
            rows.append(cd)
    box = (150, 80, 850, 900)
    cw, ch = 210, 246
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cw * len(STATES) * 2 + 120, ch * len(rows) + 40), (238, 238, 232))
    dr = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
    for j, (e, b, m, lab) in enumerate(STATES):
        for k, sfx in enumerate(("", "_s")):
            dr.text((120 + (j * 2 + k) * cw + 8, 10), f"{lab}{' (profil)' if sfx else ''}", fill=(20, 20, 20))
    for i, cd in enumerate(rows):
        meta = json.loads((cd / "rig.json").read_text())
        dr.text((8, 40 + i * ch + ch // 2), cd.name, fill=(20, 20, 20))
        for j, (e, b, m, lab) in enumerate(STATES):
            for k, sfx in enumerate(("", "_s")):
                names = [f"badan{sfx}", f"kepala{sfx}", f"wajah{sfx}", f"mata_{e}{sfx}", f"alis_{b}{sfx}", f"mulut_{m}{sfx}"]
                im = compose(cd, meta, names, box).resize((cw - 6, ch - 6), Image.LANCZOS)
                bg = Image.new("RGBA", im.size, (250, 250, 246, 255)); bg.alpha_composite(im)
                sheet.paste(bg.convert("RGB"), (120 + (j * 2 + k) * cw, 40 + i * ch))
    sheet.save(out)
    print("lembar:", out, len(rows), "tokoh")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/metrik_cahaya.py
# sha256: 2fcdc75331bc95e8a05f68549e6b56170226a12ea6d7292edcf23594fbc13c7b
==============================================================================================================
"""Metrik cahaya sederhana untuk still (PNG): black crush, sorotan terbakar, kecerahan median, dan kontras lokal.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/metrik_cahaya.py <png...>"""
import sys

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

print(f"{'berkas':40s} {'hitam<3%':>9s} {'gelap<8%':>9s} {'terbakar>97%':>13s} {'median':>7s} {'p95':>6s}")
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    a = np.asarray(Image.open(p).convert("RGB"), dtype=np.float32) / 255
    y = a @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])
    print(f"{p[-40:]:40s} {100 * (y < 0.03).mean():8.1f}% {100 * (y < 0.08).mean():8.1f}% {100 * (y > 0.97).mean():12.2f}% "
          f"{np.median(y):7.3f} {np.percentile(y, 95):6.3f}")

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/paket_phase4.py
# sha256: 46b424338ab6ac6422f58b9251fde129c585c0c049bc36fb3ab340e39d6b7a02
==============================================================================================================
"""Rakit paket review Phase 4 (output/phase4/) dari SATU build: semua file dicek build_id-nya lewat manifest.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/paket_phase4.py stories/benchmark_warung
Membuat 10_source_code.txt dan 11_configuration.txt; menyalin manifest. Still/video dibuat terpisah (make.py)."""
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from engine import manifest  # noqa: E402
from engine.common import Story  # noqa: E402

story = Story(sys.argv[1])
out = ROOT / "output" / "phase4"
out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
man = json.loads((story.build / "manifest.json").read_text())
bid = man["build_id"]

files = sorted(set(ROOT.glob("engine/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("engine/sets/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("engine/colab/*.py"))
               | {ROOT / "make.py"} | set(ROOT.glob("scripts/dev/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("tests/*.py")))
with open(out / "10_source_code.txt", "w") as f:
    f.write(f"# Source code Phase 4 — build {bid}\n# source_hash manifest: {man['inputs'].get('source_hash')}\n")
    f.write("# Daftar file (sha256[:12], baris):\n")
    for p in files:
        t = p.read_text()
        f.write(f"#   {p.relative_to(ROOT)}  {hashlib.sha256(t.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}  {t.count(chr(10))}\n")
    for p in files:
        f.write(f"\n\n{'=' * 100}\n# FILE: {p.relative_to(ROOT)}\n{'=' * 100}\n")
        f.write(p.read_text())

with open(out / "11_configuration.txt", "w") as f:
    f.write(f"# Konfigurasi build {bid}\n\n## story.yaml ({story.dir.relative_to(ROOT)})\n")
    f.write((story.dir / "story.yaml").read_text())
    f.write("\n\n## manifest.inputs\n" + json.dumps(man["inputs"], indent=1))
    f.write("\n\n## render (dari manifest outputs)\n")
    seen = set()
    for o in man.get("outputs", []):
        key = json.dumps(o.get("settings"), sort_keys=True)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            f.write(f"- {o['kind']}: {key}\n")
(out / "00_BUILD_MANIFEST.json").write_text(json.dumps(man, indent=1))
print("paket:", out, "build", bid)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/paket_phase41.py
# sha256: 1e6c1c840113e1a12ea210e3d6a1a25392b9c5c672d7cbe0742aae35b4d09aa4
==============================================================================================================
"""Rakit paket review Phase 4.1 (output/phase41/) dari SATU build: semua file dicek build_id-nya lewat manifest.
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/paket_phase41.py stories/benchmark_warung
Membuat 10_source_code.txt dan 11_configuration.txt; menyalin manifest. Still/video dibuat terpisah (make.py)."""
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
from engine import manifest  # noqa: E402
from engine.common import Story  # noqa: E402

story = Story(sys.argv[1])
out = ROOT / "output" / "phase41"
out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
man = json.loads((story.build / "manifest.json").read_text())
bid = man["build_id"]

files = sorted(set(ROOT.glob("engine/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("engine/sets/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("engine/colab/*.py"))
               | {ROOT / "make.py"} | set(ROOT.glob("scripts/dev/*.py")) | set(ROOT.glob("tests/*.py")))
with open(out / "10_source_code.txt", "w") as f:
    f.write(f"# Source code Phase 4.1 — build {bid}\n# source_hash manifest: {man['inputs'].get('source_hash')}\n")
    f.write("# Daftar file (sha256[:12], baris):\n")
    for p in files:
        t = p.read_text()
        f.write(f"#   {p.relative_to(ROOT)}  {hashlib.sha256(t.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}  {t.count(chr(10))}\n")
    for p in files:
        f.write(f"\n\n{'=' * 100}\n# FILE: {p.relative_to(ROOT)}\n{'=' * 100}\n")
        f.write(p.read_text())

with open(out / "11_configuration.txt", "w") as f:
    f.write(f"# Konfigurasi build {bid}\n\n## story.yaml ({story.dir.relative_to(ROOT)})\n")
    f.write((story.dir / "story.yaml").read_text())
    f.write("\n\n## manifest.inputs\n" + json.dumps(man["inputs"], indent=1))
    f.write("\n\n## render (dari manifest outputs)\n")
    seen = set()
    for o in man.get("outputs", []):
        key = json.dumps(o.get("settings"), sort_keys=True)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            f.write(f"- {o['kind']}: {key}\n")
(out / "00_BUILD_MANIFEST.json").write_text(json.dumps(man, indent=1))
print("paket:", out, "build", bid)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def bid_of(story):
    return json.loads((ROOT / "stories" / story / "build" / "manifest.json").read_text())["build_id"]


def sheet(pairs, out, cols, w=240, h=336, title=""):
    rows = (len(pairs) + cols - 1) // cols
    n_img = len(pairs[0][1])
    im = Image.new("RGB", (cols * w, rows * (n_img * (h + 18)) + 30), "white")
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((6, 6), title, fill="black")
    for i, (f, imgs) in enumerate(pairs):
        x = (i % cols) * w; y0 = 30 + (i // cols) * n_img * (h + 18)
        for j, (lab, p) in enumerate(imgs):
            y = y0 + j * (h + 18)
            d.text((x + 4, y + 2), f"{lab} f{f}", fill="black")
            im.paste(Image.open(p).convert("RGB").resize((w, h)), (x, y + 16))
    im.save(out, quality=90)

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/preview_tokoh.py
# sha256: 63225b81cc98c1559f99154a5b1b706068863afd3ae8f05b93184dbbcb6ab21e

"""Pratinjau cepat gambar tokoh (tanpa Blender): .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/preview_tokoh.py stories/<nama> out.png [ekspresi...]"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
from PIL import Image
from engine import chars as C
from engine.common import Story

st = Story(sys.argv[1]); out = sys.argv[2]
exprs = sys.argv[3:] or ["neutral", "smile", "talk", "surprised"]
tiles = []
for name, cdef in st.data["tokoh"].items():
    sp = C.normalize(cdef.get("rupa", {}))
    for e in exprs:
        v = {"view": "back"} if e == "back" else {"expr": e}
        if e.startswith("view:"):
            v = {"view": e[5:], "expr": "neutral"}
        im = C.draw_human(sp, v).im.resize((C.W // 2, C.H // 2), Image.LANCZOS)
        bg = Image.new("RGBA", im.size, (90, 100, 120, 255)); bg.alpha_composite(im); tiles.append(bg)
W, H = tiles[0].size
cols = len(exprs)
sheet = Image.new("RGB", (W * cols, H * ((len(tiles) + cols - 1) // cols)))
for i, t in enumerate(tiles):
    sheet.paste(t.convert("RGB"), ((i % cols) * W, (i // cols) * H))
sheet.save(out)

# lapisan rig (termasuk 3/4) bila diminta: PREVIEW_RIG=1
import os
if os.environ.get("PREVIEW_RIG"):
    from engine import rig2d
    tiles = []
    for name, cdef in st.data["tokoh"].items():
        sp = C.normalize(cdef.get("rupa", {}))
        L = rig2d.draw_layers(sp)
        for sfx in ("", "_q"):
            base = Image.new("RGBA", (C.W * C.S, C.H * C.S), (90, 100, 120, 255))
            for ln in ["kaki_stand", "badan" + sfx, "lengan_atas_kiri", "lengan_bawah_kiri", "lengan_atas_kanan", "lengan_bawah_kanan_genggam",
                       "kepala" + sfx, "wajah" + sfx, "mata_open" + sfx, "alis_calm" + sfx, "mulut_smile" + sfx]:
                base.alpha_composite(L[ln].im)
            tiles.append(base.resize((C.W // 2, C.H // 2), Image.LANCZOS))
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (C.W // 2 * len(tiles), C.H // 2))
    for i, t in enumerate(tiles):
        sheet.paste(t.convert("RGB"), (i * C.W // 2, 0))
    sheet.save(out.replace(".png", "_rig.png"))

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/qa_kedip.py
# sha256: d4a62f9020d911c980e3fd165202ac5535247c08b0f4741e74bf992edef44c45
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.2-C: QA kedip cahaya temporal dari video (mp4) atau folder frame (f_XXXX.png).
pakai: python scripts/dev/qa_kedip.py <video.mp4|folder_frames> <plan.json> <out.json> [a b]"""
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
from engine import qa_rules as Q  # noqa: E402


def lumas_video(p, w=270, h=480):
    raw = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", str(p), "-vf", f"scale={w}:{h},format=gray", "-f", "rawvideo", "-"],
                         capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
    a = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8).reshape(-1, h, w)
    return {i + 1: float(x.mean()) for i, x in enumerate(a)}


def lumas_dir(d):
    from PIL import Image
    return {int(p.stem[2:6]): float(np.asarray(Image.open(p).convert("L").resize((270, 480))).mean())
            for p in sorted(Path(d).glob("f_*.png"))}


def main():
    src, plan, out = Path(sys.argv[1]), json.load(open(sys.argv[2])), Path(sys.argv[3])
    m = lumas_video(src) if src.suffix == ".mp4" else lumas_dir(src)
    fade = None
    if src.suffix == ".mp4" and m:                       # make.py: fade-in 0,5 s & fade-out 0,9 s (30 fps) -> bukan kedip
        last = max(m)
        fade = [(1, 16), (last - 28, last)]
        m = {k: v for k, v in m.items() if 16 < k < last - 28}
    if len(sys.argv) > 5:
        a, b = int(sys.argv[4]), int(sys.argv[5]); m = {k: v for k, v in m.items() if a <= k <= b}
    cuts = [s["f0"] for s in plan["shots"]]
    per_shot = {}
    for s in plan["shots"]:
        sub = {k: v for k, v in m.items() if s["f0"] <= k <= s["f1"]}
        if len(sub) >= 3:
            per_shot[str(s["n"])] = dict(Q.temporal_flicker(sub, cuts), kamera=s["kamera"], rentang=[s["f0"], s["f1"]])
    tot = Q.temporal_flicker(m, cuts)
    rep = {"sumber": str(src), "status": tot["status"], "total": tot, "per_shot": per_shot,
           "dikecualikan_fade": fade}
    out.write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=1))
    print(json.dumps({"status": tot["status"], "maks": tot.get("maks"), "frame_maks": tot.get("frame_maks"),
                      "shot_bermasalah": {k: (v["status"], v.get("maks")) for k, v in per_shot.items() if v["status"] not in ("PASS", "NOT_TESTED")}}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/qa_render.py
# sha256: 55f3f379897f069304aeac616fbfb5d3e32756bc97bdf0425101d4f45fb496fb
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.1-D: QA PASCA-RENDER (tanpa Blender; numpy + FFmpeg).

Pakai:
  .venv/bin/python scripts/dev/qa_render.py <story_dir> [--video out.mp4] [--frames a-b] [--keluar laporan.json]
    --video  : video hasil (default: output/<slug>.mp4). Bila tidak ada, cek video dilewati (NOT_TESTED).
    --frames : rentang frame mentah build/frames_penuh yang diperiksa (default: seluruh plan bila lengkap).

Cek (status PASS / WARN / FAIL / NOT_TESTED / NOT_APPLICABLE):
  frame_lengkap        : frame mentah ada, terbaca, ukuran seragam; jumlah frame video = rentang yang diharapkan
  frame_kembar         : frame berurutan identik (render macet) -> WARN
  black_crush          : fraksi piksel luma <= 4 (8-bit) pada video ter-grading (fade awal/akhir dikecualikan)
  highlight_clipping   : fraksi piksel kanal maks >= 253
  flicker_temporal     : turunan kedua luma rata-rata (global & 4x4 ubin) di DALAM shot (frame potongan dikecualikan)
  artefak_alpha_outline: HEURISTIK pada frame mentah: piksel terang terisolasi di dalam tinta (bintik HASHED alpha),
                         dan piksel magenta tekstur hilang. Angka heuristik -> hanya WARN, bukan bukti visual.
  durasi_av            : durasi aliran video vs audio vs plan
  sinkron_dialog_timestamp : (a) event kata animasi (plan.dialogue.kata, frame) vs timestamp kata TTS (timeline.json);
                         (b) onset energi suara di audio video di sekitar awal tiap baris vs timestamp baris.
  lip_sync_perseptual  : NOT_TESTED -- kecocokan bentuk mulut dgn bunyi butuh penilaian manusia (dipisah dari (a)/(b)).
Tidak ada klaim visual dari angka: laporan menyebut ini indikator, bukan penilaian artistik."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

QW, QH = 270, 480                       # resolusi analisis (1/4)
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
from engine.tts import PAD_BEFORE  # noqa: E402  (jeda sebelum suara; timestamp kata sudah memuatnya)


def status_of(v, warn, fail, higher_bad=True):
    if v is None:
        return "NOT_TESTED"
    if higher_bad:
        return "FAIL" if v > fail else ("WARN" if v > warn else "PASS")
    return "FAIL" if v < fail else ("WARN" if v < warn else "PASS")


def ffprobe(path):
    out = subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "stream=codec_type,duration,nb_frames,r_frame_rate",
                          "-show_entries", "format_tags=comment", "-of", "json", str(path)], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    return json.loads(out or "{}")


def decode_video(path, n_expected):
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", str(path), "-vf", f"scale={QW}:{QH}", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "-"]
    raw = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True).stdout
    n = len(raw) // (QW * QH * 3)
    return np.frombuffer(raw[:n * QW * QH * 3], np.uint8).reshape(n, QH, QW, 3)


def decode_audio(path, sr=16000):
    raw = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", str(path), "-vn", "-ac", "1", "-ar", str(sr), "-f", "s16le", "-"],
                         capture_output=True).stdout
    return np.frombuffer(raw, np.int16).astype(np.float32) / 32768.0, sr


def luma(rgb):
    rgb = rgb.astype(np.float32)
    return 0.2126 * rgb[..., 0] + 0.7152 * rgb[..., 1] + 0.0722 * rgb[..., 2]


def speech_env(x, sr, hop=0.01):
    """energi pita bicara (~300-3400 Hz) per 10 ms (FFT per jendela)"""
    h = int(sr * hop); w = h * 2
    n = max(0, (len(x) - w) // h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0), hop
    idx = np.arange(w)[None, :] + h * np.arange(n)[:, None]
    seg = x[idx] * np.hanning(w)[None, :]
    spec = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(seg, axis=1)) ** 2
    fr = np.fft.rfftfreq(w, 1 / sr)
    band = (fr >= 300) & (fr <= 3400)
    return spec[:, band].sum(axis=1), hop


def check_dialogue(story, plan, video):
    fps = plan["fps"]
    tl_p = story / "build" / "audio" / "timeline.json"
    res = {}
    if not tl_p.exists():
        return {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "timeline.json tidak ada"}
    tl = json.loads(tl_p.read_text())
    words = {l["id"]: l["words"] for l in tl["lines"]}
    # (a) event kata animasi (frame) vs timestamp kata TTS
    devs = []
    for d in plan.get("dialogue", []):
        ws = [w for w in words.get(d["baris"], []) if w["w"].strip(".,!?")]
        for k, (txt, f0, f1) in enumerate(d.get("kata", [])):
            cand = [w for w in ws if w["w"].strip(".,!?\"'").lower() == txt.strip(".,!?\"'").lower()]
            if not cand:
                continue
            t = min(cand, key=lambda w: abs(w["t0"] * fps + 1 - f0))["t0"]
            devs.append({"baris": d["baris"], "kata": txt, "frame_event": f0, "frame_audio": round(t * fps + 1, 2),
                         "selisih_frame": round(f0 - (t * fps + 1), 2)})
    a_max = max((abs(x["selisih_frame"]) for x in devs), default=None)
    res["event_kata_vs_timestamp_tts"] = {"status": status_of(a_max, 1.0, 2.0) if devs else "NOT_APPLICABLE",
                                          "selisih_maks_frame": a_max, "n_kata": len(devs), "rincian": devs[:40],
                                          "toleransi": "PASS <=1 frame, WARN <=2, FAIL >2"}
    # (b) pergeseran suara: korelasi silang amplop suara BERSIH tiap baris (nX.mp3, ditempatkan di start_ms) dengan
    #     audio di dalam VIDEO (dan mix.wav sebelum encode, untuk memisahkan efek mixing vs encode AAC).
    def lags(x_all, sr):
        env, hop = speech_env(x_all, sr)
        le = np.log(env + 1e-8)
        rows = []
        for l in tl["lines"]:
            src = Path(l.get("audio", ""))
            if not src.exists():
                src = story / "build" / "audio" / f"{l['id']}.mp3"
            if not src.exists():
                continue
            xc, _ = decode_audio(src)
            ec, _ = speech_env(xc, sr)
            if len(ec) < 20:
                continue
            lc = np.log(ec + 1e-8)
            i0 = int((l["start_ms"] / 1000 + PAD_BEFORE) / hop)    # mixer menempatkan suara di start + PAD_BEFORE
            best, bl = -2.0, None
            for lag in range(-40, 41):
                j = i0 + lag
                if j < 0 or j + len(lc) > len(le):
                    continue
                seg = le[j:j + len(lc)]
                c = np.corrcoef(seg, lc)[0, 1]
                if np.isfinite(c) and c > best:
                    best, bl = c, lag
            if bl is not None:
                rows.append({"baris": l["id"], "geser_ms": round(bl * hop * 1000, 1), "korelasi": round(float(best), 3)})
        return rows
    mixp = story / "build" / "audio" / "mix.wav"
    mix_rows = lags(*decode_audio(mixp)) if mixp.exists() else []
    if video is None:
        res["geser_audio_vs_timestamp"] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "tidak ada video", "mix_wav": mix_rows}
    else:
        vid_rows = lags(*decode_audio(video))
        good = [abs(r["geser_ms"]) for r in vid_rows if r["korelasi"] >= 0.5]
        m = max(good, default=None)
        res["geser_audio_vs_timestamp"] = {
            "status": status_of(m, 1000 / fps * 1, 1000 / fps * 2) if good else "NOT_TESTED",
            "geser_maks_ms": m, "video": vid_rows, "mix_wav_sebelum_encode": mix_rows,
            "toleransi": "korelasi silang amplop pita bicara (resolusi 10 ms), baris dgn korelasi >=0.5: "
                         "PASS <=1 frame (33 ms), WARN <=2 frame, FAIL >2 frame"}
    sts = [v["status"] for v in res.values()]
    res["status"] = "FAIL" if "FAIL" in sts else ("WARN" if "WARN" in sts else ("PASS" if "PASS" in sts else "NOT_TESTED"))
    return res


def check_raw_frames(story, plan, a, b):
    fr = story / "build" / "frames_penuh"
    missing, bad, sizes, dups, specks, magenta = [], [], set(), [], {}, {}
    prev = None
    for f in range(a, b + 1):
        p = fr / f"f_{f:04d}.png"
        if not p.exists() or p.stat().st_size == 0:
            missing.append(f); prev = None; continue
        try:
            im = Image.open(p); im.load()
        except Exception:                                   # noqa: BLE001
            bad.append(f); prev = None; continue
        sizes.add(im.size)
        h = hashlib.sha1(im.tobytes()).hexdigest()
        if prev == h:
            dups.append(f)
        prev = h
        if (f - a) % 3 == 0:                                 # heuristik artefak: tiap 3 frame, resolusi penuh
            arr = np.asarray(im.convert("RGB"))
            L = luma(arr)
            dark = L < 50
            nb = np.ones_like(dark)
            for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
                for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
                    if dy or dx:
                        nb &= np.roll(np.roll(dark, dy, 0), dx, 1)
            iso = (L > 120) & nb
            iso[0, :] = iso[-1, :] = False; iso[:, 0] = iso[:, -1] = False
            specks[f] = int(iso.sum())
            mg = (arr[..., 0] > 240) & (arr[..., 1] < 20) & (arr[..., 2] > 240)
            magenta[f] = int(mg.sum())
    sp = np.array(list(specks.values())) if specks else np.zeros(0)
    out = {
        "frame_lengkap": {"status": "FAIL" if (missing or bad or len(sizes) > 1) else "PASS", "rentang": [a, b],
                          "hilang": missing[:20], "n_hilang": len(missing), "rusak": bad[:20], "ukuran": sorted(sizes)},
        "frame_kembar": {"status": "WARN" if dups else "PASS", "n": len(dups), "contoh": dups[:20],
                         "catatan": "frame identik berurutan; bisa sah (tahan diam tanpa efek) atau render macet"},
        "artefak_alpha_outline": {
            "status": ("FAIL" if any(v > 0 for v in magenta.values()) else
                       ("WARN" if len(sp) and np.percentile(sp, 95) > 40 else "PASS")),
            "heuristik": True,
            "bintik_terang_terisolasi_p95": float(np.percentile(sp, 95)) if len(sp) else None,
            "bintik_maks": int(sp.max()) if len(sp) else None,
            "frame_bintik_terbanyak": sorted(specks, key=specks.get, reverse=True)[:5],
            "piksel_magenta_maks": max(magenta.values(), default=0),
            "catatan": "bintik = piksel terang (luma>120) yang SEMUA 8 tetangganya gelap (luma<50): pola khas alpha "
                       "HASHED/dither di garis tinta. Ambang WARN p95>40/frame. Heuristik, perlu dicek visual."}}
    return out


def check_video(story, plan, video, a, b):
    fps = plan["fps"]
    info = ffprobe(video)
    st = {s["codec_type"]: s for s in info.get("streams", [])}
    v_dur = float(st.get("video", {}).get("duration", 0) or 0)
    a_dur = float(st.get("audio", {}).get("duration", 0) or 0)
    n_exp = b - a + 1
    frames = decode_video(video, n_exp)
    n = len(frames)
    exp_dur = n_exp / fps
    out = {"durasi_av": {
        "status": "FAIL" if (abs(v_dur - a_dur) > 1.5 / fps or abs(v_dur - exp_dur) > 1.5 / fps) else
                  ("WARN" if abs(v_dur - a_dur) > 0.5 / fps else "PASS"),
        "video_dtk": v_dur, "audio_dtk": a_dur, "harapan_dtk": round(exp_dur, 4), "frame_video": n, "frame_harapan": n_exp,
        "komentar_build": (info.get("format", {}).get("tags") or {}).get("comment")}}
    # fade awal/akhir pada video penuh dikecualikan dari crush/flicker
    fade_in = int(0.5 * fps) + 3 if a == 1 else 0                  # + margin: batas fade memicu turunan kedua
    fade_out = int(0.9 * fps) + 3 if b == plan["end"] else 0
    sel = list(range(fade_in, max(fade_in, n - fade_out)))
    Ls = luma(frames)
    crush = np.array([(Ls[i] <= 4).mean() for i in sel])          # absolut: termasuk garis tinta & rambut hitam (gaya)
    raw_dir = story / "build" / "frames_penuh"
    by_grade, bg_frames = [], []
    for i in sel[::5]:
        rp = raw_dir / f"f_{a + i:04d}.png"
        if rp.exists():
            r = luma(np.asarray(Image.open(rp).convert("RGB").resize((QW, QH), Image.BILINEAR)))
            m = (r >= 10)
            by_grade.append(float(((Ls[i] <= 4) & m).mean())); bg_frames.append(a + i)
    by_grade = np.array(by_grade)
    clip = np.array([(frames[i].max(axis=2) >= 253).mean() for i in sel])
    out["black_crush"] = {"status": status_of(float(np.percentile(by_grade, 95)) if len(by_grade) else None, 0.01, 0.04),
                          "hilang_detail_oleh_grading_p95": round(float(np.percentile(by_grade, 95)), 4) if len(by_grade) else None,
                          "hilang_detail_oleh_grading_maks": round(float(by_grade.max()), 4) if len(by_grade) else None,
                          "frame_terburuk_grading": bg_frames[int(by_grade.argmax())] if len(by_grade) else None,
                          "hitam_absolut_p95_info": round(float(np.percentile(crush, 95)), 4) if len(crush) else None,
                          "hitam_absolut_frame_maks_info": int(a + sel[int(crush.argmax())]) if len(crush) else None,
                          "toleransi": "status = fraksi piksel yang di render mentah luma>=10 tetapi <=4 setelah grading "
                                       "(detail bayangan hilang): WARN >1%, FAIL >4% (p95, tiap 5 frame). Hitam absolut "
                                       "(termasuk garis tinta/rambut hitam = gaya) hanya informasi."}
    out["highlight_clipping"] = {"status": status_of(float(np.percentile(clip, 95)) if len(clip) else None, 0.01, 0.05),
                                 "fraksi_p95": round(float(np.percentile(clip, 95)), 4) if len(clip) else None,
                                 "fraksi_maks": round(float(clip.max()), 4) if len(clip) else None,
                                 "frame_terburuk": int(a + sel[int(clip.argmax())]) if len(clip) else None,
                                 "toleransi": "fraksi kanal>=253: WARN >1%, FAIL >5% (p95); lampu praktis memang boleh terang"}
    cuts = {s["f0"] for s in plan.get("shots", [])}
    g = Ls.mean(axis=(1, 2))
    th, tw = QH // 4, QW // 4
    tiles = Ls[:, :th * 4, :tw * 4].reshape(n, 4, th, 4, tw).mean(axis=(2, 4))
    worst, worst_t, rows = 0.0, 0.0, []
    for i in sel:
        f = a + i
        if i - 1 < 0 or i + 1 >= n or any(c in (f - 1, f, f + 1, f + 2) for c in cuts):
            continue
        d2 = abs(2 * g[i] - g[i - 1] - g[i + 1]) / 2
        d2t = float(np.abs(2 * tiles[i] - tiles[i - 1] - tiles[i + 1]).max() / 2)
        if d2 > worst:
            worst = float(d2)
        if d2t > worst_t:
            worst_t = d2t
        if d2 > 1.5 or d2t > 6:
            rows.append([f, round(float(d2), 2), round(d2t, 2)])
    s1 = status_of(worst, 1.5, 3.0)
    s2 = status_of(worst_t, 6.0, 12.0)
    out["flicker_temporal"] = {"status": "FAIL" if "FAIL" in (s1, s2) else ("WARN" if "WARN" in (s1, s2) else "PASS"),
                               "global_maks": round(worst, 2), "ubin_maks": round(worst_t, 2), "frame_mencurigakan": rows[:30],
                               "toleransi": "|2L[t]-L[t-1]-L[t+1]|/2 global WARN>1.5 FAIL>3; ubin 4x4 WARN>6 FAIL>12 "
                                            "(gerak cepat/kilat juga bisa memicu -> periksa frame yang disebut)"}
    return out


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("story")
    ap.add_argument("--video")
    ap.add_argument("--frames")
    ap.add_argument("--keluar")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    story = Path(args.story)
    plan = json.loads((story / "build" / "plan.json").read_text())
    a, b = (map(int, args.frames.split("-")) if args.frames else (1, plan["end"]))
    video = Path(args.video) if args.video else story / "output" / f"{story.name}.mp4"
    video = video if video.exists() else None
    man = story / "build" / "manifest.json"
    bid = json.loads(man.read_text()).get("build_id") if man.exists() else None
    rep = {"story": story.name, "build_id_adegan": bid, "rentang": [a, b], "video": str(video) if video else None,
           "catatan": "indikator numerik pasca-render; BUKAN penilaian artistik. Frame yang disebut perlu dilihat langsung."}
    cek = {}
    cek.update(check_raw_frames(story, plan, a, b))
    if video:
        cek.update(check_video(story, plan, video, a, b))
        cek["sinkron_dialog_timestamp"] = check_dialogue(story, plan, video) if (a, b) == (1, plan["end"]) else \
            {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "alasan": "klip parsial; uji dialog pada video penuh"}
    else:
        for k in ("durasi_av", "black_crush", "highlight_clipping", "flicker_temporal"):
            cek[k] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "alasan": "video tidak ada"}
        cek["sinkron_dialog_timestamp"] = check_dialogue(story, plan, None)
    cek["lip_sync_perseptual"] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED",
                                  "alasan": "kecocokan bentuk mulut dengan bunyi memerlukan penilaian manusia; "
                                            "tidak disimpulkan dari uji timestamp"}
    rep["cek"] = cek
    cnt = {}
    for v in cek.values():
        cnt[v["status"]] = cnt.get(v["status"], 0) + 1
    rep["ringkas"] = cnt
    out = Path(args.keluar) if args.keluar else story / "build" / f"qa_render_{a}_{b}.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=1, default=float))
    print("QA_RENDER", json.dumps({k: v["status"] for k, v in cek.items()}), cnt, "->", out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""Ringkasan genggam per benda per shot dari interaksi.json (orientasi, residual, skala).
  python3 scripts/dev/ringkas_genggam.py stories/<cerita>"""
import json
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

b = Path(sys.argv[1]) / "build"
d = json.loads((b / "interaksi.json").read_text())
plan = json.loads((b / "plan.json").read_text())


def shot(f):
    for s in plan["shots"]:
        if s["f0"] <= f < s["f1"]:
            return s["n"]


for nm, rows in d["per_frame"].items():
    agg = defaultdict(list)
    for r in rows:
        if r[4] in ("hand", "carry", "place"):
            agg[shot(r[0])].append(r)
    pr = d["props"][nm]
    print(nm, "tol", pr.get("toleransi_orientasi_derajat"), "skala", pr["skala_presentasi_min_max"],
          "geser", pr.get("geser_blocking_presentasi_maks_m"), "res_px_maks", pr["residual_genggam_px_maks"])
    for k, v in sorted(agg.items(), key=lambda x: x[0] or 0):
        o = sorted(x[5] for x in v)
        print(f"   shot {k}: n={len(v)} ori med {o[len(o)//2]:.1f} p95 {o[int(.95*(len(o)-1))]:.1f} maks {o[-1]:.1f}"
              f" | res_px maks {max(x[2] for x in v):.1f} | k min {min(x[3] for x in v):.3f}")
print("ik_miss", len(d["ik_miss"]), "serah", d.get("serah_terima"))

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/solid_view.py
# sha256: 56fad50b8e1975858a6be6b8b760c8e2d4aece1437921fb49586f37be9020165
==============================================================================================================
"""Solid view (Workbench) dari scene.blend: kamera shot + tampak atas orthografik di sekitar tokoh.
  blender -b scene.blend -P scripts/dev/solid_view.py -- <frame> <keluaran_kamera.png> <keluaran_atas.png>
Bidang sprite tokoh tampil sebagai persegi (tanpa alpha) -> terlihat posisi bidang presentasi vs benda/dudukan dunia."""
import sys

import bpy
from mathutils import Vector

a = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:]
f, out_cam, out_top = int(a[0]), a[1], a[2]
sc = bpy.context.scene
sc.frame_set(f)
sc.render.engine = "BLENDER_WORKBENCH"
sh = sc.display.shading
sh.light = "STUDIO"; sh.color_type = "RANDOM"; sh.show_cavity = True; sh.show_shadows = False
sc.render.resolution_percentage = 50
sc.use_nodes = False
sc.render.filepath = out_cam
bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
# tampak atas: orthografik di atas pusat semua tokoh
roots = [o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.name.startswith("presentasi_")]
pts = [o.parent.matrix_world.translation for o in roots if o.parent] or [Vector((0, 0, 0))]
c = sum(pts, Vector()) / len(pts)
cd = bpy.data.cameras.new("atas"); cd.type = "ORTHO"; cd.ortho_scale = 5.0
cam = bpy.data.objects.new("atas", cd); sc.collection.objects.link(cam)
cam.location = (c.x, c.y, 8.0); cam.rotation_euler = (0, 0, 0)
for o in bpy.data.objects:                                          # buang atap/plafon/kanopi agar tampak atas terbuka
    if o.type == "MESH" and o.matrix_world.translation.z > 2.2 and o.dimensions.x * o.dimensions.y > 1.0:
        o.hide_render = True
sc.camera = cam
sc.render.resolution_x, sc.render.resolution_y = 1080, 1080
sc.render.filepath = out_top
bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
