# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/validasi_interaksi.py
# sha256: ed269bf0e315832b65811bda815c3065e3431339f9e9ece7b61468ddb900d8bf
==============================================================================================================
"""Validasi interaksi & klip (Phase 3 Tahap A/B). Dijalankan DI DALAM Blender:
  blender -b <scene.blend> -P scripts/dev/validasi_interaksi.py -- <interaksi.json> <keluaran.json> [frame_awal frame_akhir langkah]

Per frame (semua frame, atau rentang/langkah yang diberikan):
  1 melayang     benda yang dipegang: pusat tangan (empty) harus berada di dalam kotak layar benda (+toleransi)
  2 lapisan      benda dipegang: semua titik di depan bidang tokoh (tangan terlihat) / di belakang (tampak belakang)
  3 lengan       lengan pemegang harus terlihat (tidak tersembunyi) bila tokoh tidak membelakangi kamera
  4 dingklik     dudukan seluruhnya di belakang bidang sprite; tinggi dudukan = garis pantat sprite (selisih cm)
  5 sutil        ujung bilah di dalam lingkar wajan
  6 tembus       objek 3D yang menembus bidang tokoh di dalam kotak siluet (kandidat klip)
  7 wajah        per shot: titik wajah subjek terhalang benda/tokoh lain (ray cast + alpha sprite)
Phase 4E (tambahan):
  8 genggam      titik genggam tangan berlapis (lapisan jari depan) di dalam kotak layar benda; lapisan telapak terlihat
  9 dudukan      pantat sprite vs permukaan dudukan DUNIA diukur di LAYAR (presentasi tidak menipu pengukuran)
 10 pandang      tokoh yang memasak/makan: mata mengarah ke target (wajan/piring), bukan ke kamera
 11 efek_wajah   daun jatuh / uap di depan wajah subjek (dipudarkan = lolos)
 12 temporal     lompatan posisi layar benda dipegang antar frame berurutan (bukan potongan kamera)
 13 rencana      statis dari plan.json: event mulut per baris dialog, kedip di tiap emosi bermata terbuka >4 dtk
Setiap cek -> status PASS / WARN / FAIL / NOT_TESTED (+ alasan). Cek render (crush, alpha) ada di make.py/grade.py.
Hasil: ringkasan + daftar pelanggaran (frame, objek, nilai) + status."""
import json
import math
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

import os

import bpy
import numpy as np
from bpy_extras.object_utils import world_to_camera_view
from mathutils import Vector

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..", "engine"))
import qa_rules as QA  # noqa: E402

argv = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:]
rep = json.loads(open(argv[0]).read())
out_path = argv[1]
sc = bpy.context.scene
f_a = int(argv[2]) if len(argv) > 2 else 1
f_b = int(argv[3]) if len(argv) > 3 else sc.frame_end
step = int(argv[4]) if len(argv) > 4 else 1
cam = sc.camera
RX, RY = sc.render.resolution_x, sc.render.resolution_y
plan = json.loads(open(bpy.path.abspath("//plan.json")).read())
rigs = [c["name"] for c in plan["characters"] if c.get("rig")]
O = bpy.data.objects
dg = bpy.context.evaluated_depsgraph_get()


def px(p):
    v = world_to_camera_view(sc, cam, p)
    return Vector((v.x * RX, (1 - v.y) * RY)), v.z


def visible(o):
    return not o.hide_render


def mesh_world_pts(o):
    pts = []
    for ob in [o] + list(o.children_recursive):
        if ob.type == "MESH" and visible(ob):
            me = ob.data
            M = ob.matrix_world
            n = len(me.vertices)
            co = np.empty(n * 3); me.vertices.foreach_get("co", co)
            co = co.reshape(-1, 3)
            if n > 400:
                co = co[np.linspace(0, n - 1, 400).astype(int)]
            pts += [M @ Vector(c) for c in co]
    return pts


def plane_depth(rig, p):
    m = O[f"cermin_{rig}"].matrix_world.inverted()
    return -(m @ p).y


def seg_at(tl, f):
    for a, b, mode, holder, hs in tl:
        if a <= f < b:
            return mode, holder, hs
    return None


# sprite alpha untuk ray cast wajah
_alpha = {}


def sprite_alpha(o, hit):
    """alpha piksel sprite di titik hit (bidang lokal XZ)"""
    if o.name not in _alpha:
        mat = o.active_material
        img = None
        if mat and mat.use_nodes:
            for n in mat.node_tree.nodes:
                if n.type == "TEX_IMAGE" and n.image:
                    img = n.image
        if img is None:
            _alpha[o.name] = None
        else:
            a = np.array(img.pixels[:], dtype=np.float32).reshape(img.size[1], img.size[0], 4)[..., 3]
            xs = [v.co.x for v in o.data.vertices]; zs = [v.co.z for v in o.data.vertices]
            _alpha[o.name] = (a, min(xs), max(xs), min(zs), max(zs))
    d = _alpha[o.name]
    if d is None:
        return 1.0
    a, x0, x1, z0, z1 = d
    lp = o.matrix_world.inverted() @ hit
    u = (lp.x - x0) / max(1e-6, x1 - x0); v = (lp.z - z0) / max(1e-6, z1 - z0)
    if not (0 <= u <= 1 and 0 <= v <= 1):
        return 0.0
    h, w = a.shape
    return float(a[min(h - 1, int(v * h)), min(w - 1, int(u * w))])


def ray_opaque(origin, target, ignore_prefix):
    """True jika garis kamera->target terhalang benda tak tembus pandang (sprite: cek alpha)"""
    d = target - origin
    dist = d.length; d.normalize()
    o = origin.copy(); travelled = 0.0
    for _ in range(24):
        hit, loc, nor, idx, ob, _m = sc.ray_cast(dg, o, d, distance=dist - travelled - 0.02)
        if not hit:
            return None
        name = ob.name
        is_sprite = any(name.startswith(r + "_") for r in rigs)
        if name.startswith(ignore_prefix) or name.startswith("hujan") or name.startswith("uap") or name.startswith("ngengat") \
                or (is_sprite and sprite_alpha(ob, loc) < 0.5) or name.startswith("bayangan"):
            step_ = (loc - o).length + 0.003
            o = loc + d * 0.003; travelled += step_
            continue
        return name
    return None


V = defaultdict(list)
stats = defaultdict(list)
tested = defaultdict(int)                       # cek -> jumlah sampel yang benar-benar diuji
vis = defaultdict(set)                          # benda -> frame terlihat di bingkai saat dipegang (aturan qa_rules)
cdefs = {c["name"]: c for c in plan["characters"]}
metas = {r: json.loads(open(bpy.path.abspath(f"//chars/{r}/rig.json")).read()) for r in rigs}
U = {r: cdefs[r]["height"] / metas[r]["canvas"][1] for r in rigs}
cuts = {s["f0"] for s in plan.get("shots", [])}
prev_prop_px = {}


def grip_point(holder, nm):
    """titik genggam (dunia) dari lapisan jari depan yang terlihat -> (titik, gaya) | (None, None)"""
    for style, g in metas[holder].get("grip_hand", {}).items():
        ov = O.get(f"{holder}_tangan_{style}_{nm}_f")
        if ov is not None and visible(ov):
            return ov.matrix_world @ Vector((0, 0, -g[1] * U[holder])), style
    return None, None

shots = plan.get("shots", [])
shot_of = lambda f: next((s for s in shots if s["f0"] <= f <= s["f1"]), None)
frames = list(range(f_a, f_b + 1, step))
for f in frames:
    sc.frame_set(f)
    dg = bpy.context.evaluated_depsgraph_get()
    for pname, tl in rep.get("timeline", {}).items():
        o = O.get(pname)
        st = seg_at(tl, f)
        if o is None or st is None or not visible(o):
            continue
        mode, holder, hs = st
        if mode not in ("hand", "carry") or holder is None:
            continue
        mir = O[f"cermin_{holder}"]
        view_back = O.get(f"{holder}_belakang") is not None and (visible(O[f"{holder}_belakang"]) or
                                                                   (O.get(f"{holder}_belakang_sit") and visible(O[f"{holder}_belakang_sit"])))
        mirror = 1 if mir.matrix_world.to_3x3().determinant() > 0 else -1
        side = hs * mirror
        nm = "kiri" if side < 0 else "kanan"
        pts = mesh_world_pts(o)
        if not pts:
            continue
        # Phase 4.1: hanya benda yang benar-benar di bingkai (semua titik di depan kamera); di luar bingkai = tidak relevan
        PZ = [px(p) for p in pts]
        if min(z for _, z in PZ) <= 0:
            prev_prop_px.pop(pname, None)
            continue
        P2 = [q for q, _ in PZ]
        onscreen = max(p.x for p in P2) > 0 and min(p.x for p in P2) < RX and max(p.y for p in P2) > 0 and min(p.y for p in P2) < RY
        if not onscreen:
            prev_prop_px.pop(pname, None)
            continue
        deps = [plane_depth(holder, p) for p in pts]
        tested["lapisan"] += 1
        if not view_back:
            stats["kedalaman_min_benda_m"].append(min(deps))
            if min(deps) < 0.02:
                # di belakang bidang tokoh: salah HANYA bila badan tokoh benar-benar menutupinya di layar
                # (lengan/tangan di depan benda yang digenggam itu benar)
                Cw = cam.matrix_world.translation
                occ = [ray_opaque(Cw, q, pname) for q, dq in zip(pts, deps) if dq < 0.02]
                bad = [n for n in occ if n and n.startswith(holder + "_") and "tangan" not in n and "lengan" not in n]
                if bad:
                    V["lapisan"].append((f, pname, round(min(deps), 3), bad[0]))
                else:
                    stats["lapisan_belakang_tak_tertutup"].append(f)
            gp, style = grip_point(holder, nm)
            if gp is not None:
                hp, _ = px(gp)
                tested["genggam"] += 1
                back = O.get(f"{holder}_tangan_{style}_{nm}_b")
                if back is not None and not visible(back):
                    V["telapak_genggam_tersembunyi"].append((f, pname, nm, style))
            else:
                hand = O[f"tangan_lengan_{nm}_{holder}"]
                hp, _ = px(hand.matrix_world.translation)
            tested["melayang"] += 1
            cpx = Vector((sum(p.x for p in P2) / len(P2), sum(p.y for p in P2) / len(P2)))
            if pname in prev_prop_px and prev_prop_px[pname][0] == f - 1 and f not in cuts:
                jump = (cpx - prev_prop_px[pname][1]).length
                stats["lompatan_layar_benda_px"].append(jump); tested["temporal"] += 1
                if jump > 0.06 * RY:
                    V["lompatan_benda"].append((f, pname, round(jump, 1)))
            if onscreen:
                prev_prop_px[pname] = (f, cpx)
                vis[pname].add(f)
            x0, x1 = min(p.x for p in P2), max(p.x for p in P2); y0, y1 = min(p.y for p in P2), max(p.y for p in P2)
            dx = max(x0 - hp.x, 0, hp.x - x1); dy = max(y0 - hp.y, 0, hp.y - y1)
            dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
            # skala: toleransi 2% tinggi layar
            stats["jarak_tangan_ke_benda_px"].append(dist)
            if dist > 0.02 * RY:
                V["melayang" if gp is None else "genggam_meleset"].append((f, pname, round(dist, 1)))
            arm = O.get(f"{holder}_lengan_bawah_{nm}_genggam")
            arm2 = O.get(f"{holder}_lengan_bawah_{nm}_lepas")
            if not ((arm is not None and visible(arm)) or (arm2 is not None and visible(arm2))):
                V["lengan_tersembunyi_saat_memegang"].append((f, pname, nm))
        else:
            stats["kedalaman_maks_benda_belakang_m"].append(max(deps))
            if max(deps) > -0.015:
                V["lapisan_belakang"].append((f, pname, round(max(deps), 3)))
    # sutil di wajan
    for o in O:
        if o.name.startswith("benda_sutil") and visible(o):
            blade = next((c for c in o.children_recursive if c.name.startswith("sutil")), None)
            wok = next((w for w in O if w.name.startswith("wajan")), None)
            if blade and wok:
                tip = blade.matrix_world.translation
                wc = wok.matrix_world.translation
                d_xy = math.hypot(tip.x - wc.x, tip.y - wc.y)
                stats["sutil_jarak_ke_pusat_wajan_m"].append(d_xy)
                tested["sutil"] += 1
                if d_xy > 0.17 or tip.z < wc.z - 0.2 or tip.z > wc.z + 0.12:
                    V["sutil_di_luar_wajan"].append((f, round(d_xy, 3), round(tip.z - wc.z, 3)))
    # dudukan jangkar dunia (kursi/sofa/bangku/jok): pantat logis (tanpa presentasi) di tinggi jangkar + di atas pusatnya
    for r in rigs:
        for sa in cdefs[r].get("rig_acts", []):
            jk = sa.get("kursi_jangkar") if sa["type"] == "sit" else None
            if not jk or not (sa["f0"] <= f < sa.get("f1", 10 ** 9)):
                continue
            meta = metas[r]; u = U[r]
            butt_w = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world @ Vector((0, 0, (meta["canvas"][1] - meta["seat_y"]) * u))
            Ci = cam.matrix_world.translation
            k_ = O[f"presentasi_{r}"].scale.x if O.get(f"presentasi_{r}") else 1.0
            lg = Ci + (butt_w - Ci) / k_
            dz = lg.z - jk["pos"][2]
            dxy = math.hypot(lg.x - jk["pos"][0], lg.y - jk["pos"][1])
            tested["dudukan"] += 1
            stats["jangkar_selisih_tinggi_cm"].append(abs(dz) * 100); stats["jangkar_selisih_datar_cm"].append(dxy * 100)
            if abs(dz) > 0.02 or dxy > jk.get("lebar", 0.2):
                V["pantat_tidak_di_dudukan"].append((f, r, jk["name"], round(dz * 100, 1), round(dxy * 100, 1)))
    # dingklik
    for r in rigs:
        st_o = O.get(f"dingklik_{r}")
        if st_o is None or not visible(st_o):
            continue
        seat = next((c for c in st_o.children if c.name.startswith("dudukan")), None)
        pts = mesh_world_pts(seat)
        if not pts:
            continue
        dmax = max(plane_depth(r, p) for p in pts)
        stats["dingklik_kedalaman_maks_m"].append(dmax)
        if dmax > -0.01:
            V["dingklik_memotong_tokoh"].append((f, r, round(dmax, 3)))
        top = max(p.z for p in pts)
        meta = metas[r]; u = U[r]
        cx_ = sum(p.x for p in pts) / len(pts); cy_ = sum(p.y for p in pts) / len(pts)
        butt_w = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world @ Vector((0, 0, (meta["canvas"][1] - meta["seat_y"]) * u))
        b2, _ = px(butt_w); t2, _ = px(Vector((cx_, cy_, top)))
        up_w = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world @ Vector((0, 0, (meta["canvas"][1] - meta["seat_y"] + 100) * u))
        ppc = abs(px(up_w)[0].y - b2.y) / 100.0                        # px layar per px kanvas tokoh (skala shot)
        d_c = (b2.y - t2.y) / max(ppc, 1e-6)                           # selisih dalam px KANVAS: + = pantat di bawah dudukan
        Ci = cam.matrix_world.translation                              # fisik: buang presentasi (skala thd kamera)
        k_ = O[f"presentasi_{r}"].scale.x if O.get(f"presentasi_{r}") else 1.0
        logical_z = (Ci + (butt_w - Ci) / k_).z
        stats["dudukan_selisih_fisik_cm"].append(abs(logical_z - top) * 100)
        if not (0 <= b2.x <= RX and 0 <= b2.y <= RY):                  # pantat di luar bingkai: hanya cek fisik
            if abs(logical_z - top) > 0.02:
                V["pantat_tidak_di_dudukan"].append((f, r, None, round((logical_z - top) * 100, 1)))
            tested["dudukan"] += 1
            continue
        tested["dudukan"] += 1
        stats["dudukan_selisih_kanvas_px"].append(abs(d_c))
        if abs(logical_z - top) > 0.02 or abs(d_c) > 25:
            V["pantat_tidak_di_dudukan"].append((f, r, round(d_c, 1), round((logical_z - top) * 100, 1)))
    # tembus volume tubuh: silinder badan (jari-jari ~0.15 m, dari lutut s/d bahu) vs verteks objek 3D
    if f % 10 == 0 or f == frames[0]:
        for r in rigs:
            root = O[r]
            cdef = [c for c in plan["characters"] if c["name"] == r][0]
            meta = json.loads(open(bpy.path.abspath(f"//chars/{r}/rig.json")).read())
            u = cdef["height"] / meta["canvas"][1]
            M = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world
            z_sh = (M @ Vector((0, 0, (meta["canvas"][1] - 760) * u))).z
            z_lo = (M @ Vector((0, 0, (meta["canvas"][1] - 1040) * u))).z
            c = root.matrix_world.translation
            rad = 0.15 * cdef["height"] / 1.75
            for o in O:
                if o.type != "MESH" or not visible(o) or o.name.startswith(tuple(rigs)) or o.name.startswith(("benda_", "dingklik", "bayangan", "hujan", "uap", "daun", "ngengat", "genangan")):
                    continue
                if o.parent and (o.parent.name.startswith(("benda_", "dingklik")) or (o.parent.parent and o.parent.parent.name.startswith("benda_"))):
                    continue
                bb = [o.matrix_world @ Vector(v) for v in o.bound_box]
                if min(v.x for v in bb) > c.x + rad or max(v.x for v in bb) < c.x - rad or min(v.y for v in bb) > c.y + rad \
                        or max(v.y for v in bb) < c.y - rad or min(v.z for v in bb) > z_sh or max(v.z for v in bb) < z_lo:
                    continue
                me = o.data; n = len(me.vertices)
                co = np.empty(n * 3); me.vertices.foreach_get("co", co); co = co.reshape(-1, 3)
                Mw = np.array(o.matrix_world)
                w = co @ Mw[:3, :3].T + Mw[:3, 3]
                inside = (np.hypot(w[:, 0] - c.x, w[:, 1] - c.y) < rad) & (w[:, 2] > z_lo) & (w[:, 2] < z_sh)
                # kotak besar (sedikit verteks) -> uji juga titik tengah sisi lewat bbox lokal
                if not inside.any() and n <= 64:
                    lb = [Vector(v) for v in o.bound_box]
                    g = [Mw[:3, :3] @ np.array([x, y, z]) + Mw[:3, 3] for x in np.linspace(lb[0].x, lb[6].x, 9)
                         for y in np.linspace(lb[0].y, lb[6].y, 9) for z in np.linspace(lb[0].z, lb[6].z, 5)]
                    g = np.array(g)
                    inside = (np.hypot(g[:, 0] - c.x, g[:, 1] - c.y) < rad) & (g[:, 2] > z_lo) & (g[:, 2] < z_sh)
                if inside.any():
                    V["tembus_tubuh"].append((f, r, o.name, int(inside.sum())))
            tested["tembus"] += 1
    # arah pandang saat memasak/makan
    for r in rigs:
        acts = [a for a in cdefs[r].get("rig_acts", []) if a["type"] in ("cook", "eat") and a["f0"] <= f < a.get("f1", 10 ** 9)]
        if any(a["type"] == "look" and a["f0"] <= f < a.get("f1", 10 ** 9) for a in cdefs[r].get("rig_acts", [])):
            continue                                                  # aksi 'lihat' eksplisit dari cerita: arah pandang sah
        eyes = O.get(f"mata_{r}")
        if not acts or eyes is None or "rest" not in eyes or not visible(O.get(f"{r}_kepala", O[f"cermin_{r}"])):
            continue
        if any(visible(o) for n, o in O.items() if n.startswith(f"{r}_belakang")):
            continue
        a = acts[0]
        if a["type"] == "eat":
            continue                                                  # makan: pandang ke piring hanya di fase ambil (dicatat di laporan)
        off = Vector(eyes.location[:]) - Vector((eyes["rest"][0], 0, eyes["rest"][1]))
        down = any(visible(o) for n, o in O.items() if n.startswith(f"{r}_mata_bawah"))
        tested["pandang"] += 1
        ok = down or off.z < -1e-4
        stats["pandang_ke_bawah_saat_masak"].append(1.0 if ok else 0.0)
        if not ok:
            V["pandang_ke_kamera_saat_masak"].append((f, r, round(off.x, 4), round(off.z, 4)))
    # efek di depan wajah subjek
    s0 = shot_of(f)
    if s0 and s0["subjek"] in rigs and (f % 5 == 0 or step >= 5):
        r = s0["subjek"]; meta = metas[r]; u = U[r]
        M = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world
        fc = M @ Vector((0, -0.02, (meta["canvas"][1] - meta.get("eye", [500, 455])[1]) * u))
        fpx, fz = px(fc); edge, _ = px(M @ Vector((190 * u, -0.02, (meta["canvas"][1] - meta.get("eye", [500, 455])[1]) * u)))
        rr = abs(edge.x - fpx.x)
        dc = (fc - cam.matrix_world.translation).length
        tested["efek_wajah"] += 1
        for o in O:
            if not (o.name.startswith("daun") and not o.name.startswith("daun_") or o.name.startswith("uap")) or not visible(o):
                continue
            if o.get("jaga_wajah", 1.0) < 0.5:
                continue
            P = o.matrix_world.translation
            if (P - cam.matrix_world.translation).length >= dc:
                continue
            q, z = px(P)
            if z > 0 and (q - fpx).length < rr:
                V["efek_menutupi_wajah"].append((f, r, o.name))
    # wajah subjek terhalang (per shot, setiap 5 frame)
    s = shot_of(f)
    if s and s["subjek"] in rigs and (f % 5 == 0 or step >= 5):
        r = s["subjek"]
        if visible(O[f"{r}_wajah"]) or visible(O.get(f"{r}_wajah_q", O[f"{r}_wajah"])) or visible(O.get(f"{r}_wajah_s", O[f"{r}_wajah"])):
            meta = json.loads(open(bpy.path.abspath(f"//chars/{r}/rig.json")).read())
            u = [c for c in plan["characters"] if c["name"] == r][0]["height"] / meta["canvas"][1]
            M = O[f"cermin_{r}"].matrix_world
            W, H = meta["canvas"]
            blocked, n = [], 0
            for cx in (380, 500, 620):
                for cy in (380, 470, 590):
                    p = M @ Vector(((cx - W / 2) * u, -0.02, (H - cy) * u))
                    v = world_to_camera_view(sc, cam, p)
                    if not (0 <= v.x <= 1 and 0 <= v.y <= 1 and v.z > 0):
                        continue
                    n += 1
                    hit = ray_opaque(cam.matrix_world.translation, p, r + "_")
                    if hit:
                        blocked.append(hit)
            if n:
                frac = len(blocked) / n
                stats[f"wajah_terhalang_shot{s['n']}"].append(frac); tested["wajah"] += 1
                if frac > 0.12:
                    V["wajah_terhalang"].append((f, s["n"], r, round(frac, 2), sorted(set(blocked))[:4]))

# --- cek statis rencana (plan.json): mulut & kedip
for d in plan.get("dialogue", []):
    tested["rencana_mulut"] += 1
    if not d.get("n_event_buka"):
        V["dialog_tanpa_gerak_mulut"].append((d["baris"], d["tokoh"], d["f0"], d["f1"]))
for c in plan["characters"]:
    pf = c.get("perf")
    if not pf:
        continue
    ev = sorted(c.get("events", []))
    var = metas.get(c["name"], {}).get("variants", {})
    for (fa, v), nxt in zip(ev, ev[1:] + [(plan.get("end", 10 ** 6) + 1, None)]):
        fb = nxt[0]
        eyes_v = (var.get(v) or {}).get("eyes") if isinstance(var, dict) else None
        if eyes_v in ("open", "wide") and fb - fa > 120:
            tested["kedip"] += 1
            n = sum(1 for ff, val in pf.get("mata", []) if val == "closed" and fa <= ff < fb)
            if n == 0:
                V["emosi_tanpa_kedip"].append((c["name"], v, fa, fb))

RULES = [  # (cek, kunci pelanggaran, kategori, ambang WARN (jumlah), ambang FAIL (jumlah))
    ("lapisan", ["lapisan", "lapisan_belakang"], "geometri", 1, 6),
    ("melayang", ["melayang"], "layar", 1, 6),
    ("genggam", ["genggam_meleset", "telapak_genggam_tersembunyi"], "layar", 1, 10),
    ("lapisan", ["lengan_tersembunyi_saat_memegang"], "layar", 1, 10),
    ("sutil", ["sutil_di_luar_wajan"], "geometri", 1, 5),
    ("dudukan", ["pantat_tidak_di_dudukan", "dingklik_memotong_tokoh"], "geometri", 1, 6),
    ("tembus", ["tembus_tubuh"], "geometri", 1, 4),
    ("wajah", ["wajah_terhalang"], "layar", 1, 3),
    ("pandang", ["pandang_ke_kamera_saat_masak"], "performa", 3, 15),
    ("efek_wajah", ["efek_menutupi_wajah"], "layar", 1, 4),
    ("temporal", ["lompatan_benda"], "temporal", 1, 3),
    ("rencana_mulut", ["dialog_tanpa_gerak_mulut"], "rencana", 1, 1),
    ("kedip", ["emosi_tanpa_kedip"], "rencana", 1, 2),
]
status = {}
for chk, keys, cat, w, fl in RULES:
    n = sum(len(V.get(k, [])) for k in keys)
    name = f"{chk}:{'+'.join(keys)}"
    if not tested.get(chk):
        if chk == "temporal" and step > 1:
            status[name] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "kategori": cat, "alasan": "butuh frame berurutan (langkah 1)"}
        else:
            status[name] = {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE", "kategori": cat, "alasan": "tidak ada sampel yang relevan di rentang frame ini"}
    else:
        st_ = "FAIL" if n >= fl else ("WARN" if n >= w else "PASS")
        status[name] = {"status": st_, "kategori": cat, "pelanggaran": n, "sampel": tested[chk]}
# Phase 4.1-A4: genggam semantik dari interaksi.json, hanya frame yang benda-nya TERLIHAT saat dipegang
full = step == 1 and f_a <= 1 and f_b >= plan.get("end", f_b)
for n, pr in rep.get("props", {}).items():
    rows = rep.get("per_frame", {}).get(n, [])
    fr = vis.get("benda_" + n, set())
    if not full:
        status[f"orientasi_genggam:{n}"] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "kategori": "genggam", "alasan": "butuh semua frame (langkah 1)"}
        continue
    for key, res_ in (("orientasi_genggam", QA.orientation(rows, pr.get("toleransi_orientasi_derajat") or 15, fr)),
                      ("skala_fisik_benda", QA.physical_scale(rows, pr.get("skala_min_profil", 0.9), fr)),
                      ("tekuk_pergelangan", QA.wrist(pr.get("tekuk_pergelangan_per_frame", {}), fr)),
                      # Phase 4.2-A: inversi bertanda, kontak mulut, geser presentasi, tabrakan dunia, pergelangan kontak
                      ("inversi_genggam", QA.grip_inversion(pr.get("terbalik_tanpa_izin_frame", []), fr)),
                      ("kontak_mulut", QA.mouth_contact(pr.get("kontak_mulut_per_frame", {}), fr)),
                      ("geser_presentasi", QA.presentation_shift(pr.get("geser_presentasi_per_frame", {}),
                                                                 pr.get("geser_maks_profil_m"), fr)),
                      ("tabrakan_dunia", QA.world_collision(pr.get("tabrakan_dunia_per_frame", {}), fr)),
                      ("pergelangan_fase_kontak", QA.contact_wrist(pr.get("tekuk_pergelangan_per_frame", {}),
                                                                    pr.get("kontak_mulut_per_frame", {}), frames=fr))):
        status[f"{key}:{n}"] = {**res_, "kategori": "genggam"}
if full:
    allvis = set().union(*vis.values()) if vis else set()
    status["serah_terima"] = {**QA.handover(rep.get("serah_terima", [])), "kategori": "genggam"}
    status["serah_terima_tabrakan"] = {**QA.handover_collision(rep.get("serah_terima", [])), "kategori": "genggam"}
    status["cabang_siku"] = {**QA.elbow_branch(rep.get("baur_cabang_siku", []), allvis), "kategori": "temporal"}
    status["transisi_kepemilikan"] = {**QA.transitions(rep.get("transisi_kepemilikan", []), allvis), "kategori": "temporal"}
for chk in ("render_crush", "render_alpha", "audio_sinkron"):
    status[chk] = {"status": "NOT_TESTED", "kategori": "render", "alasan": "diuji di luar Blender: scripts/dev/qa_render.py"}

summary = {}
for k, v in stats.items():
    summary[k] = {"min": round(min(v), 4), "maks": round(max(v), 4), "rata2": round(sum(v) / len(v), 4), "n": len(v)}
res = {"frame": [f_a, f_b, step], "ringkasan": summary,
       "jumlah_pelanggaran": {k: len(v) for k, v in V.items()},
       "pelanggaran": {k: v[:60] for k, v in V.items()},
       "lompatan_benda_di_potongan_kamera_m": rep.get("lompatan_di_potongan_kamera_m"),
       "ik_meleset": len(rep.get("ik_miss", [])),
       "status": status,
       "status_ringkas": QA.summarize(status),
       "efek_jaga_wajah_build": rep.get("efek_jaga_wajah"), "build_id": rep.get("build_id"),
       "catatan": "PASS validator bukan bukti visual: lihat still diagnostik dengan build_id yang sama."}
open(out_path, "w").write(json.dumps(res, indent=1, default=str))
print("VALIDASI", json.dumps(res["jumlah_pelanggaran"]), json.dumps(res["status_ringkas"]))

# FILE [ALAT]: scripts/dev/validasi_kamera.py
# sha256: 8499afd40ed13287ef3650452b30624b924abec3b4a4087b3515d83134959c57
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.2-C/D: validasi kamera & komposisi (murni Python) dari build/kamera.json + plan.json + interaksi.json.
pakai: python scripts/dev/validasi_kamera.py stories/<cerita> [out.json]
Cek: komposisi_pita (okupansi/headroom/garis mata/letak per tipe shot), wajah_subjek (berpaling/tertutup pada shot
wajah), aksi_dalam_shot (serah-terima di shot yang membingkai kedua tokoh; heuristik)."""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
from engine import qa_rules as Q  # noqa: E402


def _eaters(plan, inter):
    """jendela makan/minum per tokoh (plan) + frame kontak cekung/bibir-mulut (interaksi.json)"""
    out = []
    for c in plan["characters"]:
        for a in c.get("rig_acts", []):
            if a["type"] != "eat":
                continue
            f1 = (a.get("taruh") or [a.get("f1", plan["end"])])[0]
            k = []
            kinds = {a.get("benda")} | ({"sendok"} if a.get("benda") in ("piring", "nasi_goreng", "mangkok") else set())
            for n, p in inter.get("props", {}).items():    # benda bisa berpindah tangan: cocokkan JENIS + jendela waktu
                if n.rsplit("_", 2)[0] in kinds:
                    k += [int(f) for f, v in p.get("kontak_mulut_per_frame", {}).items() if v[0] == "kontak" and a["f0"] <= int(f) <= f1]
            out.append({"nama": c["name"], "benda": a.get("benda"), "f0": a["f0"], "f1": f1, "kontak": k})
    return out


def main():
    st = Path(sys.argv[1]); b = st / "build"
    cam = json.loads((b / "kamera.json").read_text())
    plan = json.loads((b / "plan.json").read_text())
    inter = json.loads((b / "interaksi.json").read_text()) if (b / "interaksi.json").exists() else {}
    shots = [dict(s, ke_arah=s.get("ke_arah")) for s in plan["shots"]]
    cek = {"komposisi_pita": Q.composition_bands(cam.get("komposisi", [])),
           "wajah_subjek": Q.subject_face(cam.get("shots", [])),
           "aksi_dalam_shot": Q.action_in_shot(inter.get("serah_terima", []), shots),
           "suapan_dalam_shot": Q.eat_contact_in_shot(_eaters(plan, inter), shots)}
    cnt = {}
    for v in cek.values():
        cnt[v["status"]] = cnt.get(v["status"], 0) + 1
    rep = {"cerita": st.name, "build_id": cam.get("build_id"), "ringkas": cnt, "cek": cek}
    out = Path(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else b / "validasi_kamera.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=1))
    print(json.dumps({"cerita": st.name, "ringkas": cnt, **{k: v["status"] for k, v in cek.items()}}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE [ALAT]: tests/run_regression.py
# sha256: 44d6283f14f9287d6348b0ab64342dde8ce2a98e8a46fe701db9cc2e0f1bb6b6
==============================================================================================================
"""Jalankan semua regresi (tanpa Blender) dan tulis hasil JSON.
  .venv/bin/python tests/run_regression.py <keluaran.json> [plan_phase3_lama.json]
Isi: hasil tiap test (PASS/FAIL/ERROR/SKIP), tabel dialog benchmark (sesudah) dan perbandingan dengan plan Phase 3."""
import json
import sys
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))


class _R(unittest.TextTestResult):
    def __init__(self, *a, **k):
        super().__init__(*a, **k)
        self.rows = []

    def addSuccess(self, t):
        super().addSuccess(t); self.rows.append((t.id(), "PASS", ""))

    def addFailure(self, t, err):
        super().addFailure(t, err); self.rows.append((t.id(), "FAIL", self._exc_info_to_string(err, t)[-800:]))

    def addError(self, t, err):
        super().addError(t, err); self.rows.append((t.id(), "ERROR", self._exc_info_to_string(err, t)[-800:]))

    def addSkip(self, t, why):
        super().addSkip(t, why); self.rows.append((t.id(), "NOT_TESTED", why))


def talk_cover(plan):
    """per dialog: jumlah event mulut terbuka di plan (untuk plan lama dihitung dari events)"""
    out = []
    names = {"smiletalk", "talk", "surprisedtalk", "sadtalk", "angrytalk", "pointtalk", "wavetalk"}
    for c in plan["characters"]:
        ev = c["events"]
        out.append({"tokoh": c["name"], "event_bicara": [[f, v] for f, v in ev if v in names]})
    return out


def main():
    out = Path(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ROOT / "build" / "regresi.json"
    suite = unittest.defaultTestLoader.discover(str(ROOT / "tests"), top_level_dir=str(ROOT))
    t0 = time.time()
    res = unittest.TextTestRunner(resultclass=_R, verbosity=0).run(suite)
    from engine import plan as planner
    from engine.common import Story
    bench = ROOT / "stories" / "benchmark_warung"
    p = planner.build_plan(Story(bench))
    doc = {"dibuat": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), "durasi_dtk": round(time.time() - t0, 2),
           "ringkas": {"total": res.testsRun, "pass": sum(1 for r in res.rows if r[1] == "PASS"),
                       "fail": len(res.failures), "error": len(res.errors), "not_tested": len(res.skipped)},
           "tests": [{"id": i, "status": s, "detail": d} for i, s, d in res.rows],
           "dialog_benchmark_sesudah": [{k: v for k, v in d.items() if k != "kata"} | {"kata": d["kata"]} for d in p["dialogue"]]}
    if len(sys.argv) > 2 and Path(sys.argv[2]).exists():
        old = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text())
        cov_old = {c["tokoh"]: c["event_bicara"] for c in talk_cover(old)}
        cmp = []
        for d in p["dialogue"]:
            n_old = sum(1 for f, v in cov_old.get(d["tokoh"], []) if d["f0"] <= f <= d["f1"] and v == d["buka"])
            cmp.append({"tokoh": d["tokoh"], "baris": d["baris"], "rentang_frame": [d["f0"], d["f1"]], "varian_buka": d["buka"],
                        "event_buka_phase3": n_old, "event_buka_phase4": d["n_event_buka"],
                        "status": "PASS" if d["n_event_buka"] > 0 else "FAIL"})
        doc["perbandingan_phase3_vs_phase4"] = cmp
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False))
    print(json.dumps(doc["ringkas"]), "->", out)
    for r in doc.get("perbandingan_phase3_vs_phase4", []):
        print(" ", r)
    sys.exit(0 if res.wasSuccessful() else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE [ALAT]: tests/test_dialogue.py
# sha256: d4b160f21a2adbbc219b3696f782c45100d5e6c6382ab42bd410aec02fcd23e5

"""Regresi Phase 4A — waktu dialog & event mulut.
Jalankan:  .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_dialogue -v
(tanpa Blender; memakai story.yaml + timeline.json aktual benchmark dan cerita sintetis di direktori sementara)"""
import copy
import json
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from engine import plan as planner
from engine.common import Cues, Story, StoryError, frame, normalize_timeline, to_ms

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
BENCH = ROOT / "stories" / "benchmark_warung"

# timeline persis seperti yang dihasilkan tts.py Phase 3 (start & dur dibulatkan terpisah) -> memicu bug float
PHASE3_TL = {"total": 28.622, "lines": [
    {"id": "n1", "start": 0.0, "dur": 5.254, "words": [{"w": "Jam", "t0": 0.251, "t1": 0.606}, {"w": "malam", "t0": 1.027, "t1": 3.963}]},
    {"id": "n2", "start": 5.254, "dur": 4.126, "words": [{"w": "Cuma", "t0": 5.505, "t1": 5.782}, {"w": "menyala", "t0": 7.5, "t1": 8.097}]},
    {"id": "n3", "start": 9.38, "dur": 5.854, "words": [{"w": "Pak", "t0": 9.631, "t1": 10.223}, {"w": "pedas", "t0": 13.2, "t1": 13.937}]},
    {"id": "n4", "start": 15.234, "dur": 5.134, "words": [{"w": "Tumben", "t0": 15.484, "t1": 16.165}, {"w": "Jal", "t0": 16.456, "t1": 17.067},
                                                         {"w": "Buat", "t0": 18.003, "t1": 18.323}, {"w": "siapa", "t0": 18.4, "t1": 19.017}]},
    {"id": "n5", "start": 20.368, "dur": 8.254, "words": [{"w": "Buat", "t0": 20.618, "t1": 20.921}, {"w": "istri", "t0": 20.921, "t1": 21.236},
                                                         {"w": "tahun", "t0": 24.5, "t1": 24.923}]},
]}


def _events_in(ch, a, b):
    return [(f, v) for f, v in ch["events"] if a <= f <= b]


def _variant_at(ch, f):
    cur = None
    for ff, v in ch["events"]:
        if ff <= f:
            cur = v
    return cur


class TestTimeBase(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_float_artifact_reproduced(self):
        """dokumentasi bug: perbandingan float lama memasukkan awal n4 ke interval n3"""
        n3, n4 = PHASE3_TL["lines"][2], PHASE3_TL["lines"][3]
        self.assertEqual(n3["start"] + n3["dur"], 15.234000000000002)
        self.assertTrue(n3["start"] <= n4["start"] < n3["start"] + n3["dur"])          # perilaku lama (salah)

    def test_normalized_boundaries_are_contiguous(self):
        tl = normalize_timeline(copy.deepcopy(PHASE3_TL))
        L = tl["lines"]
        for a, b in zip(L, L[1:]):
            self.assertEqual(a["end_ms"], b["start_ms"], f"{a['id']}->{b['id']}")
        self.assertEqual(L[2]["end_ms"], 15234)
        self.assertEqual(L[3]["start_ms"], 15234)

    def test_line_at_half_open(self):
        cue = Cues(copy.deepcopy(PHASE3_TL))
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_at(15.234)["id"], "n4")
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_at(15.2334)["id"], "n3")           # 15233 ms (resolusi ms)
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_at(20.368)["id"], "n5")
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_at(0.0)["id"], "n1")

    def test_line_of_from_cue_id(self):
        cue = Cues(copy.deepcopy(PHASE3_TL))
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_of("n4"), "n4")
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_of("n4:buat"), "n4")
        self.assertEqual(cue.line_of("n5:tahun+0.2"), "n5")
        self.assertIsNone(cue.line_of(12.0))
        self.assertIsNone(cue.line_of("akhir"))

    def test_line_end_after_start(self):
        cue = Cues(copy.deepcopy(PHASE3_TL))
        for l in PHASE3_TL["lines"]:
            self.assertGreater(cue.line_end(l["id"]), cue(l["id"]))

    def test_frame_rounding_rule(self):
        self.assertEqual(frame(0.0), 1)
        self.assertEqual(frame(15.234), 458)           # 457.02 -> 457 (+1)
        self.assertEqual(frame(0.05), 3)               # 1.5 frame -> setengah ke atas (aturan eksplisit)
        self.assertEqual(frame(15.234000000000002), frame(15.234))
        self.assertEqual(to_ms(15.234000000000002), 15234)


def _make_story(tmp, data, tl):
    d = Path(tmp) / "s"
    (d / "build" / "audio").mkdir(parents=True)
    (d / "story.yaml").write_text(yaml.safe_dump(data, allow_unicode=True))
    (d / "build" / "audio" / "timeline.json").write_text(json.dumps(tl))
    return Story(d)


SYN = {"judul": "uji", "narasi": [{"id": l["id"], "teks": "x"} for l in PHASE3_TL["lines"]],
       "latar": {"lokasi": "jalan_kota", "waktu": "malam"},
       "tokoh": {"a": {"posisi": [-1, 2], "rupa": {}}, "b": {"posisi": [1, 2], "rupa": {}}},
       "shot": [{"mulai": 0, "kamera": "pembuka"}]}


class TestAdjacentDialogue(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmp)

    def _plan(self, acts):
        data = copy.deepcopy(SYN); data["aksi"] = acts
        return planner.build_plan(_make_story(self.tmp, data, copy.deepcopy(PHASE3_TL)))

    def _check_dialogue(self, p, who, line):
        dl = [d for d in p["dialogue"] if d["tokoh"] == who and d["baris"] == line]
        self.assertEqual(len(dl), 1, f"{who}/{line}: entri dialog")
        d = dl[0]
        self.assertGreater(d["f1"], d["f0"])
        self.assertGreater(d["n_event_buka"], 0)
        ch = next(c for c in p["characters"] if c["name"] == who)
        for w, w0, w1 in d["kata"]:                                     # tiap kata: mulut terbuka di dalam rentangnya
            vs = {_variant_at(ch, f) for f in range(w0, w1)}
            self.assertIn(d["buka"], vs, f"{who}/{line} kata '{w}' f{w0}-{w1}: tanpa mulut terbuka ({vs})")
        return d

    def test_adjacent_lines_both_speakers(self):
        p = self._plan([{"tokoh": "a", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": "n3", "ekspresi": "senyum"},
                        {"tokoh": "b", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": "n4", "ekspresi": "kaget", "lalu": "senyum"},
                        {"tokoh": "a", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": "n5", "ekspresi": "senyum"}])
        self._check_dialogue(p, "a", "n3")
        d4 = self._check_dialogue(p, "b", "n4")
        d5 = self._check_dialogue(p, "a", "n5")
        self.assertEqual(d4["buka"], "surprisedtalk")
        self.assertEqual(d5["buka"], "smiletalk")
        self.assertEqual(len(d4["kata"]), 4)                            # hanya kata n4
        self.assertTrue(all(w0 >= d4["f0"] for _, w0, _ in d4["kata"]))

    def test_word_cue_start(self):
        p = self._plan([{"tokoh": "b", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": "n4:buat", "ekspresi": "normal"}])
        d = self._check_dialogue(p, "b", "n4")
        self.assertEqual([w for w, _, _ in d["kata"]], ["Buat", "siapa"])

    def test_numeric_start_on_exact_boundary(self):
        p = self._plan([{"tokoh": "b", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": 15.234, "ekspresi": "normal"}])
        self._check_dialogue(p, "b", "n4")

    def test_invalid_range_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(StoryError):
            self._plan([{"tokoh": "b", "lakukan": "bicara", "dari": "n4", "sampai": "n3"}])


@unittest.skipUnless((BENCH / "build" / "audio" / "timeline.json").exists(), "timeline benchmark belum ada")
class TestBenchmark(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_benchmark_dialogue(self):
        p = planner.build_plan(Story(BENCH))
        story = yaml.safe_load((BENCH / "story.yaml").read_text())
        talks = [a for a in story["aksi"] if a["lakukan"] == "bicara"]
        self.assertEqual(len(p["dialogue"]), len(talks))
        for d in p["dialogue"]:
            ch = next(c for c in p["characters"] if c["name"] == d["tokoh"])
            self.assertGreater(d["f1"], d["f0"], d)
            self.assertGreater(d["n_event_buka"], 0, d)
            for w, w0, w1 in d["kata"]:
                self.assertIn(d["buka"], {_variant_at(ch, f) for f in range(w0, w1)}, (d["tokoh"], d["baris"], w))
        by = {(d["tokoh"], d["baris"]) for d in p["dialogue"]}
        self.assertIn(("darto", "n4"), by)
        self.assertIn(("jali", "n5"), by)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

# FILE [ALAT]: tests/test_phase4.py

"""Regression test Phase 4B/4C: modul murni Python (tanpa Blender).
Jalankan: .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_phase4 -v"""
import math
import unittest

from engine import grip as G
from engine import performance as PF
from engine import schedule as SCH
from engine import seats


class TestSchedule(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_eat_cycles_cover_span_exactly(self):
        for seed in range(20):
            cyc = SCH.eat_cycles(10, 250, seed)
            self.assertEqual(cyc[0][0], 10)
            self.assertEqual(sum(L for _, L in cyc), 240)
            for (a, L), (b, _) in zip(cyc, cyc[1:]):
                self.assertEqual(a + L, b)
            self.assertTrue(all(L >= 24 for _, L in cyc))

    def test_eat_cycles_seeded(self):
        self.assertEqual(SCH.eat_cycles(1, 300, 7), SCH.eat_cycles(1, 300, 7))
        self.assertNotEqual(SCH.eat_cycles(1, 300, 7), SCH.eat_cycles(1, 300, 8))

    def test_place_window_never_straddles_cut(self):
        # benchmark: sendok ditaruh tepat sebelum potongan f282 (Phase 3: transisi tersembunyi di potongan)
        f0, f1, note = SCH.place_window(282, SCH.PLACE + SCH.RETRACT, [1, 75, 159, 199, 226, 282], 860)
        self.assertLessEqual(f1, 282 - SCH.MARGIN)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(f0, 226 + SCH.MARGIN)
        self.assertEqual(f1 - f0, SCH.PLACE + SCH.RETRACT)

    def test_fit_phase(self):
        cuts = [1, 100, 200]
        self.assertEqual(SCH.fit_phase(130, 20, cuts, 300)[0], 130)            # bebas potongan
        nf, _ = SCH.fit_phase(195, 24, cuts, 300)                               # menabrak f200 dekat awal -> sesudah
        self.assertGreaterEqual(nf, 200 + SCH.MARGIN)
        nf, _ = SCH.fit_phase(170, 24, cuts, 300)                               # selesai sebelum potongan
        self.assertLessEqual(nf + 24, 200 - SCH.MARGIN)

    def test_cycle_at_normalized(self):
        cyc = [[10, 60]]
        k, L = SCH.cycle_at(40, cyc)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(k, 24.0)
        self.assertIsNone(SCH.cycle_at(70, cyc))


class TestGrip(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_profiles_exist_for_spec_props(self):
        for kind in ("piring", "sendok", "sutil", "gelas", "hp", "kardus", "bungkus", "helm"):
            p = G.profile(kind)
            self.assertIn(p["gaya"], G.STYLES, kind)
            self.assertIn("jarak", p)

    def test_hand_angle_power_is_axis_independent_of_sign(self):
        a = G.hand_angle((0, 1), "power")
        b = G.hand_angle((0, -1), "power")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(G.wrap_deg(a - b) % 180, 0.0, places=6)

    def test_hand_angle_pinch_points_fingers_along_axis(self):
        # sumbu ke bawah layar (y-down): tangan istirahat sudah menunjuk ke bawah -> 0 derajat
        self.assertAlmostEqual(G.hand_angle((0, 1), "pinch"), 0.0, places=6)
        v = G.rot_canvas((0, 1), G.hand_angle((1, 0), "pinch"))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(v[0], 1.0, places=6)

    def test_unknown_prop_has_default(self):
        self.assertIn("gaya", G.profile("benda_tak_dikenal"))


class TestSeats(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_kursi_anchor_height_and_facing(self):
        a = seats.anchors_for({"jenis": "kursi", "posisi": [1, 2], "arah": 90, "nama": "k"})[0]
        self.assertEqual(a["name"], "k.duduk")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a["pos"][2], 0.475)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a["maju"][0], 0.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a["maju"][1], -1.0, places=6)

    def test_multi_slot(self):
        names = [a["name"] for a in seats.anchors_for({"jenis": "sofa", "posisi": [0, 0], "nama": "s"})]
        self.assertEqual(names, ["s.duduk", "s.duduk1", "s.duduk2"])

    def test_find(self):
        props = [{"jenis": "bangku", "posisi": [0, 0], "nama": "b"}]
        self.assertIsNotNone(seats.find(props, "b.duduk2"))
        self.assertIsNone(seats.find(props, "b.duduk9"))


class TestPerformance(unittest.TestCase):
    def _build(self, seed=3):
        chars = [{"name": "a", "rig": True, "cat": False, "events": [[1, "neutral"], [200, "smile"]]},
                 {"name": "b", "rig": True, "cat": False, "events": [[1, "surprised"]]}]
        V = {"a": {"neutral": {"eyes": "open"}, "smile": {"eyes": "happy"}}, "b": {"surprised": {"eyes": "wide"}}}
        dlg = [{"tokoh": "a", "f0": 40, "f1": 120, "kata": [["x", 40, 60], ["y", 62, 80], ["z", 100, 120]]}]
        return PF.build(chars, dlg, V, 400, seed, {"a": [(1, 401, -1)]})

    def test_blinks_in_every_open_eyed_emotion(self):
        out = self._build()
        self.assertTrue(any(v == "closed" and f < 200 for f, v in out["a"]["mata"]))        # neutral
        self.assertTrue(any(v == "closed" for f, v in out["b"]["mata"]))                    # surprised (wide) juga berkedip

    def test_listener_reacts_and_speaker_gestures_with_free_hand_only(self):
        out = self._build()
        self.assertTrue(out["b"]["alis"])                                                   # pendengar: alis naik
        self.assertTrue(out["b"]["angguk"])
        for g0, g1, hs, kind in out["a"]["gestur"]:
            self.assertEqual(hs, 1)                                                         # tangan -1 sibuk

    def test_seeded(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._build(3), self._build(3))
        self.assertNotEqual(self._build(3)["a"]["mata"], self._build(4)["a"]["mata"])

    def test_nod_curve(self):
        self.assertEqual(PF.nod_at([[10, 6.0]], 9), 0.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(PF.nod_at([[10, 6.0]], 10 + 4.5), 6.0 * math.sin(math.pi * 4.5 / 9), places=6)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""Regression test Phase 4.1-A4: validator HARUS menandai kasus gagal Phase 4 yang dulu lolos (false negative).
Angka diambil dari build P4-1472c66e2e / P4-2297686747 (interaksi.json):
  - galat orientasi tangan sutil ~40° (median 39,7°)
  - piring dipegang dengan skala presentasi 0,74x
  - serah-terima gelas: residual 0,148 m (110,8 px) di frame perpindahan f325
Jalankan: .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_phase41 -v"""
import unittest

from engine import grip as G
from engine import qa_rules as QA


def rows(n, ori=0.0, k=1.0, res_m=0.0, res_px=0.0, mode="hand", f0=1):
    return [[f0 + i, res_m, res_px, k, mode, ori] for i in range(n)]


class TestKnownFailuresAreDetected(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_utensil_orientation_40deg_fails(self):
        tol = G.profile("sutil")["toleransi_orientasi"]
        self.assertEqual(tol, 10)
        r = QA.orientation(rows(100, ori=39.7), tol)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(r["di_atas_toleransi"], 100)

    def test_orientation_within_tolerance_passes(self):
        r = QA.orientation(rows(100, ori=0.5), 10)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "PASS")

    def test_orientation_single_spike_warns_not_passes(self):
        rr = rows(100, ori=0.5)
        rr[50][5] = 15.0                                    # 1 frame di atas toleransi, < 2x toleransi
        self.assertEqual(QA.orientation(rr, 10)["status"], "WARN")
        rr[50][5] = 52.0                                    # > 2x toleransi -> FAIL walau hanya 1 frame
        self.assertEqual(QA.orientation(rr, 10)["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_plate_scale_074_fails(self):
        smin = G.profile("piring")["presentasi"]["skala_min"]
        self.assertEqual(smin, 1.0)
        r = QA.physical_scale(rows(80, k=0.7404), smin)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r["skala_min_terukur"], 0.7404, places=4)

    def test_plate_scale_one_passes(self):
        self.assertEqual(QA.physical_scale(rows(80, k=1.0), 1.0)["status"], "PASS")

    def test_handover_0148m_fails(self):
        ev = [{"benda": "teh", "frame": 325, "residual_pemberi_px": 58.9, "residual_penerima_px": 110.8,
               "lompatan_m": 0.148, "residual_maks_m": 0.1476}]
        r = QA.handover(ev)
        self.assertEqual(r["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(len(r["serah"][0]["alasan"]), 3)

    def test_handover_reachable_continuous_passes(self):
        ev = [{"benda": "teh", "frame": 325, "residual_pemberi_px": 0.0, "residual_penerima_px": 0.0,
               "lompatan_m": 0.0095, "residual_maks_m": 0.0}]
        self.assertEqual(QA.handover(ev)["status"], "PASS")

    def test_pick_jump_fails(self):
        ev = [{"benda": "teh", "frame": 131, "lompatan_m": 0.2836, "residual_maks_px": 74.9}]
        self.assertEqual(QA.transitions(ev)["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_wrist_bend_at_limit_fails(self):
        self.assertEqual(QA.wrist({f: 74.9 for f in range(100)})["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(QA.wrist({f: 20.0 for f in range(100)})["status"], "PASS")


class TestStatusSeparation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_not_applicable_vs_not_tested(self):
        self.assertIn("NOT_APPLICABLE", QA.STATUSES)
        self.assertIn("NOT_TESTED", QA.STATUSES)
        self.assertEqual(QA.handover([])["status"], "NOT_APPLICABLE")
        self.assertEqual(QA.orientation([], 10)["status"], "NOT_APPLICABLE")
        self.assertEqual(QA.transitions([])["status"], "NOT_APPLICABLE")

    def test_only_visible_frames_count(self):
        rr = rows(10, ori=40.0)                             # benda di luar bingkai: tidak relevan
        self.assertEqual(QA.orientation(rr, 10, frames=set())["status"], "NOT_APPLICABLE")
        self.assertEqual(QA.orientation(rr, 10, frames={1, 2})["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_summary_counts_all_statuses(self):
        s = QA.summarize({"a": {"status": "PASS"}, "b": {"status": "NOT_APPLICABLE"}, "c": {"status": "NOT_TESTED"}})
        self.assertEqual(s["PASS"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(s["NOT_APPLICABLE"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(s["NOT_TESTED"], 1)


class TestGripProfiles41(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_tolerances_per_grip_type(self):
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("sutil")["toleransi_orientasi"], 10)
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("sendok")["toleransi_orientasi"], 10)
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("piring")["toleransi_orientasi"], 20)
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("gelas")["toleransi_orientasi"], 25)
        for k in ("hp", "kardus", "bungkus", "helm"):
            self.assertIn("toleransi_orientasi", G.profile(k))

    def test_dof_declared_for_grips_with_free_parameter(self):
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("sutil")["dof"], "arah_gagang")
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("piring")["dof"], "sudut_tepi")
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("gelas")["dof"], "sudut_badan")
        self.assertIsNone(G.profile("hp")["dof"])

    def test_signed_power_grip_tip_on_thumb_side(self):
        # ibu jari sprite di x = -side; ujung alat ke kiri layar (-x) -> tangan kanan (side +1) tanpa rotasi
        self.assertAlmostEqual(G.hand_angle((-1, 0), "power", side=1), 0.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(G.hand_angle((1, 0), "power", side=-1), 0.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(abs(G.hand_angle((1, 0), "power", side=1)), 180.0, places=6)

    def test_physical_size_constant_for_plates_and_cups(self):
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("piring")["presentasi"]["skala_min"], 1.0)
        self.assertEqual(G.profile("teh")["presentasi"]["skala_min"], 1.0)


class TestEyeStatesAndProfile41(unittest.TestCase):
    """Phase 4.1-B: 'bawah' (fokus) harus tampak TERBUKA, tidak sama dengan 'ngantuk'."""

    @staticmethod
    def ink(eyes):
        import numpy as np
        from engine import chars as C
        c = C.Canvas()
        C.eye_shape(c, 500, 455, eyes, 1.0, sx=1)
        a = np.array(c.im.getchannel("A")) > 128
        rows = np.where(a.any(axis=1))[0]
        return a.sum(), (rows.max() - rows.min()) if len(rows) else 0

    def test_focus_down_is_open_not_sleepy(self):
        area_open, h_open = self.ink("open")
        area_down, h_down = self.ink("bawah")
        area_sleepy, h_sleepy = self.ink("ngantuk")
        self.assertGreater(area_down, 0.75 * area_open)        # fokus: mata tetap terbuka
        self.assertLess(area_sleepy, 0.65 * area_open)         # ngantuk: setengah tertutup
        self.assertGreater(area_down, 1.3 * area_sleepy)

    def test_all_eye_states_distinct(self):
        import numpy as np
        from engine import chars as C
        ims = {}
        for e in ("bawah", "ngantuk", "sedih", "closed", "happy"):
            c = C.Canvas(); C.eye_shape(c, 500, 455, e, 1.0, sx=1)
            ims[e] = np.array(c.im.getchannel("A")) > 128
        keys = list(ims)
        for i in range(len(keys)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(keys)):
                a, b = ims[keys[i]], ims[keys[j]]
                iou = (a & b).sum() / max(1, (a | b).sum())
                self.assertLess(iou, 0.8, f"{keys[i]} vs {keys[j]} terlalu mirip (IoU {iou:.2f})")

    def test_rig_declares_new_states_and_profile(self):
        from engine import rig2d as R
        for e in ("bawah", "ngantuk", "sedih", "closed", "happy"):
            self.assertIn(e, R.EYES)
        self.assertIn("fokus", R.BROWS)
        self.assertEqual(set(R.SHOULDER_S), {-1, 1})
        self.assertLess(R.SHOULDER_S[1][0] - R.SHOULDER_S[-1][0], R.SHOULDER_Q[1][0] - R.SHOULDER_Q[-1][0])

    def test_sad_expression_uses_sad_eyes(self):
        from engine import chars as C
        self.assertEqual(C.EXPR["sad"][0], "sedih")
        self.assertEqual(C.EXPR["sleepy"][0], "ngantuk")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""Regression test Phase 4.2 (gerbang A): kasus gagal Phase 4.1 yang dilaporkan reviewer HARUS tertangkap.
Angka dari build P4-4b69684991 / P4-21e3aadc90:
  - 178 frame sendok & 140 frame sutil: target genggam dibalik 180° TANPA mencerminkan tangan (ibu jari di sisi salah)
  - f240: cekung sendok di dagu, bukan di mulut
  - piring: geser presentasi 0,0757 m (tak berbatas) di 538 frame
  - piring Jali masuk papan meja samping gerobak (~10 cm)
  - Sri f500-501: pergelangan 74,8°
Jalankan: .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_phase42 -v"""
import math
import unittest

from engine import actions as ACT
from engine import grip as G
from engine import qa_rules as QA


class TestSignedGrip(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_thumb_side_matches_tip_for_both_variants(self):
        """untuk setiap arah alat & sisi tubuh: varian pronasi (theta = hand_angle) dan supinasi (theta + 180,
        tangan dicerminkan) keduanya menempatkan IBU JARI ke arah ujung alat"""
        for side in (-1, 1):
            for deg in range(0, 360, 15):
                ax = (math.cos(math.radians(deg)), math.sin(math.radians(deg)))
                th = G.hand_angle(ax, "power", side)
                for sup, t in ((False, th), (True, th + 180)):
                    tdx, tdy = G.thumb_dir(t, side, sup)
                    self.assertGreater(tdx * ax[0] + tdy * ax[1], 0.99, (side, deg, sup))

    def test_phase41_flip_without_mirror_is_inversion(self):
        """Phase 4.1: theta = th + 180 TANPA cermin -> ibu jari menjauhi ujung alat (inversi tidak sah)"""
        ax = (1.0, -0.4)
        th = G.hand_angle(ax, "power", -1)
        tdx, tdy = G.thumb_dir(th + 180, -1, supinated=False)
        self.assertLess(tdx * ax[0] + tdy * ax[1], 0)

    def test_grip_inversion_rule(self):
        self.assertEqual(QA.grip_inversion(list(range(75, 253)))["status"], "FAIL")   # 178 frame sendok
        self.assertEqual(QA.grip_inversion([])["status"], "PASS")

    def test_utensil_profile_declares_forearm_rotation(self):
        self.assertIn("supinasi", G.profile("sendok")["rotasi_lengan"])
        self.assertIn("pronasi", G.profile("sutil")["rotasi_lengan"])


class TestEatPrimitive(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_phases_cover_cycle(self):
        k = 0
        for name, a, b in ACT.EAT_PHASES:
            self.assertEqual(a, k); k = b
        self.assertEqual(k, ACT.CYCLE)
        self.assertEqual([n for n, _, _ in ACT.EAT_PHASES],
                         ["sendok", "angkat", "dekat_mulut", "kontak", "tarik", "kembali", "istirahat"])

    def test_spoon_levelled_before_it_rises(self):
        """akar masalah f240: tangan mendahului cekung ke wajah. Orientasi 'di mulut' harus tercapai lebih dulu."""
        for i in range(0, 480):
            st = ACT.eat(i / 10)
            if st["fase"] in ("angkat", "dekat_mulut"):
                self.assertGreaterEqual(st["level"] + 1e-6, min(1.0, st["s"] / 0.82), st)

    def test_contact_at_mouth_and_mouth_open(self):
        for k in range(18, 23):
            st = ACT.eat(k)
            self.assertEqual(st["fase"], "kontak"); self.assertEqual(st["s"], 1.0); self.assertEqual(st["maju"], 0.0)
            self.assertEqual(st["mulut"], "buka")

    def test_contact_frames(self):

    def test_drink_lip_contact_phase(self):
        self.assertEqual(ACT.drink(20)["fase"], "kontak")
        self.assertEqual(ACT.drink(20)["s"], 1.0)

class TestProvenanceHardening(unittest.TestCase):
    def _mk(self, tmp, cfg_ok=True, manifest_scene=True):
        import json
        from pathlib import Path
        from engine import provenance as PV
        b = Path(tmp) / "st" / "build"; fr = b / "frames_final"; fr.mkdir(parents=True)
        (b.parent / "story.yaml").write_text("judul: x\n"); (b / "plan.json").write_text("{}")
        (b / "scene.blend").write_bytes(b"blend")
        man = {"build_id": "P4-x", "inputs": {"source_hash": "src"},
               "scene_build": {"scene_blend_sha256": PV.sha256_file(b / "scene.blend")} if manifest_scene else {}}
        (b / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(man))
        (fr / "f_0001.png").write_bytes(b"png1")
        PV.record_chunk(fr, b / "scene.blend", [(1, "f_0001.png")], {"engine": "EEVEE"}, "lokal")
        if not cfg_ok:
            (b.parent / "story.yaml").write_text("judul: diubah\n")
        return PV, fr

    def test_config_hash_change_fails(self):
        import tempfile
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            PV, fr = self._mk(t, cfg_ok=False)
            r = PV.validate(fr, 1, 1, "P4-x")
            self.assertEqual(r["cek"]["config_hash_sama"]["status"], "FAIL")
            self.assertEqual(r["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_missing_scene_identity_is_fail_not_pass(self):
        import tempfile
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            PV, fr = self._mk(t, manifest_scene=False)
            r = PV.validate(fr, 1, 1, "P4-x")
            self.assertEqual(r["cek"]["checksum_adegan_sama"]["status"], "FAIL")
            self.assertEqual(r["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_clean_chunk_passes(self):
        import tempfile
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            PV, fr = self._mk(t)
            self.assertEqual(PV.validate(fr, 1, 1, "P4-x")["status"], "PASS")


class TestSeatFootprint(unittest.TestCase):
    """Phase 4.2-D: bangku memanjang dilihat serong -> bidang sprite harus di depan seluruh jejak dudukan"""
    def test_bench_exposes_box_and_support_exceeds_radius(self):
        import math
        from engine import seats
        a = seats.find([{"jenis": "bangku", "nama": "b", "posisi": [0, 0], "arah": 0}], "b.duduk1")
        self.assertIn("setengah", a)
        hx, hy = a["setengah"]
        vx, vy = math.cos(math.radians(20)), math.sin(math.radians(20))   # kamera 20 derajat dari normal bangku
        support = abs(vx) * hx + abs(vy) * hy
        self.assertGreater(support, a["lebar"] + 0.15)       # cara lama (jari-jari 0,2) menutupi kaki

    def test_end_slot_footprint_includes_offset(self):
        from engine import seats
        a = seats.find([{"jenis": "bangku", "nama": "b", "posisi": [0, 0]}], "b.duduk")   # slot ujung (y -0,45)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a["setengah"][1], 0.75 + 0.45)

    def test_round_stool_unchanged(self):
        from engine import seats
        self.assertNotIn("setengah", seats.find([{"jenis": "kursi", "nama": "k", "posisi": [0, 0]}], "k.duduk"))

        from engine import qa_rules as Q
        shots = [{"n": 4, "f0": 300, "f1": 440, "kamera": "mcu", "subjek": "wulan", "ke_arah": None}]
        ev = [{"frame": 398, "pemberi": "wulan", "penerima": "harjo", "benda": "kopi"}]
        self.assertEqual(Q.action_in_shot(ev, shots)["status"], "WARN")


class TestEatVisibility(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_contact_anchored_into_eater_shot(self):
        from engine import schedule as SC
        cyc = SC.eat_cycles(1, 282, 7123)
        shot = (226, 281)
        cyc2, notes = SC.eat_cycles_visible(1, 282, 7123, [shot])
        self.assertTrue(SC.contact_in(cyc2, shot[0] + 6, shot[1] - 6))
        self.assertEqual(sum(L for _, L in cyc2), 281)          # tetap menutup rentang persis
        self.assertTrue(all(L >= 24 for _, L in cyc2))

    def test_no_change_when_already_visible(self):
        from engine import schedule as SC
        cyc = SC.eat_cycles(1, 300, 5)
        a, b = next((c0, c0 + L) for c0, L in cyc)
        self.assertEqual(SC.eat_cycles_visible(1, 300, 5, [(a, b + 10)])[0], cyc)


class TestEatShotRule(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_eating_shot_without_bite_warns(self):
        from engine import qa_rules as Q                  # benchmark sebelum penjadwal sadar-shot: kontak hanya f222-226
        shots = [{"n": 5, "f0": 226, "f1": 281, "kamera": "medium", "subjek": "jali"}]
        e = [{"nama": "jali", "benda": "piring", "f0": 1, "f1": 254, "kontak": [118, 170, 222, 223, 224, 225, 226]}]
        self.assertEqual(Q.eat_contact_in_shot(e, shots)["status"], "WARN")
        e[0]["kontak"] += [233, 234, 235]
        self.assertEqual(Q.eat_contact_in_shot(e, shots)["status"], "PASS")

# FILE [ALAT]: tests/test_phase42_b.py
# sha256: d2681de284a68403ee0afc2160d2387f7d0211a74ed8404d241c63660869629c
==============================================================================================================
"""Regression test Phase 4.2-B: lapisan khusus kulit (blush) TIDAK boleh keluar dari kulit wajah tampak yang sama.
Kasus reviewer: Sri (berhijab) close-up f420/f475 — blush menimpa hijab. Diuji di GENERATOR sprite (bukan gambar render).
Jalankan: .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_phase42_b -v"""
import unittest

import numpy as np

from engine import chars as C


def _blush_overflow(sp, view, masked=True):
    """jumlah piksel blush (lapisan wajah) di luar kulit; blush digambar tanpa detail lain"""
    turn = {"front": 0, "q": 1, "s": 2}[view]
    m = C.skin_mask(sp, view)
    c = C.Canvas()
    det = {"kulit": sp["kulit"], "tua": False, "skin_mask": m} if masked else None
    yo = 30 if sp["hijab"] else 0
    if turn >= C.PROFILE_TURN:
        C._skin_only(c, det, lambda cc: cc.ellipse(C.HX + 112, 548 + yo, 40, 28, (236, 120, 110, 150), outline=False))
    else:
        tx = int(70 * turn)
        C._skin_only(c, det, lambda cc: [cc.ellipse(x, 545 + yo, 52 * k, 30, (236, 120, 110, 150), outline=False)
                                         for x, k in ((330 + tx + int(35 * turn), 1 - 0.35 * turn),
                                                      (670 + int(tx * 0.45), 1 - 0.2 * turn))])
    a = np.asarray(c.im)[..., 3] > 8
    skin = np.asarray(C.skin_mask(sp, view)) > 0
    return int((a & ~skin).sum()), int(a.sum())


class TestSkinMask(unittest.TestCase):
    SPECS = {
        "berhijab": {"kulit": "langsat", "hijab": "pink", "baju": "krem"},
        "helm": {"kulit": "sawo", "topi": ["helm", "hijau"], "baju": "hijau"},
        "rambut": {"kulit": "sawo", "rambut": ["cepak", "hitam"], "baju": "kuning"},
    }

    def test_blush_never_outside_skin(self):
        for nm, raw in self.SPECS.items():
            sp = C.normalize(raw)
            for view in ("front", "q", "s"):
                out, tot = _blush_overflow(sp, view)
                self.assertGreater(tot, 0, (nm, view))
                self.assertEqual(out, 0, (nm, view, out, tot))

    def test_unmasked_hijab_overflows(self):
        """reproduksi Phase 4.1: tanpa mask, blush tokoh berhijab keluar dari kulit pada sedikitnya satu tampak"""
        raw = self.SPECS["berhijab"]
        sp = C.normalize(raw)
        self.assertTrue(any(_blush_overflow(sp, v, masked=False)[0] > 0 for v in ("front", "q", "s")))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

# FILE [ALAT]: tests/test_qa_render.py
# sha256: a445c27667869183f5ac1e5d5f775b8a3f8f2fd2f815550fc91dd995dc2ce6c2
==============================================================================================================
"""Phase 4.1-D: QA pasca-render harus MENDETEKSI kasus buruk buatan (bukan hanya lolos pada data baik).
  - suara tergeser 120 ms dari timestamp -> sinkron dialog FAIL; tanpa geser -> PASS
  - satu frame lebih terang di tengah shot -> flicker FAIL; video stabil -> PASS
  - frame mentah hilang -> frame_lengkap FAIL
Jalankan: .venv/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_qa_render -v  (butuh ffmpeg)"""
import importlib.util
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
import wave
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("qa_render", ROOT / "scripts" / "dev" / "qa_render.py")
QR = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(QR)
SR = 16000


def write_wav(p, x):
    with wave.open(str(p), "wb") as w:
        w.setnchannels(1); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(SR)
        w.writeframes((np.clip(x, -1, 1) * 32767).astype(np.int16).tobytes())


def syllables(dur, seed):
    """sinyal mirip ucapan: ledakan nada 300-2000 Hz dengan jeda tak teratur"""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.arange(int(dur * SR)) / SR
    x = np.zeros_like(t)
    s = 0.1
    while s < dur - 0.2:
        L = rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25)
        m = (t >= s) & (t < s + L)
        f = rng.uniform(300, 1500)
        x[m] += 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t[m]) * np.hanning(m.sum())
        s += L + rng.uniform(0.03, 0.2)
    return x


class Base(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        (self.d / "build" / "audio").mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.d / "build" / "frames_penuh").mkdir(parents=True)

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.d)


class TestDialogueSync(Base):
    def make(self, shift_s):
        lines, total = [], 6.0
        mix = np.zeros(int(total * SR))
        for k, st in enumerate((0.5, 3.0)):
            x = syllables(2.0, k)
            p = self.d / "build" / "audio" / f"n{k + 1}.wav"
            write_wav(p, x)
            lines.append({"id": f"n{k + 1}", "start_ms": int(st * 1000), "audio": str(p),
                          "words": [{"w": "a", "t0": st + QR.PAD_BEFORE + 0.1, "t1": st + 0.5}]})
            i = int((st + QR.PAD_BEFORE + shift_s) * SR)
            mix[i:i + len(x)] += x
        mix += 0.01 * np.random.default_rng(9).standard_normal(len(mix))
        write_wav(self.d / "build" / "audio" / "mix.wav", mix)
        (self.d / "build" / "audio" / "timeline.json").write_text(json.dumps({"lines": lines}))
        return self.d / "build" / "audio" / "mix.wav"

    def test_shifted_voice_fails(self):
        v = self.make(0.12)
        r = QR.check_dialogue(self.d, {"fps": 30, "dialogue": []}, v)
        self.assertEqual(r["geser_audio_vs_timestamp"]["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_aligned_voice_passes(self):
        v = self.make(0.0)
        r = QR.check_dialogue(self.d, {"fps": 30, "dialogue": []}, v)
        self.assertEqual(r["geser_audio_vs_timestamp"]["status"], "PASS")

    def test_animation_word_event_offset_fails(self):
        self.make(0.0)
        tl = json.loads((self.d / "build" / "audio" / "timeline.json").read_text())
        t = tl["lines"][0]["words"][0]["t0"]
        plan = {"fps": 30, "dialogue": [{"baris": "n1", "kata": [["a", round(t * 30 + 1) + 4, round(t * 30 + 1) + 10]]}]}
        r = QR.check_dialogue(self.d, plan, None)
        self.assertEqual(r["event_kata_vs_timestamp_tts"]["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(r["geser_audio_vs_timestamp"]["status"], "NOT_TESTED")


class TestFlickerAndFrames(Base):
    def video(self, levels):
        n = len(levels)
        fr = self.d / "build" / "frames_penuh"
        for i, lv in enumerate(levels):
            Image.new("RGB", (108, 192), (int(lv),) * 3).save(fr / f"f_{i + 1:04d}.png")
        out = self.d / "v.mp4"
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-framerate", "30", "-i", str(fr / "f_%04d.png"),
                        "-f", "lavfi", "-t", f"{n / 30:.4f}", "-i", "anullsrc=r=48000:cl=mono",
                        "-c:v", "libx264", "-crf", "1", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest", str(out)], check=True)
        return out, {"fps": 30, "end": n, "shots": [{"f0": 1}]}

    def test_flicker_detected(self):
        lv = [100.0] * 90
        lv[45] = 112.0
        v, plan = self.video(lv)
        r = QR.check_video(self.d, plan, v, 1, 90)
        self.assertEqual(r["flicker_temporal"]["status"], "FAIL")

    def test_steady_passes(self):
        v, plan = self.video([100.0] * 90)
        r = QR.check_video(self.d, plan, v, 1, 90)
        self.assertEqual(r["flicker_temporal"]["status"], "PASS")
        self.assertEqual(r["durasi_av"]["status"], "PASS")

    def test_missing_frame_fails(self):
        self.video([100.0] * 30)
        (self.d / "build" / "frames_penuh" / "f_0010.png").unlink()
        r = QR.check_raw_frames(self.d, {"fps": 30, "end": 30}, 1, 30)
        self.assertEqual(r["frame_lengkap"]["status"], "FAIL")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
